### 1. [What Does a Zoom Actually Add? Decomposing Fine-Grained Perception Failures in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26904v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26904

**作者：** Zhixuan Ge、Anqi Li、Kexin Geng、Wei Qiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26904v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26904v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把 VLM 中「放大后答对」的失败逐例归因到最廉价的修复手段——复用缓存 token、隔离区域、对已见像素重新 tokenize、获取新像素，并在三个模型、三个基准的 2753 个样本上比较不同首过像素预算下的归因分布。

**相关性简评：** 直接检验「模型缺像素」这一与视觉细节保留、证据获取和再渲染预算相关的核心假设，其分层归因与放大倍率饱和结果可供核查 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中 LOOK 的真实收益来源。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does a Zoom Actually Add? Decomposing Fine-Grained Perception Failures in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Zooming into a region often turns a wrong answer from a vision-language model (VLM) into a right one, and a growing literature takes this as evidence that the model lacked the pixels. But a zoom changes several things at once. It removes the surrounding context, re-tokenizes, at a higher density, pixels the model has already seen, and, if the first pass was downsampled, exposes detail the model never received. We separate these factors for each failure. Holding the first pass fixed, we vary only the second look and assign every failure to the cheapest one that fixes it: reusing cached tokens, isolating the region, re-tokenizing the seen pixels, or acquiring new ones. The study covers 2,753 zoom-rescued failures of Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3 on V\*, ZoomBench and TextVQA, at first-pass budgets from 17x fewer pixels than the source to native resolution. First, most rescued failures did not lack the pixels: upsampling the pixels the model had already seen fixes 47-73% of them even at 17x downsampling, against 1-3% for a random region. Second, the first-pass budget decides how often new pixels are needed: depending on the benchmark, new pixels are required for 23-50% of the rescues at 17x and for 12-23% at 4x. The missing-pixel reading and the accounts that dispute it thus hold at different budgets. Third, the useful magnification is large and differs between objects and text: seen pixels fix more failures up to 6-12x their first-pass size per side on objects and saturate by 4x on text, and even native pixels gain from being rendered beyond native resolution. Allowing any magnification, 68-94% of the failures at 17x need no new pixels. These models more often need a better rendering of evidence they already have than new pixels, which motivates re-tokenizing a region before acquiring it.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 17 figures, 19 tables. Code and per-sample outputs: https://github.com/gezhixuan/zoomdecomp

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Recoverable Symbols, Unstable Execution: Auditing Internal Pathways and Decision Identity in Visual State Tracking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27715v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27715

**作者：** Ruiqi Liu、Jinming Li、Mo Wang、Yang Lu、Yanlan Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27715v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27715v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究视觉状态跟踪中「符号可解码但答案随无关上下文改变」的落差：冻结 ViT 编码符号块序列，循环执行器判断候选是否等于末状态，并用 donor 视图、符号监督解码与同模型内部通道双向交换来审计；发现误差主要经由输入相关的旋转相位传导，恢复原相位能修复多数新错误，而由此导出的一致性目标并未超过普通数据增强。

**相关性简评：** 直接检验「内部状态可恢复不等于被稳定使用」这一关键假设，其符号可解码性检查与相位通道交换干预可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留、视觉旁路与 latent 内容因果作用的真实落差。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recoverable Symbols, Unstable Execution: Auditing Internal Pathways and Decision Identity in Visual State Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Visual features can retain every symbol a task needs while a model built on them still changes its answer when irrelevant parts of the image change. We study this gap in visual state tracking, where a recurrent executor reads a sequence of symbol tiles through a frozen ViT encoder and decides whether a displayed candidate equals the final state. Our diagnostic pairs each input with a donor view that preserves every queried tile, checks with a symbol-supervised decoder that the full symbol chain is recoverable from both feature sequences, and swaps individual internal channels of the same fitted executor in both directions. On 3,526 pairs in which every symbol is recovered in both views, changing the surrounding context lowers the balanced accuracy of a rotary recurrent head by 17.13 points. The loss runs mainly through the input-dependent rotation phase: restoring the original phase repairs 351 of 391 new errors and brings accuracy back to 88.54%, yet 142 individual predictions still differ from the original ones. A locked test on 192 unseen program families reproduces the pathway (28 of 29 new errors repaired; 382 of 384 predictions unchanged), and the effect partly carries over to single-image canvases. Consistency objectives derived from this analysis reduce the internal discrepancy they target but do not outperform ordinary data augmentation.

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [Depth-Linked Residual Tokens for Multimodal Retrieval and Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26752v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26752

**作者：** Jiangfan Zhang、Wentao Bao、Xian Wu、Xiangjun Fan、Jianpeng Cheng、Jun Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26752v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26752v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DepThink：在单次 pre-filling 前向中追加 K 个可学习 residual token，用轻量可学习残差递推耦合其隐藏状态，使后序 token 逐步捕获前序遗漏的信息，并给出方向可达性与一步下降的理论分析；在多模态检索与生成上优于可学习 token 基线、匹配或超过 looped 架构，同时加速训练与推理。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及以并行 latent token 替代串行循环的状态生成问题，且明确针对 token 间表示冗余与互补信息缺失，可作为核查多 latent 假设的表达多样性、信息坍缩及计算—收益权衡的线索，但其 residual token 是否真正承载视觉证据并影响答案仍需读原文。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Depth-Linked Residual Tokens for Multimodal Retrieval and Generation

**abstract/A1：** Iterative architectures such as looped language models improve parameter efficiency and reasoning capacity of transformers, yet their sequential backbone iterations introduce substantial computational latency. Conversely, parallelizable learnable special tokens (such as the register tokens in Vision Transformer) enable efficient single-pass inference, but often suffer from the absence of sequential refinement and exhibit redundant representations across tokens. To address these challenges, we introduce Depth-Linked Residual Tokens (DepThink), an efficient framework designed for multimodal retrieval and generation. Operating within a single pre-filling forward pass, DepThink appends a sequence of K learnable residual tokens whose hidden states are coupled through a lightweight, learnable residual recurrence. This mechanism enables each residual token to progressively capture complementary information missed by preceding tokens. Theoretically, we prove that DepThink provides strictly expanded geometric directional reachability over standard learnable tokens and guarantees a provable one-step descent advantage. We evaluate DepThink across multimodal retrieval and generation tasks. DepThink consistently outperforms learnable-token baselines and matches or exceeds looped architectures across dense (2B, 8B) and sparse mixture-of-experts (30B-A3B) backbones under both LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning, while accelerating training by up to 11.9 and inference by 4.3 over looped architectures.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [Spatial Query Execution as Verifiable Rewards for Multi-step Spatial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26787v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26787

**作者：** Jiyoon Pyo、Dongwon Jung、Zekun Li、Nayeon Kim、Muhao Chen、Yao-Yi Chiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26787v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26787v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建含显式几何的合成 2D/3D 场景，用确定性空间查询执行生成中间结果与最终答案，并分别转化为自然语言推理轨迹用于 SFT、作为可验证奖励用于 GRPO，以提升 LVLM 的多步空间关系推理。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 多步空间推理中的中间状态监督与轨迹训练，可供核查「中间推理状态是否真被用于作答」以及轨迹监督相对答案监督的收益，但其状态以语言轨迹和符号集合呈现，尚非 latent 表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatial Query Execution as Verifiable Rewards for Multi-step Spatial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain unreliable on spatial reasoning problems that require composing grounded relations across multiple objects. Yet, scaling reasoning supervision is challenging since human-annotated visual datasets often rely on fixed relational annotations and coarse object localization, making it difficult to obtain precise intermediate reasoning states across diverse spatial configurations and complexities. We introduce FARON (Fully Automated Reasoning Over spatial relatioNs), a framework that executes spatial queries to provide both execution-grounded reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards. Rather than relying on existing annotated data, FARON constructs synthetic 2D and 3D scenes with explicit geometries, enabling configurable object arrangements, spatial relations, and multi-step compositions. Deterministic query execution yields intermediate sets and final answers, providing precise supervision without manual relation annotation. Our pipeline converts intermediate states into natural-language reasoning traces for supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while the execution-derived answers provide verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Controlled experiments show that trace-based SFT improves multi-step spatial reasoning over final-answer supervision and that subsequent group relative policy optimization (GRPO) provides further gains. The resulting models also demonstrate transfer across external spatial reasoning benchmarks and multiple LVLM backbones without benchmark-specific fine-tuning. These results show the potential of using spatial query execution as a unified, flexible source of reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards for multimodal spatial reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26902v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26902

**作者：** Qiang Zhou、Jianing Deng、Siyan Dong、Jingxian Chen、Han-Yi Lin、Jingtong Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26902v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26902v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Baton：在长视频具身场景的小-大 VLM 级联中，把小模型对历史帧的视觉 KV 缓存经无标签蒸馏的逐层线性映射交给大模型，使升级时大模型只需编码当前帧，从而省去重看历史的开销，并在 RoboMME、OpenEQA 上评估。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉细节能否保存在内部状态（KV 缓存）中并跨模型复用，以及按需升级所代表的动态计算分配；其「忠实复现大模型缓存不如从答案蒸馏有效」「增益集中在中间三分之一层」的发现，可供核查缓存内容、层级深度与视觉证据保留之间的关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs

**abstract/A1：** An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model's visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model's cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model's own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model's layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model's headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [What Does the Visual Encoder Buy? Visual Information Gain in Encoder-Free Multimodal Pretraining](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26917v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26917

**作者：** Zhixuan Ge、Anqi Li、Wei Qiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26917v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26917v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出视觉信息增益（VIG），以图像替换为均匀图时的文本损失增量衡量视觉贡献，并将其分解为全局统计与布局信息，用于比较无编码器与冻结 SigLIP 编码器的多模态预训练及公开检查点。

**相关性简评：** 该诊断直接关系到 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据，可供核查 LVR 中的视觉旁路与信息坍缩；其 patch 打乱和 nats 量化也可能用于评估 latent 推理状态对布局与细节的保留。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does the Visual Encoder Buy? Visual Information Gain in Encoder-Free Multimodal Pretraining

**abstract/A1：** Recent scaling studies ask whether vision-language models still need a visual encoder by comparing encoder-free and encoder-based models on the next-token loss of the text in image-text data. This loss can fall without any use of the image, and two runs with the same loss can differ in what they take from it. Visual information gain (VIG) is the loss increase when the image is replaced by a uniform one. It splits exactly into global information, carried by image statistics, and layout information, which shuffling the patches within the image destroys. We apply it to decoders with 158M to 1.04B parameters that read pixel patches through a linear embedding, evaluated every billion tokens up to 40B, and to 19 public checkpoints. In our decoders the image lowers the loss by 3% to 46%, depending on the image type. Global information is present after the first billion tokens. Layout information starts near zero, grows slowly with training and does not grow with model size on five of six image types, and a larger batch reaches the same loss with less of it. With a frozen SigLIP 2 encoder in front of the same decoder the loss is lower only when the arrangement of the image is intact. On shuffled images the losses with and without the encoder agree to within 0.04 nats on five of six image types, so the net advantage of the encoder in the loss is layout information, above all the ability to read text in images. Public encoder-free models at production scale (Gemma 4 12B, NEO, Mono-InternVL) do acquire layout information. On the same documents they have 0.53 to 0.76 nats of it, against 0.65 to 0.82 for recent encoder-based models and 0.04 to 0.07 for our decoders, and their released training stages show it rising. These results agree with the reported parity of the two families when both are trained from scratch and place the reported advantage of a pretrained encoder in layout information.

</details>

### 7. [When Perception Collides with Priors: Diagnosing Modal Polarization in Video Understanding Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26972v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26972

**作者：** Yao Tong、Jiaxin Qi、Yan Cui、Jinli Ou、Yuhua Zheng、Jianqiang Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26972v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26972v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文识别视频理解 MLLM 在视觉证据与内部先验冲突时出现的「模态极化」失败模式，即认知惯性（固守先验）与知觉惯性（盲信画面），并构建 TheaterBench 反常识基准加以诊断。

**相关性简评：** 其关于模型是否真正采纳视觉证据、先验如何压过感知的失败分析，与 LVR 中视觉信息保留、证据筛选及答案使用问题直接相关；可核查其冲突场景是否也能解释 latent 推理中的视觉旁路与信息坍缩。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Perception Collides with Priors: Diagnosing Modal Polarization in Video Understanding Models

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong video comprehension, largely benefiting from extensive training on realistic video data. However, when video perception severely conflicts with internal priors or common sense, we identify a critical failure pattern termed Modal Polarization, where models fail to integrate modalities and instead bifurcate into two distinct extremes: Cognitive Inertia, where models rigidly adhere to priors despite contradictory visual evidence, and Perceptual Inertia, where they blindly trust video perception while ignoring logical inconsistencies. To investigate this, we introduce TheaterBench, a benchmark designed to expose Modal Polarization using counter-intuitive scenarios that violate physical laws or common sense - situations rarely encountered in standard training distributions. Extensive experiments reveal that current state-of-the-art (SOTA) models exhibit severe Modal Polarization, and many de-hallucination methods merely rebalance the trade-off between Perceptual and Cognitive Inertia rather than fundamentally resolving the conflict. This work serves as a foundational step for evaluating and enhancing the perceptual-cognitive equilibrium of future video understanding models.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages (main paper with appendix), 11 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [SceneRuler: Measuring Physical Quantities in Video via Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26994v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26994

**作者：** Yumeng He、Gezheng Kang、Fanlian Zeng、Yin Yang、Chenfanfu Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26994v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26994v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究视频中的定量物理推理：给定视频、问题与若干已知尺度先验，按「最小充分重建」为每个问题只构建足以测量该量的最小场景，由 VLM 写出程序结构、物理运动律拟合确定数值（可用重力锚定米制尺度），并将各参数与所求量标记为已确定或未确定，最终答案由以该场景为证据的模型给出。

**相关性简评：** 其「以测量检验证据充分性」和对未定量输出空读数的机制，为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与 STOP/弃权决策提供了具体可核查线索；可关注这种显式、可追溯的中间量表示与 latent 推理状态的对应关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SceneRuler: Measuring Physical Quantities in Video via Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Many questions about videos ask for a number, such as how tall a person is, how far a cart travels, or how fast a ball leaves a ramp. We study this task, quantitative physical reasoning from video, where the inputs are a video, a question, and a prior of known facts such as object sizes, speeds, and distances from the camera to named objects. End-to-end video-language models lack an explicit metric scale and physical consistency, and their errors cannot be inspected. We present SceneRuler, which instead recovers a measurable physical scene and answers with that scene as evidence. Its central principle, Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction, builds, for each question, only the smallest scene that suffices to measure the asked quantity, and tests sufficiency on measurements rather than assuming it. The scene is written as a program whose structure a vision-language model writes and whose magnitudes are left to measurement. Physics-guided Fitting measures them through physical motion laws, so that gravity itself can anchor the metric scale, and marks every parameter, and the read-out of the asked quantity, as determined or undetermined. Each question receives a read-out and an answer. The read-out is the asked quantity evaluated on the scene, and it is empty when the scene does not fix that quantity. The answer is always given by a model that takes the scene as evidence. We evaluate on QuantiPhy and also apply the method to 27 in-the-wild videos that we collected, which have no ground-truth answers. On its validation split, the final answer reaches a mean relative accuracy of 0.709 against 0.624 for the strongest baseline, and the reconstruction determines the asked quantity on 47 of 158 questions, where the final answer reaches 0.781. Because every intermediate quantity is explicit, each answer can be traced to the objects, scale, and motion behind it.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [$R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27070v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27070

**作者：** Yilin Zhao、Jinyu Wang、Wenming Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27070v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27070v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 R² 框架，用于识别并减少 VLM 中冗余的视觉工具调用：先用样本级工具效用分类（CAT）发现七个基准中最多仅 12.44% 的样本真正受益于工具，再提出 Visual Feedback Dependency 指标评估单次调用的依赖程度，并用 RA-GRPO 将依赖信号与调用成本结合入训练，在精度相当下减少约 12% 的工具调用。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中「LOOK 的收益是否真实、何时停止」问题：其 CAT 分解与 VFD 依赖度量可供核查单次视觉证据调用的因果贡献与成本权衡；需注意其重点是调用冗余与训练奖励设计，而非 latent 状态的内部表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use

**abstract/A1：** Recent studies have shown that visual tools can improve multimodal reasoning. However, the effects of tools vary substantially across samples, and benchmark accuracy alone does not reveal whether tool interactions are beneficial or redundant. We present $R^2$, a framework that recognizes and reduces redundant visual tool use in vision-language models. We first introduce a sample-level Category Analysis of Tool Utility (CAT), which categorizes samples according to whether a model can benefit from tools. This analysis reveals that benchmark scores may obscure a model's tool-use ability. Across seven benchmarks, samples benefiting from tools account for at most 12.44% of each benchmark, while the majority gain little from tool interaction or are even harmed by it. To assess individual calls, we propose the Visual Feedback Dependency (VFD) metric, which compares the likelihood of the same subsequent reasoning under actual and alternative visual feedback without an external evaluator or trajectory regeneration. Redundancy-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO) combines this dependency signal with a category-conditioned base call cost to assign relative efficiency advantages among correct trajectories, supplementing outcome-based supervision. Across the seven benchmarks, RA-GRPO reduces the mean number of tool calls by 12.39% relative to Vanilla GRPO at comparable benchmark accuracy. Evaluated by tool-utility category, RA-GRPO improves category macro-average accuracy over Vanilla GRPO by 3.35 percentage points.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 7 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [One state, many questions: Opsis gives a frontier model typed eyes for pathology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27102v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27102

**作者：** Zhixuan Ge、Wei Qiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27102v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27102v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Opsis：在冻结病理编码器上建立「类型化提问」接口，切片只编码一次，问题在运行时指定算子、参数与候选，输出为候选上的概率分布；实现为 10M 参数解码器（Opsis-S）和嵌入 Qwen3-VL-8B 的版本（Opsis-V），在乳腺与泛癌问答上评测，含未训练过的复合问题。

**相关性简评：** 与「视觉证据筛选」及「答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容」直接相关：单一编码状态按问题条件化读出，可与逐题线性探针、读描述或看缩略图加切片等基线对照，供核查信息坍缩与按需取证；复合问题的组合表现亦涉及状态复用与贡献分配，值得近期阅读，但任务域较专用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One state, many questions: Opsis gives a frontier model typed eyes for pathology

**abstract/A1：** A synoptic pathology report is a checklist of questions with fixed answer sets, many composed from others. Pathology foundation models answer them accurately with one classifier trained with labels for each, and frontier language models, which compose freely, cannot fit a slide of fourteen thousand tiles in their context. On 189 breast report questions, frontier models that read a written description of the slide or look at a thumbnail and tiles of it answer at 0.50 to 0.66, below the majority class at 0.67. A description is written before the question is known, and a look covers a handful of tiles. We present Opsis, which lets the model ask instead: a typed question interface on a frozen pathology encoder. A slide is encoded once, a question names an operator, arguments and candidates given at runtime, and the answer is a probability over the candidates. We build it as a 10 M parameter decoder on frozen Virchow2 tokens (Opsis-S) and inside Qwen3-VL-8B (Opsis-V). Asking Opsis-S, four frontier models answer the breast questions at 0.78 to 0.79, and on 3,797 pan cancer questions asking is 8.6 points above reading. On 995 composite questions that Opsis-S was never trained on, a frontier model composing its answers reaches 0.76, against 0.69 when it looks at the slide and 0.78 for ABMIL trained on each composite. On a pan cancer question set over 10,712 TCGA slides, one Opsis-S decoder matches linear probes trained per question on TITAN and PRISM2 embeddings (average accuracy 0.69 against 0.70 and 0.68). Opsis-S also separates CAMELYON16 metastases without training on that cohort (AUROC 0.86) and localises necrosis from report labels alone (tile AUROC 0.93).

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 10 figures, 12 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/gezhixuan/opsis

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Bel: Step-Level Policy Improvement of Visual Agents Without Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27222v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27222

**作者：** Jiajun Fan、Ziwen Wang、Zhaoyu Zhu、Changyi Yang、Neil He、Chaoran Cheng、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27222v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27222v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出无需训练的 Bel：把每个候选动作分别绘制在其作用的图像副本上，由决策模型给出执行该动作正确的概率并统一打分，仅在贪心分数低于阈值且明显落后时替换动作，并在 GUI 与机器人指向基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 给出「动作要在落点处作视觉判断」的实证，与视觉证据筛选、步骤级验证及替换/停止决策直接相关，可供核查视觉信息在推理评价中的作用；未涉及 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bel: Step-Level Policy Improvement of Visual Agents Without Training

**abstract/A1：** A visual agent acts on what it sees: it taps and types on a screen, or points on a camera image to show a robot where to act. One wrong step can fail its task, yet the right action is often among its own samples, passed over by its greedy decode. Choosing it is a visual judgment, since an action is best judged where it acts, not by its text or coordinates alone: as text, a tap is two numbers; drawn on the screen, it is the Post button of an empty draft. We find that this is decisive: given the screenshot and a tap's coordinates, a decision model chooses among GUI-R1-3B's samples significantly worse on average than when the tap is drawn where it lands. Bel turns this finding into a method that trains nothing and needs only a frozen policy's samples and an image on which each action can be drawn. It marks each candidate on its own copy of the image it acts on, asks a decision model for the probability that executing it is the right decision, so that every kind of action shares one scale, and acts on another candidate only when greedy scores below a threshold and trails it by a margin. On recorded GUI steps, for each of three policies, Bel significantly beats greedy decoding on mobile, web and desktop screens alike and, on the same candidates, gives each a significantly higher mean step success than voting, CATTS, the trained GUI-Critic-R1 and two prompted Qwen3-VL judges shown the same marks; it also significantly improves the much larger Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B on every step-level test set. With GUI-R1-3B's thresholds unchanged, Bel significantly raises live AndroidWorld and MiniWoB++ task success for two other policies and robot-pointing success for three policies on each of two groups of benchmarks. Judging each action where it acts thus gives step-level policy improvement of visual agents without training, with significant gains for GUI agents and robot pointing.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [EAGLE: Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27264

**作者：** Zhongjian Zou、Quan Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27264v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EAGLE 即插件框架：先用细粒度数据筛选可靠定位的注意力头，按实体提取目标区域，再构建分层视觉证据并用问题引导的路由选择视图组合，输入冻结的 MLLM 以提升高分辨率图像细粒度理解。

**相关性简评：** 「注意力头可靠性筛选＋实体级证据选择＋按问题路由视图」与视觉证据筛选、注意力结构与证据获取决策直接相关，可供核查视觉证据如何进入 VLM 推理而非被旁路；但方法停留在输入侧证据组织，未涉及 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EAGLE: Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained understanding of high-resolution images: attention heads vary in their reliability for grounding target entities and their attributes, while localized details are often disconnected from the broader scene context. We propose EAGLE (Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding), a plug-in framework that jointly selects grounding signals and organizes hierarchical visual evidence. EAGLE employs a fine-grained dataset to screen visually-grounded attention heads, followed by entity-aware grounding to extract target-specific regions. It then integrates hierarchical evidence construction and question-guided routing to select optimal view combinations, feeding them into the frozen MLLM to enhance fine-grained visual understanding. Experiments with Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B demonstrate that EAGLE consistently improves performance across both backbones and achieves SOTA results on V\*, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K compared with existing methods using the same backbone.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [Unimodal Shortcuts and Joint Scene-Intent Dependence in Vision-Language Safety Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27339v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27339

**作者：** Guijia Zhang、Hongbo Qiu、Guohao Cai、Hao Zheng、Jiajun Zha、Hanqing Wang、Zhenhao Zhang、Huihao Jing、Shing-Chi Cheung、Haoxuan Che、Harry Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27339v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27339v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文审计五个情境安全基准中的单模态捷径，并构造交叉场景与意图的受控评估，用线性探针检验 Qwen3-VL 与 SmolVLM2 中间状态是否线性编码场景—意图联合信息。

**相关性简评：** 它直接诊断 VLM 内部状态中是保留视觉证据还是依赖文本捷径，并指出拼接单模态表征即可恢复大部分信号、探针可解码不等于融合机制，可供核查 latent 表示的内容保留、视觉旁路与探针评价效度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unimodal Shortcuts and Joint Scene-Intent Dependence in Vision-Language Safety Representations

**abstract/A1：** The safety of a request to a vision-language model often depends on the image: asking how to serve a dish is harmless for fresh food and harmful for spoiled food. High accuracy on situational safety benchmarks does not tell us whether a model's safety representation encodes both the scene and the user's intent, because a benchmark can often be solved from one modality alone. Studying frozen vision-language safety representations, we audit five public situational benchmarks and find that each admits a unimodal shortcut: images alone predict the labels, and on same-image prompt pairs a probe that never sees the photograph reaches 0.9645 AUROC. We therefore build a controlled evaluation that crosses safe and hazardous scenes with observation and action requests, so that no single-modality rule can exceed 0.500 group-ranking accuracy. On this control, linear probes on intermediate states of Qwen3-VL-8B and SmolVLM2-2.2B reach 0.958 and 0.930, showing that the scene-intent conjunction is linearly decodable; concatenating separately encoded image and text states already recovers most of this signal, so decodability is not evidence of a fusion mechanism. The same probes screen cheaply but generalize poorly to unseen images and other benchmarks. Reliable multimodal safety evaluation therefore needs controls that rule out unimodal shortcuts before accuracy is read as evidence of joint understanding.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 8 figures, 18 tables (14 pages main paper, 25 pages supplementary material)

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Beyond What They Say: Cue Acknowledgment and Robustness in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27389

**作者：** Ali Rahimiakbr、Arvin Baghal Asl、Nima Niroumand、Mohammad Parsa Jafarnejady、Hossein Shahabadi、Amirmohammad Izadi、Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27389v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究在固定问题与正确答案的条件下，比较视觉与文本线索在有用、误导、冲突三种情形下 VLM 的答案与解释，考察模型对线索的「承认」是否伴随对线索的抵抗。

**相关性简评：** 直接关涉 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据，并揭示「说明中提及线索」与「答案是否被线索左右」的解耦，可为视觉证据筛选、latent 内容是否被答案使用的诊断与评价设计提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond What They Say: Cue Acknowledgment and Robustness in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Explanations are used to assess whether vision-language models rely on visual evidence or misleading suggestions. A response that explicitly names a cue can still follow it, making disclosure alone an incomplete basis for judging robustness. We ask whether greater cue acknowledgment accompanies greater resistance. We compare matched answers and explanations under helpful, misleading, and conflicting visual and textual cues while holding the underlying question and correct answer fixed. A paired comparison shows that a model can select the endorsed wrong answer more often while acknowledging the cues more often. Reduced silence therefore need not indicate improved resistance. Explanations may also mention a textual suggestion while omitting an accompanying visual cue. Requesting evidence and acknowledgment does not consistently improve resistance in the tested settings. Outcomes vary across models and settings, and these comparisons do not isolate a causal effect of acknowledgment. Evaluations should report cue following and acknowledgment on the same instances, distinguishing visual from textual cues.

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Evidence sufficiency assessment enables cost-effective review allocation in on-premise clinical AI agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27390v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27390

**作者：** Yiwen Qiu、Che Sun、Shu Shen、Wentao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27390v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27390v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的决定点证据充分性评估与三层顺序分诊：同一4B模型在遮蔽推理和诊断后，用去偏概率给出五级充分性评分，并以Wilson下界校准单次运行、五次一致性或全转人工的分配。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联THINK/LOOK/STOP中的停止决策与计算分配，提供以证据充分性触发升级的协议；其信号是否真正基于视觉证据、能否迁移到LVR停止判断可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evidence sufficiency assessment enables cost-effective review allocation in on-premise clinical AI agents

**abstract/A1：** On-premise clinical AI agents must auto-accept high-confidence diagnoses, deferring others to clinician review, yet token probabilities and self-reported confidence miss errors, while more reliable five-run consistency incurs fivefold compute. We propose training-free decision-point evidence sufficiency assessment and a three-tier sequential triage protocol. Masking reasoning and diagnosis, the same 4B model scores sufficiency from objective evidence via prior-debiased probabilities over five ordered levels. Calibration freezes a nominal top-50% threshold, selecting single-run, five-run consistency, or full-deferral tiers by whether the one-sided 95% Wilson lower confidence bound of pass rate among accepted cases meets the 95% clinical target line. Across 1,605 MIRA-v2, CDM, VivaBench, and in-hospital liver malignancy primary cohort cases, MIRA-v2 single-run top-50% pass rate reached 98.91%, matching five-run consistency, saving ~80% compute; CDM achieved 96.00% test attainment by escalating to the five-run consistency tier; cross-specialty, missing-imaging, and combined enriched cohorts entered the full-deferral tier, capping calibration false-certification rate at 0.00%-3.50%.

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [When Visually Rich Documents Meet Multi-Vector Retrieval for Region-level Retrieval-Augmented Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27542v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27542

**作者：** Xu Yuan、Liangbo Ning、Yujuan Ding、Qing Li、Wenqi Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27542v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27542v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MV-VisRAG：先把视觉丰富文档切成语义完整的区域，用基于 VLM 的检索器编码成区域向量与页级全局向量，经 late-interaction 检索并按匹配分数估计区域贡献，选取相关区域压缩为视觉上下文供生成，并在五个基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「区域级证据筛选 + 由匹配分数估计贡献」与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、视觉 token 成本控制直接相关，可供核查；但检索相关性分数是否等于生成实际依赖的证据，仍需读原文验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Visually Rich Documents Meet Multi-Vector Retrieval for Region-level Retrieval-Augmented Generation

**abstract/A1：** Visually rich documents (VRDs) convey knowledge through textual, visual, and layout information that text-centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Text-RAG) struggles to fully exploit. Recent Vision-RAG approaches enable retrieval and generation directly from document images, retaining this multimodal information. However, earlier Vision-RAG methods commonly adopt coarse retrieval representations and full-page visual contexts, which can obscure local evidence and introduce substantial redundancy and computational overhead. To address these challenges, we propose MV-VisRAG, a region-level Vision-RAG framework that uses semantically complete regions for both document representation and visual augmentation. Specifically, MV-VisRAG first constructs these regions through a ContentAdaptive Regionalization module. It then encodes them into local region vectors and a page-level global vector using a VLM-based retriever trained through multimodal reconstruction pretraining and retrieval fine-tuning. With this compact multi-vector representation, MV-VisRAG performs late-interaction retrieval and estimates region contributions from the resulting matching scores. These contributions guide the selection of relevant regions, which are then rearranged into a compact visual context for generation. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate retrieval and generation gains over corresponding baselines, while region-level augmentation substantially reduces visual tokens and computational cost. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xandery-geek/MV-VisRAG.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [When Are Images Needed in Multimodal Machine Translation? Text-Only Routing for On-Demand Visual Context](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27712

**作者：** Xinyu Wang、Yingfeng Luo、Yuxin Gao、Anxiang Ma、Jingbo Zhu、Tong Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27712v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多模态机器翻译中何时真正需要图像，将视觉调用表述为「源文本充分性预测」，提出仅看源文本的轻量路由 PreVIR，在计算视觉特征之前选择纯文本或多模态分支，并构建三标签视觉依赖数据集加以验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「表层歧义不等于视觉依赖」的判断与 LVR 中何时 LOOK、视觉证据是否必要及视觉计算成本权衡直接相关，可供核查纯文本信号能否预测视觉调用收益；但任务是单步翻译而非多步推理，迁移需谨慎。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Are Images Needed in Multimodal Machine Translation? Text-Only Routing for On-Demand Visual Context

**abstract/A1：** Image-guided multimodal machine translation (MMT) leverages visual context to resolve translation-relevant uncertainty. However, prevailing systems either process images unconditionally or apply gating only after visual encoding, leaving redundant visual computation unavoidable. To mitigate this computational overhead, routing decisions must rely on a pre-visual source signal. Crucially, surface-level ambiguity does not equate to visual dependence, as intra-sentence textual context often suffices for disambiguation. We therefore formulate visual invocation as source-text sufficiency prediction: determining exclusively from the source text whether translation-relevant uncertainty remains unresolved. To support this formulation, we construct a visual-dependency dataset with three fine-grained labels (no\_ambiguity, text\_resolved, and vision\_needed) and verify discriminative visual evidence for vision\_needed instances. We further propose PreVIR, a lightweight, backend-agnostic text-only router that selects between text-only and multimodal branches before computing visual features. Experiments on English-to-Chinese MMT demonstrate that PreVIR achieves a favorable COMET-budget trade-off relative to strong routing baselines. At its default operating point, it substantially reduces visual calls while maintaining translation quality comparable to always-on multimodal inference. Label-wise and case analyses show that this trade-off stems from distinguishing genuine visual dependency from contextually resolved ambiguity.

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents' Capability Frontier](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27780v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27780

**作者：** Chengchao Lv、Yin Wang、Chunzheng Zhu、Wanli Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27780v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27780v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Search-RSI，一个递归自进化训练框架：每轮由当前多模态搜索 agent 诊断自己在跳数深度、关系稀有度与干扰密度所张成任务空间中的「部分掌握」区域，同步生长新任务与其证据，并由同一策略决定其用途——探测分歧的样本用于强化学习，超出能力的样本转为提示引导示范。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据不存在却被当作策略失败惩罚」的问题刻画了证据可得性与策略贡献混淆，与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、贡献分配和监督设计直接相关；但论文围绕外部检索世界扩展与训练课程，未涉及内部 latent 推理状态，THINK/LOOK 计算与停止决策的对应关系需读原文核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents' Capability Frontier

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal search agents answer knowledge intensive visual questions by grounding them in retrieved evidence, and recent recipes train them on multi hop tasks that are synthesized from knowledge graphs and grown online to keep pace with the agent. These recipes, however, treat the searchable world as fixed: a new question can be well formed and verifiable while its evidence is absent from the retrieval backend, so the agent issues the right query, retrieves nothing, and is penalized as if its policy were at fault. Such unreachable negatives accumulate with every round of growth. We propose Search-RSI, a recursive self improvement framework that expands the search world along the agent's capability frontier. In each round, the current policy diagnoses its partially mastered regions of a task space spanned by hop depth, relation rarity, and distractor density; new tasks there are grown together with their evidence; and the same policy decides how they are used: candidates on which its probes disagree feed reinforcement learning, while those beyond its reach become hint guided demonstrations of its own. Keeping the policy inside this loop matters: an improver frozen at the start of training loses 3.2 points. On nine multimodal search benchmarks, Search-RSI keeps improving where static task growth saturates, raising the average accuracy of the recipe it builds on by 9.8 and 9.5 points at 9B and 27B, of which 7.0 points are out of reach for a world that is merely made consistent. The loop uses no external model and only privileged task structure that is removed before training, at 1.27x the compute of static growth.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [When Does Looking Closer Pay Off? Simple Foveated ViTs for Fine-Grained Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27788v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27788

**作者：** Boris Kriuk、Mohamed Sobhy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27788v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27788v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FoVT：用 224 像素整图 glance 的末层 class attention 选取区域，以 448 像素 patch 替换后由同一 ViT 分类，并在五个细粒度基准上系统比较注意力/随机选区、特征复用、置信样本跳过细化与计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LOOK/STOP 的收益与成本：注意力选区优于随机、跳过最自信样本几乎不掉点、墙钟节省小于算子节省，可供核查视觉证据筛选与动态计算预算；但属纯视觉细粒度分类，未涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Looking Closer Pay Off? Simple Foveated ViTs for Fine-Grained Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained recognition benefits from high resolution, but not every image region needs it. We study a foveated Vision Transformer (FoVT) that uses last-block class attention from a 224-pixel glance to select regions for refinement. Fine patches from the 448-pixel image replace the selected coarse cells, and the same backbone classifies the mixed-resolution input. We evaluate FoVT on five benchmarks with two seeds and 26 configurations per dataset. Attention beats random selection on all five benchmarks, by up to 2.45 percentage points overall and 4.18 points on the smallest objects. At equal transformer cost, Holm-corrected tests detect no FoVT deficit relative to a port of the official CF-ViT, but no dataset shows a corrected FoVT advantage in both seeds. FoVT requires no feature reuse, distillation loss, or selection schedule. Reusing coarse features shows no significant benefit and costs 1 to 3 points when the reuse module is trained at the head learning rate. Skipping refinement for the most confident 19 to 24 percent of images leaves accuracy unchanged to two decimal places. At the transformer cost of uniform 336-pixel inference, gated FoVT improves accuracy on four benchmarks, with the largest gains on NABirds and FGVC-Aircraft and no significant gain on Stanford Dogs. Foveation primarily saves computation: relative to a uniform 448-pixel model trained with the same auxiliary glance loss, ungated FoVT uses 44 percent fewer transformer operations, with no significant accuracy difference on four benchmarks and a 0.53-point deficit on FGVC-Aircraft. Wall-clock savings are smaller than the reduction in transformer operations.

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [StereoVLM: Binocular Vision-Language Models for Metric 3D Spatial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27915v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27915

**作者：** Chi Yan、Guoyang Zhao、Wentao Yuan、Yixiao Ge、Yanning Zhou、Dan Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27915v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27915v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 StereoVLM，一个利用标定双目几何的几何感知 VLM，按跨视角对应、视差估计、标定条件度量的深度感知到语义空间推理分层训练，并构建 Stereo3D-Bench 评测点级几何与物体级空间推理。

**相关性简评：** 其核心追问是空间预测究竟由视觉几何证据还是语义先验驱动，并以干预实验检验跨视角几何的作用，与 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留于内部状态、答案是否真正使用视觉证据及几何信息坍缩问题直接相关；分层监督中以视差监督优于直接深度监督的发现也值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StereoVLM: Binocular Vision-Language Models for Metric 3D Spatial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding but remain limited in their ability to reason about metric 3D space in the physical world. Existing VLMs typically infer 3D scene structure from one or more images without known camera calibration or cross-view geometric constraints. This makes metric 3D inference inherently ambiguous and leaves it unclear whether their spatial predictions are grounded in visual evidence or driven primarily by semantic priors. In contrast, calibrated stereo provides explicit cross-view geometry that resolves this ambiguity and offers a more reliable foundation for metric 3D perception and geometric generalization. We investigate how VLMs can learn transferable 3D representations directly from calibrated binocular observations. To this end, we introduce StereoVLM, the first geometry-aware binocular VLM for metric 3D spatial reasoning. StereoVLM follows a hierarchical learning paradigm spanning from cross-view correspondence and disparity estimation to calibration-conditioned metric depth perception and semantic spatial reasoning. We further find that disparity supervision generalizes substantially better than direct binocular depth supervision on unseen environments. To evaluate these capabilities, we introduce Stereo3D-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark across three levels, from point-level geometry to object-level spatial reasoning. StereoVLM-8B achieves 95.17% depth accuracy ($δ\_1$) and a 66.98 average object-level score on Stereo3D-Bench. Intervention results further indicate that the model's performance on Stereo3D-Bench benefits from valid cross-view geometry and cannot be explained solely by additional visual tokens or semantic priors. Hierarchical geometric supervision also yields average gains of 3.54 points across five spatial benchmarks and 4.3 percentage points in RoboCasa manipulation success.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://xpeng-robotics.github.io/stereovlm/

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Parasite Features: Causal Abstraction Through Spurious Pathways](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26711

**作者：** Denis Sutter、Qing Yao、Sasha Boguraev、Julian Minder、Antonio Di Maio、Thomas Hofmann、Kyle Mahowald、Tiago Pimentel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26711v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出因果抽象类可解释性方法可能被表示空间中两条方向的组合路径欺骗，从而发现虚假的「parasite features」，并在句法一致与两位数加法任务中用 Distributed Alignment Search 验证了这一点。

**相关性简评：** 其关于因果抽象等价检验与虚假通路的警示，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正承载任务特征、以及表示对齐搜索的因果归因是否可靠；但任务为纯文本与算术，向多模态情境迁移仍需检验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Parasite Features: Causal Abstraction Through Spurious Pathways

**abstract/A1：** Causal Abstraction defines a notion of \\emph{equivalence} between a neural network and an algorithm, thus serving as a powerful framework for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, however, we show the existence of a class of \\emph{spurious features} which straightforwardly pass such equivalence tests. Suppose there are two directions in a model's representation space where: (i) one discriminates the input based on some feature, and (ii) the other steers the model towards a certain output. Interpretability methods based on Causal Abstraction (e.g., Distributed Alignment Search) can \\emph{spuriously} simulate these directions' combined behavior by leveraging pathways passing through them, giving the impression that the model implements this feature. In such cases, we say the method discovered a \\defn{parasite feature}. Experimentally, we show that Distributed Alignment Search finds parasite features using linear alignment maps in two tasks: syntactic agreement and two-digit addition. Our paper highlights an important consideration for Causal Abstraction and contributes to the ongoing debate about best practices for mechanistic interpretability.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026. 25 Pages, 5 Figures, 2 Tables

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26910v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26910

**作者：** Congyuan Yu、Runping Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26910v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26910v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** AstronautBench 基于游戏 Outer Wilds 构建跨 episode 的智能体自我改进基准，在同一轮次中并行测量三件事：世界知识问答正确率、答案是否被智能体自己引用的画面与位置证据支持（由两个不同模型家族的 LLM judge 核查）、以及实际到达的游戏进度；八 episode 基线显示三者并不同步增长。

**相关性简评：** 其「知识正确但未必有自身视觉证据支持」的诊断视角与双 judge 证据核查流程，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容对答案的因果支撑与视觉证据筛选；但对象是具身游戏智能体的跨 episode 知识积累而非 VLM 内部推理状态生成，迁移价值需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents are expected to improve themselves from their own experience across episodes, with fixed weights. Self-improvement in an embodied world cannot be read from one measure. Existing benchmarks measure task progress, world-knowledge question answering (QA), and episodic memory, but a correct answer does not show that the agent ever observed the evidence for it, and progress does not show what the agent learned. We introduce AstronautBench, which measures one agent's self-improvement through three views on the same run: what it knows, whether its own evidence supports that knowledge, and how far it gets in the world. It is built on the game Outer Wilds, where each episode is a 22-minute loop after which the world resets, only the agent's knowledge carries over, and progress is gated by knowledge. After every episode, we measure the places reached and ship-log rumor facts revealed, the correctness of answers to 164 questions about the world, and whether each answer is supported by the frames and position the agent cites from its own recorded play. Two LLM judges from different model families check every answer. In eight-episode baselines with GPT-6.1 Sol and Gemini 4 Argon planners, which act through a learned controller or their own gamepad inputs, the three views do not move together, so any one of them alone gives an incomplete picture of whether the agent is improving. The GPT-6.1 Sol agent's correct answers grow from 14.6% to 26.8% of all questions, but at episode 8 only 59 to 68% of them are supported by its cited evidence. It reveals no new rumor facts after episode 3, while the places it reaches and its memory keep growing. More than half of the answers that become correct after episode 3 cite only evidence from earlier episodes.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [Learning Video Foresight Reasoning from Unlabeled Videos via On-Policy Self-Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26954v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26954

**作者：** Jingchen Sun、Jiaxing Zhang、Qingqiao Hu、Dingqiang Ye、Yuqi Jia、Yijun Liang、Di Fu、Yunjie Tian、Dongdi Zhao、Changyou Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26954v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26954v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Future-OPSD：利用无标注的过去-未来视频对做 on-policy 自蒸馏，并定义 foresight score 与对比式特权优势，使 VLM 在预测未来事件或下一步动作时更关注决策相关的未来信息、抑制无关上下文。

**相关性简评：** 其「前景分数+对比特权优势」本质上是对哪些未来视觉证据值得学习的贡献分配与筛选机制，可供核查如何借鉴到 latent 视觉证据加权、轨迹监督与无关上下文抑制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Video Foresight Reasoning from Unlabeled Videos via On-Policy Self-Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Video foresight reasoning requires vision-language models to anticipate future events or next actions from currently observed videos. Existing methods fit future captions, rationales, or full visual states, but face two limitations. First, they rely on annotations or external large models for supervision, limiting scalability. We address this with an on-policy self-distillation framework that turns past-future video pairs into a natural self-supervised signal without extra annotations or LLM supervision. Second, they treat the whole future clip as equally valuable, although most of it is irrelevant context information. We address this by defining a foresight score, from which we obtain a contrastive privilege advantage that drives the model to learn the most valuable future information. Since the score is intractable, we optimize a tractable lower-bound surrogate that interpolates this advantage, computed between the true and a random reference future, with a teacher-student distillation advantage; together, they amplify decision-relevant content information and suppress irrelevant context information. We call the resulting framework Future-OPSD. On open-ended prediction and closed-ended planning, Future-OPSD improves base models by up to 14.2 and 15.9 points without compromising general video understanding. Code will be available at https://jingchensun.github.io/future-opsd/

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Disentangling Verbalized Evaluation Awareness and Refusal through Neuron-Level Intervention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27032v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27032

**作者：** Jingyuan Feng、Gouki Minegishi、Andrew Gambardella、Takeshi Kojima、Yusuke Iwasawa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27032v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27032v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在大型推理模型中定位「言语化评估意识」（VEA）神经元，通过对比 VEA 与非 VEA 推理片段的激活加以识别，并做神经元级干预；发现抑制这些神经元不仅降低 VEA 率，还在无 VEA 的 rollout 中减少拒答，并进一步拆解出下投影向量中的拒答成分。

**相关性简评：** 其「文本仅解释 4–10% 行为变化」的分离手段与对未言语化内部状态探针，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被答案真正使用、以及显式轨迹与内部状态的贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Disentangling Verbalized Evaluation Awareness and Refusal through Neuron-Level Intervention

**abstract/A1：** As large reasoning models (LRMs) become more capable, reliable evaluation becomes essential. LRMs can sometimes recognize that they are being evaluated, a phenomenon known as \\emph{evaluation awareness}. This phenomenon raises concerns about whether safety behavior, particularly refusal behavior, generalizes from evaluation to deployment. Prior work manipulates \\emph{verbalized evaluation awareness (VEA)} through activation steering and observes higher refusal rates when VEA is present, but the mechanisms underlying this connection remain poorly understood. We identify \\emph{VEA neurons} by contrasting their activations in VEA and non-VEA reasoning segments. Intervening on 5,000 VEA neurons reduces the VEA rate from 12.6--51.8\\% to at most 1.4\\% across the model benchmark combinations in our main experiments, and lowers a probe score for unverbalized evaluation awareness (unVEA). In QwQ-32B, VEA-neuron intervention also reduces refusal even in rollouts without VEA; VEA text itself explains only 4--10\\% of the decrease. We further show that their down-projection vectors contain refusal components, and preserving these components during intervention partially restores refusal while maintaining VEA suppression, accounting for about half of the refusal decrease and providing a mechanistic explanation for the connection between evaluation awareness and refusal. Finally, across the OLMo 3 SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints, the VEA rate on SorryBench and StrongREJECT is similar after SFT and DPO but substantially higher after RLVR, alongside an increase in the number of VEA neurons. Together, these findings show that VEA text itself has a limited effect on refusal, and that VEA neurons integrate evaluation awarenss and refusal.

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [Auditing and Adapting kNNGuard for Long-Context Prompts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27084v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27084

**作者：** Niccolò Ronchetti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27084v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27084v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计无训练 kNNGuard 在长上下文提示上的性能崩塌（隐状态漂移、MiniLM 的 256 token 截断、长文压缩为末 token 向量造成信号稀释），并提出长度路由双 bank、长上下文 embedding 与跨中间 token 的 strided window pooling 三种无训练修补方案。

**相关性简评：** 其「末 token 单向量稀释」诊断与按中间位置池化读取隐状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度受位置与读取方式影响、以及长上下文下证据是否被保留提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Auditing and Adapting kNNGuard for Long-Context Prompts

**abstract/A1：** Training-free activation guardrails such as kNNGuard score prompts by comparing last-token LLM hidden states (the Layer Ensemble branch, kNNGuard LE) and sentence embeddings (all-MiniLM-L6-v2, Embedding-kNN) against a small reference bank (50 examples per class), combining the two branches into a Fused Ensemble (kNNGuard FE). While effective on short inputs (up to 256 tokens), standard kNNGuard degrades substantially on long inputs (over 1000 tokens): averaged across Qwen3.5-4B and Gemma-4-E4B-it, kNNGuard LE AUROC drops from 0.742 (0.777 and 0.706) to 0.554 (0.587 and 0.521) on Content Harm Moderation (Aegis-2.0), and from 0.994 (0.995 and 0.992) to 0.556 (0.598 and 0.513) on RAG Hallucination Detection (HaluBench). We trace this degradation to three causes: (1) hidden-state drift as sequence length increases, (2) hard 256-token truncation in MiniLM, and (3) signal dilution when a long document is compressed into a single final-token vector. We then evaluate three training-free adaptations: a Length-Routed Dual Bank, a long-context embedding model (Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B), and Strided Window Pooling across intermediate token positions. On Content Harm Moderation, dual-bank routing and strided window pooling raise long-context kNNGuard LE AUROC from 0.554 to 0.828 (+0.274 AUROC) and fused kNNGuard FE AUROC from 0.721 to 0.886 (+0.164 AUROC) with no latency overhead from bank routing. On RAG Hallucination Detection, where the guardrail must verify logical consistency between a generated answer and a retrieved passage, sentence-embedding kNN remains near random chance across all lengths, while length-matched LLM hidden states (kNNGuard LE) recover medium-length detection (AUROC = 0.853) but remain limited on over-1000-token passages (AUROC = 0.607) due to final-token dilution over long contexts.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27087v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27087

**作者：** Sibi Gokul、Jyoti Prakash Meher

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27087v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27087v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ORION 用辅助头监督精确的规范执行状态，把 Transformer 的 latent 轨迹锚定到合法计算流形上；在 Folding、GridShift、CLRS-30 上以 2×2 因子设计对比 RoPE 与该方法，发现 RoPE 主要恢复长度外推、ORION 主要恢复深度外推且两者收益可加。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本算法推理，但其对中间状态的精确对齐监督与深度外推结论，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹训练、状态贡献分配及长度与深度泛化工作区的迁移可能。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive Transformers generally fail on out-of-distribution reasoning due to two primary reasons: positional corruption over long sequences and structural state drift across deep execution steps. We introduce ORION, a framework that supervises exact canonical execution states via auxiliary heads to anchor latent trajectories to valid computational manifolds. Across Folding, GridShift, and CLRS-30, a pre-registered 2 x 2 factorial shows that RoPE and ORION address orthogonal axes. RoPE recovers length extrapolation (59.8%), while ORION recovers depth extrapolation (66.7%). Moreover, their combination complementarily achieves strict additive gain in joint length-depth extrapolation, while either method alone offers near satisfactory results. Negative controls confirm that the gain depends strictly on exact canonical alignment; hashed state statistics fail to constrain depth extrapolation.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Reasoning in Two Dimensions: Quantifying Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness and Efficiency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27100v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27100

**作者：** Chenruo Liu、Quan Zhang、Qi Lei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27100v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27100v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 CoT 表示为语义推理图，把模型预测依赖分解为「语义支持 vs 额外推理历史」和「通往答案 vs 偏离路径」两轴，据此给出全局与步骤级的忠实性、低效性指标，并导出图感知微调目标。

**相关性简评：** 其依赖度分解与干预式指标可供核查：能否改造为判断 VLM 答案是否真正依赖视觉证据或 latent 推理状态，而非表面轨迹，从而服务视觉旁路与信息坍缩诊断；当前对象为纯文本 CoT，迁移性待评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reasoning in Two Dimensions: Quantifying Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness and Efficiency

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning exposes intermediate steps, but a correct answer does not establish that an LLM relies on the dependence expressed by those steps or reasons efficiently. We operationalize CoT faithfulness and efficiency within a common framework. Specifically, we represent a CoT as a semantic reasoning graph and decompose the model's predictive dependence along two complementary axes: dependence explained by semantic support versus additional reasoning history, and dependence allocated to answer-reaching versus off-path reasoning. This decomposition yields global and step-level measures of unfaithfulness and inefficiency. Controlled interventions show that the proposed metrics respond to hidden dependence and induced off-path reasoning as intended. Across mathematical and multi-hop reasoning tasks, answer-level unfaithfulness is consistently associated with greater predictive uncertainty, whereas both unfaithfulness and inefficiency exhibit weak or inconsistent associations with answer correctness. We further derive theoretically grounded, graph-aware fine-tuning objectives that improve the targeted reasoning properties without directly optimizing the computationally expensive metrics or compromising answer accuracy. Results indicate that accuracy and brevity provide incomplete assessments of reasoning quality: reliable CoT evaluation should also consider whether the LLM's predictive dependence is faithfully represented and efficiently allocated.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [A Plan or an Adversarial Example? A Round-Trip Check for Latent World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27124v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27124

**作者：** Huiyuan Tian、Chih-Yung Wen、Shijian Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27124v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27124v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验 latent world model 规划结果究竟是「计划」还是模型的对抗样本，发现 CEM 迭代越多预测代价越低、却丢掉已解问题，且模型自身预测不暴露这些失败；为此提出 round-trip check，用小网络从预测状态反解动作块之和并惩罚不一致，在不重训模型、仅增 0.2%–3.1% 规划时间下改进 CEM 与 MPPI。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测代价下降但真实成功下降、模型内部信号无法自证」的诊断与反解一致性校验，可能为 LVR 的停止决策、latent 内容是否被真正使用及推理结果验证提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Plan or an Adversarial Example? A Round-Trip Check for Latent World Models

**abstract/A1：** If we optimize an image until a classifier is confident it shows a cat, we may get an adversarial example, not a cat. Planning with a latent world model is a similar search for actions whose predicted future is closest to the goal. Is the result a plan, or an adversarial example of the world model? We show that it can be either. On the released DINO-WM and LeWM, more iterations of the cross-entropy method (CEM) lower the predicted cost but lose up to half of the problems CEM had already solved. The model's own predictions do not reveal these failures. We propose a simple test that executes the plan after every iteration and counts the problems a planner solves and then loses. Unlike an image, a plan comes with its actions, which our round-trip check reads back from the predicted states with a small network, penalizing mismatches. The check reads back the sum of each block of five actions, the part that two consecutive predicted states determine. Without retraining the world model and at only 0.2% to 3.1% more planning time, it improves the widely used planners CEM and MPPI on three released world models, and gains more where planners lose more problems. Without replanning, success rises from 0.39 to 0.76 on Wall with DINO-WM and from 0.56 to 0.94 for MPPI on JEPA-WM. With replanning (MPC), it rises from 0.70 to 0.81 on Cube with LeWM.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [Analyzing Multi-Layer Visual Representations of Large Vision-Language Models for Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27128v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27128

**作者：** Satoshi Hashimoto、Hitoshi Nishimura、Mori Kurokawa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27128v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27128v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文系统分析 LVLM 的多层视觉表示，在异常检测任务上比较逐层特征与多层融合，跨多种监督级别和分类器考察各层对下游性能的贡献，并给出面向视觉中心任务的表示选择经验。

**相关性简评：** 其区分深层语义与浅/中层细粒度外观、并比较逐层与融合策略的做法，可供核查 LVR 中承载视觉证据的内部层选择与融合；但研究对象是异常检测而非推理状态生成，属方法线索而非直接主线。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Analyzing Multi-Layer Visual Representations of Large Vision-Language Models for Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, we analyze multi-layer visual representations in large vision-language models (LVLMs) and examine the effectiveness of layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion for anomaly detection. Representative LVLMs connect visual encoder features to a language model through a projector. In this design, deep visual encoder features carry high-level semantics and alignment with text representations, whereas shallow and intermediate layers retain information relevant to vision-centric tasks, such as local appearance and fine-grained shape. It is therefore important to choose layer representations and fusion methods according to the nature of the downstream task. Focusing on anomaly detection, we compare layer-wise features and multi-layer fusion across multiple supervision levels and a variety of classifiers, and examine from several perspectives how multi-layer visual representations affect downstream performance. By analyzing the resulting performance differences, we further discuss the contribution of each layer representation to anomaly detection and provide empirical insights into representation selection for vision-centric tasks.

**comment/C1：** Presented at MIRU2026

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [DepthMSA: Combinatorial Training and Budget-Aware Depth Allocation for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27198v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27198

**作者：** Pan Wang、Jie Peng、Zhimin Li、Jingxian Chen、Hui Ji、Huanrui Yang、Pengfei Zhou、Tianlong Chen、Jingtong Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27198v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27198v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DepthMSA，在多模态情感分析中为各模态分配不同的编码深度：用「组合式 anytime 训练」以一次全深度前向监督融合头覆盖所有跨模态深度组合，推理时由无需训练、成本感知的分配器在样本级 FLOPs 预算下选取验证集校准误差最低的配置。

**相关性简评：** 论文揭示模态编码器计算成本与深度收益的不匹配，其跨深度组合监督与按预算选择深度的做法，可为 LVR 中按样本分配 THINK 深度/视觉编码成本、平衡收益与开销提供方法线索，但任务与内部状态机制不同，需核查能否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DepthMSA: Combinatorial Training and Budget-Aware Depth Allocation for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis

**abstract/A1：** State-of-the-art multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) models rely on large pretrained encoders, yet typically process every modality to its full depth, regardless of its computational cost or predictive contribution. This raises a fundamental question: how should limited computation be allocated across modalities to preserve predictive performance? We reveal a substantial mismatch between the computational cost of modality encoders and the predictive benefit of processing them more deeply, suggesting that different modalities require different encoding depths. However, enabling heterogeneous depths introduces a key challenge: representations extracted at different depths must still be effectively fused. To address this, we propose $\\textbf{DepthMSA}$, a budget-aware framework that supports flexible modality depths within a single MSA model. Specifically, we introduce combinatorial anytime training, which supervises the fusion head across all cross-modal depth combinations using a single full-depth encoder pass. At inference, a training-free, cost-aware allocator selects the configuration with the lowest validation-calibrated error under a sample-specific FLOPs budget. On CMU-MOSEI, DepthMSA maintains accuracy within one percentage point of the full-depth model while reducing measured FLOPs by 45\\%, or by 82\\% with a separately trained skip-enabled variant. When a CLIP visual encoder dominates computation, the allocation shifts toward reducing visual depth, achieving 56\\% fewer FLOPs at unchanged accuracy. Compared with ADMN, an adaptive layer-allocation method that trains a controller for each budget, the skip-enabled variant reaches its accuracy with 3.2$\\times$ fewer FLOPs and lower latency, without any per-budget training. Our project is available at https://pwang322.github.io/DepthMSA/.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [GRACE: Controlled Evidence Interventions for Diagnosing and Improving Knowledge Integration in GraphRAG](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27350v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27350

**作者：** Minh Pham、Quang Nguyen、Phong Kieu、Cong Tran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27350v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27350v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 GraphRAG 基准 GRACE，对每个证据子图施加切断支撑路径、加入类型兼容干扰项、接入高度数枢纽和用反事实实体替换答案四种受控干预，并提出结合局部/全局图注意力、问题条件证据引导与校准支撑门控的 QCFSE-SA 模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「相关事实未必联合充分」的发现及校准图支撑门控，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、支持组合与停止/弃答决策；但该工作非视觉、非 latent，迁移可行性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GRACE: Controlled Evidence Interventions for Diagnosing and Improving Knowledge Integration in GraphRAG

**abstract/A1：** GraphRAG combines a language model's parametric knowledge with retrieved graph evidence, yet aggregate accuracy cannot show whether failures stem from missing paths, distractors, or conflicts with model knowledge. We introduce GRACE, a benchmark built by executing LC-QuAD 2.0 queries over Wikidata and applying four controlled interventions to each evidence subgraph: severing a support path, adding type-compatible distractors, attaching high-degree hubs, and replacing the supported answer with a counterfactual entity. Questions and option positions remain fixed across the five states. The validated benchmark contains 4,028 questions and 20,140 question-graph pairs. These comparisons reveal that question-only routing cannot distinguish evidence states, while relevance guidance can improve answerable states yet hurt when the support path is severed: relevant facts may not jointly suffice to answer. We propose QCFSE-SA, which combines typed local attention, global graph attention, and question-conditioned evidence guidance with a calibrated graph-support gate. With the factual context and generator held fixed, it improves controlled-state accuracy from 57.17% to 62.78% (+5.61 points; question-clustered 95% CI \[+5.04, +6.17\]) and broken-state accuracy from 55.41% to 67.85%. Over-abstention on semantic distractors remains an open challenge.

**comment/C1：** pages, 3 numbered figures, and five graph-state illustrations. Submitted to SOICT 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Beyond Linear Decodability: Understanding and Improving Instruction Following in World Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27401v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27401

**作者：** Bokai Ji、Jiapeng Chen、Yi Zhang、Zeyuan Ding、Yong Dai、Xiaozhu Ju、Jian Tang、Lemao Liu、Dejing Dou、Yong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27401v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27401v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究世界动作模型为何不遵循测试时语言指令，发现目标信息在跟随与不跟随模型中的线性可解码性存在显著差异，据此提出线性可解码性监督 LDS，并用先验—指令竞争机制解释行为差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息已编码但未控制行为」的诊断、线性探针监督及因果干预，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被答案使用、表征可读性与实际贡献的区分提供可核查线索；但研究对象是操作动作模型，迁移到视觉推理仍需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Linear Decodability: Understanding and Improving Instruction Following in World Action Models

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) exhibit strong manipulation capabilities yet often fail to follow test-time language instructions. Prior work shows that non-following (NF) WAMs encode information about the instructed target, but this information does not control their behavior. By comparing NF WAM with its instruction-following (IF) counterpart, we find that target information is substantially more linearly decodable in IF WAMs, despite being encoded in both. Motivated by this observation, we introduce linear decodability supervision (LDS), a simple yet effective auxiliary training objective that promotes the linear decodability of the instructed target information. On LIBERO-CF, InternW0-$Δ$ with LDS achieves a success rate of 74.6% and a grounding rate of 89.2%, surpassing the strongest baseline by 26.8 and 42.9 percentage points, respectively. The effectiveness of LDS challenges the prevailing interpretation that encoded target information does not contribute to WAMs' behavior. To reconcile this apparent contradiction, we propose a prior-instruction competition mechanism, which explains NF and IF behavior as arising from the dominance of prior and instruction signals, respectively. We provide causal evidence for this mechanism by evaluating two training-free interventions derived from it. We reinterpret LDS through this mechanism, showing that it acts as a form of instruction signal enhancement. Finally, more comprehensive evaluations across diverse manipulation tasks and visual domains demonstrate the consistent effectiveness of LDS.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, including appendices. Project page: https://ruflect-intelligence.github.io/LDS-Homepage/

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27443v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27443

**作者：** Aasheesh Singh、Vishal Vaddina

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27443v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27443v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何后训练小模型在 agent 任务中「何时向更强的 advisor 求助」，提出多阶段配方的 ADAPT，仅用策略自身 rollout 与环境结果监督，在 TextWorld、LiveCodeBench、GAIA 上以少量求助调用显著提升成功率，并验证换用未见 advisor 后仍有增益。

**相关性简评：** 其「成本感知地决定何时求助、并跨步利用外部信息」的决策训练，与 LVR 中何时调用视觉模块（LOOK）及何时停止（STOP）的收益–成本权衡可供核查，无奖励模型的自身轨迹结果监督也可能为轨迹训练与贡献分配提供线索；但对象是纯文本外部 advisor，迁移到视觉证据筛选仍需自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly attractive for cheap, high-throughput agentic deployment, but they remain weak at multi-step reasoning and tool use that long-horizon tasks demand. We study how to post-train an SLM to seek advice from a stronger model and act on that advice itself, following an advisor-executor pattern. Out of the box, this pattern fails across open- and closed-source small-model families: wiring an SLM to an advisor is a harness-design matter that is not enough on its own. A post-training recipe is needed to teach the model when to ask, in a cost-aware fashion, and how to follow advice across steps. Our method, ADAPT (ADvisor-Aware Post-Training), is a multi-stage recipe that instills this behavior, learning entirely from the policy's own rollouts supervised by the environment's outcome, with no learned reward model or human annotation. Across three domains, compared to an untrained harness baseline, ADAPT improves success rate in embodied text reasoning (TextWorld) from 24.5% to 77.3%, code generation (LiveCodeBench) from 21.8% to 56.0%, and deep web search (GAIA) from 17.5% to 49.3%, while querying the advisor sparingly (~1.0-2.1 calls per task). On the two long-horizon domains it closes 70-85% of the gap to an oracle that perfectly times when to ask for advice, and on code generation it exceeds that oracle. The gains persist when the advisor is replaced by one unseen in training, showing that the learned help-seeking policy is robust to a changing advisor rather than tied to the one it was trained with.

**comment/C1：** Published at the OE-Agent Workshop (Open and Efficient Agentic AI for Resource-Constrained Environments) at ACML 2026 14 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Turnout: Adaptive Attention Switching for Efficient Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27500v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27500

**作者：** Anna Kuzina、Mart van Baalen、Paul N. Whatmough、Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27500v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27500v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1
