摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Turnout：对预训练全注意力模型做事后线性化并保留原全注意力路径，用蒸馏训练线性分支，再用强化学习得到一个逐层在 full 与 linear attention 间切换的策略，奖励同时考虑答案正确性与内存占用，从而在推理任务上取得更优的精度—效率前沿。

**相关性简评：** 以固定大小状态替代增长的 KV cache 会压缩长生成中的细节，与 LVR 关注的视觉信息坍缩和长推理成本相近；其学习式逐层切换策略可供核查是否能作为「何时保留完整证据/增加计算」这一 THINK-LOOK-STOP 决策的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Turnout: Adaptive Attention Switching for Efficient Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Post-hoc transformer linearization replaces the key-value cache of full attention, which grows linearly with generation length, with a fixed size state. This change reduces memory consumption but often hurts accuracy. In reasoning tasks, which require long generations, this effect is especially pronounced. In this work we introduce Turnout, a method that mitigates accuracy loss as a result of linearization, while maintaining its memory-reduction benefits. We apply post-hoc linearization to a pretrained full-attention model, training a linear path through distillation while leaving the original full-attention path unchanged. During response generation, the resulting hybrid model retains both paths and uses a learned policy to control when each layer switches from full to linear attention. We train the policy with reinforcement learning using a reward that balances answer correctness and memory use. Empirical evaluation of our method shows that the learned adaptive policy achieves a better accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier on reasoning tasks compared to fixed switching and static hybrid baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [Circular Predictive Coding Unlocks Effective Depth](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27610

**作者：** Tommaso Salvatori、Matteo Forasassi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27610v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 predictive coding 在深层网络中误差信号不稳定或消失的问题，提出 circular 网络，让深度来自能量最小化而非堆叠层，并证明其收敛到平衡点近似 deep equilibrium 模型的不动点迭代；在图像分类、Sudoku 约束满足和自回归语言建模上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其用循环迭代换取有效深度、以能量最小化更新内部状态的机制，与 LVR 关注的 loop 式状态更新、动态计算和迭代推理步数直接相关，可供核查循环平衡点是否可作为 latent 推理状态的生成与训练载体；但未涉及视觉语言推理或视觉证据筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Circular Predictive Coding Unlocks Effective Depth

**abstract/A1：** Predictive coding networks perform credit assignment through an energy minimization process. Despite their ability to train models with arbitrary network topologies, they have mostly been used to train feedforward architectures to match the performance of backpropagation. However, they fail to do so when the models become too deep, because error signals become unstable or vanish as they travel through many layers. We address this limitation with circular networks, which get their depth directly from the energy minimization, rather than from stacking layers. Formally, we prove that convergence to an equilibrium of the energy functional of a circular predictive coding network approximates the fixed-point iteration process of a deep equilibrium model, showing that circularity alone suffices to unlock effective depth. Empirically, we show that circular models (i) outperform other predictive coding models of the same size on image classification tasks, matching the accuracy of state-of-the-art predictive coding ResNets and VGG networks despite having substantially fewer parameters; (ii) solve constraint satisfaction problems that require many steps of iterative reasoning, a task not feasible with feedforward models at all, reaching 99.3% test accuracy on Sudoku-hard via nested energy minimization loops; and (iii) scale to autoregressive language modeling by training a circular transformer on TinyStories and WikiText-103, before out of reach for predictive coding models. These results have direct implications for physical neural networks, where computational depth can be traded for recursion on a fixed-size circuit.

**comment/C1：** 25 Pages, many figures, preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Beyond Self-Critique: Epistemic Convergence Control for Multi-Agent Research Verification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27839v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27839

**作者：** Chrisantus Eze

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27839v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27839v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个多智能体科研主张验证框架：用 Advocate、Skeptic、Arbitrator 的非对称角色降低角色嵌入相似度，以 Belief-Delta 收敛准则替代固定迭代停止，并用独立 NLI 分类器审计逐字证据片段。

**相关性简评：** 其证据驱动的停止规则与证据锚定审计，可供核查 LVR 中 STOP 决策和视觉证据筛选的设计；角色嵌入相似度的度量方式也可能为 latent 相似度分析提供参照，但任务为纯文本验证，与视觉推理尚无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Self-Critique: Epistemic Convergence Control for Multi-Agent Research Verification

**abstract/A1：** Multi-agent systems for scientific claim verification increasingly rely on role-structured LLM debate to pressure-test hypotheses. However, when debating agents are instantiated from the same underlying base model, they share identical latent representations, making self-critique bias structurally inevitable. Static fixed-iteration protocols compound this by either halting prematurely before evidence stabilizes or wasting inference compute after convergence. We present \\sys{}, a multi-agent research verification framework that addresses both failure modes. First, we introduce an asymmetric role architecture comprising an Advocate, Skeptic, and Arbitrator with opposing epistemic directives, producing significantly lower role-embedding similarity (0.852 vs.\\ 0.918 for symmetric Multi-Agent Debate \[MAD\], $p = 5.82 \\times 10^{-8}$) and surfacing more contested claims (22.0\\% vs.\\ 15.4\\%). Second, we propose the Belief-Delta ($\\DB$) convergence criterion, an evidence-grounded stopping rule that eliminates over-iteration (0.0\\% vs.\\ 62.5\\% for Fixed-$3$) while cutting LLM calls by 2.4$\\times$. Third, each supported claim is anchored to a verbatim literature snippet audited by an independent NLI classifier, achieving a 21.7\\% hallucination rate (comparable to 25.9\\% for a Self-Refine critic, $p = 0.61$) while establishing auditable evidence grounding. Across 200 runs on 40 research hypotheses evaluated with DeepSeek-R1-32B, \\sys{} balances compute efficiency with rigorous epistemic verification.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICAART 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [Functional Depth Is Learned: Isolating the Computation That Layers Produce Only Together](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27911v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27911

**作者：** JaeSeong Kim、KyungTae Lim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27911v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27911v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 inclusion-exclusion 精确分解，度量仅当指定层共同作用时才出现的「连通计算」，并在 Pythia、OLMo、Amber 的公开 checkpoint 上追踪其随预训练增强、对困惑度的因果影响及与成对表示相似度的负相关。

**相关性简评：** 其连通计算分解与 identity ablation 可供核查 LVR 中深度收益与跨层贡献分配，并提示表示相似度不等同于实际跨层依赖；但论文对象是纯文本语言模型，多模态与 latent 推理联系需自行迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Functional Depth Is Learned: Isolating the Computation That Layers Produce Only Together

**abstract/A1：** Transformer performance scales with depth, but layer count merely counts the opportunities for sequential computation: layers could still behave as nearly independent refinements that barely depend on one another. We study how transformers develop functional depth during pretraining, introducing an exact inclusion-exclusion decomposition that isolates the connected component, i.e., the computation that appears only when a specified set of layers acts together under identity ablation. Tracking this quantity across public checkpoints of Pythia, OLMo, and Amber yields three findings. First, write-relative connected coordination strengthens from its initialization or earliest-checkpoint baseline as pretraining proceeds, across all measured interaction orders. Second, these connected components are behaviorally consequential under direct intervention: deleting them damages perplexity more than deleting a norm-matched random direction at the same site in all 35 measured model-order cells. Third, pairwise representation similarity is consistently negatively correlated with pairwise connected coordination across seven models, yet the two signals are not interchangeable: similarity- and coordination-based block-removal criteria often agree in Pythia but diverge on Amber. Together, these results suggest that architectural depth provides the opportunity for sequential computation, while pretraining progressively organizes cross-layer dependencies within that depth.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [COMPASS: Cross-modal Observation with Multi-Path Adaptive Stopping and Selection for Early Time Series Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27065v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27065

**作者：** Cheng-En Chung、Yujia Wu、Oscar Tai-Yuan Chen、Vincent S. Tseng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27065v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27065v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** COMPASS 针对多模态早期时序分类提出两阶段框架：先保留两个模态关联分支与一个融合分支，做跨模态特征交换、门控融合并逐前缀监督；再学习一个共享四动作控制器，决定继续观察还是经某个冻结分支终止输出。

**相关性简评：** 其「继续观察／终止＋选择由哪条分支给出答案」的联合决策，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 时机与依据当前证据选择读出路径提供可核查的机制线索；但工作在生理/可穿戴时序领域，不涉及 latent 推理状态，需自行判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** COMPASS: Cross-modal Observation with Multi-Path Adaptive Stopping and Selection for Early Time Series Classification

**abstract/A1：** Early time-series classification (ETSC) balances predictive performance against the amount of observed data. In multimodal settings, modality-associated and fused branches may differ in predictive ability at the same observed prefix, motivating joint decisions about when to stop and which branch should provide the final prediction. We propose COMPASS, a two-stage framework that preserves two modality-associated temporal branches and one fusion branch. The first stage combines cross-modal feature exchange and gated fusion with branch-wise supervision at every prefix. The second stage optimizes a shared four-action controller to continue observation or terminate through one of the frozen branches. Experiments on six physiological and wearable datasets compare COMPASS with multimodal adaptations of five ETSC baselines. COMPASS achieves the best average ranks in accuracy-earliness harmonic mean, AUROC, and earliness within this comparison set. At identical learned stopping times, branch selection improves mean accuracy over fusion readout on five of the six datasets, with gains of 2.35 and 2.16 percentage points on ISRUC-Sleep and UCDDB, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work on joint stopping and branch selection for multimodal early time series classification.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Beyond Final Answers: Interpretable Tumor Immune Microenvironment Reasoning from H&amp;E and mIF](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26794v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26794

**作者：** Yixuan Duan、Kexin Geng、Hanwen Xu、Wei Qiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26794v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26794v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 HERMES，一个在配对 H&amp;E 与 mIF 图像上按临床指南步骤进行推理的病理 MLLM，利用同切片 mIF 的细胞级真值构造过程奖励，逐步校验中间数量，并在结直肠癌四项任务及外部 CODEX、头颈癌队列上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「逐步核对所述数量」的过程奖励与「编辑关键中间量看答案是否随之改变」的因果检查，可供核查 VLM 推理中的证据使用与贡献分配；但推理为显式文本步骤而非 latent 状态，与内部表示层面的联系需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Final Answers: Interpretable Tumor Immune Microenvironment Reasoning from H&amp;E and mIF

**abstract/A1：** Readouts of the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME), such as CD8+ T-cell density, intra-epithelial infiltration and the Ki67 index, combine tissue architecture from H&amp;E with cell phenotypes from multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF). Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) could automate these readouts with an explanation, but two gaps remain. Pathology MLLMs read H&amp;E alone, while these readouts need the cell phenotypes that mIF measures. And their reasoning has no step-level truth to be checked against, so R1-style reinforcement learning rewards only the final answer and leaves the reasoning unverified. Same-section H&amp;E and mIF provide what both gaps lack: mIF measures every cell without manual annotation, which gives cell-level ground truth for each step that clinical guidelines prescribe, from delineating a tissue compartment to counting and normalizing the marker-positive cells within it. We therefore present HERMES (H&amp;E-mIF Reasoning with Multimodal Evidence Supervision), an R1-style MLLM that reasons jointly over paired H&amp;E and mIF in guideline steps and is trained with a process reward that checks every stated quantity against this truth. Across four field-level TIME tasks in colorectal cancer, HERMES (4B) reaches an average accuracy of 84.5%, compared with 74.7% for the strongest of twelve proprietary, general, medical and pathology MLLMs, and a higher share of its intermediate quantities is correct than for the strongest open-weight baseline. Without the process reward, reinforcement learning barely improves on supervised fine-tuning (80.0% vs. 78.8%) and fewer stated quantities are correct; editing a decisive stated quantity changes the answer as implied in all 885 test questions. Without retraining, it keeps the highest average accuracy on an external cohort imaged with CODEX (76.4% vs. 67.3%) and on head and neck cancer, a cancer type absent from training (76.7% vs. 72.5%).

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [TouchBench: Can Vision-Language Models Feel Invisible Physical Properties?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26883

**作者：** Ziyao Zeng、Ziyi Kou、Chuan Qin、Mia Huang、Roger Boldu、Zhiwen Fan、Daniel Rakita、Eric Whitmire、Li Guan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26883v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TouchBench，一个头戴视频与 137 通道全手触觉同步的自中心多模态基准，把可由图像回答的问题（Tier A）与依赖相机外物理属性（Tier B）的问题区分开，并提出将触觉统计以文本提示注入 VLM 的 Tactile-as-Prompt。

**相关性简评：** 其 Tier B 诊断显示 VLM 在任务必要信息不在视觉输入时接近随机，且文本接口优于学习式触觉编码器，可供核查「视觉旁路」与任务必要证据如何进入推理状态的问题，但未涉及 latent 推理状态本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TouchBench: Can Vision-Language Models Feel Invisible Physical Properties?

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for embodied perception and reasoning, yet their physical understanding is largely evaluated on questions answerable from images. Many properties critical to manipulation, such as an opaque bottle's fill level or an object's compliance, may only be revealed through touch. We introduce TouchBench, an egocentric vision-language-tactile benchmark that distinguishes visually answerable questions (Tier A) from those involving properties hidden from the camera (Tier B). It pairs head-mounted video with synchronized 137-channel whole-hand tactile sensing, comprising 900 clips from five participants across 11 tasks, with 900 absolute and 4,102 relative questions. Four state-of-the-art VLMs average 66.4% accuracy on Tier A relative questions but only 49.8% on Tier B, near chance (50%). To bridge this gap, we propose Tactile-as-Prompt (TaP), which presents 34 interpretable tactile statistics as text, enabling VLMs to reason with touch without a learned tactile encoder. TaP raises zero-shot Tier B relative accuracy to 62.9% and reaches 73.1% after leave-one-user-out fine-tuning, outperforming video alone (67.4%) and a learned tactile-token encoder (67.3%). The gains extend to unseen objects and users. On a separate 20-hour in-the-wild corpus, tactile text improves fine-tuned contact-question accuracy from 67.1% to 75.5%. These results highlight the limits of vision-only physical reasoning and establish language as an effective interface for tactile perception.

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Looking Right Is Not Being Right: Evaluating the Geometry of Agent-Built 3D Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26937v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26937

**作者：** Ziyang Long、Yen-Liang Lin、Shifan Zhu、Allen Tu、Meher Gitika Karumuri、Chenqi Li、Srikumar Sastry、Sarosij Bose、Mellon M. Zhang、Hsin-Jung Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26937v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26937v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出以激光扫描为参照的几何评分（曲面、深度、家具布局，0–100 分），在 30 个真实房间上评测视觉语言智能体由视频构建可编辑 3D 场景的几何准确度，并分析几何输入的作用、智能体的自我核查与修订行为，以及一种对照输入点云的几何误差报告能否提升修订收益。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选和验证环节相关：结果显示渲染图自检主要改善外观而非几何，误差报告迫使智能体比对输入与已建场景后才提升修订增益，可供核查「看哪里」与「是否验证了任务必要几何信息」之间的差距。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Looking Right Is Not Being Right: Evaluating the Geometry of Agent-Built 3D Scenes

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language agents can reconstruct rooms from video as editable 3D scenes, but convincing renders do not establish geometric accuracy. We introduce a scan-referenced evaluation and use controlled experiments to study reconstruction accuracy, the role of geometric inputs, and agents' ability to verify and correct their scenes. Our geometric score measures surface, depth, and furniture placement agreement with laser scans of 30 real rooms on a 0-100 scale. The strongest tested agent, GPT-6 Astra, scores 51.4 from video alone, while a dense reconstruction from the same video scores 75.9. Given the point cloud used for that reconstruction, Astra produces an editable scene that scores within three points of it. Agents also inherit input errors: rescaling the supplied geometry changes scene scale almost proportionally. During revision, viewing renders improves appearance more consistently than geometry. Logs of their tool calls show that agents repeatedly measure their inputs but rarely compare them with the scenes they build. To provide this missing check, we introduce a geometric error report that compares each saved scene with the agent's input points and lists the largest discrepancies. Without new geometric evidence, the report raises the revision gains of GPT-6.1 Terra and Sol by 3.9 and 5.5 points over concurrent controls.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 6 figures. Project: https://github.com/Albertlongzi/looking-right-being-right

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Can Reasoning and Image Description Enhance Yes/No Visual Mathematical Understanding?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27302v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27302

**作者：** Guangcheng Song、Weichuan Wang、Linqi Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27302v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27302v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建成对的视觉数学是/否判断评测集，在多种 prompt 与输入条件下比较主流开源 VLM，考察 CoT 显式推理与文本图像描述（替代或补充视觉输入）能否提升判断准确性与配对一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其结果（推理有助稳定判断、文本描述常无效甚至有害）可供核查 LVR 中「视觉旁路」与信息坍缩：文本化中间表示是否丢掉了任务必要的视觉细节，以及推理期是否真正使用视觉证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Reasoning and Image Description Enhance Yes/No Visual Mathematical Understanding?

**abstract/A1：** Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on general visual tasks, yet their ability to understand mathematical images remains limited. This paper investigates whether explicit reasoning and textual image descriptions can enhance VLMs' performance on binary yes/no visual mathematical questions. To achieve this, we curated a paired evaluation set to benchmark prominent open-source VLMs under varying prompt and input conditions. In particular, we leverage Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and use textual descriptions, either replacing or augmenting visual inputs, to isolate and evaluate mathematical comprehension. Our results show that explicit reasoning improves performance, especially in paired consistency accuracy, while textual image descriptions do not consistently help and may degrade performance. These findings suggest that reasoning is important for stable visual mathematical judgement, whereas description-based intermediates require more careful design.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [VMSA: Video Memory Skill Agent for Long Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27381v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27381

**作者：** Changin Choi、Jungwon Park、Jungmin Ko、Jimyeong Kim、Wonjong Rhee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27381v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27381v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 VMSA 框架处理长视频问答：构建片段字幕、实体事件、实体感知片段三类互补外部视频记忆作为检索证据源，并从成功 agent 轨迹中归纳「记忆技能」，指导迭代式证据检索、推理与验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「选哪个记忆源、何时停止检索并验证」的决策与技能库式轨迹监督，可供核查与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 策略及证据筛选的联系，但推理状态仍为外部文本记忆而非 latent 表示，需读原文确认成本与跨间隔证据连接的实现。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VMSA: Video Memory Skill Agent for Long Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Long-video question answering requires retrieving and integrating relevant evidence from videos spanning hours or even days. Recent agentic approaches organize video information into external memories and iteratively retrieve evidence to answer questions. However, effective retrieval and reasoning remain challenging, as agents must determine what information to search for, which memory sources to access, and how to connect evidence across distant temporal intervals. To address these challenges, we propose Video Memory Skill Agent (VMSA), a framework that jointly designs structured video memories and memory skills for long-video understanding. VMSA constructs three complementary memory components. Segment Caption Memory and Entity Event Memory serve as searchable evidence sources, while Entity-Aware Episode Memory provides broad yet detailed video context to guide evidence retrieval and reasoning. To further support effective memory utilization, VMSA induces memory skills from successful agent trajectories, incrementally refining or extending a compact skill library to guide iterative evidence retrieval, reasoning, and verification. Across seven long-video question-answering benchmarks, VMSA achieves the highest accuracy on all three week-long egocentric benchmarks, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.4% on Ego-R1 Bench, and the best or competitive performance on four additional benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of all three memory components and memory skills, with memory skills also improving temporal evidence localization.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [AppCon-Bench: Perception and Misperception of Material Appearance in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27453v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27453

**作者：** Santiago Jimenez-Navarro、Ana Serrano、Belen Masia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27453v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27453v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 AppCon-Bench，包含五个任务，在受控的几何与光照变化下考察 VLM 能否对材质外观作出前后一致的判断，并与人类被试对比，分析系统性差异与共性。

**相关性简评：** 属于 VLM 感知诊断类基准，直接涉及「视觉细节与中层次属性是否被保留」这一表示问题，可供核查多视角、多光照下视觉证据不变性的失败模式；但其不含 latent 状态生成或推理期机制，与 LVR 的联系限于表示诊断层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AppCon-Bench: Perception and Misperception of Material Appearance in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Identifying the same material across changes in illumination or geometry is a critical ability for robust vision systems. Humans exhibit remarkable material appearance constancy, being able to recognize the intrinsic physical properties of an object's surface material across variations in extrinsic, confounding factors such as the lighting or the shape of such object. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance across a wide range of semantic vision tasks, yet their ability to interpret subtle mid-level perceptual attributes remains less understood. We introduce AppCon-Bench, a benchmark comprising five tasks designed to assess material appearance constancy under controlled variations in geometry and illumination, and evaluate representative VLMs alongside human observers, investigating their ability to maintain consistent material judgments across viewing conditions and experimental contexts. After thorough analysis, we identify several key findings that reveal both similarities and systematic differences between human and VLM material appearance perception, highlighting the value of evaluating perceptual successes and failures beyond overall accuracy and informing areas of improvement for current VLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [Fix Where It Fails: Root-Cause-Guided Agentic Harness Optimization for Scene Graph Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27529v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27529

**作者：** Jee Eun Kim、Donghyeon Choi、Sangjune An、Hae Beom Lee、Kyong Hwan Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27529v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27529v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RoHa-SGG，用根因分析 agent 定位冻结 SGG 模型在哪些组件丢失真值三元组及原因，再由提议 agent 搜索推理代码（harness）修改，在 Visual Genome 和 PSG 上免训练提升多种 SGG 模型。

**相关性简评：** SGG 属结构化视觉关系推理，其跨模型/数据集的失败组件归因与推理期 harness 优化，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据保留、失败定位和外部计算分配提供诊断线索；但方法不涉及 latent 推理状态，需核查其根因分析能否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fix Where It Fails: Root-Cause-Guided Agentic Harness Optimization for Scene Graph Generation

**abstract/A1：** Scene graph generation (SGG) aims to detect objects in an image and predict the relationships between them. The standard PredCls, SGCls, and SGDet settings show how much performance drops without ground-truth boxes and labels, but not where or why a model fails. Knowing this would allow fixing a model where it fails, without retraining. We propose RoHa-SGG (Root-cause-guided Harness optimization for SGG), which extends agentic harness optimization to SGG. It optimizes the harness, the inference code around a frozen SGG model. RoHa-SGG has two main LLM agents. A root-cause analysis (RCA) agent finds the components where the model fails and diagnoses why, and a proposer agent searches for a harness that fixes these causes. We evaluate RoHa-SGG on Visual Genome and PSG with representative two-stage, one-stage, and multimodal-LLM SGG models. The analysis shows that the components where ground-truth triplets are lost differ across models and datasets. RoHa-SGG improves every metric on all five model-dataset combinations. Graph-constrained R@100 and mR@100 rise by up to 15.8 and 26.4 points for the multimodal LLM and by up to 3.6 and 10.1 points for the two-stage and one-stage models. With root-cause guidance, the harness search reaches the final score of an unguided search up to three times faster.

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [MoHA: Model-Conditioned Harness Adaptation for Long-Video Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27646v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27646

**作者：** Jianghan Chao、Wenhui Tan、Hang Su、Qiuyu Tian、Tanxiao Li、Bingzi Zhang、Zequn Liu、Ruihua Song、Yingce Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27646v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27646v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MoHA，将长视频理解 agent 的执行 harness 视为受模型条件约束的优化对象，依据失败校准轨迹诊断 planner 与 harness 的不匹配，并调整规划侧的概览、记忆、验证支持及观察侧的 OCR/ASR 等能力配置。

**相关性简评：** 其模型条件化的证据获取、验证与执行结构可供核查 planner-observer 分工如何影响 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益与成本，但研究位于 agent 系统层而非 latent 推理，联系仍需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MoHA: Model-Conditioned Harness Adaptation for Long-Video Agents

**abstract/A1：** Long-video understanding increasingly relies on agentic systems that actively acquire evidence rather than process fixed video representations. Their execution harnesses must coordinate language-level planning with video-level observation and evidence acquisition, yet are typically fixed across model stacks. We therefore introduce MoHA, a framework for model-conditioned harness adaptation that adapts a common initial execution harness to the specific planner and observer models it hosts. MoHA treats the harness itself as a model-conditioned optimization object: under the same task distribution and initialization, different planner-observer stacks can benefit from different executable harnesses. Starting from a common initial harness, MoHA analyzes failed calibration traces to diagnose model-harness mismatches and adapts both planner-side reasoning support, such as overview, memory, and verification, and observer-side capability assignments, including OCR and ASR specialists. After structural adaptation, MoHA separately calibrates observer execution configurations for the hosted model stack. Across three frozen planner stacks, MoHA improves Video-MME Full accuracy by 6.2-27.1 percentage points and Video-Holmes accuracy by 3.1-20.1 points over the fixed initial harness. Cross-model transfer further shows that the adapted harnesses generalize only partially to other models, highlighting the harness as a model-conditioned optimization layer for long-video understanding. Code is available at https://github.com/roverx12345/MoHA.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 3 figures. Code: https://github.com/roverx12345/MoHA

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [Can Vision-Language Models Synthesize Executable Game-Playing Programs?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27741v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27741

**作者：** Yao Tong、Jiaxin Qi、Dingyu Zhang、Jianqiang Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27741v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27741v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PlayScriptBench，用于评估 VLM 能否依据游戏状态合成可执行的游戏控制程序，并给出 Explore、Perception、API Construction、Strategy Composition 四阶段分解流水线，在 16 款游戏上测试。

**相关性简评：** 该基准把逐步交互改为一次性合成可执行控制器，联合考察视觉 grounding、游戏动态理解与长程策略，可供核查 VLM 的视觉证据保留、推理状态能否沉淀复用以及逐步调用的计算成本与停止决策问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Vision-Language Models Synthesize Executable Game-Playing Programs?

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have endowed multimodal agents with increasingly strong visual perception and long-horizon reasoning capabilities, making VLM-based autonomous game playing feasible. Existing game benchmarks primarily evaluate whether an agent can select appropriate actions from the current visual state across different scenarios and games. However, most of them adopt a step-by-step interaction paradigm where the current visual observation is sent to the agent before each action. In practical deployment, this paradigm introduces two fundamental limitations: the game loop must wait for model inference, making real-time interaction impractical, and repeated VLM calls incur substantial cost. Therefore, we propose PlayScriptBench, a script-based game benchmark that evaluates whether VLMs can synthesize an executable game-playing program from game states. This setting jointly evaluates visual grounding, understanding of game dynamics, long-horizon strategic reasoning, and executable program synthesis, providing a more comprehensive assessment of game-playing agents. To support more tractable evaluation for current general-purpose VLMs, we further introduce a four-stage pipeline --- Explore, Perception, API Construction, and Strategy Composition --- that decomposes controller synthesis into game exploration, state perception, interface construction, and strategic control, enabling agents to progressively construct an executable program. Experiments on 16 games spanning 4 genres show that even strong VLMs struggle on our benchmark, revealing a substantial gap between passive visual game understanding and executable closed-loop game control. These results establish script-based controller synthesis as a complementary direction for benchmarking VLM-based game-playing agents.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages (main paper with appendix), 3 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [ENC-QA: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Electronic Navigational Charts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27858v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27858

**作者：** Jiyoon Pyo、Jacob Arndt、Yao-Yi Chiang、Dalton Lunga

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27858v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27858v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 ENC-QA，一个基于 NOAA 电子航海图的诊断基准，用约 7.7 万个问题评估 VLM 的细粒度要素识别、空间推理及结合证据判断更新严重性的能力。

**相关性简评：** 其递进式子集与证据干预直接考察模型是否利用感知和空间证据作出判断，可作为视觉细节保留与证据使用诊断的参考，但领域专属性较强，需核查题目设计能否迁移到 LVR 分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ENC-QA: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Electronic Navigational Charts

**abstract/A1：** Nautical charts encode safety-critical maritime information through standardized symbols, attributes, text, and heterogeneous geometries. Interpreting these charts and assessing the navigational significance of successive chart updates requires recognizing nautical features, determining their spatial relationships, and applying navigational knowledge. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising approach to nautical-chart interpretation, yet systematic evaluation of their fine-grained nautical feature recognition, chart-based spatial reasoning, and application of navigational knowledge to assessing chart changes remains limited. We introduce ENC-QA, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 77,312 questions derived from 6,612 National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs). The benchmark evaluates four complementary capabilities: ENC Perception, Spatial Reasoning, Joint Perception and Spatial Reasoning, and Application. Of these, 8,227 questions form a progressive subset that decomposes selected chart-change cases into linked questions progressing from change detection to criticality assessment, enabling case-level analysis of how models use perceptual and spatial evidence to determine the navigational significance of chart changes. Evaluations of 10 open-weight VLMs reveal substantial limitations in fine-grained nautical feature recognition and feature-grounded spatial reasoning. Controlled evidence interventions show that relevant perceptual and spatial information improves change-criticality accuracy. Our findings establish ENC-QA as a diagnostic resource for nautical-chart understanding and safety-critical navigational decisions. We will release the benchmark and evaluation code upon publication.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [Model the Annotation, Not the Noise: Semantic Annotation-Channel Prompt Learning against Noisy Labels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26702v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26702

**作者：** Zibin Geng、Yitong Bao、Jia Li、Xuefeng Jiang、Sheng Sun、Yuwei Wang、Min Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26702v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26702v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 SACP，将含噪标签的 prompt learning 视为生成过程，把潜在干净标签预测与实例相关的标注生成解耦，用冻结 VLM 语义先验加轻量视觉残差构造标注通道，并以广义 EM 的软后验优化，另设后验感知记忆在测试时保留标签不确定性。

**相关性简评：** 其以软后验建模潜在干净状态、并按实例保留不确定性的做法，或可供核查 LVR 中多 latent 假设的加权与视觉证据可信度估计；但任务限于分类标签噪声，与推理状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Model the Annotation, Not the Noise: Semantic Annotation-Channel Prompt Learning against Noisy Labels

**abstract/A1：** Parameter-efficient prompt learning has become a cornerstone for adapting pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), yet it remains highly vulnerable to label noise in few-shot Web and retrieval scenarios. Existing noise robust methods typically rely on hard sample selection or deterministic label correction, which discard valuable semantic ambiguity and overlook how observed annotations are actually generated. To bridge this gap, we reformulate noisy prompt learning through the perspective of data generation, proposing SACP, a Semantic-aware Annotation Channel-based Prompt learning framework. Instead of suppressing noise heuristically, SACP decouples latent clean-label prediction from instance-dependent annotation generation. By integrating frozen VLM semantic priors with lightweight visual residuals, SACP constructs a structured annotation channel optimized via generalized expectation-maximization with soft posteriors. Furthermore, a posterior-aware memory is introduced to preserve label uncertainty during test-time inference. Extensive experiments across different synthetic benchmarks and the real-world noisy Food101N dataset demonstrate that SACP achieves state-of-the-art performance against its counterparts in different label noise patterns, especially under severe asymmetric noise.

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Memorization and Generalization in Generative Models: A Perspective From Energy-based Modeling and Associative Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26797v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26797

**作者：** Bao Pham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26797v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26797v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文（学位论文）以能量模型与联想记忆为框架，论证扩散模型（含 uniform-state 离散扩散语言模型）可视为联想记忆：训练样本周围形成吸引盆，数据增多时盆地收缩并出现混合样本的「伪态」，作者跨数据集与架构（含 Stable Diffusion）测量盆地的体积、能量与曲率，并用条件熵诊断记忆与泛化的共存。

**相关性简评：** 其吸引子动力学与盆地几何描述状态如何被检索和稳定，可能为 latent 推理状态的更新与「记忆还是新生成」判别提供分析工具，条件熵式诊断也可供核查是否能用于停止决策；但该文无视觉语言推理实验，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Memorization and Generalization in Generative Models: A Perspective From Energy-based Modeling and Associative Memory

**abstract/A1：** Generative models now write working code, produce realistic images, and hold long conversations, yet the mechanisms behind their intelligence still evade our understanding. This thesis explores that puzzle through Associative Memory (AM), in which patterns are stored as energy minima and retrieved through attractor dynamics. Beyond their capacity, AMs form new minima known as spurious states, long regarded as a failure of memory. We argue that this failure is where creativity begins, and that memorization and generalization can coexist as outcomes of one retrieval process. First, we show that diffusion models behave as AMs. With little data, a diffusion model forms a distinct basin of attraction around each training sample. As the data grows, these basins shrink, and spurious states emerge as attractors that blend stored samples yet match none of them. They are the first signs of generative capability. We characterize this transition by measuring the volume, energy, and curvature of these basins across datasets and architectures, including Stable Diffusion. We then turn to language and move beyond the energy-based view of AM. We show that uniform-state discrete diffusion models are AMs capable of retrieving unseen data, with basins of attraction that arise from conditional likelihood maximization alone. As the training set grows, basins around training examples contract while basins around unseen examples expand, until corrupted test sentences are recovered as reliably as training ones. Conditional entropy exposes this coexistence within a single sequence and cheaply diagnoses where a model lies along the transition. Together, these results suggest that AM is a general principle defined by basins of attraction, and that memorization and generalization can coexist within one model. AM thus offers a framework for asking when a generative model recalls and when it creates.

**comment/C1：** A thesis that discusses the coexistence of memorization and generalization in generative models

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [Don't Discard Your Rollouts: Reusing Teacher RL Traces for Student Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26803v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26803

**作者：** Vashisth Tiwari、Emma Strubell、J. Zico Kolter

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26803v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26803v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究如何复用教师模型 RL 训练中已采样但被常规蒸馏丢弃的 rollout：把复用视为数据选择问题，按 prompt 通过率排序，先用易题做 SFT 热启动、再用混合成败题做 DPO，并用教师 log-likelihood 筛选与学生兼容的 completion，在数学与代码任务上接近标准蒸馏效果。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RL 蒸馏，与视觉无关；但其「按难度与师生兼容性筛选轨迹、再分阶段监督」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中推理轨迹训练、监督信号分配与低质量轨迹剔除的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Discard Your Rollouts: Reusing Teacher RL Traces for Student Distillation

**abstract/A1：** RL post-training methods such as PPO and GRPO are computationally expensive, with substantial resources devoted to sampling rollouts, which contain multiple completions per prompt along with their respective rewards. Canonical distillation, however, discards these traces and uses more compute to regenerate data from the final teacher. We ask if and how we can reuse the saved teacher RL rollouts to distil student models. Reusing rollouts is non-trivial: naive SFT on the rollouts underperforms canonical distillation, and naive DPO, while performant, is unstable, collapsing on weaker students. We show that treating rollout reuse as a data selection problem using metadata already recorded during GRPO substantially reduces this gap. We use prompt pass rates to order the data, using the easiest prompts for an SFT warm start prior to using mixed-success prompts for DPO. For each prompt, we use saved teacher log-likelihoods as a proxy for selecting student-compatible completions. Across mathematical reasoning and Python coding tasks, with students from multiple model families, our two-stage recipe achieves comparable average performance to canonical distillation, while incurring no additional inference cost for dataset construction.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [NitroGen: Observations on the Action Dynamics, Visual Instruction Following, and Spatial Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26807v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26807

**作者：** Shengyuan Lin、Zhiling Li、Haoyu Nie、Ozan K. Tonguz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26807v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26807v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以在线游戏视频行为克隆训练的视觉-动作基础模型 NitroGen 为对象，冻结其控制器后在 Hollow Knight 与 Trackmania 上从动作适应、视觉指令跟随、空间探索三方面做诊断，发现它能适应当地上下文，但缺少长程控制所需的规划、恢复与探索能力。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结控制器 + 视觉指令跟随」的探测思路，可供核查 VLM 推理是否真正使用视觉证据（而非旁路），而局部适应尚可、长程规划与探索失败的 结论也可能为 STOP 决策与动态计算预算问题提供参照线索，需读原文确认探测设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NitroGen: Observations on the Action Dynamics, Visual Instruction Following, and Spatial Exploration

**abstract/A1：** NitroGen is an open vision-action foundation model for game control. It is trained by behavior cloning on online gameplay videos. In this paper, we study the frozen controller in Hollow Knight and Trackmania as examples along three dimensions: action adaptation, visual instruction following, and spatial exploration. Our results indicate that NitroGen can adapt its actions to local context, but it lacks the planning, recovery, and exploration needed for longer-horizon game control.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 7 figures and 10 Tables in the main paper, several more in the Appendices

</details>

### 53. [Self-Supervised Learning with Recursive Quadrature Filters for Neural Time Series](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26856v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26856

**作者：** Mirko Morello、Shivang Rawat、David J. Heeger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26856v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26856v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出用预测误差自监督训练递归正交滤波（RQF）编码器：窗口模式下由当前窗摘要预测后续窗摘要，密集模式下由当前状态预测未来对数功率，再在冻结或微调编码器上训练读出层，并在四个 EEG 基准上以极小参数量接近 SOTA。

**相关性简评：** 其「当前隐状态预测自身未来活动」的自监督目标与对角 SSM 状态更新，可供核查 LVR 中潜在推理状态的轨迹式监督与状态可预测性设计；但与视觉推理主线无直接关联，迁移仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Supervised Learning with Recursive Quadrature Filters for Neural Time Series

**abstract/A1：** We develop self-supervised methods for training Recursive Quadrature Filter (RQF) encoders on unlabeled time series. RQFs are biologically motivated, complex-valued temporal filters that form a special class of diagonal state-space models (SSMs). Each RQF acts as a band-pass filter whose selectivity is determined by two learnable parameters: its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We separately introduce a self-supervised learning objective based on prediction error; the encoder is trained so that its current state predicts its own future activity. In the windowed mode, the summary of the current window predicts the summary of a later window; in the dense mode, the state at the current timestep predicts future log-power. Each RQF encoder is pretrained on a dataset and then a readout (e.g., a task-specific classifier) is trained on the output of the RQF encoder, with the encoder either frozen or fully fine-tuned. For multichannel EEG and intracranial recordings, a shared encoder processes each channel separately, and a spatial readout combines the channel representations using electrode locations. Across four neural benchmarks using scalp or intracranial EEG, the pretrained RQF system performs close to the state of the art while using a small fraction of the parameters of the competing models. For example, a 110k-parameter RQF system achieves 0.9247 +/- 0.0022 test AUROC on 100-s TUAB segments, above SAMBA (0.9054) at the same context with 9 times as many parameters, and 0.9040 +/- 0.0019 on 10-s segments, comparable to EEG foundation models with 3.2M-369M parameters.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 3 figures, 12 tables. NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Foundation Models for the Brain and Body. Code: https://github.com/Sequel-Institute/rqf-ssl

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26865

**作者：** John J. Han、Adam Schmidt、Jingpei Lu、Zooey He、Muhammad Abdullah Jamal、Jie Ying Wu、Omid Mohareri

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26865v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对外科机器人模仿学习示范稀缺的问题，FlowScope 用 1149 台手术的立体视频无监督预训练 ACT 主干，任务是预测由现成深度与光流模型算出的场景流（场景点的度量 3D 运动），预训练后丢弃流预测头，部署时仍是标准 ACT、无额外推理开销。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身不涉及 LVR 或 VLM 推理，但其「预测度量 3D 运动」这一视觉预训练目标与 2D 轨迹、单深度、无尺度流的消融对比，以及深度探针分析，可能为 LVR 中内部状态如何保留任务必需的几何信息提供可供核查的训练侧线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies

**abstract/A1：** Imitation learning for surgical robots is limited by the cost of collecting demonstrations. Meanwhile, many robot-assisted procedures are recorded as stereo video, yet policies cannot learn from them directly; clinical video carries no action labels. We present FlowScope, which pretrains a surgical policy network on unlabeled clinical video by forecasting scene flow, the metric 3D motion of scene points, with labels computed by off-the-shelf stereo depth and optical flow models. We pretrain the trunk of an Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) on a dataset of 1,149 clinical procedures (1.35M clips) and discard the flow head afterward, so the deployed policy is a standard ACT with no added inference cost. On a real surgical robotic system, FlowScope raises needle pickup success from 54% to 90% and first-handover success on needle regrasp from 4% to 52% (full task: 2% to 14%) over training from scratch (n=50 trials each), and retains its gains with a generative flow-matching action head. Ablations indicate that metric 3D motion is the strongest pretraining signal among those tested; kinematics, 2D tracks, depth alone, and scale-free 3D flow, each paired with kinematics, yield smaller or no gains. Representation analyses suggest that scene-flow pretraining encodes metric needle depth more accurately, that kinematics pretraining increases reliance on the robot state, and that needle-depth probes follow the ordering of real-robot success where offline validation error does not.

</details>

### 55. [SonoAtlasWorld: Anatomy-Grounded Ultrasound World Models for Wide-Range Scanning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26867v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26867

**作者：** Zheyuan Zhang、Boris Mailhe、Hang Yu、Yikang Liu、Xiao Chen、Ankush Mukherjee、Terrence Chen、Shanhui Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26867v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26867v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建以解剖为状态的超声世界模型：用 CT 提取的持久 3D 解剖资产锚定扫描，由探头动作决定解剖与声学可见性，并训练因果超声生成器；采用三阶段流程与推理优化达到 81.2 FPS，同时提出 AFI 衡量动作跟随程度。

**相关性简评：** 将「持久解剖资产」作为外置状态锚定生成、并以静态前缀基线归一化增益（AFI）评价动作因果贡献，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据如何进入内部状态、以及 latent 内容是否真正被使用而非退化为基线。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SonoAtlasWorld: Anatomy-Grounded Ultrasound World Models for Wide-Range Scanning

**abstract/A1：** Learning robotic ultrasound policies requires interactive environments for scalable data generation and repeatable evaluation, while exploration on real patients is limited by acquisition costs and safety constraints. Generative world models offer an alternative, but image history and probe actions alone do not ensure anatomical consistency across wide-range scans and changing acoustic windows. We introduce SonoAtlasWorld, an ultrasound world model built on anatomy as state. A persistent 3D anatomical asset extracted from computed tomography (CT) anchors each scan to a specific anatomy. Probe actions determine anatomical and acoustic visibility conditions for a causal ultrasound generator trained on clinical videos. Our three-stage pipeline combines anatomy-conditioned pretraining, causal conversion and long-rollout alignment. Inference optimizations achieve pose-to-ultrasound throughput of 81.2 FPS. To evaluate anatomical control beyond image similarity, we introduce the Action-Following Index (AFI), which measures normalized reconstruction gain over a static-prefix baseline. On 40 held-out 3D volumes, SonoAtlasWorld achieves liver and macro-organ AFI of 0.428 and 0.262, compared with at most 0.117 and 0.038 for the tested action- and geometry-conditioned methods. Long-rollout alignment reduces brightness drift by 3.46x at the end of 40 repeated scan cycles. SonoAtlasWorld also generates offline policy-training demonstrations and supports anatomically grounded closed-loop evaluation. Scaling the generated training data improves key-plane acquisition success from 48.2% to 81.2% on unseen anatomies in simulation, demonstrating its utility for robotic ultrasound policy learning.

**comment/C1：** Interactive video demonstrations are available at: https://sonoatlasworld.github.io/SonoAtlasWorld

</details>

### 56. [Budget-Aware Predict-and-Verify Theorem Proving with Heterogeneous LLM Solvers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26906v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26906

**作者：** Wei Jiang、Ziran Liu、Wei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26906v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26906v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BudgetPV，一个预算感知的「预测—验证」定理证明系统：用跨求解器预测器为每个子目标选择不同成本的 LLM 求解器，并随验证反馈动态重新分配计算；预测器按初始分配与后续再分配两阶段分别用类加权交叉熵和普通交叉熵训练。

**相关性简评：** 虽属形式化推理而非视觉任务，但其「用校准概率决定在哪些未解子目标上追加计算、并以验证反馈修正分配」可供核查 LVR 的 THINK/LOOK/STOP 收益—成本权衡与停止决策；分阶段监督目标的设计也可能为 latent 轨迹的贡献分配提供线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Budget-Aware Predict-and-Verify Theorem Proving with Heterogeneous LLM Solvers

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) theorem solvers differ in both proving capability and inference cost. Applying an expensive solver to every subgoal wastes computation when cheaper models are sufficient, while always starting from the cheapest solver wastes attempts on subgoals that require stronger models. We present BudgetPV, an agentic budget-aware predict-and-verify system that allocates computation across unresolved subgoals and heterogeneous LLM solvers. BudgetPV uses a cross-solver predictor to guide solver selection for each subgoal and revises the allocation as verification feedback becomes available. The allocation problem changes as proving progresses. Early decisions mainly determine which solver should handle an untried subgoal, while later decisions determine which previously attempted subgoals should receive additional computation. We train the predictor with two objectives for these two stages. Class-weighted cross-entropy improves assignment to less frequent stronger solvers during the initial stage, while plain cross-entropy provides better calibrated probabilities for later allocation among previously attempted subgoals. We evaluate BudgetPV on Lean Workbook, miniF2F, ProofNet, and PutnamBench using three progressively expanded solver settings: a strong but expensive solver alone, adding a medium-cost solver with intermediate solving capability, and further adding a cheap solver with lower solving capability. BudgetPV requires less LLM generation computation than uniform allocation to solve the same number of subgoals across the evaluated settings. The experiments further show the computation savings obtained as medium- and lower-cost solvers are added.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 9 tables, 1 figure

</details>

### 57. [Self-Evolving Beyond Human-Crafted Experience for EDA Scripting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26912v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26912

**作者：** Yang Liu、Shengbo Tong、Xiyuan Chen、Shiwei Hou、Sen Yuan、Hao Wang、Yanfeng Li、Hongwen Li、Yiming Zhu、Zhuolun He、Wenjian Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26912v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26912v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ZhuLong-SE，一个面向 EDA 脚本编写的执行反馈式 LLM 智能体，在任务级框架（API 检索、文档响应、隔离沙箱执行、静态检查）之上加入跨任务自演化，把历史执行轨迹蒸馏为受真值锚定、反证过滤、留出回滚和防泄漏检索约束的三层记忆库，并在 EDA-Eval-PyAether 基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 不含视觉或多模态内容，但其「轨迹→记忆」的筛选与回滚机制（真值锚定、反证过滤、held-out 回滚）可供核查是否用于 LVR 中轨迹训练监督与贡献分配、抑制错误 latent 经验累积。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Evolving Beyond Human-Crafted Experience for EDA Scripting

**abstract/A1：** The growing complexity of electronic systems demands efficient design flows, yet EDA scripting remains a bottleneck: proprietary APIs are sparsely documented, and errors often appear as silent failures or cryptic exceptions. Hand-crafted knowledge helps but is difficult to maintain as toolchains evolve. We present ZhuLong-SE (Self-Evolution), an execution-grounded LLM agent that learns reusable knowledge from prior task traces. Building on ZhuLong, it enhances the task-level harness with API retrieval, documentation responses, isolated sandbox execution, and static checking, then adds governed cross-task self-evolution. Beyond solving individual tasks, ZhuLong-SE distills past execution traces into a three-tier memory store governed by ground-truth anchoring, disconfirmation filtering, held-out rollback, and anti-leakage retrieval. On the 158-task EDA-Eval-PyAether benchmark, the full task-level harness, with self-evolution disabled, achieves 92.4% pass@1 and outperforms ZhuLong on all five tested LLM backbones. On a harder instance-disjoint test set, self-evolution from the bare configuration surpasses a human-crafted-experience baseline by 2.5 percentage points (80.0% vs. 77.5% pass@1).

**comment/C1：** Submitted to DATE 2027. 7 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables

</details>

### 58. [Multi-Tier Edge Triage: Optimizing Latency for Safe Clinical Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26914v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26914

**作者：** Joshua Broyde、Sarita A. Joshi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26914v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26914v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「Multi-Tier Edge Triage」临床 agent 安全筛查架构：在本地部署小型 Tier-1 分类器就地处理明确请求，仅把模糊请求升级给云端 LLM 评审，并用长度配对的对照孪生基准检验 Tier-1 编码器，报告微调后 335M 分类器在多政策准确率上接近零样本云端评审且延迟约 39 ms。

**相关性简评：** 其按歧义程度决定是否升级到更重模型的级联门控，可能与 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的计算分配与停止决策可供核查；但对象是文本安全审查，与视觉证据和 latent 推理状态无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-Tier Edge Triage: Optimizing Latency for Safe Clinical Agents

**abstract/A1：** Deploying generative AI agents in enterprise clinical workflows requires enforcing multi-dimensional safety and compliance policies--preventing HIPAA Protected Health Information (PHI) exposure, maintaining clinical scope-of-practice boundaries, ensuring patient-appropriate reading comprehension, and preserving brand integrity--under interactive latency constraints. Synchronous outbound calls to cloud Large Language Model (LLM) judges add substantial network and decoding overhead onto the critical response path. We present Multi-Tier Edge Triage, a safety screening architecture that co-locates a compact Tier-1 sequence classifier on the clinical agent's host server, resolving clear queries in-process without a network round-trip and escalating only ambiguous queries to a Tier-2 cloud LLM judge. To evaluate Tier-1 encoders without length or unigram shortcuts, we construct four length-matched contrastive twin benchmarks seeded from MedSafetyBench, ASQ-PHI, and a synthetic four-mandate clinical assistant policy (Generic HealthCorp). While frozen local encoders match cloud models on structured PHI detection (&gt;= 98.3% accuracy in &lt;= 23.5 ms), they degrade markedly on semantic safety and symmetric-blocklist prompts. However, end-to-end representation fine-tuning closes the multi-policy accuracy gap: a fine-tuned 335M-parameter BGE-Large classifier achieves 98.90% out-of-fold accuracy versus 99.20% for a zero-shot cloud LLM judge (exact McNemar p = 0.648, zero shared errors), 97.8%-98.2% recall, and a 0.0%-0.4% over-refusal rate in 39.0 ms (&gt;14x faster than cloud LLM evaluation on our benchmark host). Per-policy error analysis reveals ceiling local recall on PHI (100.0%), brand (100.0%), and clinical scope (98.7%-99.3%) violations, isolating residual errors to reading-level checks (93.3%-95.3%) and motivating targeted multi-tier escalation.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Large Reported CHB-MIT Adaptation Gains Disappear with Pretrained Encoders and Matched Probes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26929v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26929

**作者：** Joseph Sankoorikal Johny、Antony J. Sankoorikal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26929v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26929v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文复核 Dani 与 Liebe（2026）关于 BIOT、CBraMod 在 CHB-MIT 上自监督适配带来大幅增益的结论：用其公开代码与独立流程发现配置缺失导致未加载预训练权重、探针读取重建头且 dropout 未关闭；修正后 18 组比较的平均适配效应多在 -1.1 至 +5.7 点，多数支持等价比，并顺带审计了一个被至少 14 篇论文共用的 CHB-MIT 协议。

**相关性简评：** 其「匹配探针、评估模式读取、统一调参预算、依赖校正区间」的核查方式，可能迁移到 LVR 中判断 latent 内容是否真被使用；探针挂载位置与读取头等易错点可供核查视觉 latent 可读性评测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Large Reported CHB-MIT Adaptation Gains Disappear with Pretrained Encoders and Matched Probes

**abstract/A1：** Dani and Liebe (2026) (DL) report that adapting the final encoder block of BIOT and CBraMod with self-supervised learning (SSL) improves CHB-MIT seizure-detection ROC-AUC by 36.2 and 29.5 points over linear probing. We investigate these results with their released code and an independent pipeline. The release loads pretrained weights for adaptation only when an otherwise unset configuration key is supplied, and its CBraMod probe reads the reconstruction head with dropout active. With pretrained initialisation and a probe on the encoder output in evaluation mode, the fixed-recipe adaptation gain on DL's split is -0.6 points for BIOT (95% interval \[-3.8, +2.6\]) and +0.01 for CBraMod (\[-0.02, +0.04\]) across five seeds. Across DL's split and five subject rotations on CHB-MIT and Siena Scalp EEG, with equally tuned linear probes (30 configurations, up to 500 epochs, validation-based early stopping), mean adaptation effects range from -1.1 to +5.7 ROC-AUC points over 18 comparisons (related settings and feature variants). Twelve of 18 support equivalence within +/-3 points under dependence-adjusted inference (8 of 12 rotation and 4 of 6 fixed-split comparisons); six are unresolved. After correcting rotation intervals for dependence, no positive effect has a 95% interval excluding zero. The +/-3 margin is an analysis threshold, not a clinically validated one, and smaller margins support fewer equivalences. Two fixed-probe gains near +21 points shrink under the same tuning, with smaller benefits unresolved. We also audit a CHB-MIT protocol shared by at least 14 papers. No tested configuration reproduces DL Table 1 as a whole. These findings concern the tested configurations; the encoder-level probe and subsequent checks were chosen post hoc.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages, 8 Figures. Code and analysis outputs: https://github.com/johnyj02/eeg-drift

</details>

### 60. [Improving the Optimisation Landscape for Particle Mixtures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26931v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26931

**作者：** Yan Lin、David T. Frazier、Paula Cordero Encinar、Chris J. Oates

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26931v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26931v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文证明「混合预测似然」在梯度优化下无法辨识混合体如何分解到各粒子，导致匹配较差的粒子缺失训练信号；提出个体风险与混合风险的凸组合正则化，使粒子责任上形成 reverse-KL 屏障，并在 mean-field Langevin 后验估计与多骨干 LLM 的 LoRA 集成推理基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「粒子责任饥饿」诊断与个体/混合风险权衡，可为 LVR 中训练多个 latent 假设或粒子并分配监督信号提供线索，是否适用于视觉证据下的潜在状态训练仍待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving the Optimisation Landscape for Particle Mixtures

**abstract/A1：** Several methods in statistics and machine learning represent a predictive distribution as an equally weighted mixture of models, or particles, trained via the mixture predictive likelihood. However, we prove that the mixture predictive likelihood is poorly suited to gradient-based optimisation, because it does not identify how the mixture is decomposed across particles, so that a poorly matched particle can be starved of training signal. As a remedy, we study a convex combination between the individual risk, i.e. the average log loss of the individual particles, and the mixture risk. At the particle level, this regularised risk imposes a reverse-KL barrier on the responsibilities of the particles, so that no particle can be starved of training signal. These phenomena are illustrated in two case studies: In the first, a predictively-oriented posterior is computed using mean-field Langevin dynamics, and the numerics are well-behaved only when our regularisation is employed. In the second, ensembles of low-rank adapters are jointly fine-tuned on large language models, across four backbones and eight multiple-choice reasoning benchmarks; direct minimisation of the mixture risk leads to responsibility starvation, whose severity decreases as the competence of the backbone increases, whereas our regularised estimator attains a lower held-out mixture risk on every backbone considered.

**comment/C1：** 13 figures, 14 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [SecretAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Privacy Protection against Vision Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26936v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26936

**作者：** Minda Zhao、Yue Zhang、Mengyu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.LG、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26936v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26936v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SecretAgent 多智能体框架，用 LLM 智能体迭代定位图像中泄露隐私的视觉证据，并配合外观审查智能体生成不可见的对抗扰动，以在保持图像质量的同时阻止 VLM 推断位置、PII、面部与医疗属性。

**相关性简评：** 其「迭代定位视觉证据并据反馈调整扰动」的闭环，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容贡献分配提供核查线索，但论文目标是隐私攻击而非推理机制，需进一步判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SecretAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Privacy Protection against Vision Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer private information from shared images, including locations, personally identifiable information (PII), and personal attributes such as age and sex, even from medical images. Adversarial perturbations can protect such information against black-box VLMs, but existing methods overlook image quality and introduce visible artifacts into the images they protect. We present SecretAgent, the first general multi-agent framework that protects image privacy against VLMs while preserving visual fidelity. LLM agents iteratively locate the visual evidence that reveals private information and adapt an invisible perturbation to the target VLM's answers and to an appearance reviewer agent, which rejects any candidate with visible artifacts. We instantiate SecretAgent with two complementary variants: SecretAgent-Gradient optimizes the perturbation through six surrogate encoders for cross-model transferability, and the gradient-free SecretAgent-Tool composes procedural noise and achieves the best perceptual fidelity on three of the five datasets. Experiments on four privacy applications (geolocation, PII, facial, and medical) across five datasets and six VLMs show that SecretAgent preserves image quality substantially better than existing methods while achieving state-of-the-art privacy protection in every application, and that its protection transfers to other VLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Run One, Get Two: Near-Free Streaming Safety Checks for Local LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26975v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26975

**作者：** Soheil Zibakhsh、Pedram Aghazadeh、Farinaz Koushanfar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26975v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26975v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ToRA：把第二个模型作为生成器解码步中的「额外一行」，共享权重与 KV cache，用双行 kernel 一次性读取权重，实现低开销的流式安全检测，在 Qwen3-0.6B 上以不到 4% 的每 token 延迟超过专用 guard 与 hidden-state probe。

**相关性简评：** 其「以极小增量算力在读隐藏状态上做逐 token 判定」的工程路径，可能为 LVR 中的低开销验证头或 STOP 决策提供借鉴，但属纯文本安全场景，迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Run One, Get Two: Near-Free Streaming Safety Checks for Local LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Local LLM assistants decode for one user at a time, so decoding is bound by memory bandwidth and leaves compute idle. Streaming safety checks today trade accuracy against cost. A dedicated guard model is accurate but nearly doubles the latency per token, while a hidden-state probe is cheap but less accurate. We present ToRA, an adapter that runs a second model as an extra row in the generator's decode step, sharing its weights and KV cache. Dual-row kernels read each weight once for both rows. On Qwen3-0.6B, ToRA matches or beats a dedicated streaming guard, with higher F1 on 12 of 17 benchmarks. It detects 11.8 points more unsafe responses than the probe at a similar cost, under 4% added latency per token.

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [ViewDreamer: A Robot World Model beyond Fixed Camera Setups](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26981v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26981

**作者：** Ye Mao、Jinhui Xiong、Egor Larionov、Shiyang Lu、Krystian Mikolajczyk、Wei Ye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26981v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26981v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ViewDreamer 机器人世界模型，将可变数量相机的视频生成拆成「单视图独立生成 + 通过共享 slot token 跨视图交互」，并用逐步增加视图数的课程训练，在 RoboView-Bench 上评估生成质量与跨视图一致性。

**相关性简评：** 共享 slot token 作为多视图信息的紧凑瓶颈，可供核查 LVR 中跨视图证据聚合与任务必要视觉细节是否被保留；其「视图数递增课程」也可能与长度泛化有关，但原论文面向机器人世界模型生成，与推理期 latent 状态的关系仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ViewDreamer: A Robot World Model beyond Fixed Camera Setups

**abstract/A1：** Video generation models are increasingly important for robot learning, yet most generate each camera view separately or tile all views in a fixed layout. Robot demonstrations, however, are captured by synchronized cameras whose number, placement, and order vary across embodiments. We present ViewDreamer, a single world model that handles variable sets of cameras by disentangling per-view generation from cross-view interaction. Each view follows the generation path of a pretrained single-view model, and views interact only through shared slot tokens. We stabilize multi-view training with a curriculum that gradually increases the number of views. Cross-View Slot Agreement uses visual foundation models to group geometrically and semantically similar regions across views into shared slots. On RoboView-Bench, which covers one to four cameras, ViewDreamer achieves the best generation quality among the models evaluated and improves cross-view consistency over independent per-view generation. These gains carry over to our world-action model (WAM) and action-conditioned world model (AC-WM). Our WAM outperforms the FastWAM-Joint baseline on LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and RoboDojo, and on a real Franka robot. Both variants improve out-of-domain generalization to various camera settings and scenes.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 13 figures, 21 tables. Project page: https://matchlab-imperial.github.io/ViewDreamer/

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [From Letter-of-Credit Requirements to Executable Specifications: Evaluating Verification Accuracy and Completeness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26988v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26988

**作者：** Ji Sub Choi、Whan Choi、Seo Yeon Gu、Suhyun Kim、Inyong Yun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26988v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26988v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究信用证单据审查，把信用证条款要求拆成原子条件与逻辑结构，由确定性验证器检索指定证据并逐条计算，从而按「要求级」而非仅最终结论评估审查的准确性与完整性。

**相关性简评：** 其「最终决策正确不代表每条要求真被检查」的诊断与逐条证据留痕，可供核查 LVR 中答案是否真正使用检索到的视觉证据、以及贡献分配与评测粒度问题，但属文本金融文档场景，需自行判断迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Letter-of-Credit Requirements to Executable Specifications: Evaluating Verification Accuracy and Completeness

**abstract/A1：** In letter-of-credit (LC) document examination, a correct final decision does not show that every requirement was examined. We therefore evaluate examination requirement by requirement and make each requirement executable. A language model converts the bill of lading (BL) and commercial invoice (IV) requirements of a credit into atomic conditions and logical structures, and a deterministic verifier retrieves the named evidence and computes each outcome, so every check can be compared with a gold encoding and traced to logged evidence. Because LC records are confidential, our on-premises 9B parser is fine-tuned only on synthetic pairs built from templates and human-reviewed public document values. On 345 real LC cases, final-decision accuracy proves misleading: a constant PASS rule has the highest raw accuracy while examining nothing. Although it never sees a real record during training, the fine-tuned parser detects 93% of the atomic requirements its ontology covers and reconstructs 86% of the covered requirements exactly. It verifies 82% of the evaluable requirements correctly, against 54% for a tool-using agent, and leads the agent on both satisfied requirements and discrepancies. It also completely examines 1.8 times as many cases as few-shot prompting of the same model, while direct prediction and the agent complete none. Executing the gold encodings with the same verifier shows that some of the remaining errors, missed discrepancies included, come from the operators and normalization, which a better parser alone cannot fix. Every rejection comes with a trace of the units and evidence behind it, which suits examiner-in-the-loop review.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 3rd Workshop on LLMs and Generative AI for Finance (AI4F) at ACM ICAIF 2026 (non-archival). 10 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Code: https://github.com/Ji-S/LC-Spec-Synth

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Reward Integrity in Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for LLM Guardrail Red-Teaming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26998

**作者：** Chengming Liu、Leon Viti、Hongxu Chen、Erdem Kemik、Mary-Anne Williams、Ruth Oliver

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26998v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究金融领域 LLM 护栏红队测试中对抗强化学习的奖励可靠性，指出攻击未包含规定 PII 或奖励裁判误判时会产生虚假成功，并比较多种决策模型与人工/裁判判断的一致性，主张引入专用裁判模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「奖励裁判虚高、虚假胜利需专门核验」的思路可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督信号可靠性，例如判断推理过程是否真正获得对应证据的信用；但属纯文本安全应用，与视觉状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reward Integrity in Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for LLM Guardrail Red-Teaming

**abstract/A1：** We study reward fidelity in an adversarial reinforcement-learning loop for red-teaming financial-services language-model guardrails. Apparent wins can arise when an attack omits prescribed personally identifiable information (PII), or when a reward judge credits a response that did not break the targeted policy. On attacker-generated and public benchmark examples, TypeSafe Jev checks whether the prescribed PII is present. For outcome scoring, we compare the decision models TypeSafe Jev, Laya (pretrained and post-trained), and pretrained CLM-8B with logged Opus 4.8 judgements from a replay corpus. Post-training markedly improves Laya's agreement with the judge. A separate author-reviewed off-target set establishes that Opus can award false wins. The findings motivate specialised decision-model judges for checking prescribed PII in attack inputs and scoring target responses against the intended policy.

**comment/C1：** Received the Best Paper Award at the Reinforcement Learning for LLM Agents Workshop at ACM International Conference for AI in Finance 2026 in Milan

</details>
