### 66. [S2D-Splat: Static-First Gaussian Reconstruction of Dynamic Driving Scenes with Layer-Wise Feature Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27003v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27003

**作者：** Jinheng Li、Tianchen Deng、Bufan Zhang、Qu Chen、Jianwu Fang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27003v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27003v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 S2D-Splat，一个前馈式高斯重建框架：先分离静态与动态特征再做相机与几何估计，静态分支逐层缓存中间特征并作为固定条件注入动态分支，另设实例级运动解码器估计物体轨迹。

**相关性简评：** 其「逐层缓存静态特征并作为固定条件复用」的非对称信息流，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节旁路与 latent 状态受动态内容干扰、信息坍缩的抑制提供可核查的结构线索；但论文本身属 3D 重建，不涉及推理状态生成或证据使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** S2D-Splat: Static-First Gaussian Reconstruction of Dynamic Driving Scenes with Layer-Wise Feature Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Moving objects introduce cross-frame inconsistencies that complicate camera estimation and static geometry recovery in driving scenes. Recent feed-forward Gaussian methods explicitly separate static and dynamic primitives, but often estimate camera parameters and geometry from shared features that jointly encode both components, leaving static reconstruction susceptible to dynamic interference. To address this challenge, we propose S2D-Splat, a feed-forward framework for static-first Gaussian reconstruction with layer-wise feature guidance. Our framework separates static and dynamic features before camera and geometry estimation, enabling the static branch to recover camera parameters and static 3D Gaussians with reduced dynamic interference. Intermediate static features are cached at each layer and reused as fixed conditioning at corresponding layers of the dynamic branch. This asymmetric information flow preserves the established static representations while providing stable geometric context for reconstructing dynamic 3D Gaussians. An instance-level motion decoder further queries the fused static and dynamic representations to estimate object trajectories, supporting motion-aligned Gaussian fusion and temporal interpolation. Experiments on Waymo and zero-shot evaluations on nuScenes and the MM-AU accident dataset demonstrate that S2D-Splat outperforms existing approaches on most evaluated metrics and generalizes to highly dynamic, pre-collision driving scenarios.

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [ReactDrive: Self-Reactive Driving World Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27024

**作者：** Yifan Wang、Xiaoqi Wang、Cho-Ying Wu、Wenbin He、Chenyu You、Liu Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27024v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向自动驾驶提出 ReactDrive 世界模型，定义「自我反应性」——仅由观测历史和给定的 ego 动作推演其他交通参与者的响应并生成相应视觉未来，方法上让动力学学习与视频生成共享同一 agent 状态表示，并以动力学监督把反应先验锚定在观测状态上。

**相关性简评：** 共享 agent 状态作为可监督的中间表示驱动后续视觉生成，与 LVR 中「推理状态承载任务必要实体关系、并需核查其对输出的实际贡献」问题结构相似，可供核查该状态是否真被生成使用；但它不涉及多模态推理与 latent 推理轨迹，迁移性有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReactDrive: Self-Reactive Driving World Model

**abstract/A1：** The world evolves through the dynamics of its constituent entities and their interactions, with changes in one entity influencing the behavior of others. To simulate this evolution under different actions, interactive world models need to capture the resulting agent responses as part of the world's own dynamics. In autonomous driving, errors in modeling these responses can misrepresent maneuver safety and undermine the reliability of generated videos used for policy evaluation and learning. To formalize this intrinsic requirement for driving world model, we define self-reactivity as the capability to model agent responses from observed history and a specified ego action, and generate visual futures that reflect those responses without prescribed future agent trajectories. To realize this capability, we introduce ReactDrive, a driving world model that jointly learns agent dynamics and visual generation through a shared agent state representation. Within this framework, dynamics supervision grounds a reactive dynamics prior in observed agent states, while the video objective jointly optimizes the self-reactivity and visual realism. Experiments show improved agent responses, ego controllability, and video quality, a 96.0% preference for agent reactivity in human comparisons across five baselines, and better downstream planning through augmentation with generated videos. We hope this work encourages the community to recognize self-reactivityas a foundational capability for developing and evaluating interactive world models. Project page: https://reactdrive.github.io/ .

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Toward a digital twin of the ICU patient: Forecasting and treatment response in a generative event model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27028v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27028

**作者：** Zhuoyang Lyu、Yiming Li、Peter C. Hou、Pengyu Hong、Li Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27028v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27028v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 训练 79M 参数的 decoder-only transformer TWICE，在单家医疗系统约 12.7 万 ICU 病人的电子病历上做生成式事件续写；仅靠从 50 条模拟未来中读出 19 项临床结局（无任务微调）即接近最强监督基线，并评估生成保真度与反事实治疗响应。

**相关性简评：** 「多采样模拟未来聚合出答案、增加采样比放大模型更划算」可供核查 LVR 中动态采样与潜状态推演的算力分配；其反事实对照中反向治疗也被判为有效，则提示 latent 因果贡献评估的失败风险。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Toward a digital twin of the ICU patient: Forecasting and treatment response in a generative event model

**abstract/A1：** A digital twin of a patient in intensive care would forecast the patient's course and what a treatment would change. Generative event models, trained to continue a patient's electronic health record (EHR), have been proposed as a path toward such twins. We trained TWICE, a 79M-parameter decoder-only transformer, on the ICU records of one academic health system (127,332 training patients and 1.57 billion tokens). We evaluated it on clinical prediction tasks, generation fidelity, individual trajectories and counterfactual validation. Reading 19 clinical outcomes off 50 simulated futures, with no task-specific training, TWICE matched the best of 144 supervised configurations on held-out patients (mean AUROC 0.836 vs. 0.834). It led on events that change care within a day and trailed on death. With the training data fixed, more and longer simulated futures were worth more than a 6.8-fold larger model. Its generated records matched real ones closely and were as specific to each patient as its own variability allowed. Its predicted risk rose as events approached. Pre-specified controls showed that it reproduced the side effects of drugs and was barely moved by an inert drug. On the values that treatments are given to correct, however, an opposite treatment moved its forecasts like the right one. TWICE forecasts ICU events about as well as task-specific models, and we see it as a step toward a digital twin of the ICU patient.

**comment/C1：** Work in progress

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Stateful Privacy Sniffing of LLM Agents via Behavioral Covert Channels in Web Interactions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27046

**作者：** Ruichao Liang、Wenzhong Deng、Jing Chen、Yuqing Li、Kaixuan Li、Cong Wu、Yang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27046v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 LLM 网页 agent 的隐私泄露：agent 良性访问顺序本身构成行为隐信道，泄露用户查询与画像；作者提出 SNOOP 状态化层次探测框架，由粗到细收缩假设树，从可观测路径重建私有上下文，并给出 SOR 防御。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关联，但其「按可观测动作序列逐层收缩假设树以反推隐状态」的探测范式，可能可供核查为从行为轨迹推断 latent 推理状态、检验内部状态是否外显的评估思路；需注意该设定为纯文本 agent、非推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Actions Speak Louder than Words: Stateful Privacy Sniffing of LLM Agents via Behavioral Covert Channels in Web Interactions

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents increasingly rely on Web interaction, issuing searches, following links, and reading pages, to complete complex user tasks. Since each action is driven by the agent's private context, including the user's prompt, dialogue history, memory, and connected data, it also opens a new privacy exposure surface. Prior attacks either passively analyze network traffic, which yields only coarse correlations, or hijack the agent through indirect prompt injection, which must place sensitive content in an outgoing field and is blocked by content-level defenses. Whether an attacker can actively recover fine-grained private context without any explicit transmission thus remains insufficiently understood. Our insight is that an agent's benign Web decisions themselves leak private context, as what it chooses to visit encodes whether, which, and in what order it acts, forming a behavioral covert channel. Building on this, we design SNOOP, a stateful hierarchical probing framework that narrows a coarse-to-fine hypothesis tree per task and reconstructs user queries and profiles from the agent's observable paths. Across four agents and three LLM backbones on 500 StackExchange queries and 378 multi-turn iKAT dialogues, SNOOP recovers query concepts with an average F1 of 0.89 and user profiles with an F1 of 0.80, while four representative agent defenses change its recovery by less than 0.03 F1. Since no sensitive content is ever transmitted, content-level inspection cannot detect this leakage; we therefore propose Selection-Obfuscated Retrieval (SOR), which acquires resources under a fixed budget independent of private context and confines the agent's selection to a local snapshot, cutting SNOOP's F1 to 0.39 without additional LLM inference.

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [A Continuous Relaxation of Quantization-Aware Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27056v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27056

**作者：** Kartikeya Bhardwaj、Mohammed Almousa、Chaitanya Mullapudi、Ananth Balasubramanian、Nithin Ramesan、Tuc Nguyen、Harris Teague

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27056v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27056v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RelaxQAT，把量化感知训练中的位宽改为随训练渐进变化的连续松弛，以缓解低位量化的平坦损失面问题，并在视觉与语言任务上报告了相对现有 QAT 方法的准确率提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「连续松弛＋位宽渐进退火」的离散—连续训练调度，可能为 LVR 中潜状态由连续表征逐步收紧到离散或低精度状态、以及避开平坦损失区的训练监督方式提供可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Continuous Relaxation of Quantization-Aware Training

**abstract/A1：** Quantization is a critical piece of the deployment pipeline in the era of large generative models. Low-bit quantization, particularly 2-bits or sub-2-bits, is a notoriously difficult problem, particularly when using a channelwise quantization scheme which has been shown as a leading candidate for hardware-efficiency. The key challenges with low-bit quantization range from limited quantization levels to saddle points or flat regions in the loss surface. In this paper, we look at the problem of flat quantization loss surfaces through a lens of dynamically changing bitwidth. Unlike existing techniques which either change the bit-precision abruptly or keep it fixed, we propose to gradually alter the bitwidth as the model trains. To this end, we propose RelaxQAT, a continuous relaxation of the QAT process. We also provide simple theoretical and empirical insights behind why RelaxQAT helps the QAT performance. Our extensive experiments demonstrate up to 6.3% average accuracy gains on vision and up to 7.7% better average accuracy on natural language tasks, compared to the leading QAT methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Structural Agreement Indicates Prediction Accuracy For Protein-Ligand Co-Folding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27058v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27058

**作者：** J. Henry Broster、Bojana Popovic、Diana Kondinskaia、Charlotte M. Deane、Fergus Imrie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27058v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27058v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文考察七种蛋白质-配体共折叠模型的置信度分数能否可靠筛选正确预测，发现基于置信度的选择几乎不优于随机，据此提出 CORE Score，用重复集成预测的结构一致性与聚类紧致度衡量可信度。

**相关性简评：** 其「多预测间一致性加集成支持」作为免训练可靠性信号的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中多样 latent 假设的一致度能否预测答案正确性、用于证据筛选与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structural Agreement Indicates Prediction Accuracy For Protein-Ligand Co-Folding

**abstract/A1：** Co-folding models predict the structures of proteins and their bound ligands, but their accuracy varies across targets. Their usefulness therefore depends on knowing which predictions a user can trust. Confidence scores are intended to guide that choice. However, across seven co-folding models, we show that confidence-based selection offers little improvement over choosing a prediction at random. For approximately three-quarters of structures, predictions are either exclusively correct or incorrect, leaving no opportunity for ranking to change the outcome. Moreover, high confidence does not reliably discriminate correct top-ranked predictions from incorrect ones. We find that structural agreement among predictions is a more accurate measure of likely success than current confidence scores. We capture this with the Concentration of Repeated Ensembles (CORE) Score, which combines cluster tightness with ensemble support. CORE Score discriminates top-1 success and oracle success, achieving mean ROC AUCs of 0.866 and 0.838, compared to 0.757 and 0.759 for ipTM. CORE Score also outperforms training set similarity measures, including ligand Tanimoto, protein sequence, and Runs N' Poses similarity, despite requiring no experimental ligand pose. Structural agreement is therefore a practical indicator of reliability and a baseline for evaluating future confidence estimators, without requiring an additional trained uncertainty estimator.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 9 figures, 20 supplementary figures, 3 supplementary tables. Code: https://github.com/oxpig/core-score

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [Dynamic Token Merging for Efficient Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27064v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27064

**作者：** Chris Gu、Marco Andono Sie、Nathan Zhou、Christopher Potts、Julie Kallini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27064v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27064v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 MrT5 的动态 token 合并（用轻量 delete gate 依据上下文隐状态打分、训练时作可微注意力偏置、推理时物理删除）推广到视觉模态与 decoder-only 模型，在多模态编码器内以及 prefill 阶段删除视觉 token 并压缩 KV cache，在十个基准上比较高删除率下的精度与效率折衷。

**相关性简评：** 学习式门控筛选「哪些视觉 token 值得保留」与 LVR 关心的视觉证据筛选、必要视觉细节是否留在内部状态相关，KV cache 压缩也可供核查 latent 状态与缓存的成本；但论文目标是推理加速，未研究推理轨迹或答案对 latent 内容的因果使用，迁移价值需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamic Token Merging for Efficient Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) represent each image with hundreds of visual tokens, which can make inference costly even when a prompt depends on only part of the image. Dynamic Token Merging (DTM), introduced in MrT5 for byte-level text encoders, learns which tokens to remove: a lightweight delete gate scores contextualized hidden states, acts as a differentiable attention bias during training, and physically removes tokens at inference. We argue that DTM is a general token deletion framework rather than a byte-level technique, and extend it to the vision modality and to decoder-only models. Applying DTM to T5Gemma 2 and Gemma 3 yields V-MrT5Gemma2, which deletes visual tokens inside a bidirectional multimodal encoder, and V-MrGemma3, which compacts the prompt and its key-value caches during decoder-only prefill. Across ten benchmarks, DTM's advantage over attention-based and random deletion grows with the deletion rate and is largest on document, chart, and scene-text reading. At 80% visual token deletion (approximately 60% fewer FLOPs), DTM raises accuracy over FastV from 51.8 to 63.0 on ChartQA and from 63.3 to 67.8 on TextVQA with V-MrT5Gemma2, as well as from 62.2 to 68.8 and from 71.2 to 75.1 with V-MrGemma3, retaining 90-96% of unpruned performance on both tasks with significant efficiency gains. These results support DTM as a general framework for efficient transformers.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [Decision Models Are General-Purpose Process Reward Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27068v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27068

**作者：** Jiajun Fan、Qinglong Zheng、Ziwen Wang、Bangji Yang、Neil He、Ruihan Guo、Chaoran Cheng、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27068v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27068v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DM-PRM，将回答 yes/no 的通用决策模型零样本转为过程奖励模型：对每一步提问并把标准化答案加入 advantage，用于语言智能体强化学习的步级信用分配，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上提升多种策略梯度方法。

**相关性简评：** 其无需步标签或 critic 的逐步信用分配可能为 latent 推理轨迹的训练监督与贡献判定提供线索，但工作面向文本智能体而非多模态推理，迁移到视觉证据使用与 latent 状态的可靠性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decision Models Are General-Purpose Process Reward Models

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) from verifiable outcomes gives a language agent one reward per episode: every step is credited alike, and nothing about the task is learned when all rollouts fail. The last mile, step-level credit, usually requires step labels, a learned critic, costly LLM judges or hand-written rules. We introduce DM-PRM, which turns a general-purpose decision model -- one answering yes/no questions with probabilities -- into a general-purpose process reward model (PRM), zero-shot: no step labels, no fine-tuning, no critic. DM-PRM asks one question per step and adds the standardised answers to the advantage. It plugs into any policy-gradient method; one model, with one question text per environment, serves household tasks, web shopping and multiple RL methods. With Qwen2.5-1.5B it lifts GRPO by over twenty points on ALFWorld and over ten on WebShop, overtakes GiGPO on ALFWorld and learns in far fewer iterations; it also improves GiGPO and GraphGPO on both benchmarks and reaches the highest success of our comparison, ahead of critic-based PPO and of state-of-the-art GiGPO, HGPO and GraphGPO. Its scores locate useful steps in failed rollouts, and trained agents drop idle actions and explore instead of circling. DM-PRM shows that step credit need not be learned: it can be asked for.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [Hindsight Forcing: Learning Better History from the Future in Autoregressive Video Diffusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27074v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27074

**作者：** Haonan An、Yihang Tao、Yu Guo、Renlong Chen、Jiasheng Zhang、Yuguang Fang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27074v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27074v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Hindsight Forcing 用于少步自回归视频扩散：前向让每个 chunk 完成多步 rollout 以更新 KV cache，反向则借助 latent straight-through bridge 将未来损失梯度只回传到每个历史 chunk 的一个选定去噪步，从而避免完整 BPTT 的内存开销，并让历史 chunk 学到有利于后续生成的内容。

**相关性简评：** 其「解耦前向生成与反向信用分配、跨 chunk 直通梯度」可能为 LVR 的长轨迹训练与历史 latent 贡献分配提供方法线索；但对象是视频扩散的视觉漂移，需核查该桥接能否迁移到推理状态与视觉证据的监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hindsight Forcing: Learning Better History from the Future in Autoregressive Video Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables real-time streaming generation, with each generated chunk serving as historical context for subsequent generation. To avoid the memory explosion of backpropagation through time, previous self-rollout methods typically truncate cross-chunk gradients or detach historical latents. As a result, earlier denoising computations receive no downstream feedback through the generated history on how to synthesize frames that facilitate subsequent generation, leading to visual drift and structural collapse as rollouts extend. To address this limitation, we propose Hindsight Forcing, which allows historical chunks to learn directly from future outcomes by decoupling forward generation from backward credit assignment via a latent straight-through bridge. In the forward pass, each chunk completes a full multi-step rollout to update the KV cache. In the backward pass, the bridge routes gradients from future losses back to a single selected denoising step per historical chunk, training both the context writer and historical denoiser on shared parameters without retaining the full trajectory graph. Remarkably, Hindsight Forcing requires no additional model parameters, architecture modifications, or separate trajectory replay. Extensive experiments on VBench demonstrate that Hindsight Forcing outperforms existing baselines in overall visual quality and dynamic motion, while ablation studies validate the benefit of supervising historical denoising. Code is available at https://github.com/haonanAN309/Hindsight-Forcing.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [Writing Weights or Training Them? Spatial Reasoning through Weight-Space Programming and Low-Rank Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27089v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27089

**作者：** Yalin Yang、Xiao Huang、Yanan Wu、Hao Yang、Ryan Zhenqi Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27089v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27089v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上比较权重空间编程与 rank-1 LoRA 对空间推理任务的影响：LoRA 目标提升大但严重干扰距离任务，权重编辑提升较小却保留了邻接能力。

**相关性简评：** 虽非多模态或 latent 状态研究，但其关于监督信号、参数高效适配与跨任务干扰的对照，可供核查 LVR 训练监督及推理技能保留问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Writing Weights or Training Them? Spatial Reasoning through Weight-Space Programming and Low-Rank Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Weight-space programming lets a language model inspect and edit another model's weights, offering an alternative to gradient-based adaptation. We evaluated both approaches using Qwen3.5-0.8B on a fixed reference-frame task (128 items), StepGame (200 items), and two untrained distance-comparison tasks. With thinking enabled, the public patch (p50c) raised mean accuracy from 0.0% to 22.1% on the reference-frame task and from 19.2% to 33.7% on StepGame across three sampling seeds. Randomly permuting its weight change removed the gain. Further closed-form edits did not improve on p50c, whereas a rank-1 LoRA adapter trained on worked solutions reached 100% and 98.8%, respectively, with 0.09% trainable parameters. Removing the worked solutions from supervision prevented the adapter from passing the learning gate. The trained adapters reached 82% accuracy on unseen StepGame sentence templates, 42% on held-out difficulty levels 6-10 after training on levels 1-5, and 33-50% on unseen heading-answer combinations. Their target gains coincided with a sharp decline on the distance tasks: accuracy fell from 94% and 92% under p50c to 0-2%, and their outputs applied the StepGame solution template to distance questions. With thinking disabled and reasoning requested in the visible answer, the base model also solved the distance tasks while remaining weak on the target tasks. The comparison reveals a trade-off between improving spatial task performance and preserving neighbouring skills: the public edit restored completion while preserving distance performance, whereas supervised adaptation produced much larger target gains together with interference across tasks.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [PLoQ: A Prompt-Specific Low-Rank Basis for 4-bit Quantization of LLM Activations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27092v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27092

**作者：** Faraz Tahmasebi、Michael Pellauer、Hyoukjun Kwon、Sitao Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.DC、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27092v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27092v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PLoQ 提出不改权重的 prompt 自适应低秩分解：量化前从激活中提取主导分量单独量化，并在该层 GEMM 内恢复，低秩基在 prefill 阶段按 prompt 估计、解码期复用；在 Llama/Qwen 1B–70B 六个模型上以约 4.2 bit 平均位宽超过混合精度量化基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「按 prompt 在线估计激活低秩子空间并在解码步间复用」的线索，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态的低秩假设、缓存/子空间复用以及量化造成的信息坍缩；但论文本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent reasoning。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PLoQ: A Prompt-Specific Low-Rank Basis for 4-bit Quantization of LLM Activations

**abstract/A1：** Low-bit activation quantization of large language models remains challenging, as a small number of dominant directions dictate the quantization range. State-of-the-art methods isolate these directions in a high-precision subspace through transformations calibrated offline and folded into the weights, which fixes the subspace for all prompts, leaving each prompt's few unique directions unprotected, and rules it out wherever no weight can absorb the transformation. We present PLoQ, a prompt-adaptive low-rank decomposition that extracts the dominant activation components before quantization, quantizes them separately, and restores them within the layer's GEMM, without modifying the weights. The low-rank component's basis is estimated from each prompt during prefill and reused throughout decoding. At the same bit width as state-of-the-art mixed-precision quantization (4-bit weights, activations and K/V cache with an eighth of the channels at 8 bits, about 4.2 bits per activation on average), PLoQ outperforms it on all six Llama and Qwen models from 1B to 70B parameters, at up to 8k tokens (16k on two of them), recovering up to 46% of the perplexity that the state of the art still loses to 4-bit activations. On Llama-3.1-8B at 8k tokens it adds only 4% to prefill and 8% to decoding.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 5 figures, 14 tables. Includes appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [PIU-CR: Physics-Informed Deep Unfolding with SAR-Optical Image Fusion for Cloud Removal](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27093v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27093

**作者：** Gengque Fan、Han Xu、Junxi Li、Hao Zhang、Guangcan Liu、Jiayi Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27093v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27093v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将 SAR-光学遥感图像去云表述为物理引导的优化问题，把大气散射模型嵌入数据保真项并设计跨模态结构与光谱正则，再将优化过程展开为多阶段网络，以获得可解释的重建迭代。

**相关性简评：** 其「深度展开」把每个网络阶段对应一次显式优化迭代，可能为 LVR 中可解释的 latent 状态更新与循环计算设计提供借鉴；但任务为遥感图像复原而非 VLM 推理，状态是否承载可被下游使用的推理内容仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PIU-CR: Physics-Informed Deep Unfolding with SAR-Optical Image Fusion for Cloud Removal

**abstract/A1：** Multi-modal cloud removal exploits complementary optical and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images to restore cloud-degraded remote sensing images. Most existing methods rely on black-box, data-driven architectures with limited interpretability of cross-modal interactions. Although some attempts introduce physical priors into networks, their oversimplified formulations fail to faithfully characterize the underlying physical imaging process and the networks remain loosely coupled with optimization variables. To address these issues, we propose a physics-informed deep unfolding network for cloud removal via optical-SAR image fusion, termed as PIU-CR. We first formulate cloud removal as a physics-informed optimization problem. Specifically, we fundamentally ground the optimization in the physical imaging mechanism of cloud degradation by embedding the atmospheric scattering model into the data fidelity term. Then, the regularization terms establish an explicit cross-modal interaction mechanism, overcoming the single-modal information scarcity and the obscure, uninterpretable interactions in black-box models. They are dedicated to SAR-guided structure and spectral regularization, jointly introducing cross-modal structural feature alignment and coordinated spectral consistency constraints. The resulting optimization problem is then unfolded into a multi-stage neural network with dedicated structure and spectral branches. Each stage corresponds to an explicit optimization iteration step, enabling interpretable and effective reconstruction process. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Fgq188/PIU\_CR.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [Recurrent Amplification and Exact Nonexpansiveness Criteria for Query-Driven Erasure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27109v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27109

**作者：** Haozhe Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27109v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27109v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究带 query 驱动擦除的秩一线性注意力状态转移：构造一段周期性 token 序列，使单步谱半径均小于 1 而周期乘积谱半径大于 1，从而说明扰动可递归放大；并给出通道级衰减下 O(d) 的单步非扩张谱范数判据，在 Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base 单个头上做反事实回放验证终端增益上升。

**相关性简评：** 其状态转移谱分析与擦除机制可供核查 latent 推理状态在多步更新中是否稳定、是否出现递归放大，以及这类动力学能否解释长序列/长度泛化下的信息保留与坍缩；但对象是线性注意力记忆更新而非多模态推理，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recurrent Amplification and Exact Nonexpansiveness Criteria for Query-Driven Erasure

**abstract/A1：** The Query-derived Erase Direction (QED) augments a rank-one linear attention update with a key-orthogonal query component. Under scalar decay, this correction leaves the eigenvalues of each homogeneous state transition unchanged, while the propagation of an initial perturbation depends on the ordered product of these transitions. We construct a periodic token sequence with fixed key and query projections for which every one-step spectral radius is below one but the spectral radius of the period product exceeds one. The same construction yields a sufficient condition for mean-square growth under independent uniform token sampling. For rank-one erasure with channel-wise decay, we derive a necessary and sufficient spectral-norm test for one-step nonexpansiveness that can be evaluated in $O(d)$ time. The test agrees with direct singular value decomposition on all 1,800 operators evaluated. In a counterfactual replay of a selected head of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, the terminal gain on a 4,096-token repeated-text sequence increased from 0.99938 to 1.3977 with the feature and checkpoint gate trajectories held fixed.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables (including supplementary material)

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [When Students Fail to Learn from Teachers: Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27125v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27125

**作者：** Xinnan Zhang、Zeyi Liao、Siliang Zeng、Zhongruo Wang、Jiaxiang Li、Xinyan Zhao、Huan Sun、Mingyi Hong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27125v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27125v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从学习动力学角度分析 on-policy 蒸馏为何失效：先刻画采样 token 更新无法纠正师生错配的状态（以学生落在教师 top-p 集合的质量衡量），再说明错配状态上梯度不仅难以学习还会干扰其他 token，最后以终止符错配为例展示持续训练如何抑制学生当前模式、将其推向教师不可靠的状态，从而解释 SFT 初始化的收益。

**相关性简评：** 其对「伪一致」、错配状态上 token 级监督失效及训练对后续采样分布的抑制效应的分析，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督信号可靠性提供可供核查的线索，但研究对象是纯文本蒸馏，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Students Fail to Learn from Teachers: Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a core technique for post-training large language models, enabling students to learn from dense token-level supervision on their own generations. However, even with a capable teacher, OPD training can be unstable, leading to excessive response length, repetitive generation, and severe performance degradation. One example is \\textit{spurious agreement}, where the student maintains high overlap with the teacher at the states visited but produces repetitive generations. Although such failures have been observed, the mechanisms behind these failures remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we investigate OPD through the lens of learning dynamics, tracing three mechanisms from local analysis to subsequent on-policy sampling. We first identify states where sampled-token OPD updates fail to correct teacher--student mismatch and characterize them using the student's mass on the teacher's top-$p$ set. We then show that learning from the teacher is difficult at mismatched states and that their gradients can also affect with learning at other tokens. Finally, using a termination-token mismatch as a case study, we study how continued training on mismatched states influences on-policy sampling, suppressing the student's current modes and leading the student toward states where the teacher is unreliable . These findings help explain the benefits of supervised fine-tuning (SFT) initialization and motivate targeted correction of states where student and teacher are mismatched.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [Distillation with Errors: Sparse Unbiased Backpropagation for Knowledge Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27131v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27131

**作者：** Yuyang Zhang、Yuxin Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27131v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27131v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Distillation with Errors（DWE）：利用 logit 梯度的零和结构构造稀疏成对梯度估计，并配合几何感知配对与分层采样降低方差，使知识蒸馏无需全词表输出头反传，同时保持无偏性。

**相关性简评：** 论文的分层采样与低方差无偏梯度估计或可供核查其能否用于 latent 推理轨迹蒸馏中仅对部分位置或视觉证据做监督时的贡献分配与训练成本控制，但原文未涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Distillation with Errors: Sparse Unbiased Backpropagation for Knowledge Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge distillation (KD) trains a student to match a teacher's output distribution. However, full-vocabulary KD requires costly output-head backpropagation. To reduce this cost, we introduce Distillation with Errors (DWE). Our key insight is that minibatch training already approximates the training gradient, so per-position backpropagation does not need to be exact. DWE therefore exploits the zero-sum structure of logit gradients to construct sparse paired gradient estimates whose expectation matches the full-vocabulary gradient. It then uses geometry-aware pairing and stratified sampling to further reduce estimation variance. We prove that the estimator is unbiased and evaluate DWE's gradient estimation accuracy and training efficiency. At the same sampling budget, DWE reduces hidden-state and output-weight gradient MSE by 63.5% and 57.9%, respectively, compared with importance sampling. In off-policy KD, it achieves up to a $1.95\\times$ output-head speedup and reduces training time by 10.4-15.7% relative to full-vocabulary KD. In on-policy distillation (OPD), it achieves up to a $2.20\\times$ output-head speedup. DWE maintains competitive downstream performance in both settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/dwe-lab/DWE.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [Crafting the Difference, Grounding the Evidence: Discriminative Concept Learning for Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27135v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27135

**作者：** Siyu Zhu、Feng Ding、Zixiao Jin、Kaizhi Li、Xin Dong、Haisheng Fu、Yuchen Zou、Guohe Zhang、Jie Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27135v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27135v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对零样本骨架动作识别中 LLM 描述重复混淆动作共同线索、且与骨架特征缺乏显式对应的问题，提出 CRAFT：用难负例条件下的生成与对比打分筛出区分性概念，并以可学习概念 token 从骨架中读取运动与上下文线索，在多个基线与划分上提升 ZSL 与 GZSL 指标。

**相关性简评：** 其「概念 token 由特征接地、并用难负例对比筛选区分性概念」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态如何绑定具体视觉证据、以及如何剔除混淆性冗余证据；但任务为骨架模态的动作分类而非多模态推理，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Crafting the Difference, Grounding the Evidence: Discriminative Concept Learning for Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition

**abstract/A1：** LLM-generated descriptions enrich zero-shot skeleton-based action recognition but repeat cues shared by confusable actions. Their contextual semantics also lack explicit links to skeleton features. To address these challenges, we propose CRAFT, which couples Concept Refinement and Feature-grounded Tokens. Hard-negative-conditioned generation and contrastive scoring identify concepts that distinguish each action from its closest rivals. Unique-QDPP selects complementary descriptions within each semantic slot and prevents their reuse across slots. This bank supervises learnable concept tokens that read motion and context cues from each skeleton. On fixed NTU-60 and NTU-120 splits, CRAFT improves five baselines spanning four recognition paradigms. ZSL accuracy and GZSL harmonic mean increase on every baseline-split pair, by 5.4 and 5.9 percentage points on average. Random-split experiments extend these gains to three datasets, including PKU-MMD. Code will be released after acceptance.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [Double counting in LLM evidence fusion: exact posteriors separate rule compliance from posterior gain](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27138

**作者：** Tengfei Shao、Asma Rezaei、Ali Reza Kamali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27138v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构造「分级依赖」的精确贝叶斯参照（二元世界、单报告可靠度 0.65、四个依赖水平），并用配对增益协议在 2,976 个世界上比较四种 LLM 配置的原始回答与「每个来源只计一次」指令回答，从而分离规则遵守与后验增益。

**相关性简评：** 其「表面遵守规则不等于真实证据增益」的对照设计和以精确后验为参照的 Brier 配对增益，可能可供核查 latent 内容是否真正参与答案、以及多源视觉证据的依赖加权与贡献分配，但为纯文本设定，迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Double counting in LLM evidence fusion: exact posteriors separate rule compliance from posterior gain

**abstract/A1：** Language models fuse reports in retrieval, multi-agent consensus and agent memory, where treating shared-source reports as independent can double count evidence. A common remedy is an instruction to count each source once. Its value depends on the receiver's raw baseline. Pricing it needs an exact reference. We build the graded-dependence exact reference: binary worlds with single-report reliability 0.65 and four dependence levels, whose disclosed parameters give every world an exact posterior and every rule a target risk. The paired gain protocol scores raw and instructed answers of four configurations on 2,976 worlds. Agreement and gain separate in both directions. At $δ$ = 0, claude-opus-5-5 and gpt-6.1-sol agreed with the rule within 0.001 in 744 of 744, and the instruction raised their posterior mismatch by 0.044; gpt-6-luna agreed in 165 of 744 and gained 0.0140 (95% interval 0.0064 to 0.0219); claude-haiku-5-5's gain moved from -0.0295 ($δ$ = 0) to +0.0302 ($δ$ = 1) (descriptive). The prespecified first-child endpoint contrast, gain at $δ$ = 1 minus $δ$ = 0, was 0.0342 (0.0303 to 0.0382). In the preregistered tests, the raw source-weight contrast was positive (0.259, 95% interval 0.182 to 0.337; Holm rejected zero) and the grouping endpoint difference was not detected (0.0004, -0.0023 to 0.0031). This interval excludes differences larger than 0.0031 in magnitude and lies within the registered precision bound of $\\pm$0.0046. The reference prices the rule before any model call. The paired gain, estimable as a Brier difference on labelled deployment items, decides adoption per receiver and prompt condition.

**comment/C1：** 57 pages (30 main text, 27 supplementary material), 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27148v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27148

**作者：** Allan Kazakov、Abdurrahman Javat

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27148v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27148v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 GRPO 冷启动协议下比较 LoRA、DoRA 与 QuanTA 在 RLVR 中的适配表现，发现 LoRA 随秩增大会出现「秩坍缩」，并用更新谱分析说明结构化适配器产生更分散的更新方向。

**相关性简评：** 其「有效秩／更新谱能量分布」这一诊断工具或可供核查 latent 推理状态是否坍缩到少数方向，但研究对象是纯文本 RLVR 适配，与视觉证据和推理期状态生成的关联需较多推断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is often framed as a choice between the expressivity of Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) and the efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We find that this framing misses an important variable: the geometry of the update. Under a common cold-start GRPO protocol, LoRA exhibits rank collapse: performance remains broadly stable at low rank, then fails abruptly as rank increases. On DeepMath-Hard, for example, accuracy drops from 73.1% at r=64 to 4.7% at r=128. In contrast, Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) and Quantum-Informed Tensor Adaptation (QuanTA) remain stable over a wider tested optimization region and are competitive with FFT while updating less than 0.6% of the model parameters. Spectral analysis reveals a corresponding difference in learned update structure: collapsed LoRA concentrates its update energy into relatively few effective directions, whereas structured adapters produce substantially more distributed updates. These results suggest that nominal rank and parameter count alone are insufficient to characterize adaptation in RLVR - the structure imposed on the reachable update manifold also matters. Finally, across three 8B base models, adapter behavior aligns with both frozen-weight spectral structure and reported pre-training scale, motivating the Model Maturity Hypothesis: the adaptation geometry that works best may depend on properties already encoded during pre-training. We present this as a falsifiable hypothesis rather than a causal conclusion. The code can be found here https://github.com/Shrimpstanot/structure-over-scale

**comment/C1：** Published in TMLR (10/2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [Where Structure Enters a World Model: From Predictive Fit to Physical Rollout](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27149v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27149

**作者：** Panayiotis Christou、Satyapragnya Kar、Flavjo Xhelollari、Parijat Dube、Rajarshi Das

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27149v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27149v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在行星运动 token 模型与 JEPA 式视觉模型上，比较守恒量监督、对称性表示、时间一致性约束和学习式结构化动力学对多步 rollout 的影响，发现辅助物理读出或局部加速度估计的改善并不自动转化为可靠的长程物理一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其「结构须同时被表示并用于生成未来状态」的诊断，与 LVR 中 latent 是否真正被用于答案生成、辅助读出导致的虚假改进等问题结构相似；JEPA 式隐空间 rollout 与结构化动力学头可供核查长程状态更新与信息坍缩的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Structure Enters a World Model: From Predictive Fit to Physical Rollout

**abstract/A1：** Predictive world models learn to forecast observations, but accurate short-term predictions do not necessarily produce physically coherent behavior over longer horizons. We investigate how incorporating physical structure can strengthen world models while retaining their underlying predictive objectives. Across token-based models of planetary motion and JEPA-style visual models of mechanical systems, we study conserved-quantity supervision, symmetry-aware representations, temporal consistency, and learned structured dynamics. In planetary motion, angular-momentum supervision improves autonomous next-token forecasting, while the benefits of temporal regularization depend on the accompanying physical objective and representation. In visual models, generating rollouts through supervised energy-based dynamics heads produces more faithful trajectories and phase-space evolution. Together, these findings highlight the importance of how physical structure participates in prediction: improvements in an auxiliary physical readout or a local acceleration estimate do not automatically translate into more reliable long-horizon behavior. Evaluations across initial conditions and physical parameter shifts further reveal where these benefits persist and where generalization remains limited. Our study supports physical structure as a practical means of improving predictive world models and provides a unified framework for understanding its effects on representations, learned transitions, and autonomous rollouts. The central lesson is that effective physical inductive biases should help models both represent physical regularities and use them to generate future states.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 8 figures, 46 tables (including appendices)

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [The Vulnerability Window: Decision Instability in Quantized Reward Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27155v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27155

**作者：** Omkar Shewale、Deepak Kumar、Divakar Kumar Yadav

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27155v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27155v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文考察 4-bit 量化奖励模型与生成式 judge 在 RewardBench 上的决策稳定性：尽管总体准确率几乎不变，1.1–12.3% 的成对偏好判断被改写，且翻转集中在全精度 margin 相对量化噪声很小的「脆弱窗口」内，并进一步影响 best-of-N 选择结果。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态 latent 推理，但其用 margin 与噪声尺度之比预测判断翻转的做法，可供核查作为 LVR 中量化 verifier、路由或停止信号的可靠性诊断；不过原文显示路由效果与普通 top-k 相当，迁移价值需谨慎评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Vulnerability Window: Decision Instability in Quantized Reward Models

**abstract/A1：** Reward models and LLM judges are routinely quantized to 4 bits so that scoring many candidates stays affordable, and the usual check is that benchmark accuracy barely moves. That check is close to blind. Across eight reward models from two families (0.6B-8B), three generative judges and four 4-bit quantizers, quantization rewrites 1.1-12.3% of pairwise preference decisions on RewardBench while aggregate accuracy moves by as little as 0.03 points (p = 1.00), because the errors it fixes and introduces cancel. Flips concentrate where the full-precision margin is small relative to the quantization noise scale sigma: a Gaussian approximation Phi(-\|m\|/sigma) describes the aggregate rate, over-predicting it in all 30 scalar settings and failing for generative judges, and occupancy of the resulting vulnerability window falls from 25% to 7% with model size, which is what makes small judges fragile. The churn stays within noise under benign best-of-16 and becomes clear under adversarial pressure for some quantizers (146:54 harmful:helpful flips on one family, reversing sign on another). Downstream it changes what ships: across six (model, quantizer) pairs from two families, 14.6-65.7% of problems get a different best-of-64 selection while only 2.6-9.6% get a different final answer, with selections changing 5-8x more often than answers in every pair, and the pair the benchmark calls identical (0.03 points, p = 1.00) still changes 34.5% of them. Benchmark churn ranks that downstream change (rho = 0.89); benchmark accuracy does not order it reliably. Held-out audits then locate the useful diagnostic in the margin rather than the Gaussian: every margin-based predictor orders flips identically (AUC 0.927), and on the level a directly measured churn rate wins in-distribution. As a routing signal it is matched by plain top-k. The contribution is the characterization, not the model.

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [From Acting to Learning: Experience Selection toward Continually Learning Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27159v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27159

**作者：** Yaoyuan Yan、Haoxiang Jie、Zhiyou Heng、Kailin Wang、Yumiao Li、Lifeng Liu、Anwan Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27159v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27159v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究持续学习智能体的「经验选择」：在固定经验池、多随机种子下比较九种选择器训练 4B 语言智能体，发现考虑优化器的梯度选择优于全局随机与高 NLL 选择，并强调排序规则须与选择空间共同考虑。

**相关性简评：** 同属「用哪些交互记录来训练」的问题，其梯度式贡献分配线索可能可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练与监督贡献分配，但材料未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Acting to Learning: Experience Selection toward Continually Learning Agents

**abstract/A1：** Successful agent interactions provide training data, but success alone does not determine which records a student should learn from. We examine experience selection for a 4B language agent as a component of continual agent learning. Nine selectors are compared across two fixed experience pools, eight initialization seeds, and two sampling seeds, yielding 288 trained students. Each student is evaluated on 128 development task groups with a common executor. With equal global selection freedom, 256 distinct contents, and 768 training presentations, optimizer-aware gradient selection reaches 81.45\\% and 73.19\\% success in the two pools. Its gains over global random selection are 8.25 and 5.96 percentage points; gains over global high-NLL selection are 4.10 and 4.05 points. All eight initialization-level contrasts are positive against both baselines in each pool. Relaxing coverage constraints improves gradient selection but reduces random-selection performance, showing that the ranking rule and selection space must be considered together. A separately frozen replication on 128 reserved task groups is complete; its equal-source gradient-minus-random and gradient-minus-high-NLL differences are +7.03 and +5.03 points, respectively.

</details>

### 87. [PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27164v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27164

**作者：** Xuancheng Xu、Xiaofeng Wang、Yue Ma、Zheng Zhu、Bing-Kun Bao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27164v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27164v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PhysForce 两阶段框架，用监督微调把力的大小、方向、位置经 Latent Force Intervention Encoder 映射进视频 latent 空间，并用 Physics Attribution Network 将力干预与 latent 状态的局部变化关联，再用自监督奖励强化物理一致性与泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「不暴露未来运动地将干预注入 latent」与「把状态变化归因定位到具体干预」这两点，可能为 LVR 中的 latent 状态注入、贡献分配与答案是否真正使用 latent 内容提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model

**abstract/A1：** Interactive video world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve in response to user interventions, including physically consistent state changes induced by applied forces. Existing methods often represent forces as motion trajectories, which may introduce inappropriate motion priors, causing the model to over-rely on encoded displacement patterns. More fundamentally, the model's internal representations do not reliably localize force-induced state changes, making it difficult to associate applied forces with their resulting physical responses. Also, limited force-annotated data make it difficult to produce physically consistent and temporally stable predictions in unseen scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose PhysForce, a two-stage framework that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning through three complementary components. During supervised fine-tuning, (1) the Latent Force Intervention Encoder maps force location, direction, and magnitude into the video latent space without exposing future motion; and (2) the Physics Attribution Network associates force interventions with localized changes in the latent state, strengthening the learning of physical responses to applied forces. During reinforcement learning, (3) Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning uses self-supervised rewards on out-of-distribution videos to improve physical consistency and generalization. Extensive experiments show that PhysForce improves force responsiveness, physical consistency, and generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness for interactive video world models.

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27187v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27187

**作者：** Xiao Lin、Yaoxu Lyu、Ronghao Dang、Zhili Liu、Kaijing Ma、Yang Zhou、Dingning Liu、Runzhe Teng、Jun Cen、Shanghang Zhang、Chengju Liu、Qijun Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27187v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27187v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 VLA 动作专家的预训练方式：先构建诊断模型说明单模态（VA 或 LA）预训练会使视觉-语言联合建模通路失活且不可恢复，进而提出 SCAP，在预训练阶段随机掩码视觉与文本 token，以保留联合通路、降低对视觉捷径的依赖并提升指令依从性，在多种 VLA 架构和真机操作任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「单模态预训练使联合视觉-语言通路永久失活、随机掩码可保留该通路并削弱视觉捷径」的机制，可能为 VLM 推理中视觉旁路与信息坍缩的成因及训练期缓解提供可核查线索；但论文面向机器人动作专家，不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining

**abstract/A1：** Single-modality action-expert pretraining controls competition from visual shortcuts before vision--language--action (VLA) adaptation. Yet single-modality pretraining can impair the vision--language interactions needed for subsequent joint prediction. We construct a diagnostic model in which VA or LA pretraining can disable an initially active pathway for joint vision--language modeling. In this model, subsequent training with complete visual and language inputs cannot restore the pathway, even with unlimited adaptation. We therefore introduce Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining (SCAP), a new action-expert pretraining stage that precedes full-input training. During this stage, visual and text tokens are randomly masked before entering the VLM. In this unified diagnostic model, SCAP can preserve the joint vision--language modeling pathway during pretraining and subsequent full-input training. Compared with direct full-input training, it can also reduce reliance on visual shortcuts and increase reliance on language instructions. SCAP consistently improves performance across four different VLA architectures. In real-robot experiments, it achieves 76.67% average success across three instruction-conditioned manipulation tasks, outperforming full-input pretraining by 25 percentage points.

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [cmHC: Balancing Stream Mixing in Hyper-Connections via Geometric Contraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27193v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27193

**作者：** Haowei He、Ziwen Zhao、Chenshuo Pan、Gang Lin、Zhihao Yang、Chao Wang、Xinzhang Liu、Kaidong Yu、Han Gao、Dongfang Li、Menglin Yang、Shuangyong Song、Yongxiang Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27193v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27193v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 manifold-constrained hyper-connections 中多残差流混合的「欠混合趋近多面体顶点、过混合趋近均匀重心」权衡，提出将 Birkhoff 多面体向内部几何收缩的 cmHC，给出累积混合、流差异与梯度差异的界，并在稠密与 MoE 大模型预训练及 Ascend NPU 融合算子上验证。

**相关性简评：** 多流残差矩阵的双随机约束、混合程度上界与浅层适度收缩，可能为 latent 推理中多路状态的混合与更新、以及流之间可区分性（信息坍缩）提供可供核查的方法线索；但原文面向预训练稳定性，不涉及视觉推理证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** cmHC: Balancing Stream Mixing in Hyper-Connections via Geometric Contraction

**abstract/A1：** Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) extends residual connections to multiple streams and stabilizes residual propagation by constraining stream-mixing residual matrices to the Birkhoff polytope, the set of doubly stochastic matrices. In this work, we investigate how to effectively mix residual streams within the Birkhoff polytope. We reveal a mixing trade-off: when under-mixed residual matrices stay close to the vertices of the Birkhoff polytope, hyper-connections will degenerate into a set of nearly independent streams; when over-mixed residual matrices are concentrated around the uniform barycenter of the polytope, streams will become less distinguishable from each other. We theoretically validate our findings by bounding the cumulative residual mixing, stream difference and gradient difference of mixing coefficients across layers. Motivated by these findings, we propose Contraction for Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (cmHC), an exact doubly stochastic method which geometrically contracts the Birkhoff polytope toward its interior. This contraction mitigates under-mixing by forcing the residual matrices to explore deeper into the polytope, with a provable stream mixing bound and no new parameterization. Moreover, we apply a moderate contraction at the shallow layers to prevent over-mixing, enabling stable gradient propagation on extremely large-scale model pretraining. Extensive experiments across dense and mixture-of-experts models at various parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of cmHC. We also develop fused operators for cmHC on Ascend NPUs, reducing hyper-connection execution latency by up to 44.8%.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [Improved Accuracy Scaling with Problem Complexity in LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Using Executable Symbolic Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27203v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27203

**作者：** Yu-Bin Liu、Shi-Ju Ran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27203v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27203v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 GSM-AB 基准，独立变化操作数位数与运算步数，比较可执行符号表示（Lean、Python-PoT）与自然语言推理下 LLM 算术准确率随复杂度的衰减情况。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理表示形式如何影响准确率随复杂度的标度」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 表示相对自然语言中间步骤的可靠性线索，但工作为纯文本算术、无可执行符号之外的内部状态分析，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improved Accuracy Scaling with Problem Complexity in LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Using Executable Symbolic Representations

**abstract/A1：** High accuracy on mathematical benchmarks does not establish whether reasoning remains reliable as problem complexity increases. We examine how executable symbolic representations change accuracy scaling in large language models compared with natural-language reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce the GSM Arithmetic Benchmark (GSM-AB), a controlled benchmark that independently varies operand digit length and operation count to probe numerical scale and compositional complexity. We find that executable representations can improve accuracy and slow its decline as complexity increases. Lean reduces average sensitivity to operand digit length at every tested model size. By contrast, slower degradation with increasing operation count emerges at the larger model sizes. A Python program-of-thought (Python-PoT) control shows a similar operation-axis pattern, suggesting that this finding is not specific to Lean. These findings show that reasoning representation shapes arithmetic accuracy scaling and support executable symbolic representations for more reliable reasoning on complex arithmetic tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [CreditFilter: Calibrated Token Weights over the Within-Response Entropy Rank for RLVR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27227v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27227

**作者：** Hanqing Zhao、Liuyue Xie、Rong-Cheng Tu、Xu Zhang、Davide Modolo、Dacheng Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27227v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27227v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 RLVR 中把单个 token 对结果的贡献形式化为「forking value」（下一 token 选择上成功概率的方差），并据此提出 CreditFilter：按响应内熵排名设置指数窗口对 token 加权，窗口速率对齐观测到的 fork 概率衰减，在 MATH500、AIME 上超过 DAPO。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本训练的 token 级贡献分配方法，其「按响应内熵排名重分配信用」的思路或可迁移到 latent 推理步骤或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 一类决策位置的监督权重，但 fork 价值近似在存在视觉旁路或多步 latent 状态时是否成立尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CreditFilter: Calibrated Token Weights over the Within-Response Entropy Rank for RLVR

**abstract/A1：** Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically assigns a uniform advantage to every token. Token-reweighting heuristics like the 80/20 mask favor high-entropy tokens, but fail to measure a token's actual impact on the outcome. We formalize this impact as the forking value: the variance of the success probability over the policy's next-token choices. The expected forking values along a response sum exactly to the reward variance. Token credit is thus, in expectation over a prompt's responses, a fixed budget spent at the forks. This motivates weights that redistribute credit within a response. The forking value factorizes into a fork consequence, which requires costly continuations to estimate, and a fork probability, which the softmax provides. On Qwen3-8B-Base, this fork probability decays exponentially with a token's within-response entropy rank at class-specific rates, revealing that most tokens carry negligible forking value. CreditFilter weights each token by an exponential window over the same rank. The window is calibrated to this fork-probability envelope, under an approximately flat fork consequence. CreditFilter needs no extra forward passes and is stabilized by difficulty damping and an entropy-gated fallback. Averaged over three seeds, it outperforms DAPO by 7.7 points on MATH500 and by 10.5 and 9.1 points on AIME24 and AIME25. Its dynamically adaptive variant matches it. The ablations show that both parts matter: ranking tokens within each response rather than across the batch, and matching the window's decay rate to the envelope. Code is available at https://github.com/yoctta/CreditFilter.

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [Prune to Expand: Training Prompt Enhancers with Less to Imitate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27229v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27229

**作者：** Tianhe Wu、Qilin Sun、Kede Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27229v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27229v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究 prompt enhancement 的监督数据构造，发现用密集 caption 派生的训练对，其图像生成增益大小与最终 PE 模型效果并不对应，据此提出纯文本方法 P2E，在每例删除上限内筛除与用户意图弱相关的冗余句子来精炼监督。

**相关性简评：** 其「冗余监督稀释有效信号、逐例裁剪监督句」的做法可能可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练中哪些视觉细节监督真正贡献学习信号；但论文任务为图像生成提示改写，与视觉推理状态无直接联系，迁移性待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prune to Expand: Training Prompt Enhancers with Less to Imitate

**abstract/A1：** Prompt enhancement (PE) refines concise user prompts into detailed descriptions to improve the compositional aesthetics of generated images while preserving user intent. A common practice constructs supervised fine-tuning (SFT) pairs by deriving short, user-like prompts from dense captions, implicitly assuming that captions that improve image generation also provide effective supervision. In this paper, however, we uncover a counterintuitive finding: PE models trained on caption pairs with substantial generation gains perform comparably to those trained on pairs with only marginal gains. We argue that dense captions can impose redundant supervision by requiring PE models to reproduce image-specific details weakly related to user intent. Motivated by this discrepancy, we introduce Prune to Expand (P2E), a simple text-only method that refines dense-caption supervision by selectively removing redundant sentences under a per-example deletion bound while preserving explicit user requirements and informative visual structure. Extensive experiments under identical training configurations demonstrate that P2E improves alignment with human preferences and visual quality over conventional dense-caption SFT across diverse image categories and visual generators.

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [Beyond Prompt Imitation: Capability-Aware Video Generation with Smaller Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27252v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27252

**作者：** Ziqi Ding、Yuxin Cao、Kai Ye、Yaobo Yang、Wei Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27252v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「oracle control ladder」，向小型视频生成器逐级提供目标片段的信息（dense caption、首帧、depth 视频等），用各保真度指标的闭合程度给出经验上限参照；并用 item response model 预测某一级信息能闭合哪个片段的哪项指标，作为预算受限选择策略的先验。

**相关性简评：** 按样本预测「追加某类信息/证据的收益」并在成本约束下选择信息层级，可能可供核查 LVR 中 LOOK 的证据筛选、动态获取与停止决策的收益-成本建模；但研究对象是视频生成保真度而非 latent 推理状态，联系需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Prompt Imitation: Capability-Aware Video Generation with Smaller Models

**abstract/A1：** Inference-time methods that lift a small open video generator toward a proprietary one are compared through preference scores, which never say how much of the gap to a target video they close. We introduce the oracle control ladder, which hands a fixed student progressively richer information from a target clip and reads the closure of each fidelity metric at each rung as an empirical headroom reference for the methods that must synthesize that information. On 954 clips of the principal student, a dense caption reproduces little of the appearance and almost none of the motion of a target, a first frame closes half of the appearance gap and a tenth of the motion gap, a depth video closes three quarters of the motion gap and leaves color untouched, and the two together close 64 to 94% of every fidelity gap across four teachers, while a second student agrees on the rungs that it accepts. An item response model forecasts which rung closes which clip with held-out AUC of 0.76 to 0.95 on the fidelity criteria and serves as the prior of a budgeted selection policy. When controls are free, a fixed dense-control rung is hard to improve upon, and once controls are priced the policy lies above the fixed-rung frontier at matched cost and survives critics that ruin critic-driven search.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Budgeted Safety Verification for Streaming Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27268v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27268

**作者：** Yuxin Cao、Ziqi Ding、Jiayu Zhang、Xianglin Yang、Wei Song、Jin Song Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27268v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27268v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究流式视频生成的安全审核如何在固定预算下分配：利用生成器已算好的 hidden states 得到廉价分数，据其决定何时调用昂贵的 VLM 检测器，并以观众实际看到的违规帧数（exposure）作为评价指标。

**相关性简评：** 用内部 hidden states 作廉价门控来触发昂贵验证器的「何时看」策略，以及其发现按整段而非单帧判断才有效（证据粒度影响结论），可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 的 LOOK/STOP 触发与视觉证据筛选；但论文目标是安全过滤而非推理，联系属间接借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Budgeted Safety Verification for Streaming Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** A streamed video generator releases each chunk the moment that it is produced, so a safety decision that waits for the finished clip arrives after the harm has been seen. We measure a guardrail by exposure, the harmful frames a viewer is shown, and ask where a fixed moderation budget should be spent. The accurate detector reads decoded frames with a video language model and is too expensive for every chunk, so our guard reads hidden states the generator has computed already and spends a cheap score on deciding when to call it. On 599 streams from a distilled causal generator the undefended model shows 46.25 harmful frames of 81. The video model on every chunk leaves 0.91 frames at 80.1 scored per benign stream, and the same model triggered by our guard leaves 2.35 at 5.3. Spending that budget at random leaves 37.20 frames, so the gain is in the ordering, and a frame detector in the same role gives up almost six, so the judging stage must read the chunk as a clip. The ordering is worth more as the stream lengthens, and the guard carries to a second system. An independent guardrail model agrees with our oracle at $κ=0.74$, and a blinded audit by 50 participants puts its precision at 96.1% and recall at 82.8%. Warning. This paper contains examples of generated content that some readers may find disturbing or offensive in nature.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 3 figures

</details>

### 95. [MODULE: A Mutual-Promoting Deep Unfolding Framework Towards Degradation-Robust Multi-modal Image Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27269v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27269

**作者：** Yunfei Deng、Han Xu、Jiayi Ma、Guangcan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27269v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27269v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出互促式深度展开框架 MODULE，把退化鲁棒的多模态图像融合重构为联合优化问题，分解为与任务相关的迭代子问题并展开为多阶段网络，使复原与融合之间形成双向信息流。

**相关性简评：** 其「先净化模态特征、再更新融合结果」的交替展开结构、且每阶段对应明确数学操作，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/THINK 交替式 latent 状态更新与可解释循环计算提供架构线索，可供核查双向流是否真正传递有效状态而非仅作正则。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MODULE: A Mutual-Promoting Deep Unfolding Framework Towards Degradation-Robust Multi-modal Image Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Multi-modal image fusion is crucial for comprehensive scene representation, yet real-world degradations compromise its efficacy, necessitating degradation-robust fusion paradigms. Existing degradation-robust multi-modal image fusion methods are often hindered by cascaded sub-optimal solution or black-box opacity. To address these challenges, we propose a theory-inspired mutual-promoting deep unfolding framework. It reformulates degradation-robust image fusion as a joint optimization problem, utilizing the degradation model and modality generation mechanism to explicitly model both single-task priors and cross-task dependencies. By decomposing the complex multi-task optimization problem into task-related iterative subproblems, the framework establishes a bidirectional reciprocal information flow between restoration and fusion. This process is further unfolded into a multi-stage deep neural network, where each component explicitly corresponds to a specific mathematical operation. In our framework, the fused cross-modal prior actively regularizes the ill-posed restoration, while the purified modality features continuously refine the fusion output. It ensures a transparent architecture that combines the merits of both model-based and data-driven methodologies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing superior interpretability. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Meskend/MODULE.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 96. [DermKG: A Concept-Centric Knowledge Graph for Interpretable Skin Disease Diagnosis from Dermatological Morphology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27283v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27283

**作者：** Cheng Huang、Yanmei Li、Mao Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27283v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27283v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在皮肤科形态学概念上构建概念中心知识图谱 DermKG（含疾病概念画像、共现、三层诊断层级与形态学规则），并提出原型打分并沿层级聚合的 DermKG-Net，用信息上限分析量化仅凭概念可达到的诊断精度上限。

**相关性简评：** 其「概念向量坍缩、概念-only 精度上界」的信息天花板分析可供核查 latent 推理状态是否因信息压缩而封顶，概念级推理链与规则约束也可能为视觉证据筛选与答案验证提供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DermKG: A Concept-Centric Knowledge Graph for Interpretable Skin Disease Diagnosis from Dermatological Morphology

**abstract/A1：** Dermatologists describe lesions with a standardized morphological lexicon, which makes concept-based models attractive for interpretable skin disease diagnosis. How much diagnostic information these concepts actually carry, however, is rarely quantified. We present DermKG, a concept-centric knowledge graph built on the expert-annotated SkinCon subset of Fitzpatrick17k (3,230 images, 48 concepts, 113 diseases). DermKG has 3,452 nodes of 8 types and 19,365 edges of 11 relation types, and encodes disease concept profiles, concept co-occurrence, a three-level diagnostic hierarchy, and 20 clinical morphology rules, all of which are supported by the training data (15 strong, 5 moderate). An information-ceiling analysis shows that the 3,230 lesions collapse onto only 813 distinct concept vectors, bounding concept-only top-1 accuracy at 38.9\\% for diseases and 77.6\\% for the nine categories. Within this bound, DermKG-Net, a graph neural network that scores lesions against disease prototypes and aggregates them along the hierarchy, raises category-level balanced accuracy from 0.257 to 0.411 over the strongest of 11 baselines (paired bootstrap difference $+0.150$, 95\\% CI $\[0.073, 0.231\]$) and more than doubles minority-category recall. Ablations show that most of this gain comes from the hierarchical prototype objective, with a smaller contribution from graph structure. DermKG further yields rule-based reasoning chains and concept-level explanations for individual predictions.

</details>
