### 97. [Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27286v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27286

**作者：** Jingyi Chai、Yun Liao、Hao Lu、Kemeng Zhang、Bingyang Zheng、Yujie Ge、Xiangrui Liu、Linfeng Zhang、Siheng Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27286v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27286v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向事件预测智能体，提出 Proactive Belief-State Management：用 fold 工具将冗长上下文压缩为紧凑信念状态并重置上下文；同时在训练侧设计五维过程评分 rubric，以 PPO/GRPO 对 35B 模型后训练，并给出 fold 式停止收敛与局部奖励降低梯度方差的分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「压缩为紧凑信念状态、重置上下文后继续探索」与基于 fold 的停止决策，可能为 LVR 的状态压缩、证据筛选与 STOP 机制提供可核查的线索；rubric 过程奖励也涉及轨迹级贡献分配，但文中无视觉通道，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Current large language model (LLM) agents still struggle to forecast real-world events. This stems from two primary limitations: i) standard ReAct harness accumulates reasoning and evidence within one growing context, and ii) outcome-based rewards in forecasting lead to a slow and noisy signal that cannot distinguish sound reasoning from lucky guesses. To address these, we introduce a forecasting training framework including harness design and post-training. For the harness side, we propose Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM), equipping agents with a fold tool to compress lengthy context into compact belief states and reset the context for subsequent exploration. For the training side, we develop SEIPH, a five-dimensional, outcome-blind rubric, providing process-quality rewards. Using the rubric-based reward, we post-train a 35B model with PPO and GRPO, yielding DeepForecaster-PPO and DeepForecaster-GRPO. Theoretically, we prove that idealized fold-based stopping converges and calibrated local rewards reduce segment-level gradient variance. Empirically, DeepForecaster-PPO (35B) achieves the best Brier score on both ForecastBench and Polymarket-Sim, outperforming frontier agents based on DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T), Kimi-K2.6 (1T), and GPT-5.5. DeepForecaster-GRPO ranks second on both FutureX metrics. Ablations reveal that PBM also improves the untrained base model without any training. Calibration analysis shows that DeepForecaster-PPO makes more accurate high-confidence predictions than Qwen3.6 and GPT-5.5. Trajectory analysis further shows that forecasts generally improve from the first to the final belief state as agents gather evidence and revise their judgments.

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [Preserving Pretrained CFG Structure in RL for Diffusion Models with Conditional Contrast Regularization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27288v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27288

**作者：** Yunfei Zheng、Xiaolong Wang、Dandan Zheng、Xianjun Deng、Shenghao Liu、Wei Xiang、Yuqian Li、Laurence Tianruo Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27288v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27288v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究扩散模型 RL 后训练中奖励提升与生成质量之间的失衡问题，从几何视角将 CFG 与 RL 都解释为预测空间中的修正向量，提出以冻结参考模型的条件对比作为局部结构先验的条件对比正则（CCR），在多种奖励设置与骨干上改善奖励—质量权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其用「RL 修正向量在预训练 CFG 方向上的投影」诊断训练是否保留原有结构，并据此施加正则，可能可供核查 LVR 中奖励或轨迹训练是否保持视觉与推理相关表征结构；论文本身并非多模态推理工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Preserving Pretrained CFG Structure in RL for Diffusion Models with Conditional Contrast Regularization

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important component in the post-training of diffusion models. In practice, however, standard RL often improves the target reward without reliably preserving holistic generation quality, leading to unstable reward-quality trade-offs that we view as a form of reward hacking. Since Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is known to improve visual clarity, prompt alignment, and perceptual quality in generation, we investigate whether its underlying structure can serve as a useful prior during RL. We present a unified geometric view that interprets both CFG and RL as correction vectors in a common prediction space, and identify the projection of the RL-induced correction onto the pretrained CFG correction as a useful diagnostic of quality-related structural preservation. Geometric analysis and rotation intervention further show that the pretrained CFG direction carries information useful for generation quality that reward-driven RL updates do not necessarily preserve. Based on this insight, we propose Conditional Contrast Regularization (CCR), which uses the frozen reference model's conditional contrast as a local structural prior during RL. Experiments across multiple reward settings and backbones show that CCR more consistently preserves generation quality while maintaining competitive reward, yielding a better reward-quality trade-off than standard RL and generic regularization methods.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [EgoLAM: Learning Latent Actions from Hand and Head Motion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27290

**作者：** Ziyi Ding、Yiwei Wang、Zhongxing Wei、Xianxin Lai、Yechi Liu、Xiaodi Huang、Jinghua Hou、Xiaoquan Sun、Haijier Chen、Zhe Liu、Yinan Chen、Junqiao Zhao、Xiao-Ping Zhang、Jiayu Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27290v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** EgoLAM 从第一视角视频的两帧图像中提取 latent action code，用实测手腕平移旋转作为监督信号，并把相机自身运动作为单独输入送入前向模型，从而把手部操作与视角变化解耦；在 LARYBench 与 LIBERO 上评估动作解码与策略预训练效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「任务相关动作信号 vs. 视角等无关变化」的显式分离，以及用冻结 code 探测手腕运动与合成视角、光照扰动的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容保真度评价提供可供核查的线索；但属机器人动作表示，与 VLM 推理期状态生成距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoLAM: Learning Latent Actions from Hand and Head Motion

**abstract/A1：** Latent action models let a robot policy learn from video by turning the change between two frames into a training target. In egocentric video, a cup can move across the image because a hand pushes it or because the wearer turns. Both changes help predict the next frame, but only the first describes manipulation. During training, EgoLAM supervises the action code with measured wrist motion and supplies camera motion separately to the forward model. Even when the wearer turns while pushing a cup, the training target still describes how the hand pushed it. The action encoder pools object slots around each hand, and wrist translation and rotation can be decoded from the code in a common reference frame. Extracting a code then requires only two RGB frames. Alongside LARYBench classification and regression, we measure how much wrist motion a frozen code retains and how strongly it responds to a synthetic view or lighting change. EgoLAM has the highest hand-motion decoding score among eight evaluated models. Across three LIBERO suites, policies pretrained on its codes and fine-tuned on expert demonstrations achieve 79.38% mean success, 28.73 points above expert behavior cloning and 9.76 points above the strongest other latent action model in each suite on average.

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [HookEvo: A Framework for Coordinated Plugin Evolution on Hook-Enabled Agent Runtimes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27294v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27294

**作者：** Shengze Huang、Yanyu Mao、Xiaofei Yu、Baoshen Guo、Xiaorui Wang、Zhiwen Wu、Sheng Wu、Fukang Wen、Chengxu Wu、Linhao Li、Kailai Sun、Baotong Mi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27294v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27294v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** HookEvo 利用 agent runtime 的生命周期 hook，让独立改进器从执行轨迹诊断失败、联合编辑可执行插件及任务内共享状态，并在匹配任务上与当前 harness 对比择优、逐轮迭代，冻结 Qwen3-4B 后在 ALFWorld 等四个基准报告结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「tool receipt 可写入共享状态并控制后续上下文与动作」的插件机制，可供核查作为视觉证据进入推理状态并驱动 LOOK/STOP 的架构线索；但纯文本设定与 latent 表示、视觉细节保留的联系尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HookEvo: A Framework for Coordinated Plugin Evolution on Hook-Enabled Agent Runtimes

**abstract/A1：** Repairing failures in tool-using agents can require coordinated changes to how the harness interprets tool receipts, assembles context, and checks actions. We introduce HookEvo, a framework that uses an existing agent runtime's lifecycle hooks to evolve task-specific harnesses. We instantiate HookEvo on DeepSeek Harness. In each round, a separate improver diagnoses failures from execution traces and proposes a candidate. HookEvo evaluates the candidate against the current harness on matched tasks and carries the retained version into the next round. A single candidate can jointly edit executable plugin handlers and the state they share within a task, allowing a tool receipt to inform context and control a later action. Using a frozen Qwen3-4B executor, the selected harnesses lead the reported Qwen3-4B comparisons, achieving 95.41% on ALFWorld, 70.56% on DBBench, 48.17% on WebShop, and 41.67% on OSBench. Across three improvers and three edit scopes, from skill text to single-component and coordinated executable edits, GPT-6.1 Sol shows the largest average gain from the broadest scope. Our code is available at https://huangshengzebluesky.github.io/hookevo/.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [BioCL-POS: Biological Completer-aware Learning with Predictive Observation Selection - A Wild Ginseng Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27316v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27316

**作者：** Yulu Wang、Tingting Yao、Taolue Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27316v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27316v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向野山参根形分析的点云补全，提出外部选择器 BioCL-POS：对每个候选观测子集预测固定补全器的响应（共享空间位置到补全结果最近点的位移与距离），据此估计缺失区域恢复质量并挑选输入观测，使冻结补全器单次运行即优于全输入。

**相关性简评：** 「先预测冻结模型对候选证据的响应、再选择观测」与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、证据获取和贡献估计有明确方法联系，可供核查该响应信号能否用于多模态 latent 推理的输入选择与计算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BioCL-POS: Biological Completer-aware Learning with Predictive Observation Selection - A Wild Ginseng Study

**abstract/A1：** Growers and traders rely on root morphology to assess wild ginseng, yet breakage can obscure important structural traits. We study point cloud completion to provide geometric references for morphological analysis. Does providing more observed points always improve 3D completion? We test whether selected subsets improve recovery of the original missing region while keeping the pretrained completer fixed. We introduce BioCL-POS, an external selector that predicts how the completer responds to candidate inputs. For each candidate, we predict the displacement and distance from shared spatial locations to the nearest points in its completion. We use these spatial responses to estimate recovery quality and supervise them with measurements from actual completions. At inference, we select an input from the observed geometry and run the completer once. We evaluate four frozen completers on controlled gaps in ginseng and tree data at 5%,10%, and 20% omission, and examine 30% omission on ginseng. At 5--20% omission, we outperform full-input completion and all five alternative selectors on both datasets with all four completers. We achieve lower mean reconstruction error and higher mean F-score and coverage. We thus show that selecting observations can improve geometric recovery without retraining the completer, providing more accurate geometric references for subsequent morphological analysis.

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [SAVIOR: Sample-efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for OCR Representation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27319v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27319

**作者：** Akshata A Bhat、Sharath Naganna、Saiful Haq、Prashant Khatri、Krishna Chaitanya Reddy Tamataam、Neha Arun、Niyati Chhaya、Pushpak Bhattacharyya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27319v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27319v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAVIOR：针对金融 OCR 中的高影响失败模式（竖排或旋转文字、logo 内嵌厂商名、退化扫描、复杂多栏版面等）做样本高效的数据筛选与 VLM 微调，并提出基于 token 间成对空间关系的版面保真度指标 PaIRS，同时把版面感知 OCR 结果用作文档问答上下文。

**相关性简评：** 其「成对空间关系」度量与针对失败样本的定向数据筛选，可能可供核查如何量化几何与实体关系信息在视觉证据中的保真度；但该指标作用于输出文本而非模型内部状态，与 LVR 的推理状态生成和使用只存在间接线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAVIOR: Sample-efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for OCR Representation

**abstract/A1：** Optical Character Recognition (OCR) pipelines and vision-language models exhibit high accuracy on standard OCR and document-understanding benchmarks, yet they systematically underperform on document patterns that are operationally critical in financial workflows. These patterns include vertical or rotated text, logo-embedded vendor names, fine-print regulatory clauses, degraded scans, and complex multi-column layouts. While underrepresented in public datasets, they constitute a substantial portion of real-world failure cases. We introduce SAVIOR, a sample-efficient data curation methodology that targets such high-impact failure scenarios to adapt vision-language models for robust financial OCR. We further propose PaIRS, a structure-aware evaluation metric that measures layout fidelity by comparing pairwise spatial relationships between tokens. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct with SAVIOR-Train achieves a word recall of 0.93. When the resulting layout-aware OCR output is used as context for document question answering, it attains an F1 score of 0.89, outperforming both open- and closed-source models such as GPT-4o, Mistral-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, and DeepSeek-OCR.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACVW

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [GGDriver: Glance at What Is Shared, Gaze at What Differs for Perception-Free Planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27330v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27330

**作者：** Jiawei Sun、Maosen Li、Jiamin Shi、Xiaoxu Zheng、Jiun Tian Hoe、Yechao Bai、Michael Bi Mi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27330v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27330v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GGDriver：先用共享场景特征生成轨迹假设，再把每条候选轨迹周围的走廊投影到相机视图，按掩码在原分辨率的 patch 特征上取路径对齐证据，分别用于轨迹精化与最终打分排序，并在三种场景表示与消融中比较效果。

**相关性简评：** 按候选假设定向筛选视觉证据、并分别用于精化与打分的做法，与 LVR 中「生成内容是否真正使用视觉证据」及证据贡献分配问题相通；其掩码注意与「路径对齐 vs 随机/全patch」消融可供核查借鉴，但任务是自动驾驶规划而非语言推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GGDriver: Glance at What Is Shared, Gaze at What Differs for Perception-Free Planning

**abstract/A1：** A planner deciding whether to turn left or continue straight needs to inspect different parts of the scene for each maneuver. Yet many end-to-end driving planners evaluate every candidate trajectory against the same compressed scene representation. We let each candidate select the visual evidence used to refine and score it. A global glance first produces trajectory hypotheses from shared scene features. We then project a corridor around each trajectory into the camera views and use the resulting mask to attend to patch features at the backbone's native resolution. After refinement, the scorer projects each final proposal again and ranks it using evidence along its own path. This geometric selection requires only the predicted trajectory and camera calibration. On three planners with different scene representations (learned registers, a perception-supervised bird's-eye-view grid, and a coarse image-token grid), adding this gaze improves planning scores. In ablations, path-aligned patches outperform both a matched number of randomly selected patches and attention over all patches. Using the gaze for ranking yields larger gains than using it for refinement alone. With a ViT-S backbone and no perception labels, GGDriver achieves a rater feedback score of 8.110 on the WOD-E2E test set, 94.2 PDMS on NAVSIM-v1, 48.6 EPDMS on NAVSIM-v2, and a closed-loop Driving Score of 72.18 on Bench2Drive.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [ReSA: Preserving Reference-Specified Foreground in In-Context Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27341v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27341

**作者：** Dezhao Su、Bingxu Hou、Jingdi Lei、Lin Qian、Likai Wang、Yunyun Wan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27341v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27341v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReSA：用前景/背景参考原型构成共享参考核，在测试时适应中约束预测变化，并以参考加权重建筛选最终掩码，冻结视觉编码器、无需离线任务训练，在十个对象/部件/跨域分割基准上报告平均 mIoU 提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「以参考原型加权筛选候选预测、并约束适应漂移」的机制可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与候选 latent 假设选择，但属纯分割任务，未涉及 VLM 推理状态生成或答案对 latent 的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReSA: Preserving Reference-Specified Foreground in In-Context Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** In-context segmentation uses annotated references to specify the target and its extent in a query image. Accurately transferring this reference-specified foreground requires recovering target regions while suppressing irrelevant predictions. We propose ReSA, a reference-guided framework that unifies query adaptation and mask selection through a shared reference kernel. ReSA leverages foreground and background reference prototypes to constrain prediction changes during adaptation and guide foreground selection through reference-weighted reconstruction. This shared guidance promotes consistent use of reference information across prediction refinement and mask formation. ReSA further selects among adapted predictions and aligns boundary refinement with the selected foreground threshold. We establish a theoretical bound connecting reference-response changes during adaptation and soft reconstruction. With a frozen visual encoder and lightweight test-time adaptation, ReSA requires no offline task training. Ablation studies demonstrate improved foreground retention and effective suppression of false-positive regions, while qualitative results show more complete masks with fewer isolated artifacts. Across ten object, part, and cross-domain segmentation benchmarks, ReSA achieves the highest average mIoU among the evaluated methods, reaching 62.6% in 1-shot and 71.8% in 5-shot.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [PAVE: A Harness for Geometrically Consistent Road Network Modeling with Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27352v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27352

**作者：** Yajun Liu、Jinxi Wu、Juan Pablo Millán Raya、Xuesong Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27352v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27352v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PAVE（Perceive、Anchor、Verify、Edit）流程，让代码负责可测量量、以交通工程规则作检查，VLM 只做开放判断并以路网 ID 锚定，用于从卫星与街景影像生成及编辑车道级路网，并在信号交叉口上以人工标注评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「测量与规则交代码、模型判断须与测量或二次读数对照」的分工，以及跨视角几何一致性失败的分析，可能为 LVR 中的证据核验、贡献分配与视觉信息旁路问题提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PAVE: A Harness for Geometrically Consistent Road Network Modeling with Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Transportation models, whether they simulate traffic or support planning, are only as reliable as the road networks they run on, down to the lanes and turns at each intersection. Practitioners still take such a network from GIS files and supply its junction and movement data by hand, a task that recurs with each new base year and design alternative, or they generate it from open map data. OpenStreetMap's lane tags give the correct layout for only 13 of the 44 approaches we test; open map data are often incomplete or inaccurate. Satellite and street-level imagery, in contrast, cover all 44 and show their lanes as painted. Vision-language models (VLMs) can read such imagery, but asked directly they lose geometric consistency when the same lane must be matched across a satellite crop, street-level photos taken from different poses and an ID-labelled network. We therefore present PAVE (Perceive, Anchor, Verify, Edit), a harness in which code measures what can be measured, traffic engineering knowledge enters as general rules and checks, and the model makes only the open judgements, each anchored to network IDs so that checks catch answers that contradict a measurement, a second reading or a traffic rule. Scored against human labels at signalised intersections, PAVE raises the share of exactly correct lane layouts from 49-64% with a single request to 86-91% for two VLMs, with most of the gain coming from reading one approach at a time on traced lanes. The same division of work edits a real network to a planning brief: the model names the links and nodes of each part of a facility, and code builds the edit from a design template. Of 18 edits, 16 pass every network check, against none when the model writes its own code, and 2 contain a design or coding error, against 7. An analysis shows where VLMs lose geometric consistency and which check catches each failure.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [WePM: Hierarchical Conflict Reconciliation for Unified MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27365v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27365

**作者：** Zhiqiang Yuan、Qiwei Yan、Liwei Yang、Jinzhe Liu、Yang Liu、Zhongyan Hou、Liyan Xu、Peixiang Luo、Jiapei Zhang、Ying Deng、Jie Zhou、Jinchao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27365v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27365v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出端侧统一多模态模型系列 WePM，用多粒度视觉预算分配、统一 listwise 蒸馏与多任务优化调度，在同一视觉语言主干内整合表征、生成与决策能力。

**相关性简评：** 固定视觉 token 预算在全局/区域/细粒度间的分配，可能为视觉证据筛选与细节保真提供线索；其多任务梯度冲突与损失加权处理也可供核查训练期贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WePM: Hierarchical Conflict Reconciliation for Unified MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Bringing multimodal intelligence from the cloud to users' devices keeps sensitive data local and reduces inference cost, but deploying a specialized model per capability duplicates vision-language capacity under tight memory and compute budgets. We formalize heterogeneous capability density (HCD), measuring how broadly and effectively a model serves heterogeneous task families per unit of resident inference memory. High HCD across representation, generation, and decision raises two challenges: these capabilities need visual evidence at different spatial granularities under a fixed token budget, and separately collected task corpora yield conflicting updates to shared parameters, while cross-task paired supervision is costly and ambiguous for open-ended inputs. We present WePM, a family of pocket models unifying representation, generation, and decision within one vision-language backbone. Multi-Granularity Visual Budgeting (MGVB) allocates a fixed visual-token budget across global, regional, and fine-grained banks; Unified Listwise Knowledge Distillation (ULKD) enriches separately collected data with soft teacher guidance, avoiding cross-task paired annotation; Stable and Efficient Multitask Optimization (SEMO) regulates each task's contribution through explicit loss weights and exposure schedules, with sequential gradient accumulation under memory constraints. In the joint representation-generation setting, WePM outperforms all baselines across five evaluation groups, raising the Joint score from 38.15 to 52.74. In single-task evaluation, WePM-4B attains the best average score over 30 embedding, 39 generation, and seven typed-decision benchmarks (72.86, 62.41, 61.11). Against the separate-model stack with the highest HCD, WePM achieves slightly higher balanced performance with 61.9% less resident weight memory, a 2.64-fold higher HCD.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables. Code and models: https://github.com/Tencent/WePocketModel and https://huggingface.co/collections/tencent/wepocketmodel. Zhiqiang Yuan, Qiwei Yan, and Liwei Yang contributed equally. Corresponding author: Jinchao Zhang

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [AMARIS: Merging Generalist and Specialist LLMs via Adaptive Subspace Inheritance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27369

**作者：** Hwee Young Kang、Irwin De Tao Chin、Wen-Haw Chong、Hai Leong Chieu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27369v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AMARIS，把专家 LLM 的合并表述为「预算受限的子空间继承」：在限制对基座输出扰动的正则约束下最大化专家激活捕获，用协方差驱动的广义特征方向排序构造低秩投影门，产出单一静态合并 checkpoint，并在 Qwen3 8B/32B 的金融、指令跟随、多语言 SEA 场景评测。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其「高任务效用、低干扰」的秩化子空间选择思路，可能为向 VLM 内部状态注入视觉证据时控制干扰、避免核心能力坍缩提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AMARIS: Merging Generalist and Specialist LLMs via Adaptive Subspace Inheritance

**abstract/A1：** Domain specialization of large language models (LLMs) often improves target-domain performance but degrades broad capabilities. Model merging is an attractive training-free alternative, yet common parameter-space strategies (for example, interpolation and sparsified averaging) provide limited control over task-specific interference. We introduce Adaptive Model Alignment via Ranked Inherited Subspaces (AMARIS), a covariance-driven framework that transfers specialist updates only along selected activation directions. AMARIS formulates merging as budgeted subspace inheritance: it seeks high specialist activation capture under a regularized base-output perturbation budget, then builds a low-rank projection gate from ranked generalized eigendirections. This yields a single static merged checkpoint that concentrates transfer in high-utility, low-interference directions. We evaluate AMARIS on Qwen3-based merges (8B and 32B) across finance, instruction-following, and multilingual SEA specialization. In our evaluated settings, AMARIS reaches Pareto operating points that are competitive with strong training-free baselines, including 98.37% specialist retention with 95.16% core retention for the 8B finance setting, 103.55% core retention with 90.28% specialist retention for the 8B instruction-following setting, and 99.20% core retention with 94.80% specialist retention for the 32B SEA setting. These results support subspace-constrained merging as a practical route to compositional capability integration without additional training.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [ReActWAM: Action-Consistent Caching for Efficient World-Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27372v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27372

**作者：** Zhaochen Guo、Yunke Wang、Siyu Xu、Zijian Wang、Chenghao Xia、Chengbin Du、Shinan Liu、Chang Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27372v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27372v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练加速框架 ReActWAM：用动作意图引导的前缀跳过与 chunk 内预测复用，并通过运动变化、视觉一致性和动作冲突的粗到细评估决定是否复用，配合首步差分自适应缓存 latent 与网络特征；在多个 world-action 模型与机器人基准上取得 1.41–5.44 倍推理加速而成功率基本不变。

**相关性简评：** 其「判断内部状态可否复用、何时必须重算」的缓存更新与计算跳过机制，可能为 LVR 中跨推理步 latent 复用、动态计算与成本控制的线索，可供核查这些判据是否真正依赖视觉证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReActWAM: Action-Consistent Caching for Efficient World-Action Models

**abstract/A1：** World-action models (WAMs) often generate future visual states alongside robot actions, introducing substantial computational overhead during inference. During closed-loop execution, consecutive inference cycles often predict similar action sequences, leading to redundant computation. However, reducing this redundancy requires distinguishing consistent actions from changes that demand fresh computation. To address these challenges, we propose ReActWAM, a training-free framework that combines action-intent-guided prefix skipping with inner-chunk prediction reuse. A coarse-to-fine assessment evaluates motion changes, visual consistency, and action prediction conflicts. For eligible chunks, we draw on first-step differences to adapt the cached latent and network features to the current condition, providing an estimate at a later denoising step that bypasses the redundant prefix. Subsequent inference refines this estimate while reusing stable denoising predictions within the chunk. Experiments across LingBot-VA, FastWAM and the Cosmos family on LIBERO-Long and RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrate $1.41\\times$--$5.44\\times$ GPU inference speedup, with minimal degradation in task success rates. Real-world experiments on a Franka Research 3 (FR3) robot further achieve $1.46\\times$ end-to-end speedup while maintaining success rate.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [Gumbel Substitution: Removing Sampler Drift from RL Without Trusting the Sampler](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27375v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27375

**作者：** Romain Fanucci、Malik El Hassani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27375v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27375v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「Gumbel substitution」：协调方为每个 token 位置固定跨 GPU 位一致的公共 Gumbel 噪声，训练方在相同噪声下重算自身策略会采样的 token 并据此打分，从而无需采样器上报概率即可消除任意采样分布 q 引致的漂移，并给出逐 token 匹配率用于采样器准入与支付，在 2B/9B 模型及异构无许可采样器网络上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其核心问题是「不信任采样器时如何修正 rollout 分布偏差」，公共 Gumbel 噪声耦合与逐 token 匹配率可能可供核查用于分布式随机 latent 采样的状态一致性检验或轨迹贡献分配；但论文不涉及视觉输入与 latent 推理状态，迁移可行性需研究者自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gumbel Substitution: Removing Sampler Drift from RL Without Trusting the Sampler

**abstract/A1：** When RL rollouts come from untrusted samplers on arbitrary hardware, precision and checkpoint lag, the sampling distribution $q$ differs from the trainer's policy $p$, and training on the sampler's tokens adds a drift term equal to supervised fine-tuning toward $q$. Published corrections (TIS, MIS, IcePop, score centering, GSPO, CISPO) all need $q(y)$, which a permissionless sampler reports and can misreport. We introduce Gumbel substitution. The coordinator fixes public Gumbel noise for every token position, bit-identical across GPUs, and the sampler draws $y=\\arg\\max(\\ell^q+G)$. The trainer recomputes $v^\*=\\arg\\max(\\ell^p+G)$, the token its own policy would have drawn with the same noise, and scores $v^\*$ in the sampler's context. For a sampler that follows the protocol, $v^\*$ is an exact sample from $p$ given the prefix, so the drift vanishes in expectation for any $q$, without sampler-reported probabilities. We measure the residual left by the deployed heads, bound the one added by admission gates, and state what an adaptive sampler can still do. The same comparison gives a per-token match rate that admits and pays samplers. In the lab, on a 9B model with the same head on both sides, the expected drift of uncorrected training is at least 170 to 4,800 times that of substitution. On a 2B model under strong mismatch, uncorrected training collapses while substitution stays stable, comparable to MIS with score centering given the true $q$. On a permissionless network, a 9B model was trained for 158 optimizer steps on 15.75B tokens from heterogeneous samplers, with no skipped step and 1.21% of tokens substituted; it improved on all eight held-out benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 6 figures. Code: https://github.com/reliquadotai/reliquary

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation of RL Experts: Toward Continual Post-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27376v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27376

**作者：** Zhen Zhang、Changyi Yang、Xuehai He、Chengzhi Liu、Yutong Yin、Ziyue Liu、Zhuoran Yin、Yangmin Li、Zhihan Liu、Jisen Li、Mingyu Jin、Chuhan Li、Zhaotian Weng、Ruoyu He、Wenyao Gao、Jiaxin Deng、Chenwei Wu、Hui Shen、Wenhao Chai、Chenyang Zhao、Zhuofan Ying、Kieran Liu、Andrew Chen、Pony Ma、Zhaoran Wang、Alkesh Patel、Zhe Gan、Xin Eric Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27376v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27376v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CSPD：冻结 RL 后的底座模型，把每个 RL 专家单独蒸馏成一个 soft prompt，并以拼接 prompt 的方式组合多个专家而无需联合训练或整体重合并，在数学、代码与 agent 任务上验证组合效果与吞吐开销。

**相关性简评：** 「把一种策略压成独立可拼接的输入空间 soft prompt」这一线索，可能可供核查如何把 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等推理策略封装为可组合的轻量条件向量并避免相互侵蚀；但该工作不涉及视觉推理或推理期 latent 状态生成，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation of RL Experts: Toward Continual Post-Training

**abstract/A1：** RL post-training typically produces domain experts that must then be consolidated into one model, and as new experts keep arriving, this consolidation becomes a continual process. Weight merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) must re-consolidate the whole model whenever an expert is added, and can erode capabilities outside the target domains. We propose Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation (CSPD), which keeps the pre-RL model frozen, distills each RL expert independently into a soft prompt, and composes experts by concatenating their prompts without joint training. A single soft prompt suffices to distill an RL expert, recovering at least 90% of the teacher's gain in 22 of 23 math, code, and agent settings. Independently trained prompts compose well. In a multi-expert setting, the average rises with every expert added, and the full composition matches MOPD (67.83 vs. 67.79). The composition generalizes best across six held-out benchmarks. Input-space adaptation adds little serving overhead, keeping throughput within 1.5% of the unadapted backbone and reaching 2.40x that of rank-4 LoRA. These results point toward continual post-training on a fixed backbone.

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [HR-Merge: Model Merging with Headroom-Weighted Residual Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27377v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27377

**作者：** Jiakang Huang、Xueyan Zhang、Renyi Cai、Jingcheng Niu、Jiaru Li、Yining Wang、Josh Zhang、Jiahe Jiang、Jinman Zhao、Gerald Penn、Baharan Mirzasoleiman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27377v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27377v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HR-Merge：一种无需训练的模型合并包装器，用各任务验证精度损失（headroom）对「任务向量减去合并更新」的残差加权，再以基础算子重新合并并按验证选择强度，在 LLaMA-3 的 NLI 适配器与 CLIP 多任务设置上提升归一化精度。

**相关性简评：** 其按验证余量分配各任务残差权重、并显式处理残差间干扰的合并规则，可能为多专家或多假设 latent 状态的贡献分配与相互干扰抑制提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HR-Merge: Model Merging with Headroom-Weighted Residual Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Model merging combines experts fine-tuned from a shared backbone, such as LoRA adapters of a large language model, into one multi-task model without further training. Existing methods apply one rule to every task, yet the accuracy they lose is uneven across tasks. We show that the lost accuracy remains recoverable from each task's residual, its task vector minus the merged update, and that a task's validation headroom, the share of its expert validation accuracy the merge loses, indicates where recovery pays off. Residuals from different tasks interfere, so they must be merged rather than summed. We propose HR-Merge, a training-free wrapper around any merging method. It weights each residual by its task's headroom, re-merges the weighted residuals with the base operator, and adds the result back at a strength selected on validation, falling back to the base merge when no strength helps. On six NLI adapters of LLaMA-3 8B, HR-Merge lifts TIES from 93.29 to 94.29 normalized accuracy, above all tested bases, mainly by recovering its weakest task. In vision, on three CLIP backbones, up to 20 tasks, and LoRA merging, it generally improves or preserves base accuracy and yields the best model in every setting.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 7 figures, 16 tables. A short version was accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Neural Network Artifacts as a New Data Modality

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Taking Apart and Putting Together: Tracking Objects Through Physical Transformations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27398v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27398

**作者：** Davide Gardenal、Mingxuan Liu、Flavio Vella、Quentin Guimard、Elisa Ricci

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27398v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27398v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「变换跟踪与理解」（TTU）任务及 VISTA 数据集，考察物体经组装与拆解时如何持续跟踪、识别新实体并维护状态图；并给出免训练框架 TIPO，通过分裂/合并提案生成、重叠打分与对 top 提案的 MLLM 视觉核验来更新变换标签。

**相关性简评：** 其「生成–评估–判定」流程与按需查询 MLLM 做视觉核验的机制，可作为核查 LVR 中何时触发证据获取、候选筛选及外部状态更新效率的借鉴线索，但对象级跟踪状态与模型内部 latent 表示仍有距离。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Taking Apart and Putting Together: Tracking Objects Through Physical Transformations

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Transformation Tracking and Understanding (TTU), extending video object tracking to environments where objects undergo physical assemblies and disassemblies. TTU requires continuously tracking objects through transformations, identifying new entities, maintaining a state graph of their relationships, and assigning semantic labels to transformations. While foundation models (e.g., SAM 2/3) maintain tracklets through appearance changes, they cannot update semantic identities as objects physically evolve. Prior work addresses only disassemblies, relying on expensive searches triggered only when tracking fails. We propose Tracking Instances via Proposals and Overlaps (TIPO), a training-free framework that continuously infers transformations via a generate-evaluate-judge pipeline. TIPO generates split and merge proposals from disjoint mask components, scores them against mask-decoder priors using inner and external overlap, and queries an MLLM on the top proposal for visual verification and semantic labeling. To benchmark TTU, we curate Video Instances, States, and Transformation Annotations (VISTA), a dataset including state graphs with 124 assembly and 132 disassembly events across 50 sequences and over 26,000 frames. TIPO achieves state-of-the-art tracking and state-graph accuracy on VISTA, while operating $6\\times$ to $15\\times$ faster than prior methods. Project page: https://davidegardenal.github.io/tipo-tracking

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://davidegardenal.github.io/tipo-tracking

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load: Synchronization, Rule Execution and Misfires on the Background](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27404v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27404

**作者：** Hennadii Kutomanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27404v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27404v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在受控负荷下（文本长度、答案位置、替换密度、规则数等）将语言模型错误分解为「与原文失去同步」「规则执行错误」「无关词误改」三类，通过与参考对齐逐词追踪并用统计模型拟合各类负荷下的退化规律，为七个模型生成「passport」。

**相关性简评：** 其误差分解与负荷—退化规律拟合思路可供核查：可能用于分离 LVR 中随思考长度或证据量增加而出现的「脱离视觉证据」与 latent 推理执行错误，但纯文本规则的设定与多模态推理场景差异较大。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load: Synchronization, Rule Execution and Misfires on the Background

**abstract/A1：** As the load grows, a language model makes more and more errors, but different processes stand behind this decline in quality. The model may lose the correspondence with the source text, it may keep its place in the text but execute a rule wrongly, or it may change a word that no rule concerns. In this work the degradation of a model under controlled load is separated into these three components: synchronization, rule execution and misfires. The load is set by rules that replace one word with another in a meaningless text, and each kind of load (the text length, the position of a word in the answer, the replacement density, the number of rules and the place of a rule in the list) is set separately. The alignment of the answer with the reference makes it possible to follow the outcome of every word, and a statistical model that takes into account that the spread of difficulty between requests itself depends on the load restores, for each component, the laws of its dependence on every kind of load. These laws make up the passport of a model. The paper presents the method of the separation, the principles of constructing the laws and the passports of seven models built by this method; the separation opens the way to studying the fine structure of the behavior of a neural network.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 15 figures, 5 tables. Code and passports: https://github.com/kutomanovhennadii/instruction-capacity

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Jagged Robustness: Uneven Safety Transfer and Emerging Vulnerabilities under LLM Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27424v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27424

**作者：** Ramneek Agnihotri、Yingte Xu、Hans Vath

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27424v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27424v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文审计量化对语言模型能力与安全的影响，发现在 Qwen3-4B 上安全防护与偏见会退化而真实性较稳定，称之为「Jagged Robustness」，并用不安全请求顺从率及对抗后缀攻击验证退化。

**相关性简评：** 其按能力域分项审计、结合评估方式与生成连贯性归因、并用对抗探针检验退化的做法，可供核查 latent 视觉推理状态在低精度或压缩下是否保真、以及视觉证据筛选是否更脆弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Jagged Robustness: Uneven Safety Transfer and Emerging Vulnerabilities under LLM Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Quantization enables efficient inference in language models through model parameter compression. This work audits the impact of quantization on capability and safety domains including model safeguards, instruction-following, ethics and bias. Through benchmarks, we find that model safeguards and bias can degrade under quantization, while other capabilities such as truthfulness remain more stable in Qwen3-4B. We define this uneven transfer of LLM capabilities under quantization as Jagged Robustness. We find multiple factors driving Jagged Robustness: task complexity, evaluation method and generation coherence. Moreover, we verify the degradation of model safeguards by observing increased compliance with unsafe requests under quantization, and demonstrate universal adversarial suffix attacks that are benign for the full model but jailbreak quantized models. The findings indicate that it is risky to assume that the performance and robustness can transfer evenly across different capability domains from full models to quantized models.

**comment/C1：** Code Repository: https://github.com/LucianoXu/QuantBeyoundEfficiency

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [THRIFTY: Cost-Constrained Reasoning-Data Synthesis for Small Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27436

**作者：** Aasheesh Singh、Vishal Vaddina

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27436v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对小模型推理轨迹蒸馏数据合成成本高的问题，提出 THRIFTY：用 Thompson sampling 式 bandit 控制在固定生成 token 预算下收集尽量多样的正确轨迹，在线估计各题成功概率并自适应停采已饱和题目。

**相关性简评：** 其按题目难度分配采样预算、对饱和与不可解题目自适应停止/退出的机制，或可借鉴到 LVR 轨迹训练数据采集与推理期 STOP 决策的成本分配问题，但纯文本且不涉及视觉状态，联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** THRIFTY: Cost-Constrained Reasoning-Data Synthesis for Small Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly attractive for efficient, specialized deployment, and are typically trained on reasoning trajectories distilled from a larger teacher LLM. Generating this synthetic data is a major cost of the pipeline, especially when the teacher is a frontier model. Existing collection methods, however, are largely cost-unaware, or at best spread a fixed sampling budget uniformly across queries. Such allocation ignores the wide variation in problem difficulty and the strategic over- or under-sampling it invites. Thus, we propose THRIFTY, a Thompson-sampling bandit controller that spends a fixed generation-token budget to collect diverse correct reasoning trajectories, casting the task as a bandits-with-knapsacks problem with a novel diversity-aware objective. It tracks each problem's success probability with an online Beta posterior and spends each token where it expects the most new distinct correct solutions. Because repeated solutions add progressively less value, THRIFTY adaptively stops resampling saturated problems, concentrates budget on under-covered ones, and retires the unsolvable at the budget's opportunity cost. On math (AIME, MATH-500) and code (LiveCodeBench) benchmarks, THRIFTY gathers up to 22% more diverse correct trajectories per generation token than both the standard uniform-sampling recipe and the strongest adaptive baseline, with the largest gains in the scarce-budget and hard-problem regimes.

**comment/C1：** Published at the Efficient Reasoning Workshop, COLM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [Cecilia: Towards Layout-Inclusive Optical Music Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27465v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27465

**作者：** Congren Dai、Zhancheng Guo、Xiaobing Li、Maosong Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27465v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27465v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出紧凑的规范化 MusicXML 表示 Cecilia，保留音符内容、歌词绑定与版面指令，并构建 Cecilia-OMR：基于共享视觉记忆按小节切段预测该表示，用学得计数头决定输出段数，在 OpenScore 多条件下优于 Legato 2。

**相关性简评：** 其「计数头决定输出段数」与「共享视觉记忆下分段解码」分别可能为 LVR 的停止决策和视觉证据复用提供可核查线索，但任务属乐谱识别，迁移价值需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cecilia: Towards Layout-Inclusive Optical Music Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing an editable music score requires musical events, sung text and the instructions that organise them on the page. Existing recognition targets often omit part of this information, while direct MusicXML prediction repeats substantial structural markup. We introduce Cecilia, a compact representation of canonicalised MusicXML that retains musical content, lyric attachments and layout instructions. Cecilia-OMR predicts this representation through metadata and measure-bounded music segments from shared visual memory. A learned count head determines the number of output segments. Across paired complete scores under a common tokeniser, the ratio of mean MusicXML to Cecilia sequence lengths is 27.73. Cecilia-OMR outperforms Legato 2 on Camera String Quartets and both Lieder conditions, with a four-condition OpenScore macro normalised edit distance of 0.2232 against 0.2998. Separate evaluations of lyrics, notation trees and rendered layout reveal complementary aspects of reconstruction and quantify the contribution of layout instructions. Training-stage, reward and candidate-selection studies further examine how recognition changes across model adaptation and decoding.

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [Cannot Wait to Verify: Parallel Drafting to Eliminate Verifier Idle Time in Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27477v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27477

**作者：** Divya Jyoti Bajpai、Arun Verma、Manjesh Kumar Hanawal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27477v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27477v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种可插拔的并行起草方法用于 speculative decoding：从共享隐状态出发并行评估多个 transformer 层，以隐状态空间偏差衡量一致性来决定接受或修正，无需改架构或重训，起草阶段加速最高 1.65×。

**相关性简评：** 其以隐状态偏差作为候选状态接受与修正的判据，可供核查 LVR 中并行产生 latent 步骤时的状态验证与接受机制；但对象是纯文本解码加速、无视觉证据，与主线联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cannot Wait to Verify: Parallel Drafting to Eliminate Verifier Idle Time in Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive inference in large transformer models is limited by token-level sequential execution, resulting in high latency and low GPU utilization. Speculative decoding (SD) mitigates this bottleneck by introducing a draft-and-verifier model. However, the drafting stage remains sequential, leaving the verifier idle and underutilizing compute. To address this, we propose a plug-and-play drafting adaptive drafting method that parallelizes inference across transformer layers during draft generation. Starting from a shared hidden state, our approach evaluates multiple layers in parallel and verifies consistency by measuring divergence in hidden-state space. Layer-wise draft states are accepted when deviations are small; otherwise, a correction step is applied. By exploiting redundancy across transformer layers, our method accelerates draft generation and improves hardware utilization without modifying architectures or requiring retraining. Experimental results on NLP tasks show that our method speeds up the drafting stage by up to $1.65\\times$ with minimal degradation in accuracy, while maintaining the same performance as SD.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27489v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27489

**作者：** Oliver Schön、Adrien Banse、John Lygeros、Lars Lindemann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LO、cs.RO、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27489v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27489v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究机器人端高效的自然语言到线性时序逻辑（LTL）翻译，将翻译拆解为回答一组答案预定义的问题，并用 System-One 分类器作答，在三个基准上以最多低一个数量级的运行时间与能耗达到或超过最佳本地 LLM 的准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其用固定答案集合的分类替代自回归文本生成、并关注端侧计算成本的做法，可能为 LVR 中结构化推理状态或答案的非生成式输出与 GET 成本提供可核查线索，但材料未涉及视觉或多模态，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI

**abstract/A1：** Robots can be controlled to perform tasks with guaranteed correctness when the task instructions are stated in a formal language. Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used to allow users to provide such instructions in natural language (NL), by translating NL into formal language. When deploying such translators on a robot, however, we face limited computing and energy resources, while offloading computation or data to the cloud may be infeasible or undesirable. Yet an LLM is a general-purpose text generator, whereas a formula in a fixed formal language fragment follows a well-defined syntax. We therefore ask whether text generation is needed at all. For the formal language of linear temporal logic (LTL), we show that this translation can instead be broken down into answering a set of questions with pre-defined answers. We then construct a System-One model, a recent class of NL-based classifier, to provide such answers and enable us to translate a natural language instruction into an LTL instruction. On three benchmarks, our translator matches or exceeds the accuracy of the best local LLMs available, at up to one order of magnitude lower runtime and energy consumption. We show that it generalizes to unseen atomic propositions and temporal patterns.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [Trained on Harm, Still Refusing: A Training-Time Defense for Code Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27495v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27495

**作者：** Miaoqing Tian、Ruohan Li、Qian Lu、Haipeng Qu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27495v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27495v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究有害微调下代码模型拒答能力退化的问题，发现危害信息仍可从内部表示中解码却未转化为持续拒答行为，并提出 SAFEHOLD：在首个生成位置锚定对齐模型的完整词表分布，并对整条拒答序列做路径强化监督。

**相关性简评：** 「内部表示可解码但生成行为不跟随」的表示—行为落差，以及把拒答视为持续生成过程而非单点决策、对整条轨迹施加监督，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真被答案使用、以及轨迹级贡献分配与停止决策；但无视觉或多模态成分，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trained on Harm, Still Refusing: A Training-Time Defense for Code Generation

**abstract/A1：** Fine-tuning enables safety-aligned code language models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks. However, training on untrusted data can severely undermine their ability to refuse harmful requests. Notably, we find that models comply with harmful requests even though harmfulness information remains highly decodable from their internal representations. Moreover, an initial preference for refusal does not necessarily translate into sustained refusal behavior throughout generation. Together, these findings reveal a critical gap between recognizing harmful requests and consistently refusing to fulfill them. To address this gap, we introduce SAFEHOLD, a novel provider-enforced defense framework that preserves safety alignment during harmful fine-tuning by treating refusal as a sustained generation process rather than a single response decision. Specifically, SAFEHOLD preserves the aligned model's full-vocabulary response distribution at the initial generation position through decision-boundary anchoring, while supervising the complete refusal sequence through refusal-path reinforcement. We conduct extensive experiments evaluating safety, utility, and internal mechanisms. SAFEHOLD reduces average ASR by 72.03 percentage points relative to undefended fine-tuning, outperforming five SOTA defenses by 24.32 to 70.87 percentage points. Meanwhile, it improves average HumanEval pass@1 by 10.24 percentage points over undefended fine-tuning, while maintaining an average XSTest over-refusal rate of only 2.73 percent. Overall, these results demonstrate that SAFEHOLD effectively mitigates safety degradation during harmful fine-tuning while preserving benign-task utility.

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [Beyond a Single Human Demonstration: Learning from Scored Trajectory Alternatives for End-to-End Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27515v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27515

**作者：** Xinyao Rao、Zecheng Tang、Liuxuan Sun、Zheyuan Lai、Minjun Yu、Xun Cao、Wei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27515v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27515v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** DriveSTA 面向端到端驾驶，用离线平滑变形并模拟打分得到的候选轨迹，训练自回归提议生成、提议条件下的残差流细化与质量打分器，并以共享 JEPA 编码视频特征。

**相关性简评：** 其「多候选提议＋流式残差细化＋打分器择优」可能为 LVR 的候选 latent 生成、质量引导更新与选择/停止提供线索，但属驾驶规划任务，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond a Single Human Demonstration: Learning from Scored Trajectory Alternatives for End-to-End Driving

**abstract/A1：** Human driving logs record only one trajectory per scene, leaving other feasible driving choices without explicit trajectory targets or quality labels. This limits supervision for end-to-end planners that generate and refine multiple candidate trajectories. Meanwhile, conditioning refinement on a large vision-language model adds substantial online inference cost. We propose DriveSTA, which extends autoregressive proposal generation and proposal-conditioned residual flow refinement with scored trajectory alternatives. For each training scene, we generate alternatives offline through smooth deformations of the recorded trajectory and evaluate their simulated execution against logged traffic. Diverse, high-quality alternatives supervise distinct proposals and define residual targets for quality-guided denoising, while candidates spanning the full score range train a scorer to assess driving quality. A shared joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), adapted through masked video-feature prediction, encodes observed frames once and provides temporal context for both refinement and scoring. On NAVSIM v1 Navtest, DriveSTA ranks first among the compared planners trained on NAVSIM data alone. With expanded training data, DriveSTA-Scale achieves state-of-the-art results among the compared methods on Navtest and closed-loop HUGSIM, while reducing mean planner forward-pass latency by 73% relative to ChainFlow-VLA in matched evaluations.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 9 figures, including supplementary material. Project page: https://rtwotwo.github.io/DriveSTA

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [PhiJEPA: Denoising Latent Predictions for Visual Representation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27516v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27516

**作者：** Makoto Yamada、Yao-Hung Hubert Tsai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27516v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27516v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PhiJEPA：在掩码 Transformer 中先由受损上下文预测隐藏 token，再用逐 token MLP 映射到干净教师表示，并评估分割及遮挡加模糊下的世界模型规划。

**相关性简评：** 两阶段 latent 预测和保留预训练 predictor 完成遮挡 token 的做法，可能为 LVR 在视觉证据受损时维持内部状态、补全缺失细节提供线索；但其不涉及 VLM 推理或 THINK/LOOK/STOP，迁移价值仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhiJEPA: Denoising Latent Predictions for Visual Representation Learning

**abstract/A1：** Can a predictor trained to infer hidden image regions still help a world model plan when part of an object disappears from view and the visible context is blurred? We introduce PhiJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that adapts PhiNet's two-stage prediction to masked Transformers. From sparse, photometrically corrupted context, a Transformer block predicts hidden tokens of an independently corrupted teacher view. A token-wise MLP then maps these predictions to clean teacher tokens. Controlled ablations show that the combined objective improves dense features over the tested one-stage alternatives. Pre-trained on ImageNet-1K with $5.6\\times10^{19}$ training FLOPs, PhiJEPA achieves 69.3 mIoU on linear PASCAL VOC segmentation, exceeding the evaluated I-JEPA and DSeq-JEPA checkpoints in segmentation accuracy, with 25\\% fewer training FLOPs than I-JEPA. It also improves hidden-region segmentation over Simple I-JEPA (49.1 vs.\\ 44.3 mIoU). As an application, we use the frozen encoder in DINO-WM on PushT, and reuse the pre-training predictor, which JEPAs usually discard, to complete occluded tokens without fine-tuning. In open-loop planning with simultaneous object occlusion and Gaussian blur ($σ=2$ pixels), predictor completion raises success from 33\\% to 65\\%, compared with 25\\% for Simple I-JEPA with its predictor and 40\\% for iBOT with pixel inpainting. Together, these results support two-stage latent prediction as an efficient approach to learning visual representations that remain useful under photometric degradation and support completion under partial observation.

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [EchoAlign: Echocardiography foundation model with multilevel report alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27517v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27517

**作者：** Ahmed Aly、Darya Taratynova、Lotfi Abdelkrim Mecharbat、Roman A. Sandler、Joseph Z. Sokol、Joshua Penn、Klaus H. Maier-Hein、Numan Saeed、Mohammad Yaqub

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27517v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27517v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EchoAlign 超声心动图视觉语言基础模型，基于 287 万视频/图像按「心脏结构—视频—检查」三个层级与报告文本对齐，并用可见性矩阵把监督限制在真正显示该结构的视图上，在多个外部数据集上评估分类、严重程度分级、测量与分割。

**相关性简评：** 其「按视图可见性裁剪监督、避免报告与视觉证据错配」的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与监督贡献分配提供可核查线索，但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EchoAlign: Echocardiography foundation model with multilevel report alignment

**abstract/A1：** Echocardiography assesses the structure and function of the heart. Cardiologists read each study across several standard views and report the findings visible in those views by cardiac structure. AI foundation models can learn to analyze these studies from clinical reports, reducing annotation burden and supporting clinical interpretation. However, existing models are trained by pairing each video with the full clinical report, including structures and findings its view does not show. This mismatch lets a model associate a finding with incidental or spurious signals rather than its visual evidence, which limits accuracy, anatomical grounding and clinical trust. Here we introduce EchoAlign, a vision-language foundation model developed on 2.87 million echocardiographic videos and images from 159 clinical centers. EchoAlign aligns each cardiac structure, video and study with its report text, and a visibility matrix restricts supervision to the views that actually depict each structure. We compare it with five public foundation models on 0.47 million test videos across ten unseen external datasets from more than 25 clinical centers. EchoAlign achieves the highest mean AUROC of 0.890 (95% CI, 0.881 to 0.897) for classification and the highest mean quadratic weighted kappa of 0.530 (95% CI, 0.511 to 0.544) for severity grading. Across 62 clinical findings, EchoAlign provides one model that detects findings, grades their severity, estimates measurements and segments cardiac structures.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 3 figures, 6 extended data figures, 4 extended data tables. Code and model weights: https://github.com/BioMedIA-MBZUAI/EchoAlign

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [On the Limits of Observability for Evaluating Coverage in Machine Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27543

**作者：** Ziluowen Luo、Jun Yin、Songyang Liu、Ji Qi、Chuang Hu、Kyrie You Wu、Chaozhuo Li、Senzhang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27543v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察机器遗忘评估中的覆盖度与可观测性限制：用502个重训模型做压力测试，发现隐藏嵌入能揭示预测层面漏掉的删除；给出线性评分区分n个单例删除所需共享表示秩的Ω(log n)下界，并提出结合颜色-形状基准、留一重训 oracle 与SAE激活扩展的CEO评估框架。

**相关性简评：** 「只看输出会漏掉内部状态变化」以及用SAE编码把可观测表示扩展到高维激活空间的做法，可供核查LVR中latent状态是否保留视觉证据、答案是否真正使用latent内容，但任务与视觉推理距离较远，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Limits of Observability for Evaluating Coverage in Machine Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of any requested subset of training data while preserving utility on the remaining data. Empirical evaluations measure predictive accuracy and infer training-set membership (MIAs) from model outputs or intermediate activations. However, by focusing on selected forget sets, these evaluations can overlook Coverage, the range of requests a method can fulfill. To assess this coverage, we first conduct a stress test of observability with 502 retrained models, finding that hidden embeddings reveal known deletions missed in predictions. To characterize capacity limits on coverage, we show that distinguishing all $n$ singleton deletions with linear scores can require a shared representation of rank $Ω(\\log n)$. We further extend the bound to arbitrary subsets and show when partial deletion can appear complete. Building on this analysis, we propose CEO, a framework for evaluating unlearning coverage under limited observability. Its compositional color-shape benchmark makes forgetting and retained utility measurable from the full output distribution and provides a leave-one-out retraining oracle for each target. From a fixed number of oracle observables, we estimate empirical target coverage, the fraction of targets reliably flagged when deleted and rarely flagged under any other deletions. To recover missed deletions, CEO further expands each observable representation into high-dimensional activation space with the frozen encoder of a SAE trained on the original model. Extensive experiments of 20 baselines shows that even on this simple synthetic task, only retraining from scratch covers all deletion targets.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [GameScrub: Learning HUD Removal from Real Gameplay with Recovered Targets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27547v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27547

**作者：** Mohammad Areeb Qazi、Yiyan Hu、Yanan Wang、Xuezhi Liang、Xin Li、Fan Yang、Xin Gao、Guangyi Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27547v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27547v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GameScrub：用预训练视频扩散模型对真实游戏录像中的 HUD 区域做修复以恢复伪干净目标，从而构造成对数据，训练无需推理期掩码或参考帧的 HUD 去除模型，并在 Game2World 基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 论文指出 latent 视频自编码器会跨掩码边界混合信息，并用预填充背景估计与像素空间合成限制污染范围；这一观察可供核查 LVR 中无关视觉内容如何泄漏进 latent 状态、以及视觉证据筛选与表示隔离问题，但论文本身不涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GameScrub: Learning HUD Removal from Real Gameplay with Recovered Targets

**abstract/A1：** Gameplay videos are a rich source of experience for learning virtual-world dynamics, yet their frames contain heads-up displays (HUDs) that follow interface rules rather than scene dynamics. Removing HUDs would make this footage directly usable for learning world dynamics. However, training a removal model requires paired clips with and without HUDs, whereas real recordings contain only HUD-overlaid footage. Existing work synthesizes pairs by compositing HUD graphics onto HUD-free footage, requiring a separately curated clean corpus and producing HUDs that only approximate real ones. Models trained on such pairs leave 40 to 50% of real HUD elements untouched. We instead recover pseudo-clean targets directly from real recordings by inpainting HUD regions with a pretrained video diffusion model. Preventing HUD traces from leaking into recovered targets is challenging because latent video autoencoders mix information across mask boundaries. Our training-free recovery pipeline, GameScrub-Data, pre-fills HUD regions with flow-guided background estimates before encoding, excludes HUDs from scene conditioning, and confines restoration to masked regions through pixel-space compositing. The resulting pairs train GameScrub, a video diffusion removal model that handles diverse HUDs without inference-time masks or clean reference images. On the in-the-wild Game2World benchmark, GameScrub achieves an artifact-adjusted removal score of 71.95, compared with 57.86 for the strongest reference-free GameCleaner configuration, and removes 90.06% of annotated HUD elements versus 59.07%. These results demonstrate that real recordings with recovered targets can support effective HUD removal, advancing HUD-free gameplay data for world models and controllable video generation.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Altair-Nidhogg: Which Model-Merging Theories Hold for a One-Pass Decision Encoder? Soups, Task Arithmetic, TIES and DARE Against Joint Training and Prediction Ensembling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27550v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27550

**作者：** Mohammed Nayeem Khan、Navin Kumar R、Akhil P

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27550v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27550v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在小型一次性决策编码器上系统检验权重平均、task arithmetic、TIES、DARE 等模型合并配方能否保留各专精模型的技能增益，并与联合训练和预测集成对照，同时检验各配方背后的理论预测是否成立。

**相关性简评：** 其「用权重合并组合专精能力、以增益保留比例和分布偏移量化代价」的实证框架，可能可供核查多技能 VLM 推理模块（如 THINK/LOOK/STOP 专精适配器）合并后的能力坍缩问题；但任务为纯文本多选题且无 latent 状态，与主线仅间接相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Altair-Nidhogg: Which Model-Merging Theories Hold for a One-Pass Decision Encoder? Soups, Task Arithmetic, TIES and DARE Against Joint Training and Prediction Ensembling

**abstract/A1：** Laya is a small model that answers a multiple-choice question in one pass: each option has a name and a written description. A new skill costs a training run, so we test whether published recipes for merging fine-tuned models (weight averaging, task arithmetic, TIES, DARE) work for it, and whether the theories behind them hold. All 156 models start from one released checkpoint: four plain fine-tunes, five specialists (each adds one kind of training row), joint fine-tunes on all rows, and prediction ensembles. Items are held-out, synthetic and LLM-labelled. In-family, each skill is scored on new items of its own kind, and "kept" is the share of a specialist's gain over the plain fine-tune. Averaging four specialists keeps 52% \[38, 64\] with +1.7 points of general accuracy; adding the full vectors keeps 42% \[3, 63\] with -9.0; TIES, which trims and resolves sign conflicts, keeps 64% \[50, 76\] with +1.5; joint fine-tunes, which see all the rows, keep 120% \[104, 162\] and 116% \[104, 149\]. DARE does not repair the full sum (-0.4 points), and ensembles of the models' predictions keep 26% to 54% depending on how they are combined. Swapping the seed of one specialist moves the average by up to 8 points (23 for the full sum). Of 10 predictions fixed in advance, 1 held, 1 held partly, 7 failed and 1 could not be tested. Out-of-family (benchmark questions, code-mixed tickets), the four-way average, joint models and ensemble stay within 2.6 points of the plain fine-tune, but full-vector sums lose up to 7.7. Labels are LLM-made, data synthetic, one base model.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 23 figures, 23 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Vega-Fafnir: Is Greedy Decoding the Same Decoding? Where Rounding Noise in Speculative Verification Comes From and What Removing It Costs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27554v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27554

**作者：** Mohammed Nayeem Khan、Navin Kumar R

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27554v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27554v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 bfloat16 小模型上统计同一贪心解码在缓存、padding、批处理与多 token 投机验证下产生不同文本的频率，并将首处分歧归于 logit 间隔极小的舍入噪声，进而比较 margin gate、单 token 重建缓存与定宽解码的一致性收益和额外计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「多 token 验证与缓存写入本身引入噪声」及 margin gate、缓存重建的取舍，可供核查 LVR 中并行验证与缓存复用是否会让「验证通过」不等于状态等价，以及验证成本与停止判据的设计线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Vega-Fafnir: Is Greedy Decoding the Same Decoding? Where Rounding Noise in Speculative Verification Comes From and What Removing It Costs

**abstract/A1：** Serving engines treat the ways of running a greedy language model as interchangeable: cached or not, padded or not, alone or batched, one token or several verified at once (speculative decoding). In finite precision they round differently. We measure how often the greedy text of Qwen3-0.6B (bfloat16, 128 new tokens, GSM8K and ARC prompts) differs from a reference that decodes one token per pass. Verifying 4 guessed tokens per pass changes 60% of 150 prompts and padding changes 68%; every first divergence has a top-1 minus top-2 logit gap of 0.375 or less. A split of the noise shows that cache entries written by multi-token passes are about as noisy as the multi-token pass itself. A margin gate (fall back to one token when the gap is below a bound calibrated on separate GSM8K prompts) whose fallback reads that cache does not help: 58 of 100 prompts differ, against 61 of 100 for plain verification. Rebuilding the cache with one-token passes leaves 0 of 100 different but costs 7.2 model passes per token, against 1.0 for the reference. Fixed-width decoding makes drafted and plain decoding identical (0 of 100 prompts differ) at 0.75 passes per token. On shifted MMLU and HellaSwag prompts the rebuild gate leaves 0 of 60 different, plain verification 48 of 60. On 138 further and shifted prompts the bound was exceeded at 0.6% of positions and missed 0 of 309 flips. One GPU, one drafter, small models.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 26 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [WEIR: Watershed Exploration and Drift-Aware Prefix Shrinkage for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27570

**作者：** Pixel Nomand、Elena Voss、Marcus Hale、Sofia Reyes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27570v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WEIR，一个面向多轮语言 agent 的 actor-critic 强化学习框架：维护跨迭代的规范动作前缀树，用漂移感知的收缩目标训练前缀条件 critic，并把 rollout 预算按不确定性分层并集中到价值陡崖处的「分水岭」分支；在多跳 QA、ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其前缀条件价值估计与把采样集中到高不确定分叉的机制，可能为 LVR 中的贡献分配、动态计算与停止决策提供线索，但该工作面向纯文本 agent 且无视觉状态表示，迁移到视觉证据筛选可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WEIR: Watershed Exploration and Drift-Aware Prefix Shrinkage for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multi-turn language model agents often receive only a single binary reward at the end of a long interaction trajectory, making it difficult to separate decisive decisions from routine steps or estimate accurate intermediate values. Prefix-tree methods improve sample efficiency by merging trajectories that share action histories, yet two structural flaws remain: naive pooling mixes stale off-policy outcomes into critic targets with fixed weights, and uniform or entropy-driven branching wastes continuations on dead-end states whose outcome is already settled. We introduce WEIR (Watershed Exploration and Intrinsic-Drift Shrinkage), an actor-critic reinforcement learning framework grounded in a Doob turn-variance conservation identity. WEIR maintains a cross-iteration canonical action-prefix tree with exponentially discounted visit counts and trains a prefix-conditioned critic toward a minimax drift-aware shrinkage target that adapts from single-trajectory returns at rare nodes to pooled empirical means at dense nodes. Using the same pre-update critic, WEIR allocates rollout budgets across tasks via Neyman stratification $B\_q \\propto \\sqrt{\\hat{p}\_q(1-\\hat{p}\_q)}$ and directs within-task branches to watershed forks where high survival-headroom uncertainty coincides with steep value cliffs. Across four multi-hop QA benchmarks, ALFWorld, and WebShop on Qwen2.5-3B/7B-Instruct, WEIR achieves $32.18\\%$ and $37.85\\%$ average QA exact match and $84.83\\%$ and $87.81\\%$ ALFWorld success rates, outperforming CAPO by $1.50$--$3.74$ points while generating $28$--$29\\%$ fewer rollout tokens than chain sampling.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [PRISM: Validation-Free Online Batch Selection for LLM Fine-Tuning via Preconditioned Conic Ghost Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27578v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27578

**作者：** Lin Tang、Wei Zhang、Jing Li、Hongyu Chen、Ming Zhao、Yuxuan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27578v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27578v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PRISM 提出无需验证集的在线批次选择：在预条件二阶矩度量下对齐候选梯度，将子集构造写为射线锥上的设施选址子模问题，借助分解的 ghost 内积避免逐样本梯度物化，并给出 (1-1/e) 贪心近似与非凸收敛界，在 1.5B–8B 规模 LLM 微调上优于全批与欧氏设施选址等基线。

**相关性简评：** 其预条件度量下的子模设施选址与闭式射线投影权重（自动压低正交或噪声样本），可能可供核查用于视觉证据或候选 latent 假设的筛选与加权；但论文面向通用 LLM 微调数据选择，未涉及多模态推理状态，联系属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRISM: Validation-Free Online Batch Selection for LLM Fine-Tuning via Preconditioned Conic Ghost Matching

**abstract/A1：** Online batch selection lowers the cost of fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) by replacing each candidate batch with a smaller informative subset. Existing selectors typically depend on a held-out validation or proxy set, or else rely on Euclidean facility-location objectives over unpreconditioned gradients whose diameter-shifted similarities are distorted by heavy-tailed gradient norms and cross-layer anisotropy. We introduce PRISM (Preconditioned Ray-Isotropic Submodular Matching), a validation-free online batch selector that aligns candidate gradients in a factored second-moment metric and casts subset construction as conic facility location over positive ray projections. Computing the required pairwise geometry through factored preconditioned ghost inner products avoids materializing per-example parameter gradients, while the conic objective remains normalized, nonnegative, and monotone submodular---guaranteeing a $(1-1/e)$ greedy approximation whose additive error scales with total batch gradient energy rather than the worst-case outlier diameter. Assigning each candidate to its best-aligned selected ray induces closed-form radial projection weights that automatically shrink orthogonal or noisy examples toward zero. We derive a nonconvex convergence bound showing that optimization error is governed directly by the conic representation cost. Across four LLM fine-tuning configurations spanning 1.5B to 8B parameters, PRISM outperforms full-batch training, Euclidean facility location, and zeroth-order selectors, and rivals or surpasses validation-guided baselines without held-out data.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Leakage-Free Is Not Confounder-Free: What a Patient-Level Split Cannot Certify About a Medical Imaging Benchmark](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27585v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27585

**作者：** Ignazio Gallo、Mattia Gatti、Leonardo Sgroi、Darwin Castillo、Yuliana Jiménez-Gaona、Nicola Landro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27585v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27585v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出五问审计流程来检验医学影像基准中的候选混杂因素（如采集日期/年龄），并在 DMR-IR 乳腺热成像数据上按患者级划分验证：采集日期单独预测恶性的 AUROC 高于所有影像分支，且控制该变量后判别力崩塌或无法归因。

**相关性简评：** 其「该变量能否从图像中解码、模型分数是否依赖它、控制后还剩多少判别力」的审计思路，可能可供核查 LVR/VLM 推理中 latent 状态是否真正承载视觉证据、而非依赖数据集捷径或视觉旁路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Leakage-Free Is Not Confounder-Free: What a Patient-Level Split Cannot Certify About a Medical Imaging Benchmark

**abstract/A1：** Patient-level splitting is the standard safeguard against subject leakage in medical imaging evaluation, but it cannot remove a confounder that every fold inherits: an acquisition variable aligned with the diagnosis across the whole cohort. We present a five-question audit of a candidate confounder: how well it predicts the diagnosis, whether it can be decoded from the images, whether diagnostic scores depend on it, whether the image adds predictive value beyond it and how much discrimination survives once it is controlled. We apply it to DMR-IR, the public dataset on which most machine-learning results in breast thermography rest. On 236 patients under a strict patient-disjoint protocol, the acquisition date alone predicts malignancy at AUROC 0.980, above the best of nine imaging arms (0.949), because benign and malignant patients were recruited in different years. The acquisition year is decodable from the pixels among patients who share a diagnosis, largely through a global thermal offset outside the breast, yet only one arm, the one pooling every frame of a visit, demonstrably carries it into its diagnostic score. No arm improves on the date on paired folds, and once the date is controlled, discrimination either collapses or survives only on comparisons the benchmark cannot attribute. The dataset contains only eight cross-label same-date pairs, from ten patients, which limits reliable evaluation within recorded acquisition-date groups. A second, independently acquired cohort, whose dominant channel is patient age, returns the same audit outcome from a different confounder. Mitigation may suppress such a confounder, but without cross-label acquisition support no evaluation on the benchmark can certify that what remains is pathology.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 6 figures, 18 tables. Code, configurations and aggregate results: https://github.com/ignaziogallo/breast\_thermography\_reliable\_ai

</details>
