### 130. [Quantum-Informed Regularized Poisson Learning for Graph-Based Semi-Supervised Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27609v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27609

**作者：** Hamed Gholipour、Farid Bozorgnia、Hamzeh Mohammadigheymasi、Joao Neves、Moharram Challenger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、quant-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27609v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27609v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 IPQSSL：对局部图特征做 QR 分解并抬升为单位编码，经共享变分量子电路测量得到节点级对角权重，用于稳定对称化 Poisson 系统，同时给出该算子的正定条件与 Hermitian、块编码兼容形式，并在七个含表格数据与引文网络的基准上考察正则化效果的依赖条件。

**相关性简评：** 其「用可训练测量生成节点对角权重以稳定图上的线性传播系统」可供核查是否能迁移为视觉 token 图或证据图上的 latent 状态传播、正则化与数值稳定性设计，但该文本身与视觉多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantum-Informed Regularized Poisson Learning for Graph-Based Semi-Supervised Classification

**abstract/A1：** We introduce IPQSSL, a quantum-informed regularized Poisson framework for graph-based semi-supervised classification under scarce labels. Local graph feature contexts are QR factorized, lifted to unitary encodings, and processed by a shared variational circuit whose measurements yield node wise diagonal weights that stabilize a symmetrized Poisson system. We establish positive definiteness of the symmetrized, regularized operator under explicit spectral conditions and derive a Hermitian, block encoding compatible formulation that separates the classical NISQ oriented implementation from a future fault-tolerant pathway. Across seven benchmarks spanning tabular data and a citation network, the effect of regularization is dataset- and budget-dependent: IPQSSL improves over the Poisson baselines on Iris and Wine at all but the single-label budget, reaching 0.946 accuracy on Iris at ten labels per class and at every budget on German Credit, where, however, all methods remain below the majority class rate. Original Poisson remains preferable on Breast Cancer, Heart Disease, Optdigits, and Cora, with the deficit narrowing as the label budget grows on the two largest graphs. Ablations attribute the gains to diagonal stabilization rather than variational training. The result is a resource transparent bridge between Poisson graph learning and quantum linear algebra, with an explicit empirical delineation of when diagonal regularization helps.

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [Sketch2TikZ: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Converting Hand-Drawn Sketches to Executable TikZ](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27620v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27620

**作者：** Shubham Paliwal、Arushi Jain、Monika Sharma、Manasi Patwardhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27620v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27620v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Sketch2TikZ，用多奖励强化学习将手绘草图转成可编译 TikZ 代码，组合代码结构、OCR、渲染图像与自相似度奖励，并研究课程式奖励调度对训练稳定性的作用。

**相关性简评：** 任务本身是视觉到代码的生成，与 latent 状态和推理决策无直接关系；但多奖励课程调度、奖励干扰与代理指标过优化的分析，可能可供核查 LVR/VLM 训练中视觉保真与结构约束如何分配监督信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sketch2TikZ: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Converting Hand-Drawn Sketches to Executable TikZ

**abstract/A1：** Automatic digitization of hand-drawn sketches into editable formats is crucial for modern document workflows and LaTeX integration. Converting sketches into executable TikZ code provides a structured representation of diagrams, but remains challenging due to noisy and ambiguous inputs, which hinder reliable interpretation and limit the effectiveness of token-level supervision for ensuring global diagram validity. To address these challenges, we propose Sketch2TikZ, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes sketch-to-code generation via a sequential multi-reward strategy, combining Code-Structure ($\\mathcal{R}\_{cs}$), OCR ($\\mathcal{R}\_{ocr}$), CleanDIFT ($\\mathcal{R}\_{cd}$), and Self-Similarity ($\\mathcal{R}\_{ss}$) rewards to enforce textual, visual and structural correctness. We evaluate Sketch2TikZ on the SkeTikZ and FlowChart-3B benchmarks using metrics including compilation success rate (CSR), image similarity, code similarity, and character similarity. Our method achieves near-perfect compilation success and improves visual and textual alignment over strong open-source baselines, while remaining competitive with proprietary GPT-based vision-language models. Extensive ablations demonstrate that curriculum-based reward scheduling is critical for reducing reward interference, stabilizing training and mitigating proxy over-optimization. Overall, our results highlight the effectiveness of reference-aware, multi-objective reinforcement learning for structured diagram generation from noisy sketches.

**comment/C1：** Accepted Main Track at BMVC 2026

</details>

### 132. [Automating Multi-View Quality Assessment of 3D Annotation Layouts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27621v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27621

**作者：** Hongyi Yang、Jinao Wu、Yanwei Zhang、Shengwen Fu、Haibin Ling、Bingyao Huang、Jingwei Qu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27621v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27621v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向 3D 标注布局质量评估，构建含 5 视角与 5 个质量维度人工评分的 ALQA 数据集，并用结构化监督微调小型 MLLM，将逐视角、逐维度打分聚合为视角级与样本级质量分数。

**相关性简评：** 其「逐视角、逐维度评分再聚合成整体判断」的结构化监督与多视角证据整合方式，可能为 LVR 中多视角视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供可供核查的线索，但任务本身并非推理机制研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Automating Multi-View Quality Assessment of 3D Annotation Layouts

**abstract/A1：** Text labels provide descriptive information about elements in visual scenes. In 3D visualization, the arrangement of these labels and their connections to target locations forms an annotation layout. Assessing layout quality supports comparisons between alternative layouts. However, existing quality measures often focus on specific layout properties and may not fully reflect human judgments of overall quality. Moreover, a layout may appear clear from one viewpoint but suffer from occlusion or clutter from another. To address these issues, we propose a multi-view quality assessment method based on a multimodal large language model (MLLM). We fine-tune a compact MLLM using structured supervision from human ratings to predict a rating for each quality dimension in each view. These ratings are aggregated into view-level and sample-level quality scores. To support training and evaluation, we construct the Annotation Layouts with Quality Assessment (ALQA) dataset, containing 3,216 layout samples from 1,072 3D objects across 13 categories. Each layout is rendered from 5 predefined viewpoints, and each view is independently rated by 3 human raters across 5 quality dimensions. On ALQA, our method agrees more closely with human reference scores than the evaluated MLLM baselines, and a user study demonstrates strong agreement with human preferences in layout ranking.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 133. [Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Subchunk Rollouts in Latent World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27645v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27645

**作者：** Nan Tang、Han-Tao Zhang、Jing-Cheng Pang、Hao-Nan Wang、Hao-Xiang Ren、Yi-Fei Sheng、Jun-Yin Ye、Tian Xu、Fan-Ming Luo、Hao-Xin Lin、Yang Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27645v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27645v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LS-MBPO，用轻量 latent world model 对预训练 VLA 策略做残差式基于模型的强化学习：以 Hierarchical Forcing 结合密集因果监督与自回归反馈抑制递推预测误差累积，并通过 latent subchunk rollouts 与渐进起始点的课程从短想象轨迹中学习。

**相关性简评：** 机器人动作策略并非 LVR 主线，但其对 latent 递推中误差累积的密集监督与残差修正、以及利用短想象片段训练的做法，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的训练监督与状态更新提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Subchunk Rollouts in Latent World Models

**abstract/A1：** Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can improve pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies through imagined experiences. However, existing VLA-MBRL methods primarily rely on large-scale image or video generation world models, incurring large computational overhead. Latent world models offer a more efficient alternative but face two challenges: compounding errors during recursive prediction and incompatibility with VLAs' visual input interfaces. In this work, we propose LS-MBPO, a lightweight residual MBRL framework that leverages latent world models to improve upon pretrained VLAs. LS-MBPO introduces two key techniques: (1) Hierarchical Forcing, which combines dense causal supervision with autoregressive feedback to mitigate compounding prediction errors; and (2) Latent Subchunk Rollouts, which leverage action chunks from a frozen VLA policy and a latent residual policy for successive subchunk refinement, bridging the gap between latent imagination and VLA policy execution. We further introduce a Progress-Conditioned Branch-Start Curriculum that progressively shifts rollout starting points from later to earlier task stages, enabling effective policy learning from short imagined rollouts. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and a real-world ALOHA robot show that LS-MBPO achieves superior task performance and training efficiency compared to existing methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Late-Time Dynamics of Flow Matching: Distance Geometry and Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27653v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27653

**作者：** Umberto Biccari、Junqing Huang、Kang Liu、Enrique Zuazua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27653v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27653v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析有限训练集下 flow matching 的晚期动力学，证明轨迹向最近样本指数收缩，并据此提出距离势的值/梯度监督、沿收缩路径拟合速度两种学习方式，以及分离初始化偏差、速度误差、收缩不完全与端点分配的误差估计。

**相关性简评：** 其「向最近样本收缩」的状态轨迹几何与误差分解，可供核查 LVR 循环/生成式推理状态的收敛、停止决策与训练监督设计，但原文并非视觉或多模态推理工作，迁移线索需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Late-Time Dynamics of Flow Matching: Distance Geometry and Learning

**abstract/A1：** Generative models transform simple random inputs into samples that resemble observed data. For a finite training set, a central question is how its geometry shapes the terminal dynamics and the probabilities assigned to individual samples. We address this question for flow matching with linear interpolation between independent Gaussian and empirical samples. The squared distance to the training set generates explicit paths contracting exponentially toward the nearest sample. We prove that exact trajectories restarted at prescribed late-time states approximate these paths uniformly over all elapsed times and compact sets of starting states away from Voronoi interfaces, with an error exponentially small in the inverse squared restart noise scale. The initial mass near interfaces controls the quadratic Wasserstein error between the exact and reference laws. For Gaussian and fixed Gaussian-tube restart laws, this gives a square-root rate in the restart noise scale, uniform over time, which a two-site example shows to be sharp in general. At ties, the Clarke subdifferential characterizes the graphical limit of the rescaled velocity field. This geometry motivates two learning methods: fitting the distance potential with value and gradient supervision, and fitting velocities along explicit contraction paths. A transport estimate separates initialization mismatch, velocity error, incomplete contraction, and endpoint allocation. MNIST experiments illustrate the distinct roles of support proximity, nearest-site retention, and feature fidelity. These results connect flow matching and score-based diffusion through a common limiting geometry and show how asymptotic analysis can inform the design and evaluation of generative models.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [Beyond Sample Hardness: Adaptive Data Selection through Learning Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27656v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27656

**作者：** Hai Gan、Suorong Yang、Guang Li、Baile Xu、Furao Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27656v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27656v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LeDAS 数据选择框架：用 per-sample loss 的 EMA 提取硬度、学习进展与退化三种信号，再以 contextual bandit 自适应融合权重，并加入有界陈旧补偿，在视觉与语言任务上减少训练数据。

**相关性简评：** 其按损失动态为样本分配训练优先级的机制，可能可供核查 latent 推理轨迹或监督数据的筛选与贡献分配问题；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理与 latent 状态，迁移线索需人工验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Sample Hardness: Adaptive Data Selection through Learning Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Data selection has emerged as an effective approach to reducing the model training costs. However, most existing methods often rely heavily on sample hardness, which may overlook differences in sample learning dynamics and lead to suboptimal sample selection. In this paper, we propose LeDAS, an end-to-end Learning Dynamics-Aware Selection framework for dynamic data selection, which jointly characterizes sample importance through hardness, learning progress, and deterioration, enabling adaptive sample prioritization throughout training with marginal additional computational overhead. Specifically, we track per-sample loss dynamics using exponential moving averages (EMA) to obtain three complementary signals that capture current learning difficulty, ongoing learning improvements, and potential degradation. To accommodate evolving training dynamics, we introduce Adaptive Metric Fusion, a contextual bandit-based mechanism that dynamically adjusts the relative importance of these signals based on training feedback. Furthermore, a bounded staleness compensation mechanism accounts for historical selection patterns by increasing the selection priority of samples that have not been selected for extended periods. Extensive experiments across diverse vision and language tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of LeDAS. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, LeDAS consistently achieves superior performance, matching or surpassing full-dataset training at a 50\\% selection ratio on ImageNet-1K and in LLM supervised fine-tuning. Moreover, its modular and task-agnostic design enables the integration of complementary selection signals, demonstrating strong robustness to noisy and corrupted datasets.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [GeoScaffold: From Latent to Realized Geometry in Collaborative Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27667v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27667

**作者：** Kang Yang、Yance Fang、Gaochen Cui、Xiaoqiang Jin、Deying Li、Yongcai Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27667v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27667v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断协同3D检测中融合BEV特征虽蕴含丰富几何线索、但继承自单智能体的读出头（anchor打分、框解码、NMS）未能充分利用的问题，并提出仅在训练期加入的前景掩码辅助目标，在每个正锚点提供密集空间监督，推理时移除该辅助头。

**相关性简评：** 其核心线索是把「latent 几何未被读出使用」归因于监督只要求区分前景/背景，并用训练期辅助稠密监督迫使内部表示质量进入打分排序，可能可供核查 LVR 中答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容及监督分配问题；不过该工作属3D协同检测，与 VLM 推理距离较远，迁移价值需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoScaffold: From Latent to Realized Geometry in Collaborative Detection

**abstract/A1：** Most collaborative 3D detectors fuse bird's-eye-view (BEV) features from connected vehicles and roadside infrastructure to aggregate complementary observations. Although modern communication, alignment, and fusion pipelines encode rich latent geometry in the fused BEV features, the standard readout pipeline inherited from single-agent detectors, i.e., anchor scoring, box decoding, and non-maximum suppression (NMS), does not fully exploit these geometric cues. We empirically identify and quantify this gap on UrbanIng-V2X. Collaboration improves localization at about two-thirds of positive anchors, yet when NMS resolves two overlapping collaborative boxes of one object, it keeps the better-localized box less than half of the time. A key cause is a supervision mismatch: classification scores are trained only for foreground-background discrimination, so nothing in training requires them to rank boxes by localization quality. To address this limitation, we introduce GeoScaffold, a lightweight training-only auxiliary objective. It predicts a foreground mask at each positive anchor, i.e., the cells of a grid over the anchor that lie inside the ground-truth box, to supply the dense spatial supervision that the seven-dimensional box code leaves implicit. Its loss, added to the detection loss, updates the encoder and fusion modules through the fused BEV and trains the detector toward a better-localized box at every positive anchor, including the top-scored one. The auxiliary head is removed at inference, preserving the original detector architecture, communication logic, and inference cost. GeoScaffold raises AP@0.5 and AP@0.7 by 0.98 and 0.55 points on average across all 11 fusion methods on the UrbanIng-V2X validation split, with gains extending to most methods on an unseen intersection and to all ten methods that produce detections on V2V4Real.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 11 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [CanopyFormer: Attention-Guided SegFormer for Explainable Forest Cover Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27674v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27674

**作者：** Dhinanjaya Fernando、Dinura Ginige、Kalana Lakshan、Chanupa Gurusinghe、Lasana Pahanga、Dileesha Manamperi、Thimira Sahan、Sandareka Wickramanayake

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27674v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27674v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CanopyFormer，在 SegFormer-B0 上用 Attention Consistency Loss 将注意力与真实森林掩码对齐，并给出 AAMO 对齐度量、限定于最后一层编码器的注意力 rollout 及删除测试；对齐显著提升但 Dice 略降，且对齐改善不等于因果忠实性改善。

**相关性简评：** 其删除测试（移除最受关注 patch 与随机 patch 对比）与「注意力—掩码一致性」度量，可供核查如何检验 VLM 中被关注的视觉证据是否真正因果影响输出；但这属分割任务的注意力监督，非推理期 latent 状态研究，迁移需自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CanopyFormer: Attention-Guided SegFormer for Explainable Forest Cover Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** In transformer segmentation models, attention is usually visualized after training rather than supervised. Although attention supervision has been explored for natural images and driving scenes, aerial forest segmentation typically uses attention only as an architectural component and evaluates interpretability qualitatively. We present CanopyFormer, an attention-guided SegFormer-B0 that uses an Attention Consistency Loss to align attention with the ground-truth forest mask, encouraging attention to focus on canopy regions. Since SegFormer uses spatial-reduction attention, we adapt Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout by restricting it to the final encoder stage, where the attention matrix is square. We introduce Average Attention-Mask Overlap (AAMO) to measure attention alignment and incorporate a deletion test to evaluate faithfulness. On 5108 images, our method increases AAMO from 0.0334 to 0.7476, approximately 22 times the vanilla SegFormer-B0 result, with a modest decrease in Dice score from 0.8743 to 0.8577. The deletion test reveals that improved alignment does not necessarily translate into improved faithfulness: removing the most-attended 25% of patches reduces the forest score by 12.1%, compared with 33.0% when removing random patches. Attention supervision introduces no additional inference cost, although double backpropagation makes each training step 2.6 to 2.8 times slower. The attention alignment improvement also generalizes to a second dataset, LoveDA. Additionally, a Boundary Refinement Module provides no consistent Dice improvement and reduces AAMO, while mean squared error substantially outperforms Kullback-Leibler divergence as the attention loss.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 138. [Abstract What Varies, Reuse Longer Patterns: Efficient Offline Chain-of-Thought Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27710v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27710

**作者：** Shibo Wang、Yuzhe He、Yifeng Zhao、Yunjiao Zhou、Zepeng Ning、Hanzhang Zhou、Kezhi Mao、Junlang Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27710v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27710v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CRAFT，把具体题目细节抽象为槽位，识别部分词化的表达模板并聚类措辞变体，对每个族代表只压缩一次后复用删除或改写编辑，从而用更低压缩预算生成更短、仍为普通文本的 CoT 监督。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其「抽象细节、复用共享编辑模板」的思路可能为 LVR 中缩短推理轨迹监督、复用跨样本共享推理结构提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Abstract What Varies, Reuse Longer Patterns: Efficient Offline Chain-of-Thought Compression

**abstract/A1：** Offline chain-of-thought (CoT) compression creates shorter supervision for efficient reasoning, but per-example compressors repeatedly process similar expressions across traces, wasting compressor budget. Methods that reuse identical text across examples miss longer shared expressions when names, numbers, or formulas change from one question to another. We introduce CRAFT, which abstracts problem-specific details into slots to uncover partially lexicalized expression templates (PLETs) and clusters wording variants into families. Compressing each family representative once yields shared deletion or rewriting edits, which are applied with each occurrence's own details to construct compact, ordinary-text training targets. Compared with per-example rewriting, CRAFT Rewrite reduces weighted compressor budgets by a median 90.7% with a median accuracy loss of 1.21 percentage points, while generating shorter reasoning. Against cross-sample encoding, it shortens reasoning across all eight model-dataset settings by 25.3% on average and improves accuracy in five.

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [VeilGS: Solving Darkness as a Veil of 2D Basis Maps over Retinex 3DGS](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27719v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27719

**作者：** Mohab Kishawy、Chengzhou Tang、Shahram Shirani、Jun Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27719v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27719v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VeilGS，一种低光照新视角合成的 3D Gaussian Splatting 方法：把暗化视为覆盖在清晰场景上的「veil」，每个 Gaussian 用 albedo 与 shading latent 取代球谐以分离表面颜色与光照，并由小网络预测空间变化增益的粗 basis、逐视图求解系数而非学习，渲染时去掉 veil 即得恢复场景。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测 basis、逐输入求解系数并让梯度穿过求解」的机制旨在防止表示吸收可由外部模型解释的成分，可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节被 nuisance 吸收与 latent 信息坍缩的正则化思路；论文本身面向 3D 重建，与视觉推理无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VeilGS: Solving Darkness as a Veil of 2D Basis Maps over Retinex 3DGS

**abstract/A1：** In a dark capture, the darkness is not uniform. How much light reaches a surface depends on where it lies relative to the few light sources, and the camera compresses what remains with a tone curve. Most low-light 3D reconstruction methods either apply one curve to every pixel of a view, so a dark object beside a lamp and a bright one in an unlit corner receive the same correction, or store the darkness in the 3D representation, where it can absorb scene detail. We present VeilGS, a 3D Gaussian Splatting method for low-light novel view synthesis that treats the degradation as a veil drawn over a clear scene. Each Gaussian carries an albedo and a shading latent instead of spherical harmonics, separating surface colour from illumination. Each view is darkened by a spatially varying gain followed by a global tone curve, and rendering without this veil gives the restored scene. A small network predicts a coarse basis for the gain from the observation, and its coefficients are solved for each view rather than learned. Training through this solve discourages the Gaussians from absorbing what the veil can explain. VeilGS needs no paired normal-light supervision and stores no per-view parameters. On Gaussian-DK, its restored renders score best on no-reference NIQE and CLIP-IQA and are rated highest in a blind user study with 55 participants. On LOM, it achieves results competitive with the state of the art.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://errorlogic1211.github.io/VeilGS/

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [Beyond Math, Code, and Logic: Do Reasoning Models Transfer to Scientific Argumentation?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27722

**作者：** Buse Sibel Korkmaz、Antonio del Rio Chanona

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27722v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统评测语言模型在七项科学论证任务（论点挖掘、反驳生成、篇章级推理）上的表现，发现前沿推理模型仍明显不足，并提出显式搭建论证推理过程的训练框架以提升小模型能力并泛化到未见对话场景。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本科学论证任务，不涉及视觉状态或 latent 生成；其「显式推理脚手架训练＋跨场景迁移评测」可供核查 LVR 的轨迹监督与推理迁移评价设计，可能有借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Math, Code, and Logic: Do Reasoning Models Transfer to Scientific Argumentation?

**abstract/A1：** Scientific discourse depends on argumentative reasoning: identifying claims, evaluating evidence, and constructing coherent responses. While recent advances in reasoning-capable language models have demonstrated strong performance on mathematical, coding, and logical benchmarks, their ability to engage in scientific argumentation remains unclear. We present the first systematic evaluation of language models across seven tasks spanning argument mining, rebuttal generation, and discourse-level reasoning using research papers, peer reviews, and grant proposals. Our study reveals that even frontier models with strong general reasoning skills struggle with domain-specific argumentative tasks, highlighting a fundamental capability gap. To address this, we introduce a training framework that explicitly scaffolds the argumentative reasoning process in language models, substantially improving their competence in scientific discourse. The resulting compact models approach or exceed the performance of much larger proprietary systems and generalize to unseen conversational settings, demonstrating reasoning transfer beyond task-specific supervision. These findings underscore that effective scientific argumentation is not an emergent property of scale, but requires explicit reasoning-aware training, and they point toward practical pathways for building AI systems that can contribute meaningfully to scientific discourse.

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [Average-Depth Variable Execution via Request-Local Token Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27738v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27738

**作者：** Pengjie Zheng、Mengwei Xie、Huan Yang、Si Wu、Pengfei Hu、Feng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27738v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27738v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AVERT：在每层由路由器读取当前 token 状态与该请求预算，决定是否执行 Transformer 块，被跳过的 token 经轻量残差适配器且不写 K/V，从而把每个请求的平均执行深度对齐到连续预算，并允许同一批次混合不同预算。

**相关性简评：** 其「预算条件 + 逐 token 路由决定层执行」与「跳过层即不写 K/V」的机制，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 步数/计算量的按需分配，以及跳过对缓存与注意力结构的影响；但论文本身面向推理效率而非推理状态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Average-Depth Variable Execution via Request-Local Token Routing

**abstract/A1：** Layer skipping can reduce large language model inference costs, but a request budget does not specify how to allocate layer execution across generated tokens. We introduce \\method{}, which allows tokens to execute different numbers of layers while matching each request's mean executed depth to a continuous budget. At each routed layer, a router reads the current token state and its request's budget and decides whether to execute the Transformer block. Tokens that skip the block pass through a lightweight residual adapter and write no key/value (K/V) entry at that layer. A single checkpoint serves requests with different budgets in the same batch. Across three LLaMA backbones, six quality metrics, and 13 budgets, AVERT is best or tied for best in 206 of 234 comparisons. Across single- and mixed-budget batches, the mean absolute per-request budget error is at most 1.15 percentage points. When grouping requests by budget leaves batches underfilled, mixing budgets reduces decode time per output token (TPOT) by 4.3--21.2%.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [$\\varepsilon$-SegFormer: Multi-Resolution Vision Transformers for Sparsely Supervised Segmentation in Electron Microscopy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27796v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27796

**作者：** Erfan Amidi、Sheida RahnamaiKordasiabi、Edoardo Giacomello、Florian Jug

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27796v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27796v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ε-SegFormer，一种面向电镜体数据稀疏监督分割的多分辨率 Vision Transformer：为每个有标注像素采样三种物理分辨率视图，映射到统一坐标系的 token 序列中，训练时隐藏该像素自身 token，迫使标签由上下文预测。

**相关性简评：** 其「隐藏目标像素自身 token、强制依赖上下文」的做法可供核查是否有助于抑制 LVR 中的视觉旁路与局部捷径，多分辨率视图共享坐标编码也可能为视觉证据筛选与尺度组合提供线索，但论文本身并非 VLM 推理研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $\\varepsilon$-SegFormer: Multi-Resolution Vision Transformers for Sparsely Supervised Segmentation in Electron Microscopy

**abstract/A1：** Segmenting organelles in electron microscopy (EM) volumes requires dense annotations that take experts months to produce, and every gain in imaging resolution adds to this cost. Sparsely supervised methods reduce this cost to a small fraction of labeled pixels, but the convolutional networks they build on see a limited neighborhood around each pixel, while the identity of an organelle often depends on context far beyond it. We introduce $\\varepsilon$-SegFormer, a multi-resolution Vision Transformer for sparsely supervised semantic segmentation, which builds on the architecture of MuViT and extends the training method of $\\varepsilon$-Seg. For each labeled pixel, $\\varepsilon$-SegFormer samples views at three physical resolutions centered on it, embeds their patches into a single token sequence, and encodes every token's position in image coordinates, so that attention relates all views in one shared frame. A segmentation head classifies the pixel from the tokens around it; during training, the pixel's own token is hidden, so the label must be predicted from context rather than local appearance. The model is trained in a single stage, without pretraining, with an auxiliary reconstruction loss. On the BetaSeg FIB-SEM dataset, $\\varepsilon$-SegFormer learns from about 10,000 labeled pixels among the 670 million voxels inside three cells, and, given the cell mask, segments nuclei, mitochondria and insulin granules in a slice of a fourth, unseen cell with an average Dice of 0.89 (0.91 after post-processing). Ablations show that coarser views improve accuracy and that a compact head on local tokens works best, with the wider context entering through attention in the encoder.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [Refrain: Learning Multi-Shot Consistency from Inconsistent References for A$^2$O Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27806v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27806

**作者：** Hongru Yan、Yue Wu、Jian-Wei Zhang、Liangbin Xie、Mingzhe Zheng、Shenghua Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27806v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27806v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Refrain，用自监督重建任务训练统一的多镜头视频生成模型：对同一原视频的参考片段施加不同风格编辑，要求模型在共享风格提示下还原未编辑素材，从而支持任意参考、任意时长；并提出 Shot-Debiased Consistency（SDC）指标评估跨镜头风格连续性。

**相关性简评：** SDC 通过扣除内容变化引起的基线差异来校正跨镜头风格比较，这一评价去偏思路可能可供核查，用于分析 LVR 中 latent 相似度被内容、位置等因素混淆的问题；但论文本身属视频生成，未涉及推理状态或证据使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Refrain: Learning Multi-Shot Consistency from Inconsistent References for A$^2$O Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** A comic-book character, a photorealistic cityscape, an oil-painted coffee cup -- such stylistically diverse references provide visual guidance across shots and frames, yet generating a coherent multi-shot video that unifies their visual styles while preserving subject identities and scene content remains challenging. Existing methods maintain cross-shot continuity through additional attention control or memory processing. However, they struggle to resolve disparate per-shot styles or handle reference-target duration mismatches within a single framework. We introduce Refrain, a unified model that learns cross-shot consistency through a self-supervised reconstruction task. Reference clips from the same original video are deliberately perturbed with distinct visual edits, and the model recovers the unedited source footage under a shared style prompt. The resulting pipeline yields DurMix-300K, which pairs independently edited references of varying durations with their original videos. Operating on the A$^2$O principle (Any Reference, Any Duration, One Timeline), Refrain maps image and video references onto a shared temporal canvas without auxiliary conditioning modules. For evaluation, experiments show that standard CLIP or DINO metrics frequently overlook stylistic clashes or misinterpret intended narrative cuts as inconsistency. We propose Shot-Debiased Consistency (SDC), which uses an extensive real-video bank to establish a baseline for style variation associated with content changes within shots. By discounting this baseline divergence, SDC evaluates cross-shot style shifts against real-world distributions, where smaller residual differences indicate higher stylistic continuity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Refrain achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-shot consistency, while excelling at shot timing, reference adherence, and text alignment.

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Mixture of Latent Recursions for Low-Rank Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27823v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27823

**作者：** Weixun Luo、Ye Mao、Ranran Huang、Junpeng Jing、Krystian Mikolajczyk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27823v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27823v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MoLR：对 LoRA 降维后的 latent 表示用共享变换反复精炼，并由轻量阈值路由器逐 token 决定是否继续递归，动态阈值平衡各递归深度的使用；在语言与视觉任务上以极小参数开销优于多种 LoRA 变体，且无需重训即可在推理时调节计算量。

**相关性简评：** 逐 token 的自适应递归深度与阈值式停止机制，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的计算分配、循环状态更新与推理期算力—性能权衡提供可核查线索，但其 latent 只是 LoRA 适配表示，未涉及视觉证据或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mixture of Latent Recursions for Low-Rank Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) performs task-specific adaptation using a fixed low-rank architecture, whose expressiveness is typically increased by enlarging or adding learnable modules with a proportional parameter overhead. We introduce Mixture of Latent Recursions (MoLR), an adaptive recursion framework that enhances adaptation expressiveness while preserving parameter efficiency. MoLR recursively refines the down-projected latent representation of each input using a shared transformation. A lightweight threshold-based router independently determines whether each token should undergo further refinement, while dynamically adjusted thresholds encourage balanced utilization across recursion depths. Extensive experiments across diverse language and vision tasks demonstrate that MoLR consistently outperforms representative LoRA variants with negligible parameter overhead over LoRA. Further analyses show that MoLR learns input-dependent recursion-depth allocation and enables a controllable inference-time compute-performance trade-off without requiring retraining. Code will be publicly available.

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Graph Signal Generation via Graph-Aware Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27826v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27826

**作者：** Vimal K B、Sergio Rozada、Antonio G. Marques、Elvin Isufi、Hadi J. Rad

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27826v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27826v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 graph-aware diffusion（GAD），把前向加噪改为图上的热扩散，并设计 Mode-Adaptive SNR Scheduling（MASS），为各图频率分配独立扩散率并保持相同 SNR，同时给出反向 SDE 刚性随 Laplacian 条件数变化的界，在推荐、交通插补与预测任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 按频率自适应的噪声调度与反向步数效率（仅需 3 步）可能为 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态生成中的调度和计算预算分配提供线索，但需核查其图结构假设能否对应视觉推理中的表示结构。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph Signal Generation via Graph-Aware Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Many signals are defined on the nodes of a graph, such as traffic flow at road sensors or user purchases of related products. They are often partly observed, and learning their distribution enables imputing missing values and forecasting future ones. Diffusion models learn such distributions, but their forward noise is independent across nodes and does not depend on the graph. We introduce graph-aware diffusion (GAD), whose forward process is a heat diffusion on the graph driven by Gaussian noise. In the graph Fourier basis, the forward transition is a scalar Gaussian channel at each frequency. With a shared diffusion rate, high graph frequencies are attenuated faster than low ones. We propose Mode-Adaptive SNR Scheduling (MASS), which assigns each graph frequency its own diffusion rate and keeps all of them at the same SNR. For graph-stationary signals, MASS equalizes the normalized error of the optimal linear denoiser across frequencies. We bound reverse-SDE stiffness as a function of the (shifted) Laplacian's condition number, with a logarithmic upper bound for MASS and a linear lower bound for shared rates. GAD with MASS improves recommendation accuracy by 5-19%, reduces traffic-imputation RMSE by up to 7.8% with half as many reverse steps, and improves forecasting MAE and RMSE using only 3 reverse steps.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 2 figures, 9 tables. Under review at IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence. Code: https://github.com/vimalkb7/SpectralAnalysisGAD

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [DistDNA: Language Model Fingerprinting under Stochastic Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27831v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27831

**作者：** Lifan Hu、Qian Yu、Zhaomin Wu、Bingsheng He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27831v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27831v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究随机解码（未知 temperature、top-p）下的 LLM 行为指纹识别：用提示条件的回答分布而非单条回答表征模型，以逐提示 MMD 比较分布并直接匹配参考指纹而无需训练分类器，并分析有限采样引入的方差项。

**相关性简评：** 其「用分布距离代替单样本比较、并把采样噪声与真实模型差异分离」的思路，可能可供核查 latent 推理轨迹或内部状态在随机采样下的分布级比较与不确定性估计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DistDNA: Language Model Fingerprinting under Stochastic Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Behavioral fingerprints identify a large language model (LLM) from its responses to a shared set of prompts, enabling applications such as verifying whether a deployed model corresponds to the checkpoint it claims to be. Deployed models, however, are commonly served with stochastic decoding, under which the same model may produce different responses to an identical prompt, depending on an unknown temperature and top-$p$ setting. Existing representations such as LLM DNA provide limited treatment of this variation. A natural remedy is to characterize models by their response distributions, which, however, raises two challenges. First, each distribution must be estimated from only a small number of sampled responses. Second, a change in decoding setting can alter responses to the same extent as a change of checkpoint. Consequently, fingerprint distances alone provide an incomplete basis for model identity, and identification typically relies on a classifier trained jointly on fingerprints from many checkpoints. We propose DistDNA, which represents a model by its prompt-conditional response distributions and compares models by promptwise maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). The model under examination is assigned to the known checkpoint whose reference fingerprints, collected at several decoding settings, lie nearest to its own, without training a classifier. We show that finite sampling adds variance-dependent terms to the expected squared fingerprint distance, with these terms decreasing as $O(1/R)$ for equal response counts, where $R$ is the number of responses per prompt. Experiments across 115 model checkpoint entries demonstrate that DistDNA improves identification accuracy over the strongest baseline, LLM DNA, by 10.36%.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 12 figures, 23 tables, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [DGC-Net: A Depth-Geometric Conditioning Network for Projection-Supervised OCTA Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27854v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27854

**作者：** Lucas Santiago、Bisheng Wang、Jaime S. Cardoso、Tomás Ribeiro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27854v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27854v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 OCTA 由 3D 体数据预测 2D 投影分割时轴向信息被丢弃或压缩而引发歧义的问题，提出 DGC-Net，通过深度矩条件与整体深度几何调制把轴向血管证据描述子注入投影特征再解码。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其在压缩投影表示中显式注入几何描述子以缓解信息坍缩的做法，可能为 latent 状态中视觉证据保留与旁路条件注入的设计提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DGC-Net: A Depth-Geometric Conditioning Network for Projection-Supervised OCTA Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** OCTA segmentation is commonly formulated as a projection supervised task: models observe 3D volumetric scans but are supervised by 2D en-face semantic maps. Existing 2D and 3D-to-2D methods either discard axial information or compress depth responses into latent projections, leaving vessel crossings, diffuse capillary regions, and FAZ boundaries vulnerable to projection-induced ambiguity. We propose DGC-Net, a Depth-Geometric Conditioning Network that recasts 3D-to-2D OCTA segmentation as depth-geometrically conditioned prediction, where projected features are decoded with compact descriptors of axial vascular evidence. DGC-Net preserves local depth geometry through Depth-Moment Conditioning (DM), which injects axial localization and dispersion cues into the projection stream, and captures global depth layout through Holistic Depth-Geometry Modulation (HDM), which derives a raw-volume axial prior for final 2D decoding. Experiments on the OCTA-500 6mm and 3mm subsets show that DGC-Net achieves 83.58% and 88.62% mIoU, respectively, reaching state-of-the-art performance.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Anchors That Move Without Collapsing: Exclusive-Region Anchor Routing for Vision Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27855v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27855

**作者：** Aadi Gupta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27855v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27855v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察 ViT 中 anchor token 路由的多样性问题：发现 Perceiver 式与 AnchorFormer 路径会坍缩为单一摘要，而按互斥 Voronoi 区域做硬分配不会结构性坍缩，并提出把 anchor 放在重要性图峰值上的 focus anchors，在分类任务上比较精度、FLOPs 与内存。

**相关性简评：** 其「池化图相似度」式 anchor 坍缩诊断与互斥硬分配（对比 softmax、Sinkhorn、Gumbel 方案）可能可供核查 LVR 中多个 latent 摘要保持区分、视觉证据分区与路由稳定性；但该工作属分类骨干的效率研究，不涉及推理状态生成与答案使用，迁移性需评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anchors That Move Without Collapsing: Exclusive-Region Anchor Routing for Vision Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Anchor tokens let a vision transformer exchange global information through a few summaries, but nothing forces different anchors to summarise different things. On one shared backbone we show that a Perceiver-style pathway and an AnchorFormer pathway collapse to a single summary, the latter despite its mass normalisation (pooling-map similarity 0.99), while other soft pathways, including Representative Attention, stay partly redundant (0.59-0.79). These results concern the routing modules inside our block, not the full published models. An exclusive partition of the patch grid cannot collapse by construction, and matched component by component it is 2.4-3.6 points more accurate than Representative Attention on CIFAR-100. We then propose focus anchors, which place most anchors at the peaks of a learned importance map while all anchors keep one exclusive Voronoi partition. Focus anchors beat a fixed partition on a cluttered canvas (+1.5 points, 95% CI \[0.5, 2.6\]) but not on full-HD traffic-sign recognition. There, both variants match dense attention with local windows at about a fifth of its FLOPs, and the fixed partition also trains 1.5x faster per step than dense attention with FlashAttention, though both use more memory; a ViT on a downsampled input loses half its mean average precision. Hard exclusive assignment is the only region assignment we tested that is both stable and most accurate: softmax and Sinkhorn relaxations diverge, and Gumbel-softmax trains but is less accurate. We also report the limits: a ResNet-18 is more accurate on every task we trained it on in this from-scratch regime, and anchors train faster than FlashAttention only from about 1536x1536 pixels and never use less memory.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures. Code: https://github.com/ARRY7686/Partitioned-Residual-Anchor-Routing-Transformer

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [Beyond Visual Fidelity: Comparing Categorical and Continuous World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27870v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27870

**作者：** Sarah Parisot、Melanie Fernandez Pradier、David Bignell、Sergio Valcarcel Macua、Katja Hofmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27870v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27870v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在受控2D/3D环境中系统比较离散类别预测与连续预测（next-token 自回归 vs 整帧迭代 flow matching）对动力学学习的影响，评测 rollout 有效性、校准与视觉质量，发现离散模型以更少训练算力取得更高长时程有效性。

**相关性简评：** 离散 token 与连续 latent 的状态生成、贪心解码与采样对校准的差异、以及有损视觉编码在语义指标上仍具竞争力，可为 LVR 中推理状态表示与生成范式的选择提供待核查的旁证线索，但其世界模型设定能否迁移到推理状态仍需阅读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Visual Fidelity: Comparing Categorical and Continuous World Models

**abstract/A1：** Visual world models can now generate compelling interactive worlds, but visual realism does not guarantee faithful dynamics. We systematically compare how categorical and continuous prediction shape learned dynamics under both next-token and iterative whole-frame generation. These choices are often bundled in approaches such as autoregressive token models and whole-frame flow matching. We design controlled 2D and 3D environments and evaluate dynamics validity, calibration, and visual quality. In 2D with lossless visual encoding, categorical models achieve higher rollout validity with less training compute, particularly for stochastic entities. Among categorical models, greedy decoding produces the highest rollout validity, while sampling from a next-token model yields the best-calibrated stochastic predictions. Failures in continuous models, such as disappearing game characters, echo those seen in more complex environments. Here, known dynamics let us measure their impact. In 3D, categorical models also achieve stronger long-horizon performance with less training compute. Even models with lossy tokenization remain competitive on semantic accuracy and FVD, despite lower next-frame RGB accuracy. Together, these findings make a strong case for categorical prediction as a compute-efficient route to learning world dynamics.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 12 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [LesionAgent: Training-Free Agentic Tumor Tracking and Response Assessment in Longitudinal CT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27876v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27876

**作者：** Maximilian Rokuss、Jeya Maria Jose Valanarasu、Alejandro Lozano、Benjamin Hamm、Yannick Kirchhoff、Fabian Isensee、Klaus Maier-Hein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27876v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27876v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LesionAgent：一个无需任务特定训练的 LLM agent，把冻结的 3D 模型当作工具，通过坐标标注视图在 3D 中操作，用病灶登记表跨访视记住每个病灶并核验所依赖的模型输出，数值判断交由确定性的临床指南引擎，用于纵向 CT 的病灶追踪与疗效评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「决定效果的是 harness 而非 LLM」的外置验证、记忆登记与规则计算分工，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、输出核验及数值推理是否交给确定性模块提供可核查线索；但工作属临床 3D 影像应用，与 VLM 内部 latent 表示无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LesionAgent: Training-Free Agentic Tumor Tracking and Response Assessment in Longitudinal CT

**abstract/A1：** Every few months, a cancer patient's CT scans decide whether treatment continues. 3D foundation models can now detect, segment, register and track lesions, yet none of them reads the scans as a radiologist does. A radiologist decides which findings are lesions, which are the same lesion months later and whether a new spot means progression, and checks each decision against the extensive rules of a clinical guideline. We introduce LesionAgent, an LLM agent that performs this read end to end without task-specific training. It operates frozen 3D models as tools, acts in 3D through coordinate-labelled views, remembers every lesion across visits in a registry, verifies every model output it relies on, and leaves every number to a deterministic guideline engine. On four public longitudinal CT datasets, LesionAgent tracks more lesions and measures them more accurately than every automatic baseline, and where the same 3D models chained by fixed rules call progression falsely at 33 of 56 follow-ups, it does so twice. On clinical follow-up CTs, it agrees with the radiologists' response at 20 of 23 follow-ups, including every change in response, and without training on DeepLesion, it retrieves more of its lesions than every tracker trained on it (89.0% vs. 87.4%). Its harness, not its LLM, makes the difference: given the same LLM and 3D models but not the harness, Claude Code reaches a detection F1 of 41% and LesionAgent 78%, and an open-weight LLM that can run inside the hospital rises from 6% to 73%. LesionAgent and a napari reading app for any LLM are available at https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/LesionAgent.

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [QuantStream3D: Geometry-Aligned Low-Bit KV Cache Quantization for Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27901v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27901

**作者：** Zunhai Su、Keyu Fan、Ruishan Guo、Zhongzhu Zhou、Jingzehua Xu、Bohan Sun、Hansen Feng、Ruihan Hu、Yik-Chung Wu、Ngai Wong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27901v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27901v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究流式视觉几何 transformer 中持续增长的 KV 缓存，分析量化敏感度随精度与缓存位置不均、以及缓存与几何不匹配的问题，提出几何引导的混合精度 KV 量化与几何对齐的量化感知蒸馏，在多数设置下以约 2-bit 缓存保持近无损几何精度并压缩内存。

**相关性简评：** KV 缓存被当作跨视角几何记忆，其对敏感证据的保护与量化扰动向下游预测传播的分析，可供核查 latent 视觉证据在内部状态中的保留与压缩代价，但论文本身不涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QuantStream3D: Geometry-Aligned Low-Bit KV Cache Quantization for Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Streaming visual geometry transformers retain historical key-value (KV) states as cross-view geometric memory, but growing caches constrain long-sequence inference. We systematically characterize KV quantization in streaming geometry and uncover two linked challenges: Nonuniform Geometric Sensitivity and Cache-Geometry Mismatch. Quantization sensitivity varies across precision choices and cache locations, while perturbed retrieval changes geometry-head inputs and propagates through subsequent predictions and KV writes. Guided by these findings, we propose QuantStream3D, to our knowledge the first dedicated KV quantization framework for streaming visual geometry transformers. Geometry-Guided Mixed-Precision KV Quantization protects sensitive cross-view evidence, while Geometry-Aligned Quantization-Aware Distillation jointly adapts transformer blocks and geometry heads on student-generated quantized history. Across five benchmarks and two backbones, QuantStream3D achieves near-lossless geometric accuracy in most settings with predominantly 2-bit KV caches, and many configurations surpass unquantized inference. Compared with LLM-oriented KV quantizers, it reduces TUM absolute trajectory error and NeuralRGBD completeness error by up to 57.4% and 69.7%, respectively. Integration with existing native cache-pruning methods further pushes compression while improving reconstruction quality on NeuralRGBD. Our optimized StreamVGGT backend achieves $5.05\\times$ physical KV compression and 23.1% lower latency at batch size one. Under a fixed memory budget on the same GPU, it supports $5.4\\times$ the maximum batch size and up to $1.67\\times$ the peak throughput of unquantized inference. Code and pretrained checkpoints will be made publicly available.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 5 figures. Includes appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [DiTraR: Diverse Scorers and Transcript-Guided Re-ranking for Video Corpus Moment Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27931v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27931

**作者：** Mohammad Alijanpour Shalmani、Alale Rezvani Boroujeni、Ali Amini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27931v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27931v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析视频语料时刻检索中主要错误来自「选错视频」而非边界不准，提出 DiTraR：将查询拆成探针并穷举评分候选区间的 witness-interval 评分器、多评分器能量集成，以及用冻结语言模型读取高排名视频的时间戳对白来重排但不改边界的 transcript 验证器。

**相关性简评：** 其把片段定位与证据核验显式解耦，并以集成评分和文本验证器分别改善视觉类与对白类查询，可能可供核查 LVR 中多假设评分、模态特异性证据筛选以及「先定位后核验」分工的借鉴线索；但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DiTraR: Diverse Scorers and Transcript-Guided Re-ranking for Video Corpus Moment Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Video corpus moment retrieval asks a system to find, in a large collection of untrimmed videos with subtitles, the video and the time interval that a natural-language query describes. We find that most top-ranked errors of a strong corpus-level scorer come from choosing the wrong video, not from imprecise boundaries, and that re-weighting the evidence the scorer already uses does not fix them. We present DiTraR, which addresses video selection from two complementary directions. A witness-interval scorer decomposes the query into probes and scores every candidate interval of every corpus video exactly; a multi-scorer energy ensemble averages the energies of independently trained scorers before one exhaustive search; and a transcript verifier, a frozen language model that reads the time-stamped dialogue of the highest-ranked videos, re-orders the moments without changing their boundaries. With all settings frozen on development data and a single evaluation, DiTraR reaches 24.36 and 13.55 R@1 at IoU 0.5 and 0.7 on the TVR validation split. The ensemble improves queries about visual content, the verifier improves queries about dialogue, and their gains largely add up.

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Topology Hub Optimal Transport: Eliminating Long Context Dilution at Flash Speed](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27951v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27951

**作者：** Dylan Forde

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27951v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27951v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 THOT，用 hub-and-spoke 最优传输取代稠密注意力矩阵，配合有界支撑容量门控（simplex sparsemax 或阈值 ReLU）把干扰项传输质量压至零，并给出融合 Triton 内核与因果变体，使 262K 长度下的零样本绑定召回从 softmax 门控的 3.52% 提升到 97% 以上。

**相关性简评：** 其「归一化质量随长度分散导致关键事实被稀释」的诊断与容量门控抑制干扰质量的机制，可能可供核查 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与信息坍缩问题，例如大量视觉 token 是否稀释关键细节；但原文是纯文本长上下文内核工作，无多模态或 latent 状态验证，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Topology Hub Optimal Transport: Eliminating Long Context Dilution at Flash Speed

**abstract/A1：** Long-context models use sparse or structured attention to avoid quadratic cost, yet retrieving a small set of relevant facts embedded among hundreds of thousands of distractors degrades as normalization mass disperses with length. Hub-and-spoke optimal transport (OT) replaces the dense $L \\times L$ matrix with a linear-size graph over local windows and $K$ shared hubs balanced by Sinkhorn scaling. Building on block-wise tail refinement and fixed-support transport topology (arXiv:2605.08123, arXiv:2609.07954), we introduce Topology Hub Optimal Transport (THOT): (1) bounded-support capacity gating via simplex sparsemax ($O(L\\log L)$) or sorting-free token-local thresholded ReLU ($O(L)$), driving excess distractor mass to zero; (2) a three-sweep $1+2$ annealed row-closed Sinkhorn schedule that damps bipartite residuals; (3) an exact rank-$(D+2R+1)$ One-Reference-Tile adjoint in batched split-factor Triton kernels (BF16 Tensor Cores, FP32 potentials); and (4) a chunked exclusive-prefix causal variant with verified zero future leakage. On NVIDIA L4, sparsemax THOT trained at $L=128$ reaches 97.40% zero-shot recall of fixed bindings at $L=262{,}144$ under pure task loss ($α=0$) and 98.83% at $α=0.05$ (matched by $O(L)$ thresholded ReLU at $α=0.05$, vs. 80.60% at $α=0$), while softmax-gated hub OT drops to 3.52%. In bidirectional operator microbenchmarks, our kernel runs forward+VJP in 100.1 ms at $H=8, D=64, L=262\\mathrm{K}$ (2.2x faster with 4.0x smaller compiler temporaries than latent softmax) and 1.34 ms vs. 4.37 ms for cuDNN FlashAttention at $L=4{,}096$; multi-layer LM runs use the unfused path. Zero-shot WikiText-103 multi-needle retrieval hits 100.00% (128/128 queries at $L=65\\mathrm{K}$) vs. 0.78% for short-trained RoPE FlashAttention, and compact 4-layer WikiText-4K runs show modest causal and masked LM gains.

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Model Carving: Training Transformers That Optimize Their Own Topology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27962

**作者：** Kaden Zheng、Pratyusha Sharma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27962v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Model Carving，用可学习标量门在单次训练中同时学习 Transformer 权重与拓扑，删去冗余 attention/MLP 子层，得到更小、异构且推理更省的模型，并在标准与 looped Transformer 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其可学习门控制子层保留及 looped 结构的删减，可能为 LVR 中循环深度、动态计算预算与 THINK/STOP 成本权衡提供训练期结构选择线索，但并非多模态推理工作，删层后状态更新与长度泛化的影响仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Model Carving: Training Transformers That Optimize Their Own Topology

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning has largely kept separate the choice of architecture and the process of training. A classic Transformer, for instance, trains a fixed set of attention and MLP sublayers at every layer, in the same order, and optimizes the weights within. This uniformity is a convention rather than a principled design choice, and it is likely suboptimal: the best architecture for a given task may be smaller, more efficient, and far from uniform. We believe that, ideally, training itself should shape the model's topology. To this end, we introduce Model Carving, which learns both a Transformer's weights and its architecture in a single run. By applying learnable, scalar gates to each sublayer, we allow the training loss to remove the model's own components during training -- freeing a smaller heterogeneous network inside. We evaluate Model Carving by pre-training standard Transformers from 140M to 2.7B parameters and looped Transformers from 140M to 1.3B. At equal training compute, carved models reach a lower validation loss than dense models trained with up to 1.7 times more compute. Furthermore, carved models also outperform size-matched models produced by pruning or distillation, suggesting that the best way to obtain small models for edge devices is to carve them during training, not to compress a large one afterwards. This advantage both grows with scale and carries over to downstream tasks such as few-shot evaluation and generation. Because the removed sublayers no longer run, carved models also cost up to 34% fewer FLOPs per token and decode up to 1.6 times faster without cost in quality. Our results show that architecture, like weights, should be learned and not fixed in advance, and that inside every large transformer is a smaller, faster, stronger one waiting to be carved out.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 15 figures, 19 tables. Under review at ICLR 2027. Project page: https://kadenzheng.com/model-carving/

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Non-penetrating World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27963v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27963

**作者：** Yuhang Dai、Yinwei Wu、Ziyue Lin、Han Ji、Xingyi Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27963v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27963v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究交互式视频世界模型中的物体穿透问题，提出 NoPeBench 基准评估各模型对物理障碍的响应，并给出 NoPeWorld：在冻结 DiT 的隐状态上训练小型预测头判断候选动作是否安全，再用安全动作替换，平均将违规率从 74% 降至 16%。

**相关性简评：** 「冻结骨干隐状态探针 + 候选动作安全判定与替换」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 是否确实编码任务必要的视觉证据、以及能否据读出结果筛选或校正推理路径，但该工作属视频世界模型，与 VLM 推理主线并不直接对应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Non-penetrating World Models

**abstract/A1：** We study a new problem of preventing object penetration in interactive video world models. To understand how existing models handle this, we introduce NoPeBench, a benchmark that evaluates how world models respond to physical obstacles. Our evaluation reveals that none of the open-source models can reliably prevent such failures. Surprisingly, however, these models already encode the information needed to predict such violations. We find that the hidden states of diffusion transformers (DiTs) can predict whether a candidate action will penetrate an obstacle. Building on this, we introduce NoPeWorld, a lightweight framework that detects and prevents penetration. Specifically, we train a small prediction head on top of a frozen DiT's hidden states to read out if a candidate action is safe or not. Unsafe actions are then replaced with safe ones that best preserve the original intent. Despite its simplicity, NoPeWorld substantially reduces the physical violation rate, averaged over six video world models, from 74% to 16%. Remarkably, our method shows zero-shot generalization to open-domain imagery, third-person viewpoints, and moving obstacles. We publicly release our code, the NoPeBench benchmark, and model weights. Project Page: https://nopeworld.github.io .

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 13 figures. Code: https://github.com/yuhangdai427/NoPeWorld

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26504

**作者：** Élodie Côté-Gauthier、Doina Precup、Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26504v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将内嗅皮层网格细胞解释为 successor representation 的特征向量，用固定参考策略转移矩阵的特征向量构造基，使每个 successor feature 是该基特征乘以闭式系数 1/(1−γλ\_i)，从而无需 successor-feature 网络或回放缓冲，任务切换只需对奖励做线性回归；在双目标 gridworld 中切换目标时仍保持接近最优回报。

**相关性简评：** 谱分解给出固定、可复用的 latent 状态基与闭式系数，可能为 LVR 中内部表示几何结构及任务/目标切换时的状态复用提供概念线索，可供核查；但该工作限于离散 gridworld 的纯 RL，与视觉多模态推理没有直接接口。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual reinforcement learning requires agents to adapt when the task changes while reusing what they know about the environment. Successor features separate dynamics from task-specific reward, but their effectiveness depends on the choice of base features. Taking the identification of entorhinal grid cells with eigenvectors of the successor representation as a design principle, we build this basis from eigenvectors of the transition matrix of a fixed reference policy. With such a basis, the successor features at a state-action pair are a linear combination of the base features at that same pair, which is not true in general, and the coefficients are known in closed form: each successor feature is its base feature multiplied by $θ\_i = 1/(1-γλ\_i)$, where $λ\_i$ is its eigenvalue. The long-term return is thus predicted from the same features as the immediate reward, no successor-feature network or replay buffer is needed, and adapting to a new task reduces to a linear regression on the reward. The agent acts greedily on the action values of the reference policy, which is one step of policy improvement over it and suffices to reach a single goal. In a two-goal gridworld, with the uniform random walk as reference policy, whose eigenvectors are the low-frequency Laplacian eigenvectors, agents built this way maintain near-maximal return across every change of goal, whereas SimpleSF and DQN lose most of their return at each change.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the NeurReps and WiML workshops at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [Coherence by Construction: Synthesizing CTI Knowledge Graphs and the Formal Limits of Coherence Validation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26717v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26717

**作者：** Tanzimul Alam Fahim、Mohammad Arif Hossain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26717v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26717v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「以构造保证连贯」的关系型数据生成范式：LLM 代理提出意图，确定性裁判依据显式世界模型裁定事件，仅在事件发生时产出关系，并证明对这类生成器而言，只读图特征的校验器会被结构匹配的随机图复现而失去信息量。

**相关性简评：** 其以结构匹配随机图作零假设、按组件消融归因并论证校验器可能只奖励平凡性的思路，或可供核查 latent 状态是否真携带任务相关内容而非表面统计，但属远领域类比，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coherence by Construction: Synthesizing CTI Knowledge Graphs and the Formal Limits of Coherence Validation

**abstract/A1：** Synthesizing relational training data such as knowledge graphs with a large language model typically emits structured objects one at a time, which keeps each object valid but leaves the relationships between them ungrounded. We study an alternative paradigm, generation by simulation: rather than describing a relational artifact, we simulate a process that produces it and take the artifact to be its causal record. Language-model agents propose intent and a deterministic referee resolves outcomes against an explicit world model, so a relation is emitted only when the event it represents occurs, making coherence a property of construction. We instantiate the paradigm for cyber threat intelligence knowledge graphs in the STIX 2.1 standard with three engines, among them an evolutionary engine applying language-model genetic operators to structured graph genomes, sharing a deterministic compiler that makes malformed and dangling output unrepresentable. We prove referential closure, causal grounding, and identifier stability, an ablation localizes each to the referee and world model rather than the policy, and two independent LLM judges reproduce the rubric's engine ranking. Our central result is cautionary, and we prove it: a validator reading any graph feature set a structure-matched random graph can reproduce is uninformative about coherence for a coherence-by-construction generator. Using Erdős-Rényi controls we realize the theorem four times, as structural separation, embedding transfer, inductive link prediction, and real-data augmentation each reward a triviality rather than coherence; we give the mechanism behind each and the conditions a valid benchmark must meet.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 5 figures. Includes supplementary material (Sections S-I through S-X)

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26722

**作者：** Moritz Pötzsch、Andrea Gentilini、Lisa Rossgoderer、Tom Osterfeld、Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul、Swanand Ravindra Kadhe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26722v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了约 450 万条多轮/单轮工具调用偏好对数据集 TOUMAII，并发布可从 SFT 对话数据生成含自然失败模式偏好对的管线 TOUMAII-Gen，用 3B 模型 DPO 验证其效果与现有高质量数据集持平。

**相关性简评：** 其「正例 + 貌似合理的错误调用」偏好对构造方式，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的对比式监督与错误贡献分配提供可核查的数据构造思路；但论文不涉及视觉输入或内部推理状态，与主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator

**abstract/A1：** To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline. We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the 2nd Workshop on Agentic AI Benchmarks and Applications for Enterprise Tasks (AABA4ET) at NeurIPS 2026. 27 pages, including 5 pages of main text, references, and appendices; 17 figures and 7 tables. Code: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen. Datasets: https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [Decomposing images in a PRISM: A Single-Source Multi-View Framework for Image Representation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26726v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26726

**作者：** Eduardo Campos、Willam Cardoso、Diego Furtado、Solange Oliveira、Alneu Lopes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26726v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26726v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PRISM 框架，通过显式结构映射把单一图像源转换为多个具有不同归纳偏置的视图表示，并给出度量各视图贡献的协议；其手工实例 PRISM-HD 在七个艺术与非艺术图像数据集上与经典描述子、微调深度网络组合评估。

**相关性简评：** 「单源构造多种异质表示并量化各视图贡献」可能为多 latent 假设的多样性设计与贡献分配提供类比线索，但该工作是静态分类特征，未涉及推理期状态更新或答案对 latent 内容的使用，需核查其贡献度量是否能迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decomposing images in a PRISM: A Single-Source Multi-View Framework for Image Representation

**abstract/A1：** Multi-view learning has shown that combining complementary views can improve predictive performance, yet many datasets provide a single raw source of information. How can multiple useful views be constructed from one source? We propose PRISM (Plural Representations of Images via Structural Maps), a framework that transforms an image into representations with distinct inductive biases through explicit structural maps, together with a protocol for measuring what each view contributes. We evaluate PRISM-HD, a hand-designed instantiation of PRISM in which the constructed views are summarized through representation-specific descriptors, on three artistic and four non-artistic image datasets. Analyses at a fixed feature budget show that the gains of the structural views are not explained by feature dimensionality alone. PRISM-HD by itself outperforms WND-CHARM on all seven datasets. Moreover, PRISM-HD and Fisher Vector provide complementary predictive signals: their equal-weight combination improves over the better individual representation by 5--8 percentage points on the artistic datasets. When combined with fine-tuned deep networks through a fixed parameter-free fusion rule, the PRISM-based representation improves performance on four of seven datasets and leaves it statistically unchanged on the remaining three, indicating that the constructed views provide predictive signals that are complementary to learned deep representations.

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Cross-Attack Robust Aggregation for Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26731v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26731

**作者：** Eyhab Al-Masri

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26731v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26731v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究联邦学习中恶意或故障客户端带来的投毒问题，提出 FedTether：按客户端更新相对坐标中位数的偏离计算带死区的鲁棒分数进行重加权，并作为服务端优化步骤，在多数据集、多攻击类型与部分参与条件下兼顾鲁棒性与精度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但「偏离中位数即降权」的鲁棒聚合与免鲁棒性代价的评价思路，可能启发多 latent 假设或视觉证据筛选时对不可靠来源的加权取舍，属较远领域迁移，需核查其在高维状态聚合下的可用性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Attack Robust Aggregation for Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** Federated learning (FL) lets resource-constrained IoT and edge devices train a shared model without exchanging raw data, but compromised or faulty devices can poison the global model. Existing robust aggregators each fail in some regime: naive averaging (FedAvg) collapses under high-magnitude attacks, whereas distance- and statistics-based defenses (Krum, median, trimmed mean) sacrifice accuracy on non-IID data and remain vulnerable to stealthy attacks. We present FedTether, which reweights client updates by their deviation from a coordinatewise median through a robust, dead-zoned score applied as a server-side optimizer step. Across three datasets, three attack types, and Byzantine fractions up to 40%, FedTether is the only method top-tier in every regime: it matches FedAvg under stealthy attacks with no robustness tax, exceeds robust baselines by up to 42 accuracy points, and stays accurate where high-magnitude attacks reduce FedAvg to near-random. These properties persist at 50 clients under partial participation, with no shared data or validation set.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [INCISE: Training-Free In-Context Segmentation of Surgical Videos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26734v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26734

**作者：** Yifan Xu、Bofang Zheng、Guangyu Meng、Nishchal Sapkota、Xianshi Ma、Xu Cao、Tianjiao Yu、Danny Z Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26734v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26734v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向手术视频的免训练上下文分割框架：从多段视频中挑选能覆盖目标视频局部外观的未标注帧作为参考标注，再用冻结模型分割目标视频，以极少参考帧超过微调 SOTA。

**相关性简评：** 其按外观覆盖度选取少量参考帧的思路，可能与 LVR 中预算受限的视觉证据筛选与采样策略有概念联系，但该文不涉及推理状态或答案生成，可供核查的借鉴较为间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** INCISE: Training-Free In-Context Segmentation of Surgical Videos

**abstract/A1：** Surgical video analysis requires video object segmentation (VOS), yet existing methods are commonly based on pretrained model fine-tuning using a large number of expert labels. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers the promise of bypassing this high cost by segmenting a target video using a few annotated references, which are the sole source of surgical knowledge for a frozen model. However, existing ICS methods tend to draw such references at random, which concentrate on a few views that dominate a surgical video and can miss other objects of interest (e.g., transient instruments and small structures). To address these challenges, we propose INCISE, a training-free ICS framework for surgical videos. INCISE selects unlabeled frames from multiple surgical videos that jointly cover the local appearances of a target video, and then segments the target video using these references. With only 1.7% of training frames selected and annotated as references and no target video frame labeled, INCISE obtains 67.2% mean mIoU and outperforms the best SOTA fine-tuned method by 9.2 points. We hope INCISE can help establish ICS as a practical alternative to fine-tuning in surgical video analysis and other domains with a costly labeling burden.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [Who Should Verify? Reviewer Selection with Partial Information and Outcome Audits](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26770v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26770

**作者：** The Viet Bui、Tien Mai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26770v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26770v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究在只能付费核查部分答案键与评审的情形下，如何以最低成本证明所选评审程序与最优程序相差不超过指定题数，给出精确停止规则、整数规划与对数因子近似算法，并分析搜索成本下界，在 MMLU 等数据上重放验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「按需付费核查＋停止规则＋证书成本」框架可能为 LVR 中何时停止获取视觉证据、在有限预算下比较多种 latent 推理配置提供形式化参考，但完全不涉及视觉内部状态，可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Should Verify? Reviewer Selection with Partial Information and Outcome Audits

**abstract/A1：** Selecting an AI reviewer requires evidence about what it does after reading a draft. On a fixed batch of items, a team can keep the original answers or adopt one reviewer's answers throughout. We ask how much it costs to certify that the chosen procedure falls at most a specified number of correct answers short of the best available procedure, for every completion of the unobserved evidence. When all reviews are available and only verified answer keys cost money, we give an exact stopping rule and an integer program for the cheapest certificate in hindsight. When reviews and keys both have prices, a polynomial-time algorithm using the completed record approximates the minimum certificate cost within a factor logarithmic in the number of procedures. This dependence is tight to leading order unless P equals NP. Finding a certificate can be much more expensive. We exhibit a two-procedure family with $n$ unresolved items where one purchase suffices in hindsight but the exact minimax expected search cost is $(n+1)/2$. For two binary procedures under a known independent and identically distributed item law, five counts suffice to compute an optimal search policy. We replay reviews on 2,000 MMLU questions, 896 numerical reports, and 48 Singapore electricity forecast reports. With reviews available, a targeted audit used 6.9% fewer keys on MMLU and 1.2% fewer on numerical reports than a fixed order selected on development data. Compared with selection by stand-alone accuracy, the chosen procedures gained 2.94 percentage points on held-out MMLU questions and zero on numerical reports. On electricity reports, five checks certified a tie across all 48, yet each procedure was correct only eight times.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [Tracktail: Multi-View 3D Point Tracking without Depth Input](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26779v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26779

**作者：** Katie L Rupp、Jonathan Wong、Sharmishtaa Seshamani、Ana Mafalda Vicente、Lili Karashchuk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26779v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26779v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Tracktail：无需深度输入，从多个标定视角跟踪 3D 查询点；架构先按相机分别编码视频特征、再做逐查询解码，相机交叉注意力只在解码器内的查询之间进行，并利用相机几何计算逐相机场景尺度以归一化输入与预测，在多视图基准上取得 SOTA。

**相关性简评：** 其以查询为单位、跨帧跨视角持续更新的紧凑状态与尺度归一化，可能可供核查 LVR 中几何证据如何写入低维 latent 状态并在多视角间融合；但该工作面向跟踪而非 VLM 推理，联系需较多核查。
