<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tracktail: Multi-View 3D Point Tracking without Depth Input

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Tracktail, a novel architecture designed to efficiently track query points in 3D from multiple calibrated views without any depth input. Our architecture separates the task as a per-camera video encoding of features and a per-query decoder. Notably, camera cross-attention is only performed across queries in the decoder, allowing for efficient inference (7 FPS and less than 4 GB memory for 10 views and 100 query points). We further introduce a per-camera scene scale computed from camera geometry, which allows us to normalize inputs and predictions appropriately across a vast variety of dataset scales. We train on a synthetic Multi-View Kubric dataset as well as a novel Multi-View Point Odyssey benchmark dataset, which consists of synthetic renders of humans and animals across 499 frames. Tracktail achieves state-of-the-art results on the Panoptic Studio, DexYCB, and Multi-View Kubric benchmarks, outperforming methods that rely on estimated depth. Tracktail also demonstrates a strong ability to maintain accurate tracking over longer ranges compared to existing methods. Our code, data, and weights are publicly available: https://ai-hhmi.github.io/tracktail/

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [CADENCE:Temporally Coherent Whole-Body Human Mesh Recovery that Preserves Expressive Hand Motion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26783v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26783

**作者：** Keniel Peart、Jagmohan Chauhan、Indu P. Bodala、Shelly Vishwakarma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26783v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26783v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CADENCE 单目视频全身 SMPL-X 重建系统：由共享编码器加高分辨率手部通路的逐帧估计器产生初值，再用离线时序细化器沿运动链做注意力，为每个关节、每帧选择学习的零相位滤波器组合，在平滑躯干的同时保留快速手指动作。

**相关性简评：** 其按关节、按帧自适应选择过滤强度以保住携带语义的高频手部运动的思路，可能为 LVR 中如何在时序聚合或状态更新时选择性保留任务必要视觉细节、避免信息坍缩提供可核查的类比，但属较远领域的概念借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CADENCE:Temporally Coherent Whole-Body Human Mesh Recovery that Preserves Expressive Hand Motion

**abstract/A1：** Whole-body SMPL-X estimators recover body, hand, and face meshes from single images, but applied frame by frame to video they produce jittery motion. Smoothing the output removes the jitter at a cost that body-only benchmarks do not measure: a temporal filter strong enough to steady the torso also flattens the fast finger strokes that carry meaning in sign language and gesture. We present CADENCE, a whole-body SMPL-X system for monocular video designed around this trade-off. A per-frame estimator built on a shared C-RADIOv4 encoder, with a high-resolution hand pathway that reuses the same encoder on hand crops, produces each frame's estimate. An offline temporal refiner then filters the SMPL-X rotation sequence: attention along the kinematic tree selects, for every joint and every frame, a mixture of learned zero-phase filters, so the torso is smoothed while fast finger strokes pass through. On 3DPW, the refiner reduces acceleration error from 17.4 to 5.4 mm/s2 while lowering PA-MPJPE from 41.9 to 40.2 mm; generic smoothers applied to the same predictions reach 6.2 to 7.7 mm/s2. On SignAvatars/How2Sign sign-language sequences, the refiner has the lowest hand jitter among the compared smoothers while keeping a hand-speed peak amplitude of 0.98, against 0.35 for a SmoothNet retrained on the full SMPL-X vector. The per-frame estimator also obtains the lowest full-body mesh error (25.7 mm) and hand joint error (8.3 mm) among the compared methods on EHF.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Graph-XGB: A Hybrid GNN Framework for Outdoor-to-Indoor 5G Signal Reconstruction via Aerial-Only UAV RF Sensing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26828v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26828

**作者：** David Hason Rudd、Eunkwang Shin、Nithiwat Pattrapong、Huan Huo、Xiangmeng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NI、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26828v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26828v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Graph-XGB，用改进 SpiderGNN 在楼层感知的物理图上将无人机外部 RF 测量传播为 128 维室内嵌入，经 PCA 压至 10 维后交由独立 XGBoost 回归器重建室内 5G RSSI/RSRP。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部状态无法直接采样、室内观测仅作训练监督、由边界测量推断内部状态」的设定，可能与 LVR 中由部分观测推断潜在状态及表示压缩是否保留任务必要信息的问题形式上有类比价值，但领域与机制相距较远，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph-XGB: A Hybrid GNN Framework for Outdoor-to-Indoor 5G Signal Reconstruction via Aerial-Only UAV RF Sensing

**abstract/A1：** Delivering robust In-Building Coverage (IBC) is critically impeded by severe Outdoor-to-Indoor (O2I) attenuation, a constraint that intensifies as deployments move from 5G mid-band towards mmWave and prospective 6G sub-THz carriers, where losses of 60-80 dB have been reported across modern building envelopes. While Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer scalable exterior RF sensing platforms, a fundamental inversion problem persists: interior 3D signal states cannot be directly sampled from constrained exterior flight paths. This paper proposes Graph-XGB, a hybrid inference framework that decouples spatial topology reasoning from non-linear signal regression to resolve this challenge. A modified SpiderGNN encoder, operating on a topology-aware graph constructed with a physically grounded Floor-Aware 3D Distance metric and Bridge Edge Guarantees, propagates UAV boundary measurements into 128-dimensional indoor embeddings. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) compresses these into a 10-dimensional manifold retaining 96.6% of embedding variance, fused with raw spatial features and decoded by independent, physics-tailored XGBoost regressors. This decoupling mitigates the optimization conflict between smoothly decaying path-loss signals and volatile interference-dependent metrics. Evaluated on a real-world 5G NR dataset of 613 verified nodes spanning the 10 floors of a single building, Graph-XGB attains the strongest accuracy among the architectures compared, with $R^2$ of 0.759 (RSSI) and 0.770 (RSRP), improvements of 35.5% and 64.9% over the SimpleGNN baseline, using no interior RF measurement as a model input; indoor observations enter only as training supervision, with evaluation on held-out internal nodes. Interference-dependent targets (RSRQ, SNR and CQI) remain bounded well below the path-loss metrics, delimiting the reach of purely spatial inference.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables. Accepted manuscript of a chapter published in BESC 2026, Lecture Notes in Computer Science 17175 (Springer, 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [A Sparsity Analysis of Traffic Forecasting Architectures: How Much Missing Data is Too Much?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26847

**作者：** Mohamed Hamad、Bilal Mutahir、Mohamed Mabrok、Nizar Zorba、Hamid Saeedi、Halim Yanikomeroglu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在两种交通基准上，对 GNN、Transformer 与 selective state-space 三类预测架构按从无缺失到传感器全失的稀疏程度做系统评测，并定义可用性阈值，发现全数据精度与稀疏鲁棒性之间存在明显取舍。

**相关性简评：** 其观测稀疏下的退化曲线与「可用性阈值」思路可能可供核查 VLM 在视觉证据缺失或筛选时的推理可靠性与停止决策；但对象为交通时序预测，与 latent visual reasoning 的迁移性仍需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Sparsity Analysis of Traffic Forecasting Architectures: How Much Missing Data is Too Much?

**abstract/A1：** Traffic forecasting models are typically evaluated under ideal conditions with complete data. This evaluation does not reflect the frequent sensor failures and communication outages common in real-world deployments. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of multivariate traffic prediction models to evaluate their performance under varying levels of sensor data loss during inference. We benchmark several architectural paradigms, including graph neural networks, attention-based Transformers, and selective state-space models, across sparsity levels ranging from zero to complete sensor loss, using two widely used multi-channel traffic benchmarks. To ensure consistent comparison across traffic variables, we use normalized metrics and define a usability threshold to identify when predictions become unreliable. Our results show a clear trade-off between baseline accuracy and robustness: models with the highest accuracy under full-data conditions often degrade most sharply as data becomes sparse, while less accurate models maintain more stable performance. These findings show that model selection should be based on the expected reliability of the sensor network rather than peak accuracy alone. This work provides a practical framework for assessing traffic forecasting models and highlights the importance of robustness in real-world applications.

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Scoliosis5K: Multimodal Gait Analysis via Clip-Conditioned RGB-Silhouette Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26849

**作者：** Zizhao Peng、Zihan Wang、Zheng Lv、Yan Wang、Ping Li、Fengwei An

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26849v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 Scoliosis5K 多站点步态数据集（RGB 人像裁剪、二值轮廓、二维姿态按帧对齐），在预训练 RGB 编码器内用各 patch 相对片段均值的像素偏移加权低秩更新，再融合轮廓序列与区域核岭回归分数，做受试者级脊柱侧弯步态分类，并报告完整系统与冻结编码器、置零轮廓向量下的对照结果。

**相关性简评：** 医学步态分类本身不在主线上，但其在固定权重下置零某一视觉证据通道以度量该通道对预测的边际贡献，可供核查 LVR 中「latent 是否真正使用视觉证据」的贡献分配与消融设计思路；用片段均值偏差调制编码器更新的做法也可能为视觉旁路适配提供线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scoliosis5K: Multimodal Gait Analysis via Clip-Conditioned RGB-Silhouette Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Color walking video records body boundary motion alongside clothing folds, surface appearance, and recording-environment cues. To examine what an explicit binary silhouette contributes to participant-level classification of scoliosis-related gait when a classifier already observes the paired color sequence, we construct Scoliosis5K, a multi-site collection of 4,998 corridor-walking sequences across 3,010 participant identifiers in which RGB person crops, binary silhouettes, and two-dimensional poses share each recording identifier and frame index. Our architecture uses each patch's pixel deviation from its clip mean to weight two low-rank query and value updates inside a pretrained RGB encoder, fusing the resulting video representation with a silhouette transformer sequence and regional kernel ridge scores extracted from one native-resolution RGB frame per window. Across 988 participants in five participant-disjoint cross-validation folds and three training seeds, the complete system reaches 83.77% accuracy and 79.91% macro-averaged F1, compared with 83.27% accuracy and 79.56% macro-F1 when the video RGB encoder remains frozen under identical inputs. Zeroing the silhouette vector at fixed weights shifts overall accuracy by only 0.10 percentage points while raising positive-category recall from 68.52% to 77.78% and lowering recall for both remaining categories. Macro-averaged AUROC is 0.950 across the cohort and 0.879 among participant pairs matched on acquisition site and recording-count group.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages (8-page main paper and 3-page supplementary material), 3 figures, 12 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [Beyond Demographic Discrimination: Benchmarking Normative-Descriptive Calibration in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26870v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26870

**作者：** Radhika Bhargava、Shinibali Bhattacharyya、George Mihaila、Jay Prajapati、Anwesan Pal、Yue Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26870v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26870v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出「规范—描述校准」概念与 NDCB 基准，用 697 组匹配情景分别在「预测」和「决定」两种指令下提问，并以三项指标衡量 LLM 对同一候选的区分是否随框架改变。

**相关性简评：** 其匹配对加双框架对照的评测协议、以及候选顺序偏好（位置偏差）分析，可能为核查 latent 推理状态是否受 prompt 框架与位置扰动影响提供可借用的评价设计，但需较多迁移改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Demographic Discrimination: Benchmarking Normative-Descriptive Calibration in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in socially consequential settings, where they must distinguish between normative reasoning (what should happen) and descriptive reasoning (what is likely to happen). Most fairness benchmarks evaluate demographic discrimination under a single reasoning objective, and none we know of tests whether a model treats the same item differently when asked to describe an outcome versus decide one. We introduce normative--descriptive calibration, the extent to which a model's differentiation between matched candidates depends on whether it is asked to predict or to decide, and measure it with the Normative--Descriptive Calibration Benchmark (NDCB): 697 matched-pair scenarios across six domains, each posed under both framings, and three metrics, Normative Engagement Rate (NER), Normative Leakage Rate (NLR), and Normative--Descriptive Separation (NDS). Across six LLMs, NDS separates 13 of 15 model pairs, against 7 for BBQ accuracy and none for BOLD, although these counts also reflect each benchmark's sample size. Claude Sonnet 4.6 and Claude Opus 4.6 separate the framings most strongly (average NDS $= +0.55$ and $+0.52$, with overlapping confidence intervals), whereas GPT-5.5's average NDS is negative ($-0.14$) under the single-pick instruction and near zero ($+0.02$) without it. In a simplified prompt template, removing single trigger words never changes the sign of a model's average NDS but can shift it by up to 0.46 and reorder models. Reversing candidate order reveals a further effect: when forced to choose between identical candidates, all six models favor whichever is listed first, and once order is averaged out their demographic-aligned lean is toward the marginalized candidate. Describing disparities and prescribing them are separable behaviors that differ across models.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Self-similar Organization of Information in Language](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26935

**作者：** Leon Lufkin、Cutter Dawes、Kamesh Krishnamurthy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一套关于语言中预测信息组织的理论，基于一族信息度量的标度行为，并在长文档中观察到跨尺度的标度塌缩，说明预测信息在每个可测尺度上均存在且呈自相似结构。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息在不同历史长度上如何分布、何时饱和」的量化框架，可能为 LVR 中跨尺度视觉证据筛选、动态计算分配与长度泛化的预测提供问题表述和评价视角，但未涉及多模态或 latent 状态，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-similar Organization of Information in Language

**abstract/A1：** How much information relevant to predicting the future of a passage (i.e. predictive information) is carried by the preceding text, and how is this information distributed along that history? These questions are fundamental to understanding both the statistical structure of language and what large language models (LLMs) can learn about it. Indeed, LLMs trained to predict the next token in text must exploit this predictive information, so its organization determines how the structure of language becomes available to the model to learn. We develop a theory for the organization of predictive information based on the scaling behavior of a family of information measures, and the theory predicts that if this organization has the same form at all measured length scales -- i.e., it has a self-similar structure -- then the information measures evaluated at different lengths collapse onto a common form. We observe this scaling collapse in long text documents, showing that predictive information is present at every length scale we measure and has a self-similar structure. Self-similarity is a considerably stronger property than showing the existence of long-range dependencies, and it constrains the process generating the text. More broadly, our results give quantitative support to the empirical observation that conditioning on more distant text continues to reveal new structure in language to LLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [CENTRY: A Probabilistic Latent-State Model for Continual AI Risk Estimation, Monitoring, and Regulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26940v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26940

**作者：** Bonny Banerjee、Geetha Raju

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26940v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26940v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CENTRY 概率框架，把 AI 风险归因于数据、硬件、模型三类内在来源，用结构化隐变量模型（一个快速交互子空间加三个慢速风险子空间）结合变分下界进行持续风险估计、监测与调节，并给出安全性、收敛、可辨识性等理论刻画。

**相关性简评：** 其快/慢隐子空间分解与隐风险状态的可辨识性、一致性分析，可能为 LVR 中区分承载任务内容的 latent 状态与无关状态、以及状态更新的贡献归属提供概念参考，但领域相距较远，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CENTRY: A Probabilistic Latent-State Model for Continual AI Risk Estimation, Monitoring, and Regulation

**abstract/A1：** Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, yet existing AI safety frameworks primarily define, categorize, or govern AI risk rather than proposing implementable models and methods for continually estimating, monitoring, and regulating risk throughout an AI system's operational lifetime. This paper proposes CENTRY, a probabilistic framework that enables continual risk estimation, monitoring, and regulation of a deployed AI system from its interactions with its environment. The framework models AI risk as arising from three intrinsic sources: datasets, hardware resources, and models/algorithms. A structured latent-variable model is developed in which the latent representation is decomposed into a fast interaction subspace that captures the evolving AI--environment dynamics and three slow latent subspaces that encode the long-term risk states associated with the dataset, hardware, and model components. Rather than requiring a ground-truth risk label at every time step, the component-risk states are treated as latent variables and are learned by maximizing a variational evidence lower bound, optionally informed during training and continual refinement by evidence-supported behavior--cause records. The resulting framework unifies continual probabilistic inference, online risk estimation, and adaptive risk regulation in an implementable architecture. Under the explicit assumptions stated for the corresponding results, we establish formal guarantees spanning safety, learning, inference, and operations, including finite-horizon and asymptotic risk-budget compliance, regret and convergence bounds for adaptive regulation, consistency and identifiability of component-risk ratings, and detection-delay, false-alarm, containment, and computational-complexity characterizations.

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [Distributional Algebro-Geometric Persistence (dAGP): Wasserstein-Stable Polynomial-Relation Signatures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26986v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26986

**作者：** Edisy Kin Wai Chan、Andersen Ang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26986v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26986v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 dAGP：用正交多项式特征下的矩矩阵 G\_m(μ) 的小特征值方向刻画在分布上近似消失的低阶多项式关系，并按次数与容差统计得到双参数整数签名，同时给出其在 1-Wasserstein 距离下的 Lipschitz 稳定性与实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「小特征值即近似退化关系」的谱计数思路可能可供核查用于检测视觉 latent 分布沿深度或位置的低维坍缩与视觉信息丢失，但原文面向点云分布的几何签名，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Distributional Algebro-Geometric Persistence (dAGP): Wasserstein-Stable Polynomial-Relation Signatures

**abstract/A1：** A circle and a quartic superellipse have the same homology, but their defining equations have degrees two and four, respectively. We study this algebraic distinction for point-cloud distributions. For a probability measure $μ$ on $Ω=\[-1,1\]^d$ and an orthonormal polynomial feature map $φ\_m$ of degree $m$, the moment matrix $G\_m(μ)=\\mathbb{E}\_{X\\simμ}\[φ\_m(X)φ\_m(X)^\\top\]$ satisfies $c^\\top G\_m(μ)c=\\mathbb{E}\_μ\[f\_c(X)^2\]$, so coefficient directions associated with its small eigenvalues represent low-degree polynomials that nearly vanish on $μ$. Counting these near-relation directions across degree $m$ and the tolerance $\\varepsilon$ gives a two-parameter integer signature. We call this representation distributional algebro-geometric persistence (dAGP), the population counterpart of the point-cloud algebro-geometric persistence (AGP) construction. For empirical measures, dAGP is the sample-normalised counterpart of point-cloud AGP. We also show that $μ\\mapsto G\_m(μ)$ is Lipschitz in the $1$-Wasserstein distance, so its ordered eigenvalues vary Lipschitz-continuously under transport of mass, while its threshold counts remain unchanged under perturbations satisfying a spectral-margin condition. We give an explicit support-localised Lipschitz constant and identify the regime in which the resulting guarantee is informative. Experimentally, the population noise-floor prediction agrees with the finite-sample mean to $0.1\\%$, within sampling error. At a selected tolerance, relation onset orders the conic, quartic, and non-algebraic closed curves, while their count profiles distinguish them from the two-dimensional control. Calibration identifies an empirical tolerance range. A $100$-repetition sensitivity study quantifies onset recovery and shows that the count grid and two continuous spectral baselines nearly saturate the four-family discrimination task.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 7 figures. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Geometric Distributional Deep Learning (GDDL)

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [JevRisk: Zero-Shot Financial Risk Assessment with a Language Decision Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26999v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26999

**作者：** Yaodong Yang、Li Huang、Junpeng Liang、Yi Ma、Hongyao Tang、Xingyu Fan、Jinpeng Li、Furui Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26999v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26999v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 JevRisk：先用代码依监管规则算出精确的金融风险指标，再由语言决策模型结合客户画像与活动记录做情境化判断，输出零样本风险评分，并在反洗钱与信用风险任务上评测。

**相关性简评：** 「先精确计算证据、再由语言模型情境化判断」的分解可供核查外部证据如何进入模型的推理状态并左右决策，其无标签的决策评价也可能启发 STOP/判定环节的评估；但领域与视觉推理距离较远，联系仅为类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** JevRisk: Zero-Shot Financial Risk Assessment with a Language Decision Model

**abstract/A1：** Banks must monitor every customer's financial risk, such as money laundering and credit default, by interpreting financial activity in the context of the customer's profile. Existing risk monitoring systems of banks are mostly based on expert-designed risk indicators, which apply predefined thresholds to every customer and cannot adapt to their personal situations. Supervised models recognise risk patterns from labelled customers, but each label often needs an expert's review or months of repayment history, which is slow and costly. Financial risk assessment therefore calls for a zero-shot decision system that minimises the need for labels and generalises across customers and risk types. To this end, we present JevRisk, a system for zero-shot financial risk assessment with a language decision model. JevRisk works in two steps. First, following regulatory guidelines in the finance domain, JevRisk computes exact risk indicators with code. Second, the language decision model (e.g., \\jev{}) reads these indicators together with the customer's profile and activity, judges the case in context with broad world knowledge, and returns the score of high risk. We evaluate JevRisk on the anti-money laundering task with public synthetic data from Scotiabank and on the credit risk task with real data from Home Credit and Lending Club. On both tasks, JevRisk outperforms rule-based and unsupervised methods, and is on par with or better than supervised methods (e.g., GBDT and TabPFN) trained on about a thousand labelled customers.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 1 figure, 15 tables, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Many Plans at Once: Diverse Top-Quality Planning in Continuous Time](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27012v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27012

**作者：** Mahyar Jahani-nasab、Hamid Rezatofighi、Mor Vered、Buser Say

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27012v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27012v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MPAO，面向连续时间物理系统的多方案规划：采样满足边界条件、未经训练的神经网络生成轨迹，训练同分布网络共享的修正项，保存所有已验证方案，并依据已发现结果自适应采样，从而在给定预算内输出多个低成本且互相区分的方案。

**相关性简评：** 其「低成本加区分度阈值」的多假设保留、以新发现驱动再采样的 anytime 机制，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的多样性维持、候选证据筛选与动态计算/停止决策提供类比线索，尚需核查其标准能否对应推理状态而非仅轨迹空间。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Many Plans at Once: Diverse Top-Quality Planning in Continuous Time

**abstract/A1：** Planners for physical systems usually return a single trajectory, which leaves no alternative when an unmodeled constraint invalidates the chosen route or downstream preferences change. A set of plans computed in advance provides ready-made alternatives and warm-starts for fast adaptation, but only if the plans are both low-cost and mutually distinct. Discrete planning addresses this with top-quality and diverse planning, but these ideas do not transfer to continuous time, where every valid trajectory has infinitely many valid near-copies, and existing trajectory planners converge to one or a few solutions. We present Many Plans at Once (MPAO), which treats every plan it finds as a reason to search elsewhere: it samples untrained neural networks whose outputs meet the boundary conditions by construction, trains corrections shared by networks from the same distribution, stores every validated plan, and adapts its sampling to what it has found, which makes it anytime. We define the goal by bringing the cost bound of top-quality planning and the distinctness threshold of diverse planning to continuous time. Under equal budgets against five baselines, MPAO is the only method that solves all 40 instances across four domains. On contact-rich manipulation, it is the only method with plans within 25% of the best known cost on one task, and returns about ten times as many such plans as the strongest baseline on the other two.

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [Ruby: State-of-the-Art Potency Prediction across Discovery Regimes Using Learned Structural Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27015v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27015

**作者：** Michael Atkin、Dominique Beaini、Jules Berman、Rebecca Boiarsky、Jamin Chen、Alejandro Dobles、Sergey Edunov、Evan Feinberg、Nate Gruver、Will Harvey、Deidre Johns、Nina Jovic、Steven Kearnes、Allison Keys、Rohit Kundu、Ken Leidal、Gerald Lelais、Michael V. Levine、Indrawan McAlpine、Gabriel Monteiro da Silva、Igor Morozov、Pranav Murugan、Adi Nagle、Frank Pickard、Wesley Qian、Gianluca Scarpellini、Ansh Sharma、Richard Strong Bowen、Wojtek Swiderski、Laura Tapias、Carl Tilbury、Cyr Vetcher、Han Wang、William Wang、David Charles Williams、Drausin Wulsin、Han Yoon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27015v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27015v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Ruby 是小分子效价预测器，复用结构基础模型 Pearl 的中间表示且不要求每个化合物有显式结合构象，同时发布带溯源与审计标注的公开训练数据集，并在多个公开基准和零样本实验中取得领先。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其可调推理开销在两个数量级上权衡富集率与计算成本、并随累积实验数据再校准，可为 THINK/STOP 的计算分配与停止决策评价提供远距离类比，机制是否可迁移可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ruby: State-of-the-Art Potency Prediction across Discovery Regimes Using Learned Structural Representations

**abstract/A1：** Small-molecule drug discovery proceeds through repeated design-make-test-analyze cycles. The goal is often to maximize potency while keeping other properties within the ranges required for a viable therapeutic candidate. Compound prioritization places different demands on computational methods as drug discovery progresses from hit identification through hit-to-lead and lead optimization, with changing computational budgets and increasing amounts of on-target experimental data. We introduce Ruby, a Robust Universal Binding affinitY predictor that uses intermediate representations from the structural foundation model Pearl without requiring an explicit binding pose for every compound. Ruby is trained on proprietary data plus GenesisPotency-2026, an audited public-source potency training dataset released for academic and commercial use under a CC BY-SA 4.0 license with row-level provenance and audit annotations. Ruby achieves the highest aggregate early enrichment across seven public hit-identification targets among evaluated methods, with adjustable inference effort trading off enrichment and computational cost over more than two orders of magnitude. Ruby achieves the highest average correlations across four public benchmarks: OpenFE, CASP16, FEP+ 4, and OpenBind. Ruby significantly outperforms other methods in zero-shot prediction across eight biochemical and cellular assays from five ongoing Genesis-Incyte programs, widening its performance advantage relative to the public benchmarks. Ruby continues to improve more than other methods when all are given the opportunity to recalibrate using earlier assay measurements. Together, these contributions provide an annotated training resource for the field and a state-of-the-art potency predictor for broad screening, prioritization within related chemical series, and assay-specific prediction as experimental measurements accumulate.

</details>
