### 175. [Integral Multipliers and Arithmetic Obstructions for Two-Hidden-Layer ReLU Maxima](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27018v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27018

**作者：** Pengcheng Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、math.CO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27018v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27018v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究用整数参数、两层隐藏层的 ReLU 网络精确表示最大值函数，给出六输入与七输入 max 的最小正整数输出倍数界，并借助整立方填充、同调障碍与有限构造证书刻画七输入问题的剩余困难。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其关于 ReLU 网络精确表示能力与不可绕过障碍的结论，可能可供核查「内部状态能否精确承载某类比较/选择型函数」这一表示容量问题，联系较远且未涉及推理动态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Integral Multipliers and Arithmetic Obstructions for Two-Hidden-Layer ReLU Maxima

**abstract/A1：** We study exact representations of maximum functions by ReLU networks with two hidden layers and integer parameters. The least positive output multiplier is two for the six-input maximum, and either two or six for the seven-input maximum. We obtain these bounds from integral cubical fillings and a coefficient-preserving passage from polytope identities to networks. For the coordinate-facet complex of the six-simplex, we compute a coning index of six over the integers and three after inverting two, allowing arbitrary integral directions within each facet. A class in the homology of the unrestricted kernel complex characterizes the remaining seven-input problem; its vanishing is equivalent to an integral filling of a fixed target. We also construct a finite character that separates rational from three-local representability in a dictionary containing two known constructions and all pairs of edge-disjoint three-edge forests. This obstruction permits nonsymmetric coefficients and is confined to the specified dictionary. Control of lower-dimensional remainders yields a sufficient criterion in the top-dimensional quotient. Exact certificates and verification programs accompany the finite calculations.

**comment/C1：** 22

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Mind World Models: Joint Learning of Motor EEG Representations and Action-Conditioned World Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27029v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27029

**作者：** Weikang Qiu、Zheng Huang、Tinglin Huang、Yujun Yan、Rex Ying

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27029v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27029v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Mind World Model，将当前帧之前的 EEG 视为世界模型的 latent action：由「心模型」编码 EEG 并预测未来帧的表示，从而在无动作标注的自然 EEG 与第一视角视频上做运动表征预训练，并引入侧化感知 Transformer 分离左右半球共享与特异特征；在 9 项分类和 7 项回归任务上评测运动解码与动作条件下的预测。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线关联较弱，但把生理信号当作 latent action、用「预测未来帧表示」作为监督目标的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的可学习性、状态监督与预测目标设计提供类比线索，可供核查其 latent 是否承载实际运动信息。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mind World Models: Joint Learning of Motor EEG Representations and Action-Conditioned World Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Motor decoding from electroencephalography (EEG) supports non-invasive brain-computer interfaces, yet existing methods either rely on costly cue-based trials and coarse categorical labels or use pretraining objectives that do not directly capture how movement unfolds, limiting their ability to learn transferable motor representations. To this end, we propose the Mind World Model (MWM), a framework that learns motor EEG representations from scalable naturalistic EEG and egocentric video without action annotations. MWM treats EEG observed up to the current frame as the latent action of a world model: a mind model encodes the EEG, and a world model uses it to predict the representation of a future frame, yielding a movement-grounded pretraining objective. We further introduce the Lateralization-Aware Transformer (LAT), which uses frame averaging to separate shared bilateral from side-specific lateral features, encoding hemispheric correspondence to reduce the burden of learning left-right relationships from data. MWM pretraining produces two models: a mind model for downstream motor decoding and a world model that can be adapted to prediction conditioned on real actions, opening a scalable and flexible path to collect egocentric data for embodied AI. Across 9 classification and 7 regression tasks, the mind model improves task-averaged balanced accuracy by 5.9% and Pearson correlation by 16.2% relative to the strongest baseline in each task family. Compared with an inverse-dynamics latent-action baseline, the world model achieves lower prediction error and benefits 3.3 times more from matched real actions within recordings and 4.7 times more on held-out subjects.

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [Action-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27044v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27044

**作者：** Yutong Xia、Yangyou Liu、Yuxuan Liang、Yuankai Wu、See-Kiong Ng、Roger Zimmermann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27044v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27044v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PairST 基准，在交通、输电与配水网络上构造来自同一状态的配对参考／干预未来，以分别评估受影响区域精度与静默区域误响应；并提出 ActionST，在无干预参考预测上写入显式响应，由节点-时间寻址查询、状态条件响应写入器和图感知局部性门控共同决定响应强度。

**相关性简评：** 其反事实配对评价与「局部性门控的残差式状态写入」可能为 LVR 提供线索：如何判断 latent 状态是否因果地使用了视觉证据，以及如何限制证据注入的强度与范围；跨领域迁移尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Action-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** We study spatio-temporal forecasting in networked systems under specified actions. The challenge is to capture the action's effects without predicting spurious changes in unaffected regions. However, direct supervision and evaluation of these effects require paired futures with and without the action, whereas observational trajectories reveal only the realized future. We introduce PairST, a benchmark comprising six datasets across traffic, power transmission and water distribution, with paired reference and intervened futures simulated from the same system state. These pairs enable separate evaluation of affected-region forecast accuracy and quiet-region response error. We further propose ActionST, which forecasts the intervened future by writing an explicit response onto an intervention-independent reference forecast. Specifically, node-time addressing constructs a query for each response position, a state-conditioned response writer computes candidate responses, and a graph-aware locality gate controls their strength. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ActionST at new action targets, with the lowest mean absolute error on five datasets and the best average rank. Our source code and the PairST benchmark are available at https://github.com/yutong-xia/ActionST.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [Rethinking Cross-Sensor Domain Adaptation for Bridge Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27060v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27060

**作者：** Linghui Huang、Zipeng Wang、Shunjie Chen、Peng Guan、Jiandong Wang、Siqing Li、Luming Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27060v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27060v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文重新审视桥梁点云 TLS→MLS 跨传感器域适应协议，分析采样密度、表面粗糙度、颜色与采集视角在 8 cm 稀疏体素表示中的体现，并在不同训练轮次下比较自适应 BN、熵最小化、Mean Teacher 与置信度自训练的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其用受控输入扰动（如 4 mm 位移仅改变 4.4% 体素占用、颜色置零几乎无影响）来归因哪些视觉因素真正进入表示，并指出评测方差与多种方法增益相当，可能为核查 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留及增益贡献分配提供借鉴，但领域距离较远，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Cross-Sensor Domain Adaptation for Bridge Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Segmentation models for bridge point clouds lose accuracy when trained on terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) data and applied to mobile laser scanning (MLS) data. This study re-examines the SemanticBridge TLS-to-MLS protocol to determine how sampling density, surface roughness, color, and acquisition viewpoint are expressed in the 8-cm sparse-voxel representation, and how adaptation gains depend on source-model convergence. Using one sparse-convolution U-Net, we combine raw-point statistics with controlled input perturbations and compare adaptive batch normalization, entropy minimization, Mean Teacher, and confidence-filtered self-training under 100- and 300-epoch schedules. TLS and MLS have similar nearest-neighbor spacing after preprocessing (12.08 and 12.02 mm), and a 4-mm perturbation of TLS points changes voxel occupancy by only 4.4%. Color distributions differ systematically, but zeroing color on identical voxels changes target mean intersection over union (mIoU) by only +0.02, and by +0.06 after the color augmentation is scale-corrected. The sensors also observe different portions of the bridge, yielding different class proportions. A 300-epoch source-only model obtains 57.80 mIoU on the paired-bridge protocol. Confidence-filtered self-training improves the 100-epoch baseline, but from a converged model it changes target mIoU by -0.97 (source-selected checkpoints) and -2.44 (final-50-epoch means). Repeated evaluation (standard deviation 0.27-0.64) and seed variation (1.88 across three seeds) are comparable to several method differences. Adaptation results on this benchmark therefore depend on representation scale, source-model convergence, and evaluation variability.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [V2VFusion: Text-Controlled Video-to-Video Diffusion for Degradation-Aware Video Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27063v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27063

**作者：** Jiajun Chen、Han Xu、Yunfei Huang、Zhuoya Zhang、Guangcan Liu、Jiayi Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27063v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27063v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 V2VFusion，将多源退化视频融合重构为 latent 扩散空间中的视频到视频条件生成，并结合文本引导的退化条件 ControlNet 与状态调制的分层 MoE 处理复合退化、维持时序一致。

**相关性简评：** 其按扩散 latent 状态筛选专家并路由的机制，可能为 LVR 中依据内部推理状态动态选择计算或更新模块提供线索；但研究任务是视频融合与退化抑制，与视觉推理的证据使用和停止决策无直接联系，值得核查其状态表示是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** V2VFusion: Text-Controlled Video-to-Video Diffusion for Degradation-Aware Video Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Video fusion integrates complementary information in multiple source video streams into a single sequence for complete scene perception. Existing methods perform frame-wise fusion and lack unified spatio-temporal modeling across sequences, damaging temporal coherence across sequences. When dealing with degradations, they also ignore temporal dynamics and overload a single network with diverse degradations, leading to limited and imbalanced degradation suppression. To address these issues, we propose V2VFusion, the first unified video-to-video fusion framework that directly generates temporally coherent, high-quality fused videos from degraded multi-source inputs. By reformulating video fusion as a video-to-video conditional generation problem in latent diffusion space, our approach holistically models spatio-temporal dependencies across entire sequences and intrinsically ensures sequence-level consistency. On this basis, a text-guided degradation-conditioned ControlNet bridges high-level textual degradation descriptions with low-level visual priors, enabling interpretable and controllable guidance for structure-preserving fusion beyond purely data-driven conditioning. To handle heterogeneous and compound degradations, a state-modulated degradation-aware hierarchical mixture-of-experts module decouples expert selection into diffusion-state-aware filtering and degradation-aware routing. It prevents unreliable routing and fosters expert specialization to mitigate representation conflicts among diverse degradations. Experiments on infrared-visible, multi-exposure, and multi-focus video fusion tasks and complex degradations validate that V2VFusion surpasses existing methods in fusion quality and temporal stability. The code is available at https://github.com/ChenDoubleJ/V2VFusion.

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [Online Learning for Minimizing Maximum Tardiness in Single-Machine Scheduling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27067v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27067

**作者：** Yiyang Chen、Kohei Hatano、Eiji Takimoto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27067v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27067v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究单机调度中最小化最大延迟（OMMT）的在线学习：每轮先选一个固定作业的排列，环境随后揭示处理时间与截止期，并以 α-regret 与最优固定排列比较；随机情形给出 FTL 的 regret 界与复杂度，对抗情形证明下界并用 FTPL 得到对 n 的对数级 α 依赖。

**相关性简评：** 其「每轮先决策、反馈事后到达、用 α-regret 对比最优固定策略」的在线学习框架，可能可供核查用于形式化 latent 推理中的动态计算分配与停止策略及其评价，但与原论文问题相距较远，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Learning for Minimizing Maximum Tardiness in Single-Machine Scheduling

**abstract/A1：** We study online learning for minimizing maximum tardiness (\\OMMT) in single-machine scheduling. In each round, the learner selects a permutation of \\(n\\) fixed jobs, representing a schedule. The environment then reveals a processing time and a due date for each job, and the learner incurs a vector-valued loss whose components correspond to the tardiness of the individual jobs. The goal of the learner is to minimize \\(α\\)-regret, the difference between the learner's maximum cumulative tardiness and \\(α\\) times that of the best fixed permutation in hindsight, for as small \\(α\\) as possible. We investigate OMMT under both stochastic and adversarial environments. In the stochastic setting, we prove that the expected \\(1\\)-regret of the Follow-the-Leader (FTL) algorithm is \\( O\\!\\left(\\frac{n\\log n}{Δ^2}\\right) \\), together with a corresponding high-probability bound, where \\(Δ\\) is a distribution-dependent gap parameter. At round \\(t \\in \\{1,\\dots,T\\}\\), FTL runs in \\(O(tn^2)\\) time and \\(O(tn)\\) space. In the adversarial setting, we prove that, for any \\(0&lt;c&lt;\\log\_2 n/16\\), the \\(α\\)-regret is \\(Ω(T)\\) whenever \\(α&lt;\\log\_2 n/16-c\\). We then reduce OMMT to a surrogate online learning problem and apply Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL), obtaining an expected \\(α\\)-regret bound of \\(O(n^2\\sqrt{T})\\) for \\(α=O(\\log n)\\). Thus, the logarithmic dependence of \\(α\\) on \\(n\\) is tight up to constant factors. Each FTPL update admits a mixed-integer programming formulation whose size is polynomial in \\(n\\) and \\(t\\).

**comment/C1：** Appears in the Proceedings of the 52nd International Conference on Current Trends in Theory and Practice of Computer Science (Sofsem 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [Reliable Behavior Discovery via False Discovery Rate Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27104v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27104

**作者：** David Madras、Zi Wang、Marco Andreetto、Ken Burke、Caroline Pantofaru、Utsav Prabhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27104v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27104v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把大生成模型中的行为发现形式化为一类问题，强调需控制误发现率（FDR），并提出 DISCO：基于 coarsened e-processes 的迭代搜索方法，在温和条件下可证明控制 FDR，且通过适应数据依赖的假设数量提升检验功效，在合成数据与「模型既是发现引擎又是对象」的实证设定中验证。

**相关性简评：** 其多重假设检验与 FDR 控制框架可能为 LVR 中大量候选 latent 假设、视觉证据筛选的可靠性提供统计表述，可供核查在假设数自适应且数据依赖时如何约束误发现，但与 VLM 推理内部状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliable Behavior Discovery via False Discovery Rate Control

**abstract/A1：** Reliable methods for discovering behavior patterns in large generative models are increasingly essential as models become both more complex and capable. We formalize behavior discovery as a problem class of interest and highlight the task of controlling false discovery rate (FDR) as crucial for guaranteeing reliability in behavior discovery. Building upon the multiple hypothesis testing literature, we propose DISCO (Discovery through Iterative Search with COarsened $e$-processes), a method which provably controls FDR in behavior discovery under mild conditions. DISCO improves power over previous related eBH-based approaches by allowing for adaptivity to a data-dependent number of hypotheses to be tested. We demonstrate empirically that DISCO controls FDR with higher power than comparable approaches on both synthetic data and a novel empirical evaluation of FDR-controlling methods in settings where large generative models act as both discovery engines and targets. Code is available at https://github.com/google-deepmind/disco .

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [RemoteEvolver: Expanding Earth Observation Capabilities through Agent Harness Evolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27110v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27110

**作者：** Shengxiang Xu、Liang Yao、Shuyi Chen、Yishu Wang、Shimin Di、Fan Liu、Bishun Yao、Hanghui Guo、Xinyu Liu、Chengye Liang、Yuyu Luo、Min-Ling Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27110v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27110v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RemoteEvolver：在基座模型参数固定的前提下，用一个外部 harness 依据执行反馈演化遥感智能体的工具与工作流，按需获取缺失工具并把已验证的执行经验蒸馏为可跨任务复用的技能，从而在后续任务中共同支撑规划与执行。

**相关性简评：** 其「识别能力缺口—按需调用工具—验证后沉淀技能」的循环，或可为 LVR 中何时补充视觉证据、如何复用成功轨迹提供问题表述上的启发；但该方法停留在 harness 层，不涉及 latent 状态表示与生成，借鉴价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RemoteEvolver: Expanding Earth Observation Capabilities through Agent Harness Evolution

**abstract/A1：** We are interested in developing a continually self-improving remote sensing agentic system. It should identify and address its capability gaps, retaining the resulting improvements to enhance performance on subsequent tasks. However, existing systems typically rely on fixed toolsets and confine their experience accumulation to re-orchestrating predefined capabilities, constraining both capability expansion and the transfer of improvements across tasks. As the infrastructure for agentic systems, the harness manages tools and workflow knowledge as editable, persistent resources outside the base model. These resources can therefore be updated and reused across tasks without additional post-training. Based on these observations, we propose RemoteEvolver, an external harness for remote sensing agents that drives tool and workflow evolution through execution feedback. With the base model parameters fixed, RemoteEvolver acquires missing specialized tools on demand and distills verified successful execution experience from individual tasks into agent skills that can be reused across tasks. The acquired tools and accumulated skills jointly support planning and execution on subsequent tasks. Experiments on Earth-Bench and OpenEarthAgent-Bench show that RemoteEvolver achieves average accuracy gains of 8.18 and 23.86 percentage points over its initial state when tools are fully available and partially missing. Under the same settings, it also outperforms the state-of-the-art methods by an average of 6.77 and 17.10 percentage points, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [CoEvoBench: Can LLMs Drive Recursive Co-Evolution of Agentic Systems?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27116v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27116

**作者：** Chuan Xiao、Baojie Wang、Haizhao Fan、Hongzhe Du、Xander Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27116v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoEvoBench：让 LLM 充当研究者，在 24 小时预算内联合改进目标 agent 的模型与 harness，并在独立测试任务上评分以考察五个 agentic 领域的递归协同演化能力。

**相关性简评：** 其「两个相互耦合的组件中该改哪一方」的评测设定，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据获取、latent 推理与停止决策之间的贡献分配问题类比，可供核查；但与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoEvoBench: Can LLMs Drive Recursive Co-Evolution of Agentic Systems?

**abstract/A1：** The performance of an LLM agent depends on both its model and its harness, the code that mediates its interaction with tools and environments. Can LLM researchers drive this co-evolution as a recursive improvement process, deciding round after round which component to change when each change alters the value of the other? Existing benchmarks evaluate post-training or data research with the harness out of scope, or harness optimization with the model fixed, leaving this joint decision unmeasured. We introduce CoEvoBench, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs as researchers co-evolving the model and harness of a target agent across five agentic domains. Researchers start from a common base model and initial harness, train and revise within a 24-hour budget, and submit a model-harness pair that is scored on separate test tasks. Across five frontier researchers, the strongest closes more than four fifths of the gap to an instruction-tuned reference system, and co-evolution succeeds on interaction tasks but rarely on long-horizon coding. These results suggest that current LLMs can make a strong first improvement, but cannot yet turn feedback from an improved system into the next one. CoEvoBench provides a controlled testbed for measuring the research capabilities that recursive co-evolution requires.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 6 figures. Code: https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/CoEvoBench

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [Portable Active Recovery under Timed and Position-Dependent Visual Outages](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27121v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27121

**作者：** Jinyuan Zhang、Shengshuo Jiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27121v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27121v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把四旋翼在视觉中断后的恢复建模为最坏条件下的主动可观测性问题，在无外部定位的立体视觉惯性平台上提出零重训的可插拔恢复模块，协调视觉丢失检测、限 jerk 制动、有界后退探测、稳定回归与高度积分微调，并在 1,920 次飞行的预注册基准上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是仅凭观测“悬停等待还是主动移动以重新获取视觉证据”，与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的收益判断、证据可获取性区分可能有概念联系，可供核查；但属机器人控制，不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Portable Active Recovery under Timed and Position-Dependent Visual Outages

**abstract/A1：** When a camera blackout depends on physical position rather than elapsed time, stable hovering prevents visual recovery and mission completion. On stereo visual-inertial quadrotors without external localization, an exogenous timed blackout and an endogenous position-dependent dark zone appear identical at loss onset yet require opposite responses: hover-and-wait completes 93/96 timed blackouts but 0/96 spatial outages (13 hard failures), whereas blind continuation traverses many spatial zones yet fails heavily during timed blackouts. We formulate visual-outage recovery as a worst-condition active-observability problem and introduce a portable recovery module at the reference interface of an unchanged host controller with zero retraining. Using deployable telemetry alone, the module coordinates visual-loss detection, jerk-limited braking, bounded backward probing, settled rejoining, height-integral trim, and an optional gated prior-map branch. On a preregistered 48-scene benchmark (1,920 flights), our full MPPI configuration reaches 88.5% \[81.2, 94.8\] worst-condition completion, outperforming development-selected DATT-L1 by +29.2 pp \[17.7, 40.6\] and exceeding all six external baselines after Bonferroni correction. Attached without retraining to SimpleFlight, the module lifts worst-condition completion by +17.7 pp \[7.3, 28.1\] to 86.5% (and 90% across 1.0x/1.25x speeds, 0/80 hard failures). Trace diagnostics reveal that survival hinges on visual-inertial re-acquisition asymmetry at vision return -- not blind IMU drift suppression - and uncover a bias-drift map crossover confirmed on 72 held-out EuRoC windows.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 6 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [TrajRealm: A Large-Scale Point Tracking Dataset with Diverse Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27136v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27136

**作者：** Ni Yang、Weiguang Zhao、Jiafei Cao、Yankai Jiang、Haoyu Guo、Tao Yang、Chunhua Shen、Junting Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27136v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27136v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** TrajRealm 构建了首个达到千万帧规模的公开合成点跟踪数据集，融合游戏与具身仿真环境，提供 RGB、深度、相机参数以及持续的 2D/3D 点轨迹，并配套含 Interaction 与 Locomotion 八个子类的基准来评测现有跟踪器。

**相关性简评：** 其长时点对应与几何一致的 3D 轨迹标注可能为 LVR 中跨帧保持视觉证据、维护持久视觉状态这类问题提供训练或评价线索，但能否与多模态推理状态耦合仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TrajRealm: A Large-Scale Point Tracking Dataset with Diverse Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** General-purpose point tracking requires large-scale training data with diverse dynamics, yet existing datasets struggle to combine rich dynamic content, accurate scene geometry, and sufficient scale. We introduce TrajRealm, the first public synthetic point-tracking dataset to reach the ten-million-frame scale, constructed from games and embodied simulations. TrajRealm uses the rich environments and motion systems of modern games to capture diverse targets, activities, and interactions across diverse worlds, while embodied simulations add robot manipulation and indoor navigation data. The dataset provides RGB observations, depth, camera parameters, and persistent 2D and 3D point trajectories. These annotations provide precise, geometrically consistent supervision across foreground objects and static environmental surfaces. We also build TrajRealm-Bench with eight subcategories under Interaction and Locomotion to evaluate tracking across diverse scenes. Benchmark evaluations reveal the strengths and limitations of existing trackers in handling complex motion and maintaining long-term point correspondences. Training on TrajRealm improves the performance of existing tracking methods on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 5 figures. Project page: https://purplehihi.github.io/TrajRealm/

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction for Long-tailed Graph Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27141v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27141

**作者：** Jiawei Chen、Wenjun Wang、Wei Yu、Qiyao Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27141v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27141v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向长尾图分类，提出 DMPRP：以图表示为查询，用受 KDEX 启发的动态专家记忆模块生成残差向量来增强原表示，并用头部图在节点、根子图与随机游走邻域三个粒度采样的共现三元组训练该模块。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「外部记忆＋专家路由生成残差来更新内部表示」以及多粒度共现监督，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态更新与多层级轨迹监督提供可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction for Long-tailed Graph Classification

**abstract/A1：** Long-tailed graph classification (LTGC) is common in real-world graph datasets, where a few large head graphs provide rich structural information while many smaller tail graphs offer fewer structural cues, making it harder to learn discriminative representations for tail graphs. To address this size-based imbalance, we propose Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction (DMPRP), which transfers structural patterns from head graphs to tail graphs. Given a graph representation as a query, a dynamic expert memory module inspired by Knowledge-diverse Experts (KDEX) generates a residual vector that augments the original representation. We train the module using co-occurrence triplets sampled from head graphs at the node, rooted subgraph, and random-walk neighborhood levels. Experiments on five graph classification benchmarks show that DMPRP improves tail performance, and ablation studies show that both the expert module and random-walk supervision contribute to these gains. A controlled experiment on PTC further examines pattern transfer when smaller graphs are denser than larger ones. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DMPRP-73A5.

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [How Does SAM-3 Adapt? Capacity, Update Geometry, and Domain Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27142v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27142

**作者：** Allan Kazakov、Abdurrahman Javat、Duygu Cakir

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27142v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27142v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 比较 LoRA、HiRA、KRAdapter、QuanTA 与全量微调在五个分割数据集上的适配效果，并用更新谱 SVD 分析与 LoveDA 域迁移实验解释为何不同方法性能相近。

**相关性简评：** 其「容量饱和、相似性能来自不同更新子空间」的结论可能为判断 LVR 中 latent 增益究竟源于容量还是特定表示子空间提供类比性分析视角，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Does SAM-3 Adapt? Capacity, Update Geometry, and Domain Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Promptable segmentation foundation models are increasingly used in specialized domains, but it remains unclear how SAM-3 should be fine-tuned when adapting it beyond its original training distribution. We study this question by comparing five adaptation families --LoRA, HiRA, KRAdapter, QuanTA, and Full Fine-Tuning-- across five segmentation datasets spanning medical imaging, remote sensing, and dense material segmentation. Across these datasets, compact parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods often match or approach Full Fine-Tuning in DSC and IoU, suggesting that strong in-domain adaptation does not always require updating the full model. However, this performance cannot be explained by nominal adapter rank or trainable parameter count alone. To investigate why different methods perform similarly, we analyze their update spectra and their behavior under LoveDA Urban/Rural transfer. Our SVD analysis shows that Full Fine-Tuning updates are spectrally compressible. However, successful PEFT methods do not simply recover the same update directions; instead, they often concentrate changes in different components and subspaces. The domain-transfer study further shows that methods with similar in-domain performance can respond differently under distribution shift. Together, these results suggest that SAM-3 adaptation is often capacity-saturated in practice, while competitive fine-tuning arises through method-specific update paths rather than a single low-rank approximation to Full Fine-Tuning.

**comment/C1：** Published in ECCV 2026 MEDFMB Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [GaLF-ST: Gaussian Latent Fields for Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27143v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27143

**作者：** Baichuan Yang、Yifan Luo、Yong Liu、Bing Su

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27143v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27143v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为空间转录组建模提出 GaLF-ST：用可学习的高斯中心与各向异性协方差决定各位置 latent 向量的权重，由共享非线性解码器把加权平均映射为多基因表达，并以表达误差联合更新高斯几何、latent 与解码器，同时给出跨七组数据的统一基准。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关联，但其「按空间几何加权聚合 latent 并与内容联合优化」的隐场参数化，可能为按位置调制 latent 状态、避免两阶段表示不匹配提供远距离参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GaLF-ST: Gaussian Latent Fields for Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Spatial transcriptomics (ST) measures gene expression at known tissue locations to reveal tissue organization and cellular function. Expression prediction requires modeling both local spatial relationships and associations among genes. Existing methods typically model expression using coordinate networks without explicit spatial parameterization. To address limited spatial sampling and sparse expression observations of ST, some methods firstly fit intermediate embeddings and then train expression predictors, but a latent representation suited to expression reconstruction may be difficult to predict from spatial coordinates. The two training stages can impose different demands on the latent representation. In this paper, we propose GaLF-ST, a Gaussian latent field method in which learnable Gaussian centers and anisotropic covariances determine the weights of latent vectors at each location. A shared nonlinear decoder maps their weighted average to multigene expression. In GaLF-ST, observed expression error jointly updates the Gaussian geometry, latent vectors, and decoder parameters. We further establish a unified benchmark across seven dataset groups and three held-out fractions, comparing GaLF-ST with four representative methods using the same locations, splits, gene panels, and scoring rules. Across tissues and observation densities, GaLF-ST achieves leading overall reconstruction performance, recovering spatial gene expression patterns and preserving tissue domains from limited observations while substantially reducing runtime and GPU memory usage.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables. We will release the appendix and code with a future version of this preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Hierarchical Hybrid-Coordinate Flow Matching for Accurate and Physically Plausible Apo-to-Holo Protein Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27162v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27162

**作者：** Minjong Kim、Jaemin Sim、Dongwoo Kim、Myunghyun Jang、Eun-Sol Kim、Juyong Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27162v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27162v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CrypticFlow，用分层流匹配结合笛卡尔与内坐标两种表示：先由 E(3) 等变图网络生成稀疏 Cα 锚点，再由条件 midpoint Transformer 递归填充局部内坐标并约束相邻锚点距离，以提升 apo→holo 构象生成的全局精度与局部几何合理性。

**相关性简评：** 其「全局稀疏锚点＋局部递归细化＋几何约束解码」的混合表示，以及回归生成中误差放大（lever-arm）的分析，可能为 LVR 的潜状态表示选择、分层状态更新与递归误差累积问题提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hierarchical Hybrid-Coordinate Flow Matching for Accurate and Physically Plausible Apo-to-Holo Protein Generation

**abstract/A1：** Predicting holo-like protein conformations from apo structures is essential in structure-based drug design. Most existing apo-to-holo models operate in Cartesian space and achieve strong global accuracy. However, high global accuracy does not ensure physically valid local backbone geometry. Internal-coordinate models offer local control and validity, but regressive generation can amplify angular errors, known as the lever-arm effect. We introduce CrypticFlow, a hierarchical flow-matching model that combines these two complementary representations. An ESM-3-conditioned E(3)-equivariant graph network first generates sparse C$α$ anchors in Cartesian space. A conditional midpoint Transformer then recursively fills the gaps in local internal coordinates. During recursive generation, the decoder constrains the distance between each midpoint and any sequence-adjacent parent anchor to the corresponding apo C$α$-C$α$ distance. On the D3PM test split, CrypticFlow achieves the lowest median and mean C$α$ RMSD at published baseline capacities. Across five independent training models, the median and mean direct RMSD are 1.333 $\\pm$ 0.056 angstroms and 2.766 $\\pm$ 0.041 angstroms, respectively. The quality of local geometry also improves substantially. Our model achieves 99.58 $\\pm$ 0.02% adjacent-distance fidelity and 95.87 $\\pm$ 0.25% pseudo-angle fidelity, measured within 0.2 angstroms and 20° of the holo reference. Then, we reconstruct the predicted C$α$-only traces into all-atom structures using the cg2all coarse-to-all-atom backmapping method and evaluate their physical and geometric quality with MolProbity. Our model achieves a MolProbity score of 3.034 $\\pm$ 0.054, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results show that a hierarchical flow-matching model with hybrid coordinates can recover global accuracy while retaining local fidelity.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 11 figures, 25 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [BusemannFormer: Horospherical Self-Attention for Hyperbolic Graph Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27167v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27167

**作者：** Youheng Yao、Ziyao Zeng、Wenbo Liao、Tianqi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27167v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27167v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Horospherical Self-Attention：每个 query 在双曲空间的理想边界上选一个方向，用 Busemann 深度为 key 打分，构成 token 间双曲注意力核，并集成到全双曲图 Transformer BusemannFormer 中，附带用 query 深度调节注意力集中度的 log-depth 变体与线性复杂度边界字典扩展，在层级图与引文图节点分类上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其双曲注意力核与按 query 深度调节注意力集中度的机制，可能为 LVR 中层级化 latent 状态几何与动态计算分配提供线索，但论文本身面向图节点分类、不涉及多模态推理，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BusemannFormer: Horospherical Self-Attention for Hyperbolic Graph Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Hyperbolic neural networks excel on hierarchical graph data, yet existing hyperbolic Transformers largely adapt conventional attention without explicitly exploiting the ideal boundary, a geometric structure closely tied to tree hierarchy. We propose Horospherical Self-Attention (HSA): each query selects an ideal-boundary direction and scores keys by their Busemann depth along that direction, yielding a token-to-token hyperbolic attention kernel. We establish a flat-limit connection to dot-product attention, characterize score level sets as horospheres, and bound key-side gradients. HSA is integrated into BusemannFormer, a fully hyperbolic graph Transformer, with a log-depth variant that uses query depth to modulate attention concentration. We further introduce a finite-boundary-dictionary extension supporting linear-complexity global attention. Experiments on hierarchical and citation graphs show that BusemannFormer-LD outperforms the evaluated baselines on several node-classification benchmarks and achieves competitive performance across diverse graph structures.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [FedQCal: Quadratic Programming with Dual-Space Calibration for One-Shot Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27170v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27170

**作者：** Shunsei Koshibuchi、Hiroshi Kera、Kazuhiko Kawamoto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27170v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27170v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FedQCal：用服务器小规模标注集训练的参考模型给出对齐约束，通过最小范数二次规划选择客户端聚合系数，并借助对偶变量由分类损失确定阈值，附带精确性与扰动界分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「参考对齐约束 + 最小范数系数校准」思路可能启发多种 latent 假设或多路并行推理状态的加权合并与阈值设定，但论文不含视觉推理与内部状态环节，可供核查其权重分配与误差放大分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FedQCal: Quadratic Programming with Dual-Space Calibration for One-Shot Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** One-shot federated learning must combine heterogeneous client models after a single upload. Different local class distributions make it difficult to decide which client updates to retain. We propose FedQCal, which uses a reference model trained on a small labeled server set to define alignment requirements for aggregation. The key idea is to retain sufficient reference alignment while avoiding unnecessarily large aggregation coefficients. A minimum-norm quadratic program (QP) enforces these requirements. Classification loss on the same server examples selects a merge through two dual variables, so the alignment thresholds need not be prescribed in advance. We prove that the selected coefficients remain exact QP solutions at their induced thresholds. A perturbation bound explains how coefficient norm limits the amplification of projection errors. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and SVHN show gains over one-round FedAvg in most settings. Ablations support reference-guided weighting and coefficient calibration, even with one labeled server image per class.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 2 figures (including supplementary material)

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [Learning Adaptive Defense Strategies: Fine-Tuned LLMs for Network Security Games under Network Mobility](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27180v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27180

**作者：** Md Abu Sayed、Asif Rahman、Ahmed Ryen、Ahmed Hemida、Charles Kamhoua、Christopher Kiekintveld

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27180v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27180v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文考察微调后的 LLM 能否在蜜罐分配这一零和博弈中充当无需求解器的在线均衡预测器：作者构建了 270 个带求解器均衡标签的安全博弈，对六种开源模型做 LoRA 微调，并在 1524 个网络移动事件上以可利用度、均衡支撑准确率和分布距离进行评估。

**相关性简评：** 网络移动导致攻击图反复变化、需要重算均衡，这一设定可能为 LVR 中「环境变化后是否重算 latent 状态」提供类比；其「输出有效性而非预测精度是进一步进展的瓶颈」的发现，也可供核查 latent 推理答案可用性与停止决策的评价方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Adaptive Defense Strategies: Fine-Tuned LLMs for Network Security Games under Network Mobility

**abstract/A1：** Large language models(LLM) show promise for strategic reasoning, yet their ability to compute game-theoretic equilibria under dynamic conditions remains largely unexplored. This work investigates whether fine-tuned LLMs can serve as solver-free surrogates for online equilibrium computation in honeypot allocation, a setting in which network mobility continuously reshapes the underlying attack graph and forces repeated re-solving of a two-player zero-sum game. We construct a dataset of 270 security games on networks of 8--25 nodes with solver-grounded equilibrium labels, and evaluate four classes of predictor over 1{,}524 held-out mobility events: non-learned baselines, six open-weight architectures evaluated zero-shot, the same six after LoRA fine-tuning, and four proprietary models as an external reference point. Predictions are scored without post-hoc repair against exact game-theoretic criteria: exploitability against an optimal attacker best-response, equilibrium support accuracy, and distributional distance. Fine-tuning reduces exploitability across every architecture, and fine-tuned open-weight models of modest size outperform every zero-shot predictor, including proprietary ones; no zero-shot model at any tier reaches the floor set by uniform allocation. Stratifying by mobility type exposes a qualitative shift that aggregate metrics conceal: zero-shot models do not condition on whether a departing node lies on an attack path, while fine-tuned models recover this distinction. We further find that validation loss is negatively rank-correlated with task performance, and that output validity, rather than predictive accuracy, is the binding constraint on further progress.

**comment/C1：** Submitted in ICNC

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [GEG-SAM: Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model for Weakly Supervised Polyp Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27183v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27183

**作者：** Ao Wang、Lei Zhang、Lituan Wang、Zhenwei Zhang、Xiangbao Gong、Zhiyi Zhang、Shunyao Wang、Xianglei Yuan、Zizhou Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27183v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27183v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 GEG-SAM，将内镜医师注视点序列建模为具有时间动态与空间拓扑的结构化探索关系，并与注视对齐的视觉上下文结合生成 gaze-conditioned tokens，注入冻结的 SAM 编码器以调制其特征，用于弱监督息肉分割。

**相关性简评：** 把注视轨迹视为连续的视觉搜索与证据积累过程，并据此调制冻结编码器特征，可能与 LVR 中 LOOK 式视觉证据筛选、视觉旁路注入及按序采集证据的设计有概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GEG-SAM: Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model for Weakly Supervised Polyp Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Endoscopist gaze offers an unobtrusively acquired record of lesion-oriented visual exploration during routine clinical inspection, providing relevant supervision for tasks. However, existing methods treat fixations as isolated spatial prompts directly associated with the target, overlooking the fact that natural gaze records a continuous visual search process in which fixations are temporally connected, spatially organized, and coupled with the visual content. Interpreting each fixation independently based only on its local visual content overlooks the temporal evolution and spatial organization of the gaze trajectory, whereas modeling relations among fixations alone cannot reveal the visual semantics of the regions they attend to. To this end, we propose the Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model (GEG-SAM), a principled weakly supervised framework designed to explicitly exploit structured gaze relations and their corresponding visual cues to modulate SAM for polyp segmentation. Specifically, GEG-SAM captures structured gaze relations by modeling the temporal dynamics and spatial topology of gaze exploration. It then associates these relational representations with fixation-aligned visual context to derive gaze-conditioned tokens, which are further injected into the frozen SAM encoder to modulate its pretrained image features. In this way, GEG-SAM effectively exploits the rich information embedded in gaze exploration to guide SAM toward lesion-oriented segmentation. Extensive experiments on five public polyp segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving the mean Dice by 9.30 percentage points over the strongest weakly supervised baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [What Does Matching a Human Intervention Effect Establish? Benchmark Checks for LLM Simulations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27186v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27186

**作者：** Tengfei Shao、Asma Rezaei、Ali Reza Kamali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CY、stat.ME

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27186v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27186v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文讨论如何判定 LLM 模拟“匹配人类干预效应”，指出等价性检验中零参照阈值会让空效应自动通过，并用 70 项实验再分析与预注册审计核查 GPT-4、Claude 的通过是否属于零参照通过。

**相关性简评：** 其“先给出零参照阈值再宣称效应成立”的检验逻辑可能可供核查 LVR 评价：模型在缺乏视觉证据或有效 latent 内容时同样越过阈值的情形，与该文的零参照通过同构，可用于设计“是否真正使用证据”的对照判定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does Matching a Human Intervention Effect Establish? Benchmark Checks for LLM Simulations

**abstract/A1：** LLM simulations are validated by equivalence with human effects, yet a zero reference passes whenever the human 90 percent interval lies inside the margin, as the human benchmark shows before simulation. When the zero reference fails, planned precision gives independent simulations the margin up to which passing is a 5 percent test of response. In an exploratory 70-experiment reanalysis, 72 percent of GPT-4's $\\pm$0.20 SD passes were zero-reference passes; GPT-4 passed 16 contrasts the zero reference failed (1.58 expected without response). In a preregistered audit, Claude Sonnet 5.5 passed three intention targets, all zero-reference passes, and the registered belief-gradient control; norm beliefs changed 0.065 SD against humans' 0.351 (zero-reference check added after unblinding; norm beliefs emphasized after inspection). Registered tests detected no overestimation; average-effect intervals excluded overestimation above 0.08 SD. A group audit's precision floor is 1.33 facts against a 2.9-fact effect. Report the zero-reference threshold beside every equivalence decision.

**comment/C1：** 50 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [Lift Out, Kick In: Decoupling Event Effects for Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27191v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27191

**作者：** Yunying Zhu、Zichun Cao、Siwei Sun、Xiaocen Guo、Shengsheng Lin、Jing Hu、Enyun Yu、Zhichao Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27191v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27191v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Loki 这一事件感知时间序列预测框架：用共享的 Event Response Function 按事件类型与相对时间位置刻画事件效应，先「Lift Out」从历史观测中剥离事件引起的偏离，再用同一函数「Kick In」已知未来事件的影响，在五个基准上取得最低标准化 MAE。

**相关性简评：** 其把持续动态与事件扰动分离、并以共享响应函数同时做历史归因与未来注入的做法，可能为 LVR 中区分任务必要视觉证据与上下文扰动、以及潜在状态的归因—更新提供概念借鉴，但领域距离较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lift Out, Kick In: Decoupling Event Effects for Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Real-world time series are often shaped by exogenous events whose effects are superimposed on the system's underlying temporal dynamics. Historical observations therefore entangle persistent dynamics with event-induced deviations, making it difficult to determine which patterns should be extrapolated and which should be attributed to specific event contexts. Existing event-aware forecasting methods can incorporate event information or model event-related variation, but typically treat historical event attribution and future event generation separately. We propose Loki (Lift Out, Kick In), a general event-aware forecasting framework built on a shared Event Response Function (ERF). The ERF models event effects according to event type and relative temporal position and is shared across the historical and forecast windows. Loki lifts out estimated event-induced deviations from historical observations before forecasting, then uses the same ERF to kick in responses to known future events. This formulation separates event-response modeling from temporal forecasting while coupling historical attribution and future generation through a shared mechanism. Across five forecasting benchmarks, Loki achieves the best standardized MAE on all five datasets, reducing error by approximately 2.2%-11.7% over the strongest non-Loki baseline. Ablations and controlled analyses further support the roles of Lift Out, Kick In, shared response modeling, and compatibility across forecasting backbones.

**comment/C1：** Equal Contribution: Yunying Zhu, Zichun Cao

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [EDAGoat: An EDA-Aware Agent for Project-Level GPU Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27196v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27196

**作者：** Chengxi Li、Liang Xiao、Yutao Wang、Wenhao Lin、Xinzhuang Xiong、Han Zhao、Evangeline Young

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27196v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27196v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EDAGoat，一个面向项目级 GPU EDA 优化的编码智能体：用四类跨项目优化模式引导代码修改，并以强制验证模式检查编辑是否触犯相关 EDA 约束，在五个项目上评测完整求解时间及设计正确性与质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「先验证再采纳」的编辑—检验循环及以全部公开/隐藏输入通过为合格标准，可能为 LVR 中的证据获取、候选轨迹筛选与停止决策提供参照，但领域相距较远，迁移价值有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EDAGoat: An EDA-Aware Agent for Project-Level GPU Optimization

**abstract/A1：** GPU acceleration has transformed electronic design automation (EDA), yet further improving expert-engineered GPU tools still demands substantial manual effort. Automating this work calls for coding agents that can trace bottlenecks across host and device code while preserving correctness and design quality. We introduce EDAGoat, to our knowledge the first EDA-aware GPU optimization agent for this task. Its optimization enhancement (OE) mode guides edits with four cross-project GPU optimization patterns. Its mandatory validation (MV) mode requires the agent to test the edited program against the EDA constraints its changes could affect. Our benchmark covers five GPU EDA projects across four task families and 55 circuit inputs, measuring complete-solve runtime and verifying the resulting designs. Each configuration runs three independent searches per project. A search qualifies only if its solver passes correctness and design-quality checks on all public and hidden inputs. With DeepSeek, generic prompting qualifies in 7 of 15 searches and OE in 6 of 15, despite a higher best speedup on four projects. Adding MV raises this to 13 of 15 and, on every project, exceeds both generic baselines in mean and best speedup and OE in mean speedup; the geometric mean of per-project mean speedups rises from 1.11x (1.36x with OE) to 1.53x. With GPT-6 Astra, EDAGoat qualifies in all 15 searches and improves both speedup metrics over generic prompting on every project, lifting the same geometric mean from 1.26x to 1.57x. These results show that agents can use EDA knowledge to find further speedups in expert-tuned tools while preserving design correctness and quality, establishing EDA-aware GPU optimization as a new direction for agent research.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [Backbone-Specific LoRA Geometry: Orthogonality, Superposition, and Provenance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27201v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27201

**作者：** Kazuhiko Kawamoto、Keigo Watanabe、Hiroshi Kera

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27201v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27201v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究跨 CLIP backbone 共享 LoRA 更新的条件，论证不同 backbone 的更新方向可作为「来源签名」，并提出基于余弦相似度的 backbone 识别与内积检验，用于免坐标对齐地复用和追溯 adapter。

**相关性简评：** 其关于低秩更新的正交性、叠加与来源可辨识性的分析，可能为核查 latent 状态叠加、多源贡献归属及无需对齐的相似度比较提供几何视角，但对象是参数空间而非推理状态，迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Backbone-Specific LoRA Geometry: Orthogonality, Superposition, and Provenance

**abstract/A1：** Sharing one low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update across pretrained models simplifies deployment. Composition methods typically combine updates from a common backbone, while cross-model transfer transforms updates for a target backbone. The conditions for sharing updates across backbones while preserving accuracy and distinguishing individual contributions remain unclear. Our idea is to use backbone-specific update directions as signatures of their training backbones. We derive a cross-backbone correlation bound from attention symmetries and equivariant LoRA learning under independent, uniform pretrained feature orientations. A local gradient-flow model explains positive mean correlation between independent runs on the same backbone and task. These patterns motivate cosine-based backbone identification and inner-product tests for registered-adapter reuse without coordinate alignment. Experiments on CLIP backbones show that shared sums achieve nearly the same mean accuracy as separate adapters. Cosine scores reliably identify source backbones across the evaluated tasks. Registered adapters remain detectable in sums of adapters from different backbones.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 5 figures (including supplementary material)

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [A Stationarity-Based Regret Framework for Time-Varying Nonconvex Composite Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27218

**作者：** Nadav Hallak

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27218v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究时变非凸复合优化，提出以局部平稳性（一阶最优性条件）定义的 regret 框架，并分析 proximal-gradient 方法在嵌套多调用与完全增量单调用 oracle 下的次线性 regret 保证及计算与性能的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，但「多调用 vs 单调用」的逐步计算预算与动态目标下的局部最优性度量，可能为 latent 多步/循环推理中评估每步计算收益、收敛与停止判据提供形式化参照，值得核查其度量是否可移植。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Stationarity-Based Regret Framework for Time-Varying Nonconvex Composite Optimization

**abstract/A1：** This paper studies time-varying nonconvex composite optimization, where decisions are made sequentially and classical value-based regret notions are generally ill-posed. We introduce a stationarity-based performance framework defined through local regret measures built from first-order optimality conditions, and identify minimal structural requirements under which such measures constitute sound performance criteria. This abstraction encompasses canonical optimality measures, including the proximal-gradient residual, and collapses to classical dynamic regret in convex settings. We then focus on the proximal-gradient residual as a concrete and algorithmically meaningful instantiation for general composite problems. In convex problems, we show that the resulting local regret is equivalent to standard dynamic regret. In nonconvex settings, we establish that sublinear stationarity-based regret is unattainable without temporal regularity, and introduce a gradient-variation budget that captures this necessity. Under this model, we analyze online proximal-gradient methods and prove sublinear regret guarantees in both nested multi-call and fully incremental single-call oracle regimes, highlighting the tradeoff between per-round computational effort and achievable performance. Finally, we demonstrate how the proposed framework extends to nonconvex multiplayer games by interpreting equilibrium-seeking dynamics through stationarity-based regret, and corroborate the theoretical findings with numerical experiments.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [A preference for local interaction between layers emerges during training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27258v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27258

**作者：** Jiheon Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.dis-nn

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27258v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27258v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 512 个五层模加网络上，于训练各阶段对单层施加扰动并测量其传播到其余各层的强度，发现训练早期响应弱且距离次序不稳定，随训练推进相邻层相互响应显著强于远层，即局部性在训练中自发形成。

**相关性简评：** 逐层「推一把再看传播」的度量思路可能可供核查 LVR 中深度与位置对 latent 状态贡献分配的影响，以及训练如何塑造层间信息局部化；但该工作纯属机制观测，与视觉多模态推理无直接联系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A preference for local interaction between layers emerges during training

**abstract/A1：** A deep neural network is a stack of layers, and all layers are trained to lower one shared error. A change in one layer can therefore reach every other layer. Because the error is shared by all layers, one would expect the other layers to respond alike, whether they are next to the changed layer or far from it. We find that early in training the response is weak and its order by distance is not stable. As training proceeds, the response grows, and neighboring layers come to respond to changes in each other more strongly than layers further apart, in almost every network. This locality forms during training, although training links every layer to every other. We show this by pushing one layer of a copy of the network at many stages of training and measuring how much of the push reaches each of the other layers. The experiments use 512 five-layer networks trained on modular addition with each of two optimizers, AdamW and stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics.

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [No Perfect Guardrail &amp; No Perfect Guard: On the Decidability of Output Policies for Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27260v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27260

**作者：** Ahmed Amer、Loren Kinczel、Darrell D. E. Long

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27260v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27260v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文给出LLM输出策略的可判定性边界：当任何有限串都可能是模型输出时，存在既能阻断全部禁止输出又放行全部允许输出的可计算 guard，当且仅当该策略可判定；不可判定策略下任何 guard 都会无限次误判，并据此讨论算术真值、程序行为与有害指令等情形及治理含义。

**相关性简评：** 其中「guard 与 execution monitor 之分」和「有界、可拦截情形才可精确执行」的区分，可能可供核查对 latent 推理轨迹做验证、干预或停止判断时是否存在可计算边界；但论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，该联系需自行论证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** No Perfect Guardrail &amp; No Perfect Guard: On the Decidability of Output Policies for Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Expecting guardrails to provide a general guarantee of safety fails to recognize the limits of computational enforcement and policy specification. We argue that promising such perfection is an ethical failure of precision, and that invoking it to abdicate responsibility is a moral failure. Guardrails can support responsible action, but cannot relieve researchers, developers, policymakers, or users of their obligations to examine what they claim, permit, and prevent. We establish a precise computational boundary. If every finite string is a possible output of a language model, a computable guard that blocks every forbidden output and passes every permitted one exists if and only if the policy is decidable. For an undecidable policy, every guard misclassifies infinitely many strings, and every guard that blocks all forbidden outputs excludes infinitely many permitted ones. We apply this characterization to arithmetic truth, program behavior, and harmful instructions to an idealized recipient. We distinguish guards from execution monitors, identify bounded and interceptable cases admitting exact enforcement, and explain why the results establish no positive lower bound on average error. Exact enforcement does not establish that a policy adequately captures safety. Conservative exclusion may withhold warnings, remedies, or challenges needed to recognize the policy's own defects. Human oversight cannot substitute for judgment: an apparent controller can become an instrument of purposes they have ceased to examine. Responsible governance requires continued critical challenge and the intellectual and moral humility to recognize possible flaws in our machines, rules, policies, and ourselves. The impossibility of a general assurance of perfection is a condition responsible practice must confront, not a responsibility that another guardrail - or another guard - can remove.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, including a one-page executive summary and a self-contained proof appendix. Sustainable Systems Laboratory Technical Report SSL-TR-2026-002, Preprint v0.1

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [InterBand-EMG: A Cross-Band Foundation Model for Generalizable Hand-Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27289v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27289

**作者：** Zongheng Guo、Tao Chen、Dalong Du、Yijian Yang、Heng Liu、Lun Shu、Chen Luo、Junhao Tu、Lei Shu、Mingzhou Wu、Yifan Liu、Yu Zhang、Zhenqiang Liu、Shilei Wen、Jie Zhou、Manuela Ferrario

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27289v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27289v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出跨频带 sEMG 基础模型 InterBand-EMG，结合频带特定量化、跨频带重建与结构化掩码码预测学习可迁移表示，用于连续手部姿态估计，并发布八通道 EMG-姿态数据集 SynapMyo。

**相关性简评：** 其「频带量化 + 跨带重建 + 掩码码预测」可能为 latent 推理状态的离散码本设计与片段级监督提供参考，但任务本身与 VLM 推理无直接联系，需核查掩码结构与码本是否具迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InterBand-EMG: A Cross-Band Foundation Model for Generalizable Hand-Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables continuous hand-pose estimation, but learning representations that generalize across users and movements remains challenging. We introduce InterBand-EMG, which combines band-specific quantization with cross-band reconstruction and structured masked code prediction to learn transferable EMG representations for hand-pose tracking. We also introduce SynapMyo, an eight-channel EMG-to-pose dataset comprising 1,227.74 hand-hours from 499 users across 68 movement categories. Experiments on emg2pose and SynapMyo show that InterBand-EMG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in joint-angle accuracy across all three generalization settings on both datasets. The source code and the SynapMyo dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance of this paper.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 8 figures

</details>

### 202. [What Stays in the Picture? From Redundant Instructions to Dependency-Aware Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27388

**作者：** Zi-Jun Wang、Yu Lin、Zhilin Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27388v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究「复杂冗余指令图像编辑」：CRIE-Agent 先将多次修订的指令归并为对象级最终意图，再按依赖关系分块执行（先建立被引用对象），并在验证失败时从源图整段重执行，同时构建含 1 万条标注数据的 CRIE-S 基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「修订历史→最终意图」的状态归并、依赖排序执行与「验证触发回滚并弃用旧尝试」的机制，可能为 LVR 中作废证据的丢弃、错误中间状态的清除与停止/重试决策提供概念参照；但该方法属图像编辑智能体流程，与 latent 推理状态的关系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Stays in the Picture? From Redundant Instructions to Dependency-Aware Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** We introduce complex redundant-instruction image editing, where a single request specifies multiple objects, their attributes, and spatial relations while revising or canceling earlier editing requirements. Direct editing can fail to recover this intent, while splitting the request into successive steps can preserve obsolete edits and visual errors. Our key insight is to resolve the revision history into a final editing target that jointly guides planning and verification, so that obsolete requests do not become visual edits. Based on this insight, CRIE-Agent learns to consolidate revisions into object-level final intent and executes the retained objects in reference-first blocks, establishing referenced objects before their dependents. Verification then guides complete re-execution from the source image, with conditional layout hints, preventing visual errors from failed attempts from being carried into subsequent ones. We also construct CRIE-S, a benchmark containing 10,000 complex and redundant editing instructions with exact final intent annotations. Across five image-editing backends on CRIE-S, CRIE-Agent raises complete editing success from 0% under direct editing to 27.5--50.0%.

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [Learned Network Dynamics for Model Predictive Traffic Signal Control under Incidents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27435v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27435

**作者：** Dang Viet Anh Nguyen、Carlos Lima Azevedo、Tomer Toledo、Filipe Rodrigues

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27435v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27435v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GNN-MPC：用图神经网络学习「动作条件」的路网排队动力学，在滚动时域内通过近似最佳响应协调各路口相位，并加入接受检验保证预测代价不高于初始方案，在含突发事件的微观仿真中降低行程时间、提高完成行程数。

**相关性简评：** 其「学习到的动作条件动力学＋部分可观测下对隐藏需求的推断」与 LVR 中预测式工作区、证据获取和方案校验（接受检验）可能有类比价值，但领域相距较远，需核查是否真能迁移到视觉推理状态建模。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learned Network Dynamics for Model Predictive Traffic Signal Control under Incidents

**abstract/A1：** Model predictive control (MPC) of urban traffic signals relies on a model of how queues respond to signal decisions, which is hard to specify and calibrate in heterogeneous networks, particularly when incidents change capacities and turning shares. Measurements add a second limitation: the controlled lanes at the stop line are often short, so queues extending upstream of them fall outside lane-level measurements. We propose GNN-MPC, a receding-horizon controller that plans with action-conditioned network dynamics learned by graph neural networks. Phase sequences are coordinated across intersections by approximate best responses, with an acceptance test that keeps the predicted cost no higher than that of the initial plan. In microscopic simulation under non-recurrent incidents, GNN-MPC reduces travel time and increases completed trips relative to all tested controllers, on a homogeneous grid and a heterogeneous 21-intersection network. Controlled comparisons on the heterogeneous network distinguish the roles of observation and prediction. With nearly as many vehicles entering the network, GNN-MPC reduces travel time by 13% relative to MaxPressure with the same upstream measurement, and GNN-MPC without the upstream representation does not reproduce its performance. Under the same traffic measurements, planner and objective, replacing the learned dynamics with calibrated store-and-forward predictors increases travel time by 31-34%. Observing hidden demand and predicting its response to control are thus complementary requirements of network-level predictive control.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages main manuscript, 26 pages supplementary material (39 pages total)

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Supervaluationism about vagueness and indeterminacy: A judgment-aggregation perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27463v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27463

**作者：** Christian List、Paul Egré、Franz Dietrich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27463v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27463v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文引入判断聚合理论，研究在多个相互竞争的解释或规范下汇总判定结果的一类聚合函数，并给出超赋值论的公理化刻画，进而与次赋值论、认知主义真理论及 Strong Kleene、LP 逻辑作公理化比较。

**相关性简评：** 「在所有规范下为真才算确定」这一聚合准则可能为多 latent 假设或多条推理轨迹的答案确定性、置信度与 STOP 决策提供概念框架，可供核查其公理区分是否能对应不同的证据筛选规则。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Supervaluationism about vagueness and indeterminacy: A judgment-aggregation perspective

**abstract/A1：** Supervaluationism about vagueness and indeterminacy is based on the idea that some sentences, or predicates within them, have multiple competing interpretations or specifications. A sentence is deemed determinately true if and only if it is true according to all specifications; and determinately false if and only if it is false according to all specifications. In all other cases, the sentence is assigned no determinate truth-value. Supervaluationism, however, is only one way of taking into account the verdicts of different specifications -- in technical terms, "aggregating" those verdicts. In this paper, we introduce insights from the theory of judgment aggregation to study a broad class of different aggregation functions. This allows us to offer an axiomatic characterization of supervaluationism, which, in turn, locates it precisely within a larger logical space of possible approaches to vagueness and indeterminacy. Among other results, we axiomatically compare supervaluationism with subvaluationism, the epistemic theory of vagueness, and Strong Kleene and LP logics.

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [PathOPEN: A pathologist-curated histopathology dataset for visual question answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27469v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27469

**作者：** Awais Naeem、Zhongling Xu、George Huang、Amy Coffey、Nkechi Okonkwo、Mario Saab-Chalhoub、John Lee Sang、Dina Kokh、Muhammad Zulfiqar、Ankita Shukla、Hairong Wang、Chandra Krishnan、Ying Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27469v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27469v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了一个病理组织学视觉问答数据集：由病理学家挑选 157 个病例、229 张原始图像，编写开放式、五选一和是非三种题型的题目，并为 201 道开放题同时提供正确与错误答案，配以器官、病理类别、图像放大倍数等元数据和 6,870 张增强图像，另做多评分者打分与六个模型的零样本评测。

**相关性简评：** 正误答案配对与放大倍数等元数据可能可供核查模型回答是否真正依赖视觉证据、以及按视觉细节粒度做分组评估，但其本身不涉及 latent 推理状态或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 类机制，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PathOPEN: A pathologist-curated histopathology dataset for visual question answering

**abstract/A1：** PathOPEN is a histopathology dataset for visual question answering. Two clinical pathologists selected 157 cases with 229 available original images and wrote open-ended, five-option multiple-choice and close-ended (yes/no) questions. They supplied both a correct and a wrong answer for 201 of the 309 open-ended questions. Each case records the organ, pathology category and regional anatomy, and each image records its magnification. The dataset includes 6,870 augmented images and four manifests that select subsets to improve balance by individual metadata field. Five additional pathologists and two vision-language model judges rated items with scoring rubrics, and six models answered questions zero-shot across the three formats. The metadata support subgroup evaluation, and the paired answers can be used to construct preference examples.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages including supplementary material and appendices, 8 main figures and 1 supplementary figure. Dataset: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24358341

</details>

### 206. [Verifying and Strengthening Neural Tangent Kernel Theory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27487v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27487

**作者：** Hao Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27487v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27487v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用 Lean 4 对两层网络的神经切核（NTK）理论做机器验证，在显式宽度条件下给出梯度流存在唯一性与残差指数收敛的端到端定理，并用行协方差集中代理收紧宽度证书、指出 Hessian 算子范数为 Θ(√p) 从而需改用 L² 参数几何。

**相关性简评：** NTK 的「核漂移」「线性化界」与 Hessian 分析给出的是训练中内部表示移动幅度的可证明刻画，可能为判断 latent 推理状态究竟被特征学习改写还是停留在惰性训练区提供一种量化与形式化核查工具。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifying and Strengthening Neural Tangent Kernel Theory

**abstract/A1：** Writing Neural tangent kernel (NTK) theory explains why very wide neural networks trained by gradient methods converge in the lazy-training regime. Its standard proof combines matrix concentration, random Gram matrices, continuous-time dynamics, spectral perturbation, and a bootstrap argument, but has lacked machine-checked verification. We present a Lean\\~4 formalisation for two-layer networks, proving an end-to-end theorem: for a smooth bounded activation (instantiated with $σ=\\tanh$), frozen biases, paired $\\pm 1$ initialisation, and explicit width conditions, gradient flow exists uniquely with probability at least $1-δ$, and $$ \\\|r(t)\\\|^2 \\leq \\\|y\\\|^2 e^{-λ\_0 t/2}. $$ The sole data assumption is population NTK non-degeneracy, $λ\_0&gt;0$. Initial coercivity, kernel drift, the movement bootstrap, the residual ODE, and flow existence and uniqueness are formally proved. The formalisation also sharpens the standard analysis in two ways. First, replacing the conventional matrix-Bernstein variance proxy with the classical row-covariance proxy yields estimated concentration-width certificates for $\\tanh$ that are $36$--$53\\times$ smaller at $(m,d)=(16,8)$ and $94$--$121\\times$ smaller at $(32,16)$. Lean certifies the inequality; the ratios are numerical. Second, the network Hessian has operator norm $Θ(\\sqrt{p})$ on the sup-norm parameter ball (a paper calculation), preventing the expected $O(1/\\sqrt{p})$ linearisation bound via sup-norm Taylor estimates. An $L^2$ parameter geometry resolves this issue. Required widths remain polynomial but far beyond practical scales, limiting applicability.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, Accepted by KDD 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Learning Unknown Oscillators From Bandit Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27492v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27492

**作者：** Priyank Jayraj、Poonam Goyal、Navneet Goyal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27492v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27492v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究由未知振荡器驱动的半 bandit 问题：每轮从四个奖励通道中选两个观测，频率误差会改变后续奖励排序；作者提出「检查后承诺」控制器，仅在预测检查失败时才重新拟合并收集观测，用连续置信集约束 regret，给出平方根级界，并在合成实例上报告 49 次拟合替代 1,023 次、平均 regret 仅高 1.5%。

**相关性简评：** 「检查失败才重新拟合、否则继续承诺」的调度及其 regret 成本核算，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的触发条件与计算收益—成本权衡提供形式化线索，但领域距离较远，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Unknown Oscillators From Bandit Feedback

**abstract/A1：** We study semi-bandits driven by unknown oscillators. Each round, a learner selects two of four reward channels and observes their individual rewards. Frequency errors change future reward rankings and the resulting measurements. We show when committing to one fitted oscillator lets its greedy choices expose prediction errors. Our controller bounds reward loss (regret) over a continuous confidence set and holds the longest block within budget. An online regression policy gathers observations when the check fails. Regret grows at a square-root rate, up to logarithmic factors, across all frequencies and extends to two competing oscillators. On 96 fresh synthetic instances, checked commitment uses 49 fits instead of 1,023, with 1.5% higher mean regret than fitting every round. Matched isolated timings give a 4.69-fold speedup. The confidence sets also certify trajectory regret.

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Solar-Conditioned Flow Matching for Multitemporal Aerial Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27501v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27501

**作者：** Wenwen Fang、Bingwei Hui、Xin Chen、Zhongzhen Sun、Zhenfeng Shao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27501v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27501v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Solar-Flow，在航空可见光到红外图像翻译中用太阳高度、热相位时钟与飞行高度作为连续物理条件，经 adaLN 注入预训练 flow matching 生成器的每个 Transformer 块，实现单张可见光图像向一天多个时刻红外图像的多时相生成，并构建覆盖 14 个时段组的配对数据集。

**相关性简评：** 单张图像对应多个合理红外外观，本质是未观测物理状态导致的证据歧义，其用连续相位变量条件化并外推到未见时刻的做法，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的表示与条件外推提供概念线索，但联系较远，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Solar-Conditioned Flow Matching for Multitemporal Aerial Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation

**abstract/A1：** Aerial visible-to-infrared (V2IR) image translation provides scalable thermal data across the full diurnal cycle. However, existing methods predominantly learn a static mapping within narrow acquisition windows. As the thermal state evolves throughout the day, a single visible image may correspond to multiple plausible IR appearances, making V2IR translation an inherently multitemporal task. This letter proposes solar-conditioned latent flow matching (Solar-Flow), which upgrades aerial V2IR translation from static mapping to multitemporal generation. First, we design three computable physical factors, namely the solar elevation, the thermal phase clock (TPC), and the flight altitude. The TPC counts the elapsed heating and cooling hours on a continuous 24-hour axis anchored at sunrise, so the thermal state stays well defined even at hours without training samples. Second, we inject the condition into every Transformer block of a pretrained flow matching generator through adaptive layer normalization. This design preserves translation fidelity and enables multitemporal generation. Finally, we construct a paired aerial V2IR dataset covering 14 hour groups. Solar-Flow achieves multitemporal generation on this dataset, where a single visible image is translated into plausible IR images at multiple times of day, even at hours that were never seen in training.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables. Submitted to IEEE GRSL

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [Breaking the Barrier of Hard Samples: A Data-Centric Approach to Synthetic Data for Medical Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27511v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27511

**作者：** Maynara Donato de Souza、Cleber Zanchettin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27511v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27511v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Profile2Gen 数据为中心框架，针对医学回归任务中难学样本，指导合成数据的生成与精炼，并将 DataIQ 数据价值评估扩展到回归任务，在六个医学数据集上以约 1.8 万次实验验证其降低预测误差、提升可靠性的效果。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或 latent 推理无直接关联，但其「难学样本识别＋数据价值量化（DataIQ）」思路可能为 latent 推理轨迹训练的样本难度加权与监督贡献分配提供可核查的度量线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Breaking the Barrier of Hard Samples: A Data-Centric Approach to Synthetic Data for Medical Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Data scarcity and quality issues remain significant barriers to developing robust predictive models in medical research. Traditional reliance on real-world data often leads to biased models with poor generalizability across diverse patient populations. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a promising solution, yet challenges related to these samples' representativeness and effective utilization persist. This paper introduces Profile2Gen, a novel data-centric framework designed to guide the generation and refinement of synthetic data, focusing on addressing hard-to-learn samples in regression tasks. We conducted approximately 18,000 experiments to validate its effectiveness across six medical datasets, utilizing seven state-of-the-art generative models. Results demonstrate that refined synthetic samples can reduce predictive errors and enhance model reliability. Additionally, we generalize the DataIQ framework to support regression tasks, enabling its application in broader contexts. Statistical analyses confirm that our approach achieves equal or superior performance compared to models trained exclusively on real data.

**comment/C1：** 54 pages, including appendices. Published at ICML 2025. Original publication: https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/de-souza25a.html

</details>
