### 210. [Can Language Models See the Circuit in Pixelated RFIC Passives?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27559v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27559

**作者：** Jonathan Zhou、Kaushik Sengupta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR、physics.app-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27559v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27559v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察前沿语言模型在禁用工具条件下能否读取18×18与27×27像素化RFIC无源器件布局，预测20–100 GHz的输入阻抗与损耗，并在五次EM仿真预算内通过改动最多十二块瓦片来调试七个不达标设计。

**相关性简评：** 属RFIC设计应用，与VLM推理主线距离较远；但其在有限仿真预算下反复获取证据、筛选布局并决定停止的设定，可能为LVR中几何信息保留与证据获取成本提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Language Models See the Circuit in Pixelated RFIC Passives?

**abstract/A1：** The emergence of inverse-designed, pixelated passives for microwave and millimeter-wave applications has been a major development in the field of RFIC design. These devices, which appear to be QR-code-like in structure, exist in a generalizable, high-dimensional design space that includes conventional passive topologies. However, these structures are naturally unintuitive for designers, and failures or deviations in performance are difficult to debug. In this work, we ask whether a frontier language model can understand, predict, and edit the responses and layout structure of pixelated passives. The agent, run with all tools disabled, is first asked to read layouts on 18x18 and 27x27 tile grids and, in a single reply, predict the input impedance and loss of the structure from 20-100 GHz. On eight GA-synthesized layouts and five random fills, the predicted Smith-chart locus follows full-wave simulation results closely, with a systematic shift that places features about 16% lower in frequency than EM. On multipath loop structures it identifies notches successfully but shows a smaller frequency bias, about half that of an ideal transmission-line model that ignores bends. The agent is then asked to debug seven designs that fail their specification, and given a budget of five EM simulations and at most twelve changed tiles, it is able to improve all seven and bring five designs within specification. These experiments suggest language models can serve as an interpretability and debugging tool for traditionally non-intuitive inverse-designed passives.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 24 figures (5 main, 19 supplementary), 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Regret in Online Convex Optimization and Applications to Contextual Search and Online Inverse Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27572v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27572

**作者：** Shinsaku Sakaue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27572v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27572v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究在线凸优化中的 small-loss regret，提出无需预知 Δ\_T\* 与时间视界、每轮仅用一个次梯度的算法，达到极小极大最优的 O(1+√Δ\_T\*) 界，并用于带比较翻转的上下文搜索与在线逆优化。

**相关性简评：** 按实际累计损失自适应收紧的 regret 界及噪声比较下的搜索决策，可能为 LVR 中依据证据质量动态分配计算与停止判断提供概念参照，但迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Regret in Online Convex Optimization and Applications to Contextual Search and Online Inverse Optimization

**abstract/A1：** We give small-loss regret bounds for online convex optimization (OCO), using one subgradient per round. Let $Δ\_T^\\star$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed decision in hindsight, measured relative to the per-round minima. Our algorithm achieves the regret bound $O(1+\\sqrt{Δ\_T^\\star})$ without knowing $Δ\_T^\\star$, up to domain and gradient-scale factors. The bound is minimax optimal, including its domain dependence, on $\\ell\_p$ balls for every $1\\le p\\le\\infty$. No smoothness or condition bounding gradient norms by loss values is assumed, and neither loss values nor the time horizon are needed. Algorithmically, we extend the metric-update revocation of Gupta et al. (2026), originally developed for online inverse optimization, to general OCO using the follow-the-regularized-leader framework and a mixture of multiple base learners. We also apply our algorithm to contextual search and online inverse optimization. In $d$-dimensional contextual search with comparison flips of unknown bias $κ&gt;0$, we obtain minimax expected cumulative absolute error $Θ(\\sqrt{d}/κ^2)$ under Euclidean normalization using polynomially many arithmetic operations, resolving an open question of Liu et al. (2021). For online inverse optimization with the Euclidean unit-ball utility domain, our algorithm bounds the regret relative to observed actions by $O(\\sqrt{d}+d^{1/4}\\sqrt{D\_T})$, where $D\_T$ is their cumulative suboptimality; this matches the regret rate of Gupta et al. (2026) when $D\_T=0$. Our guarantee extends to other utility domains; for example, on the probability simplex, the bound improves to $O(\\log(d+1)+\\sqrt{D\_T\\log(d+1)})$.

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [Adapting AI Weather Foundation Models to a Changing Climate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27605v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27605

**作者：** Xinlei Xiong、Wenbo Hu、Shuxun Zhou、Lingxi Xie、Richang Hong、Qi Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27605v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27605v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 AI 天气基础模型与气候变化后分布不匹配的问题，提出在线适配框架：在低维敏感子空间内做流依赖校正，并用残差记忆与逐变量门控保留持续偏差；在 Aurora-1.3B 的 ERA5 时序流上，69 个输出场的 MSE/MAE 相对最强基线降低约 6.9%/6.45%。

**相关性简评：** 其把误差拆为持续偏差与状态依赖误差、并以门控残差记忆维护状态修正的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的持久偏置修正与动态更新提供概念借鉴，需核查门控与敏感子空间的选择依据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adapting AI Weather Foundation Models to a Changing Climate

**abstract/A1：** AI weather foundation models learn from historical archives, while the climate distribution encountered during deployment continues to evolve. This growing model--climate mismatch motivates continual adaptation without repeated full-model retraining, but the structure of weather forecast errors determines how such adaptation should be performed. We develop an online adaptation framework that exploits two complementary error structures: geographically persistent biases and flow-dependent errors that vary with the atmospheric state. Analysis of Aurora-1.3B reveals strongly concentrated forecast-loss sensitivity in its decoder, motivating flow-dependent corrections within fixed low-dimensional sensitivity subspaces. A residual memory tracks persistent errors remaining after this correction, while a variable-wise online gate controls the contribution of the persistent component. We evaluate Aurora-1.3B using ERA5 from 1959--1968 for offline initialization and a strictly chronological 1969--2022 stream for online evaluation, with updates restricted to previously available verification targets. For 2-metre temperature, distributional departure from the historical reference, measured directly from ERA5, is associated with larger frozen-model errors and greater adaptation gains, while the adapted model maintains more stable skill across evaluation periods. Across 69 output fields and five lead times from 6 to 72 hours, our method reduces MSE and MAE by 6.90\\% and 6.45\\%, respectively, relative to $δ$-Adapter, the strongest of six online adaptation baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [Potential emergence in LLM agents: a statistical physics perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27618v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27618

**作者：** Zhuo-Yang Song、Hua Xing Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.stat-mech、cs.AI、cs.CL、nlin.AO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27618v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27618v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把 LLM agent 的中间状态作为可测量变量，借助统计物理中的全局势、配分函数与 KL 正则转移分布，分析后训练如何组织推理状态，并在两个 Qwen3.5-9B 任务上追踪势结构、平均奖励与状态熵。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但其状态转移分布、势结构与状态熵可能为刻画 LVR 内部推理状态的组织方式、训练监督效果及表示坍缩提供可核查的分析视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Potential emergence in LLM agents: a statistical physics perspective

**abstract/A1：** There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. States thus provide a quantitative description of how post-training organises agent behaviour.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [Benchmarks' Last Exam](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27622v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27622

**作者：** James Zhu、Zekki Cat

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27622v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27622v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文综合八个前沿基准、749 个任务的质量审查与 767 次公开模型评测，检验 prompt、环境与 grader 是否就「成功」达成一致，并按评分架构统计缺陷方向（扣分还是放行捷径）以及容器隔离修复的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其对「shortcut pass」与评分器缺陷的量化思路，可能可供核查 VLM/LVR 评测中答案是否真正依赖视觉证据而非捷径；论文本身不涉及多模态推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarks' Last Exam

**abstract/A1：** Public benchmarks guide model selection, post-training rewards and claims of progress in artificial intelligence, yet leaderboards report aggregate scores without verifying whether each task's prompt, environment and grader agree on what counts as success. How often that agreement fails in contemporary evaluations, and in which direction confirmed defects move scores, has remained unmeasured across grading designs. Here we synthesize task-level quality reviews of 749 tasks across eight frontier benchmarks and 767 public model evaluations spanning closed-form question answering, document rubrics and agentic execution to assess whether prompts, environments and graders align. Task validity stratifies sharply by grading architecture. Closed-form benchmarks revised after public error reporting reach 92.5% verified validity, compared with 29.3% across six multi-step suites that nevertheless already supply 161 verified complex tasks. Rubric suites fail through mismatches between prompts and reference checklists, whereas execution harnesses add incomplete state assertions, shared-channel leaks and grading-time collisions. Across confirmed defects, 71.7% withhold credit from compliant solutions while 28.3% permit shortcut passes, indicating that raw leaderboard ceilings understate frontier capability on many practical tasks. Grading committed patches inside a fresh container cuts confirmed runtime bypasses from 14.7% in shared containers to 2.7% (a 5.5x drop, and 0% in read-only or static suites), showing how targeted maintenance restores existing benchmarks at low cost. As the initial milestone of the long-term Benchmarks' Last Exam initiative, this synthesis launches sustained expansion across wider benchmark coverage and deeper evaluation-quality dimensions so that pursuing leaderboard gains directly advances genuine model capability.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables. Initial milestone of the Benchmarks' Last Exam initiative; public reports, structured data, and code archived at https://benchmarks-last-exam.com/benchmarks/ and https://github.com/ZekkiCat/Benchmarks-Last-Exam

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [Learning Inside Types: Epistemic Typestates for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27631v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27631

**作者：** Lorenzo Bacchiani、Mario Bravetti、Giovanni Pau、Roberto Girau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27631v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27631v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「认知类型态」（epistemic typestates）：为多智能体强化学习中的每个智能体构造一个类型协议，状态刻画其信念（从自身观测与动作无法排除的世界），并只允许在所有可能世界上都安全的动作；智能体在类型内学习，类型逐个构造与检查、运行期无需通信，文中给出类型可靠性与团队安全性证明、完备的综合算法，并将 shielding 视作特例。

**相关性简评：** 其「信念=不可排除世界集合」的状态抽象与逐智能体独立合成、可作为约束嵌入学习过程的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态如何保留任务必要信息、以及推理期何时必须获取更多证据提供可核查的形式化参照，但无视觉或多模态推理实验支持。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Inside Types: Epistemic Typestates for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Type disciplines make programs safe by construction: components checked against each other's types compose into programs that cannot go wrong in the ways the types exclude. Types suit reinforcement learning: a type constrains what an agent may choose, not how it chooses, so the guarantee holds for every policy a learner produces. We introduce epistemic typestates, a type discipline for safe multi-agent reinforcement learning. An agent's type is a protocol whose states record its beliefs, the worlds it cannot rule out from its own observations and actions, and permits only actions that are safe in every world of the belief, whatever the others do within their public types. Agents learn inside their types, which are built and checked one agent at a time and run without communication. We prove type soundness and team safety, give a synthesis that is sound and complete, build types from guards when worlds are infinite, and show that shields are a special case. All proofs are mechanised in Rocq. On nine benchmarks, no typed learner causes a safety violation, and task performance does not differ significantly from that of two recent multi-agent shielding methods. Where a contract-based method fails at five and ten agents, types for ten agents are built in under a second. In the two continuous benchmarks, Lagrangian learners cause thousands of violations in every training run.

</details>

<a id="paper-216"></a>

### 216. [Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Networks for Fault Detection and Structural Learning in Chemical Processes: Use Case on the Tennessee Eastman Process](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27698v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27698

**作者：** Rayane Ammar Khodja、Alexandre Voisin、Victor Costa、Fanny Casteran、Benoit Celse、Benoît Iung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27698v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27698v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出面向化工过程的时空 GNN 故障检测框架：将正常工况空间拆成工况子空间并用转移函数相连，以重构损失加 Deep SVDD 单类目标训练，用 GATv2 学习邻接矩阵，并在 TEP 上检验注意力权重能否还原 22 对已知物理传感器连接。

**相关性简评：** 「重构驱动的注意力未能还原物理拓扑」这一负结果可供核查，提示用注意力或 latent 相似度推断实体关系需外部验证；其工况子空间与转移函数分解也可能类比动态推理状态表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Networks for Fault Detection and Structural Learning in Chemical Processes: Use Case on the Tennessee Eastman Process

**abstract/A1：** Classical Fault Detection and Diagnosis (FDD) methods, including many data-driven approaches, assume a static normal operating space and interpret deviations from a fixed reference as fault indicators. When Operating Conditions (OCs) vary over time, this assumption breaks down: legitimate transitions trigger false alarms while moderate faults go undetected. We propose a spatiotemporal Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework that decomposes the normal operating space into OC-specific subspaces linked by transition functions, with a dual learning objective combining reconstruction loss and a Deep SVDD one-class term. It learns adjacency matrices through Graph Attention Networks (GATs) and integrates spatial modelling with temporal encoding to represent process dynamics under evolving OCs. This paper evaluates the framework's foundational components (fault detection, GATv2 spatial graph learning and temporal encoding) on the Tennessee Eastman Process (TEP) benchmark, training only on fault-free data. The architecture achieves competitive detection from reconstruction error alone, with similarity-based feature selection improving both accuracy and graph structure diversity. We then evaluate the physical interpretability of the learned attention matrices against 22 ground-truth sensor pairs derived from the TEP control structure and process topology. GATv2 attention fails to recover all known physical pairs across multiple hyperparameter configurations, suggesting a structural limitation of reconstruction-driven attention rather than a tuning issue. This challenges a common assumption in GNN-based FDD: that learned attention weights provide a basis for fault diagnosis and root-cause analysis. The architecture detects faults effectively, but the learned graph does not encode the physical topology needed for interpretable diagnosis, motivating physics-informed graph construction.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables. Presented at PHME 2026, Oslo, Norway

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [MetaRigor: From Agentic Automation to Verifiable End-to-End Meta-Analysis Through Scientific Decision Boundaries](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27713v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27713

**作者：** Yifeng Xie、Chenke Li、Yangshuo Miao、Jingyuan Zheng、Mingxuan Liu、Feiwei Qin、YaQi Wang、Huiyu Zhou、Yifei Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27713v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27713v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MetaRigor——一个 LLM 工作流框架，用 Study×Target 单元、Task Contracts、程序化规则与来源绑定把科学决策边界显式化，以实现可验证的端到端元分析，并在证据获取、抽取、偏倚与证据确定性评估、稿件生成五个阶段评测。

**相关性简评：** 其将语义判断交给模型、形式规则与来源核验交给程序的分工，以及结果身份跨阶段一致性和溯源追踪，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、证据溯源与「LOOK」阶段的可验证性提供概念参照；但该工作属文献证据合成领域，与视觉推理无直接关联，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MetaRigor: From Agentic Automation to Verifiable End-to-End Meta-Analysis Through Scientific Decision Boundaries

**abstract/A1：** Automated meta-analysis requires not only accurate numerical extraction but also the consistent preservation of result identity across stages, including the associated study, comparison, population, outcome, time point, and analysis conditions. We present MetaRigor, an LLM workflow framework for verifiable end-to-end evidence synthesis. MetaRigor organizes study results as Study x Target units and operationalizes scientific decision boundaries at the task level through Task Contracts, programmatic rules, and source binding. The model performs semantic interpretation that depends on study context, whereas programs execute formalizable rules, computations, validation, and source verification. We conducted controlled evaluations across five stages: evidence acquisition, data extraction, risk of bias assessment, certainty of evidence assessment, and manuscript generation. MetaRigor achieved an F1 score of 86.29% for complete result delivery and 80.20% agreement for domain-level certainty of evidence judgments. Generated manuscripts received scores of 86.00 under human evaluation, exceeding all three baselines. A seven-case downstream end-to-end evaluation further yielded an aggregate F1 score of 77.01%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 9.78 percentage points. Cross-system tracing of archived outputs from three systems further showed that local mismatches or omissions in result identity could alter statistical inputs and the objects subjected to risk of bias assessment, with these errors subsequently propagating into the final report. Ablation experiments showed that task constraints and programmatic rules played distinct roles across different stages. These findings indicate that, in LLM-driven evidence synthesis workflows, consistently maintaining explicit result identity, decision boundaries, and provenance relationships is critical to improving end-to-end verifiability.

</details>

<a id="paper-218"></a>

### 218. [Solving the pre-image problem in kernel PCA: The reproducing property is all you need](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27718

**作者：** Joel-Pascal Ntwali N'konzi、Stefan Klus

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27718v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 kernel PCA 的 pre-image 问题，利用 RKHS 的 reproducing property 推导出可证明一致的 reconstruction map，并将其扩展到随机傅里叶特征近似，实验显示其精度可比且计算更高效、更稳定。

**相关性简评：** 可证明一致的「潜空间→输入空间」重构映射，可能为检验 latent 状态中视觉细节能否被忠实解码、信息是否坍缩提供一种探针工具，但其与多模态推理的实际可迁移性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Solving the pre-image problem in kernel PCA: The reproducing property is all you need

**abstract/A1：** Kernel PCA extends PCA to nonlinear dimensionality reduction by implicitly mapping data into a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) via the kernel trick, with applications ranging from image denoising to out-of-distribution detection. While its projection map is well understood, appropriately defining the associated reconstruction map is a longstanding challenge since projecting back from the RKHS to the input space is non-trivial. This is known as the pre-image problem. Existing approaches have tackled this problem by either relying on nonlinear optimization, which is prone to numerical instabilities and sensitive to initialization, or by solving a separate supervised learning problem, incurring additional computational overhead. Furthermore, although the projection map of kernel PCA reduces to that of standard PCA when a linear kernel is used, most of these approaches are not guaranteed to recover PCA's reconstruction map in this case, a consistency property that one would naturally expect to hold. In this paper, we leverage the reproducing property of the RKHS to derive a reconstruction map for kernel PCA that is provably consistent. Our derivations rely on the mild assumption that the RKHS either contains linear functions or the kernel is universal, which holds for standard kernels. We further extend the method to the random Fourier features (RFFs) approximation of kernel PCA by introducing a corresponding notion of RFF reproducing property. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method performs comparably with state-of-the-art methods across kernel choices, while remaining more computationally efficient and maintaining consistent performance in settings where these baseline methods drastically fail.

**comment/C1：** This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [A Switching Mechanism for Selecting Environment-Adaptive Personalized Gains in Hearing Aids](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27726v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27726

**作者：** Nafisa Zarrin Tasnim、Nasser Kehtarnavaz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27726v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27726v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出助听器的个性化增益切换机制：用单类语音活动检测与一致性检查区分前景语音和背景声，再以贝叶斯变点检测结合历史环境记忆在线识别、复用和发现背景声环境，从而自动选择对应增益。

**相关性简评：** 其「变点检测＋环境记忆」的在线状态识别与切换、前景/背景证据分离，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的选择与切换、视觉证据筛选及状态缓存复用提供概念线索，但领域相距较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Switching Mechanism for Selecting Environment-Adaptive Personalized Gains in Hearing Aids

**abstract/A1：** Personalized amplification gains for hearing aids have been shown to be preferred over prescriptive gains. Since the preferred settings can vary depending on the background audio environment, this paper presents a switching mechanism or system that automatically selects personalized gains for a current background audio environment. To prevent foreground speech from interfering with background environment analysis, a one-class voice activity detector is designed to identify speech activity from speech mixed with noise. A consistency check is conducted to enable distinguishing foreground speech from background speech, preserving babble as a background environment of interest because hearing aid users often struggle to understand speech in background babble. Sound segments without foreground speech are then analyzed by an online discovery method combining Bayesian change-point detection with a memory of previously encountered environments. This discovery method detects changes, recognizes returning environments, and discovers new ones without a predefined number of environments. Experimental results show that three-seed mean macro-averaged F1 scores of 85.53%-91.98% across eight speech/background-corpus pairs covering 36 background noise types were achieved. Furthermore, the discovery method achieved adjusted Rand indices of 0.91 and 0.96 on two families of test streams. In the same-recording-return condition of audio streams combining speech with four background audio environments often encountered by hearing aid users, the switching mechanism selected the correct personalized gain profile after 105 environment changes, recalled the stored gain profile at every return, and made no switches to an incorrect gain profile.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 6 figures, 22 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [DPMA: Learning to Compress Mixture-of-Experts Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27759v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27759

**作者：** Jon Irureta、Jon Imaz、Philipp Wiesner、Gorka Azkune、Javier Fernandez-Marques、Alex Iacob、Andrej Jovanović、Aizea Lojo、Nicholas D. Lane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27759v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27759v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DPMA，在全局专家预算约束下用可学习系数通过梯度下降联合决定剪枝、参数合并与路由吸收三种操作，并学习逐层专家分配；原权重冻结，仅以校准数据的输出对齐来优化系数与分配。

**相关性简评：** 「全局预算 + 可微的逐层容量分配与操作选择」可供核查是否能借鉴到 LVR 中每层或每步 latent 计算的预算分配与操作决策，但本文属 MoE 压缩、与视觉推理无直接联系，迁移仅属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DPMA: Learning to Compress Mixture-of-Experts Models

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer high computational efficiency, but storing all experts requires substantial memory and limits deployment on resource-constrained hardware. Most retraining-free MoE compression methods commit to either pruning or merging experts, often with a uniform compression ratio across layers. We introduce DPMA (Differentiable Prune Merge Absorb), which jointly learns compression operations and layer-wise expert allocation through gradient descent under a global expert budget. Separate learnable coefficients allow DPMA to prune experts, merge their parameters, and absorb their routing contributions by forwarding routing weights to retained experts. These coefficients and expert assignments are optimised to match the original model's outputs on calibration data while its weights remain frozen. Across four MoE models, eight multiple-choice benchmarks, and five compression ratios, DPMA achieves the highest accuracy averaged over benchmarks and compression ratios on each model among the evaluated methods. At $75\\%$ expert reduction, it outperforms the strongest baseline on every model, with gains of up to $6.7$ percentage points.

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [A Benchmark Protocol and Fairness Analysis Towards Open-Set EEG Seizure Type Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27783v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27783

**作者：** Targol Bakhtiarvand、Terrance E. Boult、Jugal Kalita、Adham Atyabi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27783v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27783v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 TUSZ 癫痫语料上提出首个 EEG 发作类型开集识别协议，用临床分类学把常见类型划为 known、罕见类型划为 unknown，并在部分微调的 EEG 基础模型上评测七种 post-hoc 开集打分器与被试级拒绝公平性。

**相关性简评：** 其「宁可弃答也不强行给出错误标签」的选择性预测设定，以及按被试统计的拒绝率不均，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与证据不足时的弃答评价提供参照，但属远端临床领域、未涉及视觉 latent 状态，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Benchmark Protocol and Fairness Analysis Towards Open-Set EEG Seizure Type Classification

**abstract/A1：** Automated EEG seizure type classification systems are typically trained and evaluated under the closed-set assumption, where all seizure types encountered at deployment are seen during training. In clinical practice, this assumption fails: patients present with rare or atypical seizure types that fall outside the training label space, and a system that forces a wrong label is worse than one that abstains. We introduce the first open-set recognition protocol for EEG seizure type classification on the Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3), defining known and unknown classes using the clinical taxonomy itself: seizure types with sufficient subject representation (at least 12 subjects) form the known set, and rare types form the unknown set. We evaluate seven post-hoc open-set scorers (MSP, MaxLogit, energy, Mahalanobis, kNN, GHOST, and COSTARR) applied to a partially fine-tuned EEG Foundation Model (EFM), using a subject-disjoint leave-one-class-out (LOCO) protocol over three seeds that tests whether methods can detect seizure types excluded from fine-tuning. On true unknown classes, the best scorer (Mahalanobis) reaches a mean AUROC of 0.66, and differences among the top scorers are within seed-to-seed variation. Held-out known seizure types from unseen subjects are detected only slightly above chance (AUROC 0.54-0.58 for all scorers). Per-subject rejection rates are highly uneven for all scorers, and GHOST does not significantly improve per-subject fairness over energy scoring (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank, p &gt;= 0.25). We release the protocol, subject splits, and code as an initial baseline; our results indicate that open-set seizure type recognition remains a hard, open problem.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV) 2027 Workshop. Code and evaluation protocol are publicly available

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [Redesigning Neural Networks to Not be Confidently Wrong even to Strong Adversarial Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27811

**作者：** Michael W. Spratling、Heiko H. Schütt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27811v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出以皮层简单细胞／复杂细胞交替层级为蓝本的浅层网络（少于十对层），用新的选择性—容错表示组件做图像分类，在未知类与纯噪声上给出低置信预测，使对抗样本可被识别，同时保持有竞争力的泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「不为不存在物体给出高置信判断」的行为，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据是否支持答案、弃答与停止决策的评价提供线索，可供核查低置信度能否作为无证据信号；但该文是纯视觉分类架构，无多模态 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Redesigning Neural Networks to Not be Confidently Wrong even to Strong Adversarial Attacks

**abstract/A1：** Early deep learning models attempted to simulate visual perception using a neural hierarchy that alternated between two types of layer: one modelled on cortical simple cells (that was designed to increase selectivity), and one modelled on cortical complex cells (that aimed to increase tolerance). We show that such a neural network (NN) architecture, when implemented with novel components that are better able to learn selective and tolerant representations, is capable of accurate and robust image classification and improved alignment between the behaviour of the model and the perceptual abilities of humans. Firstly, the proposed models are shallow, fewer than ten layer-pairs, which is more consistent with the rapid feedforward object categorisation performed by the biological visual system. Secondly, they are far less prone to producing classification predictions with high confidence for samples from unknown classes or for image consisting of pure noise, and hence, like humans are not easily fooled into perceiving objects that are not present in the image. Thirdly, for the proposed NNs adversarial samples cause predictions with low confidence, enabling them to be accurately identified. In contrast to humans, our networks still do not correctly classify adversarial samples. However, the behaviour of our networks is a significant step towards human-like behaviour compared to standard DNNs that produce highly confident, but incorrect, classification predictions for adversarial samples. The increased robustness to adversarial and unknown samples is achieved while maintaining competitive performance in terms of generalisation to novel samples from the training categories. This enables models of this type to be competitive with state-of-the-art DNNs across multiple different evaluation metrics and benchmark tasks.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://codeberg.org/mwspratling/STEAL/

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [Auditable Financial Knowledge Graphs: Schema-Driven Construction and Intrinsic Quality Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27835v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27835

**作者：** Samarth Sarin、Lovepreet Singh、Dhagash Mehta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27835v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27835v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 schema 驱动的流程，用 LLM 分阶段从金融文档抽取实体、量化观察与关系，经实体归并和时间约束构建带来源信息的知识图谱，并给出与下游检索解耦的内在质量评估框架。

**相关性简评：** 其「独立于下游任务的内在质量评估」与细粒度 provenance、冲突属性管理等思路，可能为 latent 推理状态的可审计性与视觉证据溯源筛选提供评价类比，但仅为概念层面线索，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Auditable Financial Knowledge Graphs: Schema-Driven Construction and Intrinsic Quality Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Financial documents combine narrative disclosures, accounting metrics, corporate structures, management commentary, and time-dependent facts, making them difficult to represent using conventional chunk-based retrieval-augmented generation systems. This paper presents an auditable, schema-driven methodology for constructing temporal financial knowledge graphs from page-delimited documents parsed using a general purpose document intelligence service. Overlapping document windows undergo phased large language model extraction of structural entities, quantitative observations, and domain-specific relationships. Candidate graph elements are validated against predefined schemas and consolidated through scope-aware exact, normalized, fuzzy, embedding-based, and LLM-assisted entity resolution. Temporal merge constraints, deterministic edge deduplication, conflict-aware property management, and fine-grained provenance preserve issuer identity, reporting-period validity, and traceability to source evidence. The contributions of this work comprise the proposed methodology for financial knowledge graph construction, the creation of an evidence-grounded temporal knowledge graph from heterogeneous financial documents, and an intrinsic framework for evaluating the quality of the resulting graph. The evaluation framework assesses seven weighted dimensions, two hard validation gates, and structural diagnostics. By separating graph-quality assessment from downstream retrieval and answer generation, the framework supports systematic diagnosis and iterative refinement of the constructed graph.

**comment/C1：** The paper was accepted in ICAIF Conference 2026 and presented in Milan, Italy

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [OpenAHBU: Open-sourced Automatic Human Behaviour Understanding Toolkit Through Facial Behaviour Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27862v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27862

**作者：** Zijian Wu、Yupeng Huo、Cheng Luo、Yichen Huang、Junan Long、Haijun Lin、Lu Liu、Hatice Gunes、Bernard Ghanem、Siyang Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27862v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27862v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 发布 OpenAHBU 开源人脸行为理解工具箱，覆盖 11 项功能（landmarks、blendshape、AU、表情、效价/唤醒、疼痛、抑郁与人格），多数功能在聚合多数据集语料上重训，并用 SMTE 模块借助相关支持任务的预测增强各功能。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身是人脸情感感知工具箱而非 VLM 推理，但 SMTE 按功能自适应借用支持任务预测的做法，可能为 latent 推理中多源视觉证据加权与假设组合提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OpenAHBU: Open-sourced Automatic Human Behaviour Understanding Toolkit Through Facial Behaviour Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Automatic facial behaviour understanding (AFBU) is fundamental to various human-centred applications, yet existing academically available toolkits offer limited functionality, closed training pipelines, and weak generalisation. This paper presents OpenAHBU, a fully open-sourced, generalisable toolkit integrating 11 AFBU functions across three semantic levels: (i) short-term external facial attributes, including landmarks, blendshapes, Action Units, and expressions; (ii) short-term facial behaviour-related human psychological states, including valence/arousal and pain; and (iii) long-term facial behaviour-related psychological traits, including depression and personality. Particularly, we re-developed eight functions, where at least four deep learning models covering different computational complexities are developed for each function, providing flexible deployment on resource-constrained and high-throughput computing scenarios. More importantly, models of most functions are trained on a comprehensive corpus aggregating multiple academically available datasets, ensuring robust cross-dataset generalisability.In addition, we include a Synergistic Multi-task Enhancement (SMTE) module to efficiently and adaptively enhance each OpenAHBU function by leveraging the complementary cues contained in its most-related support function's predictions.Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our OpenAHBU outperformed existing open-source toolkits not only in functional coverage but also the predictive performances, while providing superior deployment flexibility across diverse computational scenarios.With fully publicly available training and inference scripts, pre-trained weights, user-friendly GUI, Python library, and cross-platform compatibility, our OpenAHBU provides a reproducible, scalable and extensible toolkit at https://github.com/OpenAHBU/OpenAHBU.

**comment/C1：** Main paper including appendix

</details>

### 225. [SLICR: Strategy Logic-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27865

**作者：** Arshia Rafieioskouie、Matthew Lucas、Tzu-Han Hsu、Borzoo Bonakdarpour

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27865v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SLICR，用 Strategy Logic 配合 LTL 描述多智能体目标与约束，并将其转化为奖励塑形，使部分可观测对抗环境下的强化学习能够显式推理各智能体策略及其相互应对方式，并在 Predator-Prey、Google Research Football 与扩展 SMAC-HARD 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「对其他智能体策略的显式量化与响应」可能为 LVR 中把关于他人策略与部分观测证据的信念写入 latent 状态、并据此决定继续获取证据或停止提供概念参照，但需核查其表示是否真为内部状态而非仅奖励工程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SLICR: Strategy Logic-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) translates formal specifications of desired behavior of agents into shaped rewards. This paper introduces SLICR, a significant paradigm shift that advances the state of the art to specification-guided RL in adversarial settings under partial observability. We employ Strategy Logic (SL), which allows explicit quantification over agents' strategies and uses the linear temporal logic (LTL) to specify the agents' objectives and constraints. This enables reasoning not only about how agents should act to satisfy a specification, but also about how they should respond to the strategies and behaviors of other agents. We evaluate SLICR on three popular benchmarks: Predator-Prey, Google Research Football, and our home-grown extension of SMAC-HARD, demonstrating its effectiveness, scalability, and ability to adapt to diverse adversarial strategies.

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [From Gist to Verbatim: The Controllable Path of One Image Through a Diffusion Prior](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27875v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27875

**作者：** Luca Saglietti、Luca Giammanco、Marc Mezard、Chiara Plizzari、Luca Biggio

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27875v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27875v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「记住单张图像」当作控制问题：用学习到的条件信号驱动冻结扩散模型逼近目标图，并追踪训练中记忆从保留语义主干（gist）、外观多样，逐步收敛到精确复现（verbatim），发现抽象阶段更接近文本条件且更利于编辑、单样本识别与检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「哪些细节必须存储、哪些可交给生成先验补全」的 gist/verbatim 划分及记忆作为相关性结构探针的思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节在 latent 状态里的保留程度与视觉旁路重建提供概念参照；但属图像生成与记忆场景，与推理期状态生成的迁移可行性需要核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Gist to Verbatim: The Controllable Path of One Image Through a Diffusion Prior

**abstract/A1：** Modern generative models are typically studied for their ability to generalize, while memorization is treated as a failure mode. We invert this perspective and ask how a generative model can recall a single datum. We frame memorization as a control problem: a learned conditioning signal steers a frozen diffusion model toward a target image, and we follow the formation of this memory throughout training. Before converging to exact reproduction (verbatim), memory passes through a regime of abstraction: samples preserve the target's semantic content (gist) while remaining diverse in appearance, leaving predictable detail to the generative prior. This semantic organization emerges despite the absence of labels, captions, or any explicit semantic objective. In the abstraction regime, the learned memory approaches ordinary text conditioning and supports editing, one-shot recognition, and retrieval more effectively than in the near-perfect reconstruction regime. These results suggest that memorization can serve as a probe of the relevance structure encoded by a generative model: what must be stored about a datum, what can be reconstructed from prior knowledge, and how learned memories interpolate between reconstructive recall and literal data storage.

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Renormalized Entropy from Symmetries: Simplicity Bias and Neural Heat Death](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27889v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27889

**作者：** Vasco Portilheiro、Samuel Liebana、Andrew Saxe、Peter Orbanz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27889v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27889v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从参数对称性出发，在 Gaussian 正则下的 Langevin 动力学中取零正则极限并引入重整化，推导出一种新的熵，用以刻画对称性诱导的隐式偏置：线性网络偏好奇异、低秩函数，齐次激活网络偏好失活神经元，并可能出现输出趋零的「神经热死亡」平衡。

**相关性简评：** 其函数空间熵与平衡态集中于退化解的结论，或可作为解释 latent reasoning 中表示坍缩、latent 更新失效与信息消失的概念工具，但论文无多模态或推理实验，联系仅为概念层面，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Renormalized Entropy from Symmetries: Simplicity Bias and Neural Heat Death

**abstract/A1：** Neural networks have parameter symmetries: parameter transformations that do not affect the represented function. How do these symmetries affect the functions that neural networks ultimately learn? We study the implicit bias arising from these symmetries by calculating the equilibrium distribution over represented functions under Langevin dynamics with Gaussian regularization. To isolate the effect of symmetries on this distribution, we study the limit in which the strength of the regularization is taken to zero. In certain cases, we show that the zero-regularization and infinite-time limits commute. In the low temperature limit, we recover known effective regularizations. At positive temperature, a renormalization procedure allows us to derive a novel entropy which predicts symmetry-induced preference for certain functions. For linear networks, we obtain explicit entropy terms that favor singular and low-rank functions. For networks with homogeneous activations, we find entropy terms that favor inactive neurons, which can lead to neural "heat death" in which equilibrium mass concentrates on networks with vanishing output. We interpret these results through the neural tangent kernel, as a simplicity bias selecting for functions that vary slowly under variations in the parameters.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 14 figures (including appendices)

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [Speculative Decoding Is Less Speculative Than You Think](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27891v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27891

**作者：** Guanning Zeng、Andrea Zanette

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27891v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27891v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DICE：在投机解码中预先采样并与验证阶段同步随机数，使 drafter 结合已知采样结果，把候选树构建与异步预取的计算集中到当前采样运行更可能采用的续写上，从而提升解码吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其「用受限草稿计算预判目标路径并定向分配计算」的构想，可能与 LVR 中动态计算预算、候选分支筛选及预测式预取有概念联系，但对象是 token 级加速而非推理状态，可供核查迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Speculative Decoding Is Less Speculative Than You Think

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by preparing candidate continuations before the target model generates them. Its efficiency depends on how well limited drafting computation anticipates the path the target will actually follow. Anticipating this path involves two sources of uncertainty: the target model's predictions and the randomness of sampling. Yet the former requires a target-model forward pass, while the latter can be fixed before drafting. Building on this observation, we introduce DICE (Draw-Informed Candidate Selection), which pre-samples and synchronizes verification randomness across drafting and verification. By combining these known draws with predictions from an existing drafter, DICE allocates computation toward continuations more likely to be used in the current sampling run. This principle guides node selection throughout draft-tree construction and extends to asynchronous prefetching, where DICE predicts which next-round drafts to prepare. Without additional drafter training or changes to the target sampling distribution, DICE achieves state-of-the-art decoding throughput across diverse settings, with speedups of up to 24.6pp over strong baselines.

**comment/C1：** Preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [Star Panoptic Quality: Selectively Evaluating Split and Merged Instances](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27913v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27913

**作者：** Erik Großkopf、Paula Tamara Buzduga、Hendrik Möller、Florian Kofler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27913v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27913v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 StarPQ 评价框架：在预测与参考实例的二分图中构造 star forest，将 clean、merge、split 三类失败模式分离，再按模式分别加权，使参考标注有偏时也能有针对性地评估全景分割质量。

**相关性简评：** 其将实例匹配失败分解为合并／分裂并分项加权的诊断思路，可能可供核查于刻画 latent 视觉状态中实体被合并、拆分或信息坍缩的情形；但原文仅为分割评测指标，未涉及推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Star Panoptic Quality: Selectively Evaluating Split and Merged Instances

**abstract/A1：** Detecting individual instances is crucial for many segmentation problems. However, expert instance annotations are often unavailable, so instances are approximated heuristically (e.g., as connected components of semantic maps), which can merge touching objects or split fragmented ones. Even when expert annotations exist, expert disagreement or structural delineation biases can yield split or merged reference instances, corresponding to over- or under-segmentation, respectively. Panoptic Quality (PQ) and related metrics capture both segmentation and detection quality while weighting all instances equally, regardless of size. Yet when the references are biased, these metrics can neither detect split or merged instances nor distinguish valid from invalid overor under-segmentation. We present Star Panoptic Quality (StarPQ), a novel evaluation ramework, which closes this gap in two steps: First, we construct a so-called star forest in the bipartite graph whose clean, merge, and split stars isolate different failure modes. Second, we calculate the final metric by individually weighting each failure mode, allowing practitioners to account for instance delineation biases. Across various experiments, we demonstrate that StarPQ not only enables targeted penalization or eniency towards specific failure modes, but also serves as a diagnostic tool to reveal their prevalence.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [SUAF: Spectral Universal Advantage Features for Zero-Shot Policy Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27919v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27919

**作者：** Huaze Tang、Runqing Miao、Tianren Peng、Lam Nguyen、Chao Wang、Jairong Li、Wenbo Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27919v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27919v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究无奖励表示应捕获何种统计依赖、保留哪些成分才能改进策略，构造以动作为中心的正滞后典型依赖矩阵，并用其 SVD 得到成对谱优势特征（SUAF），配合 Soft-HGR 目标从无奖励数据中学习。

**相关性简评：** 其关于「保留前几个谱模态可得最优秩约束近似、丢弃其余成分如何影响决策」的分析，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态该保留哪些任务必要信息、以及信息坍缩的诊断与筛选相关，但面向 RL 表示而非视觉推理，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SUAF: Spectral Universal Advantage Features for Zero-Shot Policy Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Reward-independent representations enable reinforcement learning agents to reuse knowledge across tasks, but limited representation capacity raises three fundamental questions: what statistical dependence should these representations capture, which components of that dependence should be retained for policy improvement, and how does discarding the remaining components affect decision-making? We address these questions through canonical dependence analysis. For a reference policy, we construct an action-centered, positive-lag canonical dependence matrix (CDM) that isolates the additional predictive contribution of the current action beyond the current state. Its singular value decomposition yields Spectral Universal Advantage Features (SUAF), paired features that recover delayed advantage through linear reward projections. We show that the spectrum quantifies mutual information between current action and future state-action visitation conditioned on current state and determines reward specific approximation errors. Retaining the leading modes gives optimal rank-constrained approximations under both isotropic average-case and reward-uniform minimax criteria. Separating the immediate reward contrast also avoids the spectral floor arising from direct compression of the full advantage operator. To learn SUAF from reward-free data, we develop an action-centered Soft-HGR objective with bidirectional temporal-difference estimation. We further connect spectral approximation errors to sufficient conditions for policy improvement. Finite-state experiments validate the spectral predictions and feature-learning method, while zero-shot evaluations on discrete tasks and continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate the utility of the learned representations across rewards.

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [ESSI: Computationally Efficient Semantic Search for Sparse Autoencoder Concepts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27921v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27921

**作者：** James Baker

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27921v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27921v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ESSI 面向扩散模型 SAE 特征定位，用分割掩码标注 SDXL 激活，对 SAE 特征排序并二分搜索仍可线性分离概念的最小特征集，再复用该分类器校准 steering 强度，并在概念插入与物体遗忘上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「概念到特征」的搜索与最小充分特征集验证，可能为 LVR 中 latent 假设筛选和视觉证据选择提供方法线索；但研究对象是图像生成 steering 而非推理状态，线性可分判据能否迁移尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ESSI: Computationally Efficient Semantic Search for Sparse Autoencoder Concepts

**abstract/A1：** Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose diffusion model activations into interpretable features, but steering a model with them requires identifying which features map to a given semantic concept, if any. Existing methods for this concept to feature task are expensive, relying on grid searches or repeated passes through the frozen UNet. We propose Efficient Semantic Sparse Identification (ESSI). Using segmentation masks to label SDXL activations as concept or non-concept, ESSI ranks SAE features and binary-searches for the smallest set on which a linear classifier still separates positive from negative samples while not falling below a performance threshold, then reuses that classifier to calibrate the steering strength. We evaluate the same selected features on both concept insertion and object unlearning. Compared with prior work, ESSI is as cheap or cheaper, and performs well on both insertion and removal.

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Agnostic PAC Learning for Partial Concept Classes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27946v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27946

**作者：** Liang-Yu Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27946v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27946v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究有限 VC 维部分概念类（取值含「⋆」即未定义）在 agnostic PAC 框架下的学习问题，构造确定性学习器并给出与已知下界吻合的最优样本复杂度 O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²)。

**相关性简评：** 「部分概念」与必须输出全定义假设的设定，可能为 LVR 中「latent 状态仅部分确定答案、需要据缺失证据给出总答案」的监督量与泛化分析提供概念化工具，可供核查其是否映射到视觉证据缺失或 STOP 决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Optimal Sample Complexity of Agnostic PAC Learning for Partial Concept Classes

**abstract/A1：** Let $\\mathcal H\\subseteq\\{-1,+1,\\star\\}^{\\mathcal X}$ be a partial concept class of finite VC dimension $d$. We construct a deterministic agnostic PAC learner that, for every $\\varepsilon,δ\\in(0,1)$, uses $O((d+\\log(1/δ))/\\varepsilon^2)$ i.i.d. examples from an arbitrary distribution $\\mathcal D$ over $\\mathcal X\\times\\{-1,+1\\}$ and returns a total concept $\\hat h:\\mathcal X\\to\\{-1,+1\\}$ satisfying $\\text{er}\_{\\mathcal D}(\\hat h)\\leq \\min\_{h\\in\\mathcal H}\\text{er}\_{\\mathcal D}(h)+\\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, where $\\text{er}\_{\\mathcal D}(h)=\\mathbb P\_{(x,y)\\sim\\mathcal D}\[h(x)\\neq y\]$. This establishes the optimal sample complexity, matching the lower bound of Alon et al. (FOCS 2021).

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [Ranking-Preserving Coreset Selection for Efficient Evolutionary Hyperparameter Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27959v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27959

**作者：** Mamoona Tasadduq、Maryam Bashir

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27959v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27959v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 RPC 框架，用进化优化挑选一个小规模 coreset，使子集上评估超参数配置的相对排序与全量数据排序的 Spearman 相关性最大化，从而加速 GA、CHC、PSO 的演化式超参搜索。

**相关性简评：** 「以保持配置排序为目标选择数据子集」可能为视觉证据筛选提供一条线索：核查能否用「保留候选间相对优劣」作为筛选 latent 视觉 token 或证据的指标，而非仅保留绝对正确性；其进化搜索子集的做法也可供参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ranking-Preserving Coreset Selection for Efficient Evolutionary Hyperparameter Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is essential for high predictive performance, but its computational cost becomes prohibitive when candidate configurations require repeated training on large datasets. This paper proposes a ranking-preserving coreset (RPC) selection framework for accelerating evolutionary HPO. The method constructs a compact coreset that preserves the relative ranking of hyperparameter configurations observed on the full training data. Coreset selection is formulated as an evolutionary optimization problem that maximizes Spearman rank correlation between full-data and subset-based configuration rankings while penalizing subset size. The selected coreset is then used for HPO with three evolutionary algorithms: Genetic Algorithm (GA), Cross-generational elitist Selection, Heterogeneous Recombination and Cataclysmic Mutation (CHC), and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). Experiments on seven tabular, two text, and two image benchmark datasets show that RPC substantially reduces HPO time while maintaining test accuracy comparable to full-dataset HPO across decision tree, text classification, and image classification tasks. Across datasets and optimizers, HPO-stage speedups range from approximately 1.14x to 8.80x, while the one-time coreset construction cost is accounted for separately through end-to-end runtime and break-even analysis. On 20 held-out configurations excluded from coreset construction, RPC achieves higher ranking correlation than same-size random sampling on 8 of 11 datasets (dataset-level Wilcoxon signed-rank test, p = 0.014). These results show that ranking preservation extends beyond the configurations used during selection. The one-time construction cost can be amortized when the coreset is reused across HPO runs.

</details>

<a id="paper-234"></a>

### 234. [A Methodologically Rigorous Benchmark of Linear Regression,RNN, and LSTM for S&amp;P 500 Forecasting:Identifying Sources of Performance Inflation in Financial DeepLearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27978v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27978

**作者：** Hari Sitaula、Joseph Opitz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27978v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27978v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在统一的预处理与时间序评估流程下，对线性回归、RNN 与 LSTM 的标普 500 单日预测进行基准比较，并审计三类会抬高结果的做法：小波去噪后的评估目标、缺少朴素持续性基线、以及 best-of-N 重复报告，随后在修正协议下重评各模型。

**相关性简评：** 金融时序本身与 LVR 无关，但其「朴素基线缺失、多重重复取最优、评估目标被预处理污染」的审计框架可能可供核查 latent 推理收益是否只是基线或挑选偏差所致。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Methodologically Rigorous Benchmark of Linear Regression,RNN, and LSTM for S&amp;P 500 Forecasting:Identifying Sources of Performance Inflation in Financial DeepLearning

**abstract/A1：** This study examines one-day-ahead forecasting of the S&amp;P 500 closing level using a 2006-2025 dataset that includes fundamental price data, macroeconomic indicators, and engineered technical features. We benchmark three model families, namely linear regression, vanilla recurrent neural networks (RNN), and long short-term memory networks (LSTM), within a unified preprocessing and chronological evaluation pipeline. A central contribution of this work is a methodological audit of common practices in the deep-learning-for-stock-prediction literature. We identify three sources of result inflation that frequently appear in published methodologies: wavelet-denoised evaluation targets, the absence of a naive persistence baseline, and best-of-N replicate reporting. We then re-evaluate all model families under a corrected protocol that controls for each. Performance is reported using root mean squared error, mean absolute percentage error, and the Pearson correlation coefficient, averaged across N = 30 independent replicates per configuration. We compare models on accuracy, stability across replicates, and architectural depth sensitivity, using the naive persistence forecast (predicting today's close as tomorrow's close) as a primary baseline against which all candidate models must demonstrate improvement.

</details>

### 235. [Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26663v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26663

**作者：** Rong-Ching Chang、Hao-Chuan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26663v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26663v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究通过人类头脑风暴实验（N=168）考察 AI 伙伴数量（一个或两个）与行为配置方式（按人类平均行为校准或按参与者定制）如何影响人类产出想法数量与多样性。

**排除原因：** 属于人机协作团队的行为实验，讨论的是人类发帖量与想法多样性，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据使用、latent 状态生成或训练监督，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams

**abstract/A1：** Generative AI has improved individual productivity, but whether adding a plurality of AI partners helps or hurts remains open. We crossed AI partnership (one versus two) with behavioral formation (calibrated to average human behavior versus customized by the participant) in a brainstorming experiment ($N$=168) with human-human dyads as reference. Two-AI rooms posted more ideas to the shared board, but human-authored ideas dropped substantially. With one AI partner, formation mattered: humans paired with an average-calibrated AI posted more than any other condition. With two, this effect vanished. Humans continued talking but converted fewer ideas into board posts. AI social talk predicted more human posts, while AI posting volume predicted fewer. AI presence increased global diversity of human-authored ideas, but only a second AI increased within-session differentiation. The number of AI partners and their formation can reshape what the human contributes; single-agent results do not generalize to multi-agent teams.

</details>

<a id="paper-236"></a>

### 236. [On the loss landscape of the linear neural network: beyond critical points](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26671v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26671

**作者：** Antoine Lejay、Lionel Lenotre、Sa{ï}d Toubra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.NE、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26671v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26671v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究线性神经网络损失景观的中观几何结构，提出「critical thalweg」概念，用以统一包含所有临界点，并在更弱假设下重新给出 Baldi &amp; Hornik 结论的几何证明，同时给出梯度下降沿该结构下降的经验观察。

**排除原因：** 属纯理论优化与线性网络损失景观分析，未涉及视觉或多模态表示、latent 状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，材料中也无可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the loss landscape of the linear neural network: beyond critical points

**abstract/A1：** While the critical points of the loss landscape in linear neural networks have been extensively studied since the seminal work of Baldi \\&amp; Hornik in the late 1980s, a description of this landscape at an intermediate or global scale is still lacking. Such a characterization is crucial, particularly as a substantial body of literature indicates that gradient descent circumvents critical points. The present article introduces an intermediate structure, termed the critical thalweg, which encompasses all critical points and exhibits key geometric properties. Beyond providing a geometric proof of Baldi \\&amp; Hornik's results using simpler assumptions than the existing literature, this framework offers a global view of the loss landscape that can be easily visualized in a two-dimensional plane. Empirical observations suggest that gradient descent is attracted toward the critical thalweg, along which the loss continues to decrease to its global minimum.

</details>

### 237. [Forehead ROI Localization under Real-World Imaging Degradations via Cross-View Pseudo-Labeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26712

**作者：** Geetanjali Sharma、Gaurav Jaswal、Aditya Nigam、Raghavendra Ramachandra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26712v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出置信度与一致性引导的伪标签方法（CCGP），用原图与水平翻转视图的检测置信度和几何一致性筛选无标签人脸图像，以适配 Grounding DINO 实现额头 ROI 定位。

**排除原因：** 属于人脸生物特征检测中的域适应与伪标签工作，虽涉及视觉证据定位，但不研究推理期 latent 状态、证据筛选决策或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等机制，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系；其跨视图一致性过滤或可作极弱线索，但材料未显示与推理状态使用相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Forehead ROI Localization under Real-World Imaging Degradations via Cross-View Pseudo-Labeling

**abstract/A1：** Forehead region-of-interest (ROI) localization is important for forehead-creases biometrics, particularly when the lower face is occluded. However, localization remains challenging under varying image quality, pose, and illumination. We propose Confidence and Consistency Guided Pseudo-Labeling (CCGP), which adapts Grounding DINO using unlabeled facial images filtered by detection confidence and geometric consistency between original and horizontally flipped views, without modifying the detector architecture or loss. We evaluate CCGP on five external datasets and 350 images spanning seven imaging conditions. CCGP achieves the highest mean intersection over union (mIoU) on four of five external datasets, improving from 0.415 to 0.580 on RMFRD compared with supervised Grounding DINO. On the quality-stratified subset, the largest gains occur under blur (0.487 to 0.719) and low contrast (0.615 to 0.710). CCGP also outperforms conventional self-training on RMFRD (0.580 versus 0.551 mIoU). Despite a slight in-domain performance gap (0.861 vs. 0.866 mIoU) and challenges with off-angle poses, CCGP improves cross-dataset localization under challenging imaging conditions.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, submitted and accepted to the 2027 IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision Workshops (WACVW)

</details>

### 238. [SCATR-ITS: a scalable transformer for large-area individual tree segmentation from LiDAR point clouds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26715v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26715

**作者：** Lassi Ruoppa、Verneri Seppänen、Antero Kukko、Juha Hyyppä

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26715v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26715v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SCATR-ITS，一个面向大范围 LiDAR 点云单木分割的可扩展 Transformer，通过基于学习嵌入的 superpoint 级处理、尺寸感知后处理和动态 query 数量来加速推理，并在多个数据集及 142 km² 芬兰国家级扫描数据上验证速度与精度。

**排除原因：** 属于林业遥感 3D 实例分割的工程加速工作，虽涉及 superpoint 降采样与动态 query 数量，但面向感知效率而非 VLM 推理状态的生成、使用或停止决策，与 latent visual reasoning 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SCATR-ITS: a scalable transformer for large-area individual tree segmentation from LiDAR point clouds

**abstract/A1：** Individual tree segmentation (ITS) from LiDAR point clouds is one of the most widely studied segmentation tasks in forestry. Although deep learning methods define the state of the art on the majority of ITS benchmarks, their high computational complexity and long runtime currently prevent their adoption in large-scale operational workflows. In this work, we present SCATR-ITS, a scalable transformer model specifically designed for efficient ITS over large geographical areas. Based on the ForestFormer3D framework, SCATR-ITS transitions to superpoint-level processing to accelerate inference, with the superpoints constructed from learned embeddings. The architecture incorporates size-aware superpoint-level postprocessing, a dynamic query count module to handle high variation in forest structure, and a maximal input stride designed to minimize prediction redundancy. SCATR-ITS achieves accuracy on par with the state of the art on the FGI-EMIT and FOR-InstanceV2 datasets with an up to 8.5$\\times$ higher inference speed. In addition, SCATR-ITS has a significantly lower GPU VRAM footprint during both training and inference, and exhibits excellent zero-shot generalization across multiple datasets. To demonstrate large-area ITS capabilities, SCATR-ITS is applied to a 142 km$^2$ region of Finnish national laser scanning data where it achieves an average throughput of 13.8 min/km$^2$, $\\sim$18.6$\\times$ faster than ForestFormer3D and approaching the speed of the unsupervised watershed algorithm, which it vastly outperforms in accuracy and segment quality. Overall, SCATR-ITS delivers state-of-the-art accuracy at speeds where operational-scale adoption becomes viable, paving the way toward countrywide deployment. The source code for SCATR-ITS and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/ruoppa/scatr\_its.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 13 figures

</details>

### 239. [SparseHPS: A Unified Model for Human Pose and Shape across Depth Cameras and LiDARs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26730v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26730

**作者：** Ilya Zaychuk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26730v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26730v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 SparseHPS，用一套权重从稀疏体素点云中回归 SMPL-X 人体姿态、形状与全局平移，并用前七帧 token 的因果记忆处理流式输入。

**排除原因：** 属三维人体网格感知回归任务，多传感器统一与时序 token 记忆只服务于几何估计的时序融合，未涉及推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，缺乏可核查的 LVR 联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SparseHPS: A Unified Model for Human Pose and Shape across Depth Cameras and LiDARs

**abstract/A1：** Recovering 3D human pose and shape from point clouds is central to motion capture, robotics and self-driving vehicles, because depth cameras and LiDARs sense a person directly in metric 3D and work under any lighting. However, current approaches are each tailored to one sensor type: point density differs by more than ten times between devices, public datasets are labeled with incompatible body models, and LiDAR approaches need long input sequences to offset sparse scans. In this work, we present SparseHPS, a novel sensor-agnostic network that recovers SMPL-X pose, shape and global translation from a human point cloud with one set of weights, for single frames and streams alike. Specifically, SparseHPS encodes the cloud with a LitePT backbone over sparse voxels, represents every joint by a learnable query decoded with one common head, localizes the body by voting over voxels, and draws temporal context from a causal memory of tokens from the seven preceding frames. A single model is trained on ten datasets that mix rendered clouds with real depth-camera and LiDAR captures, all converted to one body model. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art results on the SLOPER4D and FreeMotion LiDAR benchmarks, where SparseHPS lowers the root-relative joint error by 22% and 11%, respectively, compared with the strongest prior method, which needs 32 frames. On real Azure Kinect captures of held-out HuMMan subjects, SparseHPS attains a joint error of 34.7 mm. Moreover, the current frame alone already yields the bulk of this accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables. Project page: https://ghostlate.github.io/SparseHPS

</details>

### 240. [ConRef: Auditing Reference Reliability in 2D/3D Registration Benchmarks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26758v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26758

**作者：** Jianfeng Yue、Feng Li、Tianyu Song、Julius Watrinet、Ulrich Eck、Nassir Navab

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26758v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26758v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ConRef 多视角框架，用于审计 X-ray/CT 2D/3D 配准基准中参考位姿的可靠性，估计附加参考位姿并识别潜在异常 X 光。

**排除原因：** 属医学影像几何配准的基准标注审计，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 计算，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的状态、训练或决策联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ConRef: Auditing Reference Reliability in 2D/3D Registration Benchmarks

**abstract/A1：** Purpose: Accurate 2D/3D registration is crucial for image-guided orthopedic interventions, enabling the alignment of preoperative 3D anatomy with intraoperative X-ray observations for surgical navigation. Public benchmarks have facilitated the development and evaluation of increasingly accurate registration methods. However, as registration errors approach the uncertainty of benchmark reference poses, measured performance may be limited by reference inaccuracies rather than algorithmic errors alone. Methods: We propose Consensus Reference (ConRef), a multi-view framework for auditing reference poses in existing X-ray/computed tomography (CT) registration benchmarks. By jointly exploiting multiple X-ray observations, ConRef estimates an additional reference pose, quantifies its uncertainty, and identifies potential X-ray outliers. We validate the framework using independent anatomical evidence and a newly acquired X-ray/cone-beam CT (CBCT) phantom dataset with bead-based reference poses. Results: On our BeadPhant dataset, ConRef differed from independent bead-based references by 0.08-0.12 mm, while discrepancies with original references reached 2.06 mm on the Femur dataset. It also flagged 22 DeepFluoro X-rays as potential outliers. On Femur F5, reference choice changed the submillimeter success conclusions of the same registrations. Conclusion: Our findings demonstrate that reference discrepancies can influence the evaluation of high-accuracy 2D/3D registration methods. By providing an additional, independently validated reference and identifying potential inconsistencies, ConRef enables systematic auditing of existing benchmarks and supports more reliable evaluation of registration error.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 1 table; includes 4 pages of supplementary material

</details>

### 241. [ChatAtom: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Molecular Dynamics Simulation and Visualization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26782v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26782

**作者：** Trupti Mohanty、Ming Du、Mitchell A. Wood、Brad L. Boyce、Remi Dingreville、Mathew J. Cherukara

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26782v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26782v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ChatAtom 是一个面向分子动力学模拟的多智能体 LLM 系统，用编排、生成—审阅脚本、远程计算调度和结果分析把自然语言请求转成完整 MD 工作流，并提供浏览器可视化与可加载技能。

**排除原因：** 这是科学计算工作流自动化应用，所处理的“可视化”只是仿真帧的呈现，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChatAtom: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Molecular Dynamics Simulation and Visualization

**abstract/A1：** Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation resolves atomic-scale motion and predicts the structural, mechanical, and dynamic properties of materials, making it cen- tral to materials design and discovery. Yet an MD study requires an expert-driven workflow: designing the simulation, writing reliable input scripts, executing them, and analyzing the results with separate tools. Here we present ChatAtom, a multi-agent large language model system that translates a natural-language request into a complete MD workflow without the user writing input scripts or analysis code. Each agent has a defined responsibility within the workflow. An orchestrator plans the study; the LAMMPS and OVITO agents each use a generator-reviewer pair to prepare and review simulation and visualization scripts before execution; a high-performance computing agent dispatches jobs to remote resources; and an analysis agent interprets the completed run. The work- flow is accessible through a browser interface that streams thermodynamic data and rendered frames during local execution, allowing users to follow the simu- lation as it progresses. The architecture also supports new capabilities through loadable skills without modifying the agents, enabling autonomous hypothesis generation and testing. We evaluate ChatAtom through script-generation bench- marks, an end-to-end study of crack propagation, and a closed-loop campaign. By bringing script generation, execution, visualization, and analysis into one reviewed and observable pipeline, ChatAtom lowers the barrier to conducting MD studies and broadens access to advanced simulation workflows.

</details>

### 242. [Realistic Robustness Evaluation of Tabular Data Driven Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26818

**作者：** Miguel Silva、Eva Maia、João Vitorino、Isabel Praça

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26818v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 AURORA，一个用于表格数据分类模型的开源鲁棒性评测工具，整合多种对抗扰动方法与评测指标，并生成可视化报告。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是表格数据的对抗鲁棒性评测，与视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态表示、证据筛选和 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策无明显关联，扰动与距离约束也不构成可迁移的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Realistic Robustness Evaluation of Tabular Data Driven Models

**abstract/A1：** The increasing adoption of Machine Learning (ML) models in high-stakes applications has raised critical concerns regarding their robustness against adversarial attacks. While extensive research has focused on adversarial example generation in image and text domains, the study of such examples in tabular data remains comparatively limited, despite its widespread use in real-world systems. This work introduces AURORA, a modular, open-source tool for evaluating the robustness of ML models on tabular data classification. It integrates multiple adversarial perturbation methods and evaluation metrics within a unified framework to systematically assess model behavior under adversarial conditions. Central to AURORA is its evaluation report, which focuses on robustness analysis by quantifying performance degradation, comparing the impact of different perturbation strategies, and identifying model vulnerabilities across features and input regions. The report is complemented by visual artifacts that facilitate the interpretability of robustness patterns. Additionally, AURORA incorporates a distance adjustment mechanism that penalizes implausible adversarial samples based on feature-level constraints, ensuring that the robustness assessment remains grounded in realistic perturbations and is therefore more reliable.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables, FPS 2026 conference

</details>

### 243. [3D Open-Vocabulary Detection via Visual Alignment and Generative Captions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26842v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26842

**作者：** Thomas E. Huang、Alex Zihao Zhu、Hao Xiang、Tianyi Cai、Jingwei Ji、Yuliang Zou、Jiafan Yu、Xin Huang、Mingxing Tan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26842v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26842v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向户外场景的 3D 开放词汇目标检测，提出将 VLM 的提案级语义嵌入蒸馏到 3D 特征，并用 MLLM 生成 caption 增强语义对齐。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于 3D 检测中的语义迁移与训练监督，不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，对象级嵌入对齐也难为 LVR 的表示与贡献分配提供具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 3D Open-Vocabulary Detection via Visual Alignment and Generative Captions

**abstract/A1：** Open-vocabulary 3D object detection remains a significant challenge for outdoor scenes, with existing methods often struggling to effectively transfer the rich semantic knowledge from powerful 2D Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into a 3D context. We propose a novel, two-part framework for this task. First, we introduce a direct, object-level distillation method. We generate class-agnostic 3D proposals to source regions from the entire scene, without relying on any manual labels. We leverage a spatially-aware VLM to extract a semantic embedding from each proposal's projected 2D bounding box and supervise our 3D model to predict this embedding on a per-instance basis. Second, to enhance zero-shot capability and inject domain-specific information, we employ a powerful Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as an image captioner to generate rich textual captions for each of these proposals. These captions are integrated into the training objective to directly align the 3D features with these rich semantics, creating a composite loss that combines direct feature distillation with caption-based semantic enrichment. Our experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach, establishing a new state of the art in 3D open-vocabulary detection on the Waymo Open Dataset and nuScenes.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26868
