摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文评估工业异常检测中用程序化噪声合成缺陷训练的效果及基准到真实数据的迁移差距，属于纯感知判别任务，不涉及多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可借用的方法与评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Effectiveness of Synthetic Defects for Anomaly Detection in Industrial Small Load Carriers

**abstract/A1：** Recent industrial anomaly detection methods use procedural noise to generate synthetic training defects. Their effectiveness in real-world settings and the impact of different noise generation methods have not been sufficiently investigated. We evaluate the effectiveness of training using synthetic defects for both supervised and unsupervised methods, the impact of different noise generation methods, and the differences in detection performance between benchmark datasets and a real-world small load carrier (SLC) dataset. Trained on synthetic defects only, a supervised classifier reaches 0.65 AUROC on real SLC defects, while three unsupervised methods using the same kind of noise (Perlin) reach 0.87--0.90. Except for SuperSimpleNet, which fails with the foreground-confined fractal Brownian motion (fBm) recipe, models trained with the custom recipe or with nine other noise types do not differ reliably from those trained with Perlin noise. Finally, results with synthetic defects on benchmark datasets do not carry over to the SLC dataset, possibly because the synthetic defects differ too much from the real ones (a larger angle in feature space). These results call for caution when training models on synthetic defect images.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to ESANN 2027

</details>

**内容简述：** 该文对语音部分编辑深度伪造定位系统 SAL 做按编辑类型与生成器分组的条件化评测，发现删除操作（尤其 SSR-Speech 生成）最难，聚合分数会掩盖局部弱点。

**排除原因：** 属于语音伪造检测的细分评测分析，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取与停止决策，分组归因也只针对伪造类型，未见可供 LVR 借鉴的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Robust Partial-Edit Deepfake Localization: An edit and Generator Conditioned Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Partial-edit deepfake localization aims to identify manipulated regions within otherwise authentic speech. Recent systems report strong overall results, but aggregate scores do not reveal whether this performance holds across different editing conditions. In this work, we present an edit- and generator-conditioned analysis of Segment-Aware Localization (SAL) using WavLM-Large and XLS-R representations on the VoiceCraft and SSR-Speech subsets of PartialEdit. We categorize edits along three transcript-derived axes, namely content, meaning change, and editing operation, and evaluate each generator separately. While both representations achieve strong overall performance, localization is noticeably weaker on SSR-Speech. From our experiments, we found that the deletion is the hardest operation, and deletions produced by SSR-Speech are the most challenging condition for both representations. In contrast, edit content, meaning change, and sentence novelty have smaller effects. These findings show that a single aggregate score can mask important weaknesses, and that partial-edit localization should be evaluated separately across generators and edit types.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to ICASSP 2027 Workshop

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26942

**作者：** Ashley Zhang、Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer、Aleksandr Zverianskii、Daniel Ng、Tasana Pejovic、Antía García、Jacob Clyne、Ravpreet Setia、Akshay Utture、Etan Jacob Ginsberg、Shriyash Kaustubh Upadhyay、Fazl Barez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26942v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26942v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文提出以开发者是否采纳 bot 建议作为「显示偏好」信号的代码评审基准，属软件工程质量控制评测，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策；其行为式评价思路与 LVR 的 latent 内容因果贡献核查之间也缺少可核查的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CodeReviewBench: An Automated Benchmark for Evaluating the Software Factory

**abstract/A1：** As AI agents ship more code with minimal human oversight, code review becomes the critical quality-control checkpoint -- but one without a reliable, objective verifier: review quality is nuanced and ultimately a judgment call by the PR author. Existing benchmarks are structurally misaligned with this: annotators are third-party reviewers, like the bots they evaluate, not the PR authors who actually decide what to fix. They also rely on small, static issue sets that require full re-evaluation as tools update, limiting how frequently progress can be tracked. We introduce CodeReviewBench, built on a fundamentally different signal: revealed developer preferences -- if a developer acts on a bot's suggestion, we treat it as useful; if they ignore it, we do not. This signal is mined daily from the public GitHub event stream, yielding 500K+ judged PRs across 18 tools, and refreshes continuously as tools evolve. We validate the developer preference against an independent offline benchmark: the two agree on precision rankings and independently detect the same product updates within days of vendor releases. Together, these form the benchmark.

</details>

### 247. [Causal Diffusion for Extreme Super-Resolution in Interactive Games](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26963v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26963

**作者：** Christina Zhang、Jenna Kang、Colin Groth、Akshay Jindal、Stavros Diolatzis、Qi Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26963v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26963v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CIDER，一个面向交互式游戏的因果扩散超分框架，仅用当前与历史低分辨率帧和深度、运动矢量引导，从 120p 合成 960p 高频细节，并通过轻量编码器、解码器微调与蒸馏达到约 13 FPS。

**排除原因：** 属图像/视频生成式的画质增强工作，「causal」仅指时间上不使用未来帧，与 LVR 关注的内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策无实质联系，也无具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Causal Diffusion for Extreme Super-Resolution in Interactive Games

**abstract/A1：** Existing game super-resolution methods typically operate at moderate upscaling factors, struggling to recover fine visual details from extremely low-resolution inputs (e.g., 120p). At such resolutions, super-resolution shifts from reconstructing existing information toward synthesizing missing visual details. While video diffusion models offer powerful generative priors for this challenging task, their dependence on future frames limits their applicability to interactive games. We present CIDER, a causal diffusion framework for extreme game super-resolution that synthesizes high-frequency details from current and past low-resolution frames, guided by G-buffers (depth and motion vectors) to preserve scene structure and temporal consistency. We collect data from two AAA games (Cyberpunk 2077 and Black Myth: Wukong). To reduce inference load, we develop a lightweight latent projection encoder tailored to ultra-low-resolution inputs, fine-tune a decoder (TAE) on our high-resolution outputs, and distill the diffusion model to reach interactive frame rates. Our experiments demonstrate high-fidelity 8$\\times$ upscaling from 120p to 960p at around 13 FPS, outperforming state-of-the-art game upscalers in visual quality and showing potential for 16$\\times$ magnification.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OPCP 运行时治理层，在代理提议与供应商执行之间中介能力调用并记录不确定结果，用脚本化故障、重启与供应商替换实验评估重试、幂等与路由成本。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦工具调用的授权、供应商切换与重复副作用抑制，明确不评估语言模型，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Keep the Process, Change the Supplier: Runtime Governance for Tool-Using AI Agents

**abstract/A1：** Tool-using AI agents propose actions whose authorization and external effects cannot be inferred from generated text alone. We study an execution boundary that separates agent proposals from business-controlled authority, context disclosure, and supplier selection. The Open Process Control Plane (OPCP) mediates capability calls and records uncertain outcomes when a supplier may have acted without returning an acceptable reply. We evaluate this runtime with scripted requests and synthetic suppliers: 2,400 fault episodes across four policies, 60 process-termination/restart episodes, 19,000 timed calls across 19 configurations, five targeted mutations over 43 fixtures, and 160 substitution records. No language model or autonomous agent is evaluated. OPCP and a conservative composition agree on all measured fault outcomes. Both recover after definite refusal without cross-supplier duplication; retry-all duplicates effects in every tested lost-reply-after-commit case. Persistent runtime state prevents cross-supplier replay in the restart experiments, while same-supplier duplicate prevention also requires supplier idempotency. At 128 suppliers, best-score routing has a median latency of 1.411 ms versus 0.261 ms for pinned routing. All five selected mutations are detected, and record enumeration exposes acceptance of an empty regression set. These results characterize an execution mechanism relevant to agent governance, including its costs and assumption boundaries. They do not establish improved agent task success, prompt-injection resistance, or real-vendor model portability.

</details>

### 249. [Affine-Image Propagation for Tight and Scalable Neural Network Verification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26978v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26978

**作者：** Hong-Ming Chiu、Haoyu Li、Huan Zhang、Richard Y. Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26978v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26978v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 ℓ2 对抗鲁棒性的神经网络边界传播验证（affine-image 约束与对角占优松弛），属纯认证方法，未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成或答案对证据的使用，与 LVR 主线无具体可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Affine-Image Propagation for Tight and Scalable Neural Network Verification

**abstract/A1：** While bound propagation methods are highly effective for certifying neural networks against $\\ell\_{\\infty}$ adversaries, tight and scalable verification for $\\ell\_{2}$ adversaries remains a significant open challenge. Existing methods incur geometric loss by enclosing preactivation sets in elementwise intervals or axis-aligned ellipsoids, and functional loss by restricting the linear bounds constructed over those domains. Consequently, tight $\\ell\_{2}$ verification is currently restricted to Lipschitz-regularized networks where these approximations are accurate. In this paper, we introduce affine-image SDP-CROWN, a novel bound propagation method that better approximates preactivation domains using affine-image constraints and jointly optimizes the linear bound to adapt to the resulting domain. To bypass the prohibitive computational cost of dense semidefinite constraints required by the affine-image representation, we replace them with scaled diagonal-dominance conditions, allowing a GPU-accelerated implementation. Our method preserves the bound propagation recursion and can be integrated with branch-and-bound. On five MNIST and CIFAR-10 non-Lipschitz-regularized networks, our method improves verified accuracy over state-of-the-art methods by 17% to 57.5%. With branch-and-bound, these improvements increase to between 27% and 63.5%.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27007v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27007v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作从 BMJ Best Practice 临床指南自动抽取结构化标准与分支决策树，生成覆盖诊疗全流程的医学情景，并用经临床医生校准的 4 分制 autorater 评估模型与医生管理方案的指南依从性。

**排除原因：** 属纯文本医学管理基准，评估的是端到端临床方案对指南的依从度，不涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成或答案对内部证据的使用；其分支决策树与分级 rubric 仅是评分框架，未提供可核查的 LVR 迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scalable Evaluation of Medical AI using Evidence-based Practice Recommendations

**abstract/A1：** Rigorously evaluating medical artificial intelligence (AI) requires testing open-ended clinical management across diverse presentations and stages of care. However, existing benchmarks rely on manual expert authoring, limiting coverage across conditions, conditional care pathways, and evolving guidelines. We develop an automated pipeline that extracts and verifies structured clinical criteria and branching decision trees from practice recommendations to generate scenarios spanning the care continuum, including nuanced presentations and practice variations across regions and guideline editions. Applied to 436 BMJ Best Practice clinical monographs (354 US, 82 International), this yields 3,200 scenarios spanning differential diagnosis, investigations, treatment, and complications, plus targeted suites for regional specificity (731 US/International pairs) and temporal freshness (630 scenarios). To grade responses, we develop a clinician-calibrated autorater that scores management plans on a 4-point rubric stratifying deviations by clinical severity. Across Gemini models, scaling improves adherence, while web search (+0.8 percentage points \[pp\] on average) and multi-sample synthesis (+2.2 pp) combine additively (+2.8 pp). In context, full monographs consistently trail condensed excerpts. On targeted suites, web search narrows international performance gaps (+4.2 pp) and improves adherence to newly updated guidance (+4.6 pp). In a study with Primary Care Physicians (n = 9) across 200 scenarios, AI assistance raises adherence from 50.5% to 70.0%, driven largely by fewer clinical omissions. Overall, our results demonstrate a scalable evaluation setup using an evidence-based corpus originally written to guide human clinical decision-making, and demonstrate its viability to assess medical AI and its assistance on clinician decision-making.

</details>

### 251. [Fair Property Tax: Reducing Regressivity in Machine-Learning Valuation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27009v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27009

**作者：** Nicolas Acevedo Villena、Haihao Lu、Michael Wagner、Timothy Sparer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27009v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27009v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究房产税评估中 LightGBM 估值的累退性问题，通过训练损失惩罚提升公平性，属于表格数据的预测公平性议题，与视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用等主线没有具体联系，也无方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fair Property Tax: Reducing Regressivity in Machine-Learning Valuation

**abstract/A1：** Advanced machine-learning models are increasingly used for property valuation in tax assessment. Although these models can achieve high predictive accuracy, they may systematically assign higher valuation-to-sale ratios to lower-priced properties, a pattern known as regressivity. In collaboration with the Cook County Assessor's Office (CCAO), we develop and evaluate two training methods that substantially reduce this regressive pattern while maintaining price-scale predictive accuracy. The Direct method has been incorporated into CCAO's public residential valuation software. Retaining the LightGBM model class used at CCAO, we propose a Direct penalty on squared covariance between log valuation errors and log sale prices, and an observation-additive Surrogate that yields a weighted squared-error loss. We evaluate both methods in our implementation of the CCAO workflow, using seven chronological validation folds, a held-out test set, and a 2025-forward test set. Measured by the magnitude of price-related bias (PRB), regressivity falls by approximately 49% on held-out sales and 39% on 2025 sales at a Surrogate setting selected by cross-validation, relative to the same LightGBM model fitted without the penalty. Price-scale $R^2$ is essentially unchanged. Its small rise, from 0.893 to 0.897 on held-out sales and from 0.904 to 0.908 on 2025 sales, reflects overall valuation level. Log-price RMSE rises by 3.1% and 1.7%, respectively. Paired sale-bootstrap 90% intervals for the PRB reductions lie within about two percentage points of these estimates. In an additional eight-county benchmark, both methods reduce regressivity in every county, with higher log-price prediction error as the expected tradeoff. These findings demonstrate a practical way to reduce measured regressivity within an existing assessor workflow while preserving price-scale predictive performance.

**comment/C1：** 60 pages, 16 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-252"></a>

### 252. [Same Facts, Different Decision: Fact-Invariant Manipulation and Provenance-Based Defense in Typed Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27016v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27016

**作者：** Yujie Wang、Haotian Chi、Shunrong Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27016v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27016v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究类型化决策模型（如 Jev）在收到外部文本评论时，可在不新增事实的情况下被说服改变决策，提出基于论证理论四个杠杆的 LEVER 攻击，以及要求去掉外部评论后仍能复现同一决策的 Provenance Gate 防御。

**排除原因：** 对象为非视觉的类型化决策模型，既无视觉证据筛选也无 latent 推理状态生成与更新，事实不变性与来源门控只涉及纯文本扰动和决策复现，与 LVR 的表示、生成或训练监督缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Same Facts, Different Decision: Fact-Invariant Manipulation and Provenance-Based Defense in Typed Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** Typed decision models such as TypeSafe's Jev answer only with a probability, an option, or a score, so software can act on them directly. Their inputs, however, often include text from outsiders. Prior attacks test this risk poorly. Injected instructions rarely work on these models, and added facts can make a changed decision correct. We show that outside comments can persuade these models to make a wrong decision without adding any fact. We propose LEVER, an attack that writes such comments with four levers from argumentation theory, uses a separate verifier to discard any comment that adds a fact, and searches for effective comments guided by the model's output probabilities. We further propose the Provenance Gate, a defense that accepts a changed decision only if the model reaches it again without the outside comments. On three benchmarks, LEVER flips about a third of Jev's correct decisions, 2.8 times as many as the strongest prior attack under the same limit on facts, and more on five other models. The gate blocks all of these attacks with at most one extra query and still accepts trusted updates.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 7 figures, 14 tables

</details>

### 253. [Real-Time On-Drone Hyperspectral Anomaly Detection for Wilderness Search and Rescue](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27017v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27017

**作者：** Jacqueline Liu、Gyabaah Kyere、Chengyan Liu、Kenneth Chiu、Lynn Marie、Church、Sean Gleason、Jayson Boubin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27017v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27017v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SABL，一套部署在无人机上的边缘计算流水线，用指数滑动 Reed-Xiaoli 异常检测结合 DBSCAN 空间聚类，从高光谱流数据中实时圈定野外搜救候选区域。

**排除原因：** 工作属遥感高光谱异常检测的工程部署，采用固定流程的传统统计方法与聚类，不涉及多模态模型的内部表示、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Real-Time On-Drone Hyperspectral Anomaly Detection for Wilderness Search and Rescue

**abstract/A1：** Wilderness search-and-rescue (WSAR) teams use uncrewed aerial systems (UASs) to survey terrain for indicators of missing persons that are rare, blend into the background, or lack thermal signatures. Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) provides rich spectral information, but its high data volume challenges real-time on-drone processing. We present SABL, an edge-computing pipeline for on-drone Spectral Anomaly-Based Localization. SABL integrates hyperspectral streaming with exponentially moving Reed-Xiaoli (ERX) anomaly detection and DBSCAN spatial filtering to group pixel-level anomalies into candidate search regions. SABL is evaluated on data collected during a 10-day deployment with Quinhagak Search and Rescue (QSAR) in remote Alaska. It processes hyperspectral scanlines at 5.67-5.73 ms per line, meeting the camera's 10-ms acquisition interval; DBSCAN requires only 6.5-12.5 ms per 100-line update for filtering. SABL achieves approximately 56% and 99% object-level precision on QSAR-selected search indicators across two scenes, with corresponding recall of 55% and 29%, providing significant background filtering while identifying difficult-to-see anomalous search indicators.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 9 figures, and 4 tables

</details>

### 254. [TSIR: Ray-Traced Triangle Splats for Inverse Rendering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27030v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27030

**作者：** Wentao Yang、Chentao Shen、Zhengqi Zhang、Bolin Chen、Yan Ye、Xiangru Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27030v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27030v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 TSIR，一种两阶段逆渲染方法：先从多视图图像重建可重光照的三角形 splats，再直接追踪这些表面以估计材质并实现重光照。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦三维表面重建、材质估计与重光照，未涉及多模态推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据使用或动态计算决策，缺乏可借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TSIR: Ray-Traced Triangle Splats for Inverse Rendering

**abstract/A1：** Inverse rendering enables realistic relighting for visual effects and augmented reality. However, overlapping Gaussian primitives complicate surface recovery and require costly multi-layer traversal and compositing for light transport. We propose TSIR, a two-stage inverse rendering method that reconstructs relightable triangle splats from multi-view images. The first stage prepares opaque triangle splats with learnable vertex shading normals. The second stage directly traces these surfaces to estimate materials through Monte Carlo integration of direct illumination and indirect reflections, reducing reliance on multi-layer compositing. Anti-aliasing improves rendering quality while maintaining efficiency. Experiments demonstrate higher relighting quality than the evaluated baselines and more efficient anti-aliasing than supersampling.

</details>

### 255. [Hypergraph Structural Entropy-Enhanced Hierarchy-Aware Semi-Supervised Graph Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27043v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27043

**作者：** Guangjie Zeng、Hao Peng、Angsheng Li、Li Sun、Chunyang Liu、Yicheng Pan、Shengze Li、Philip S. Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27043v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27043v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HypSEE 框架，用行走诱导与嵌入导出的超图建模高阶关系，结合超图结构熵引导的层次池化与锚图一致性策略，提升半监督图分类。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是静态图的图级分类表示，既无视觉或多模态推理，也不涉及推理期状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 的具体联系线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hypergraph Structural Entropy-Enhanced Hierarchy-Aware Semi-Supervised Graph Classification

**abstract/A1：** Semi-supervised graph classification aims to learn graph-level representations by leveraging a large number of unlabeled graphs alongside a small set of labeled ones, effectively mitigating the issue of label scarcity. Despite significant progress by various semi-supervised learning strategies, complex relationships among graph vertices beyond pairwise edges, especially hierarchical structures, remain underexplored by existing methods. In this work, we propose a Hypergraph Structural Entropy-Enhanced hierarchy-aware framework, namely HypSEE, for semi-supervised graph classification. Specifically, we first construct walk-induced and embedding-derived hypergraphs to capture hierarchy-aware local and long-range higher-order relationships within graphs. Then, we devise a hierarchical hypergraph pooling approach guided by hypergraph structural entropy (HSE) to facilitate hierarchical representation learning in hypergraphs. Lastly, we employ an anchor graph-based consistency strategy to mutually align the representations from these two hypergraph views, enhancing the learning efficacy on unlabeled data. Extensive experiments on 8 benchmark datasets demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our approach.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, Accepted by KDD 2027

</details>

### 256. [Towards Text-Mask Consistency in Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27050v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27050

**作者：** Jie Gui、Hang Tu、Wen Sha、Xiuquan Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27050v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27050v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对医学图像分割中文本与掩码不一致的问题，提出两阶段 C2Seg：用聚类感知软标签对比学习缓解模板化文本造成的假阴性，并以双向互补注意力与 KAN 门控加强图文融合。

**排除原因：** 属于医学分割的图文对齐与融合改进，目标是掩码与文本一致，未涉及 latent 推理状态的生成、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线的具体联系不足。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Text-Mask Consistency in Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models for medical image segmentation often produce masks that conflict with the accompanying text, especially under multi-site/multi-lesion descriptions. We trace this failure to two factors: (i) highly templated and repetitive clinical language causes one-to-one hard contrastive learning to yield numerous false negatives, weakening cross-modal alignment; and (ii) predominantly vision-driven, one-way cross-attention lacks a language-dominant, spatially aware pathway, hindering effective injection of textual semantics into the spatial visual domain. To this end, we propose Consistency-enhanced Two-stage Segmentation (C2Seg). In the pretraining stage, Cluster-aware Contrastive Learning uses a frozen strong baseline to construct an intra-batch text similarity matrix as soft labels, thereby alleviating false negative conflicts and producing more discriminative visual representations. In the fusion stage, we introduce a Bidirectional Complementary Attention Module, where each modality dominates attention along its own path, fostering deep interaction and structural consistency between visual and textual representations. In order to enhance the expressive power of multimodal features, we further adopt KAN-based Attention Gating. Without updating the language encoder, our approach significantly improves text-mask consistency and segmentation accuracy on four public medical imaging datasets. The source code is available at https://github.com/JGuiidev/C2Seg.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICLR 2026 (poster)

</details>

### 257. [Accelerating Muon with Spectral Residual Correction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27077v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27077

**作者：** Zhixin Ren、Yao Lyu、Shengbo Eben Li、Liping Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27077v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27077v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AIMuon：保留 Muon 的动量递归，用梯度—动量残差经另一矩阵符号变换来修正谱步，并以谱—核范数对偶确定权重，在多个小模型上以更少步数达到 Muon 的验证损失。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于 LLM 预训练的优化器步长修正，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成与使用，也未提出可供核查的推理阶段线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accelerating Muon with Spectral Residual Correction

**abstract/A1：** Muon constructs matrix updates by applying a matrix-sign transformation to momentum. A common route to accelerating Muon is to modify the gradient or momentum before this transformation. We explore a complementary direction: refining the parameter step while retaining Muon's momentum recursion. Derived from the parameter subproblem of the ADMM-Inspired Momentum framework, AIMuon uses the gradient--momentum residual to correct the tentative spectral step. The residual undergoes a separate matrix-sign transformation, while spectral--nuclear duality determines the relative weighting of the momentum and residual directions. In practice, both directions are approximated with Newton--Schulz iterations and their combination is rescaled to match Muon's update magnitude. We establish a stochastic stationarity bound for the corresponding exact iteration. On GPT-2-small, GPT-2-medium, and Qwen3-0.6B, AIMuon reaches Muon's final validation loss with 8.98\\%, 10.05\\%, and 6.49\\% fewer optimization steps, respectively, while achieving lower final validation loss than Muon, Muon-Nesterov, and MONA.

</details>

### 258. [UDA-Restoration: Bridging Unsupervised Domain Adaptation and Multi-Degradation Denoising for Enhanced AV Semantic Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27097v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27097

**作者：** Zainab Bibi、Mohsin Kamal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27097v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27097v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 UDA-Restoration，一种基于扩散引导的无监督域适应方法，用于自动驾驶场景下的图像去噪与复原，并验证复原质量提升可改善下游语义分割。

**排除原因：** 工作属像素级图像复原与域适应预处理，不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、latent 生成或证据获取决策，摘要中也未见可供核查的状态表示或停止机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UDA-Restoration: Bridging Unsupervised Domain Adaptation and Multi-Degradation Denoising for Enhanced AV Semantic Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous vehicles rely heavily on vision-based perception systems for safe navigation and decision-making. Adverse weather conditions, sensor noise, moving artifacts and illumination changes, however, often degrade the image quality and hence reduce perception performance. This paper introduces a diffusion-guided unsupervised domain adaptation algorithm for autonomous driving environments, namely UDA-Restoration, which is designed to enhance and denoise images. The proposed method follows the paradigm of Noise-Space Domain Adaptation (Noise-DA), which is based on multi-source degradation learning and diffusion-guided adaptation that spans across synthetic and real-world degradations. The framework has been extensively tested and shown to restore image quality and to be robust to unseen degradation scenarios, such as snow, haze, sensor distortions, and more. The quantitative results indicate that Noise-DA outperforms the evaluated self-supervised and unsupervised baselines while matching the best supervised restoration performance. In addition, semantic segmentation experiments show that the performance of downstream perception tasks is directly improved by better image restoration. The outcomes show the promise of unsupervised domain adaptation based on diffusion as a powerful and scalable method for image restoration tasks in autonomous driving systems under various environments.

</details>

### 259. [MAPES: Multi-Value Alignment Pipeline via Expert Specialization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27133v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27133

**作者：** SungHo Kim、SangKeun Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27133v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27133v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 MAPES，通过将监督信号分解为参数隔离的价值特定专家，再经偏好感知合并与轻量协调阶段，实现多价值对齐。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦 LLM 多价值偏好对齐与参数子空间合并，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，也未给出可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAPES: Multi-Value Alignment Pipeline via Expert Specialization

**abstract/A1：** Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences requires simultaneously satisfying multiple, often competing values. Existing preference optimization methods collapse these into a single implicit signal, and even multi-objective extensions optimize within a shared parameter space, leaving cross-value conflicts unresolved. We propose MAPES, a multi-value alignment paradigm in which conflict resolution is a first-class concern at every pipeline stage: it first decomposes supervision into value-specific experts trained in isolated parameter subspaces, then recomposes them via preference-aware value merging and a lightweight harmonization stage that aligns the merged model with the expert ensemble. Across five preference alignment benchmarks, MAPES outperforms strong direct preference optimization (DPO) variants and multi-objective baselines on the benchmark-level average, with no large degradation on GSM8K and GPQA; matched controls isolate the contribution of each stage.

</details>

### 260. [Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27161v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27161

**作者：** Wenpeng Mu、Tanfeng Sun、Zhenyu Shao、Qiang Xu、Xinghao Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27161v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27161v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 RACE，仅用真实照片训练，融合全局兼容性（冻结与可学习表示、扰动响应）与局部分析（重建静态残差描述子），并通过真实图像统计对齐分数、校准固定阈值，以泛化到未见生成器的图像伪造检测。

**排除原因：** 这是生成图像取证/二分类检测工作，目标是判别真伪而非多模态推理；虽涉及全局与局部证据融合及表示敏感性，但无内部推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策等具体可借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection

**abstract/A1：** Evolving generative models challenge detector generalization, as methods trained on generated examples may rely on generator-specific cues. By contrast, authentic photographs share regularities rooted in physical image formation and acquisition, providing a common reference beyond individual generators. Building on these regularities, we propose Real-Anchored Complementary Evidence (RACE), which learns complementary global and local detection evidence using only authentic training images. First, the global pathway combines frozen and learned representations to assess proximity to authentic references. Next, it incorporates responses to controlled perturbations, complementing static compatibility with representation sensitivity. The local pathway then reconstructs complete trace descriptors, using intermediate features and signed perturbation responses as context while restricting detection evidence to static terminal-residual reconstruction errors. Finally, authentic-image statistics align the global and local scores for fusion, and an independent authentic set calibrates a decision threshold held fixed across test generators. Across six benchmarks, RACE achieves the highest average AUROC of 96.59% among the compared methods, reaching 97.18% on five out-of-domain benchmarks. Together with robustness to image perturbations and variation in authentic-image sources, these results support authentic-image regularities as a transferable basis for detection across diverse generators and image conditions.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27208v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27208v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B 通过 rank-16 LoRA 适配到手写数学表达式识别任务，输出线性化 Symbol Label Graph，并与 ViT-Transformer 基线及零样本配置对比，辅以数据组成消融。

**排除原因：** 属于特定识别任务的参数高效微调与刷分型工作，生成的是显式线性化标签图而非可分析的推理内部状态，也未涉及证据获取、停止决策或 latent 内容是否被真正使用，「局部 token 重叠与全局结构正确性差距」仅是输出层评测观察，缺少可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Qwen3-VL for Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) requires joint recovery of symbol identity and two-dimensional syntax, so a locally plausible transcription may still be structurally wrong. We study whether a compact vision-language model can be adapted to generate linearized Symbol Label Graphs (SLGs) under limited computing resources. Starting from a 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B checkpoint, we apply rank-16 Low-Rank Adaptation to the vision, language, attention, and MLP modules and train with instruction-formatted CROHME 2019 examples. We compare this model with an internally implemented ViT-Transformer baseline, a zero-shot Qwen3-VL configuration, and additional internally evaluated alternatives. On the 1,198 valid CROHME test examples, the CROHME-only model obtains 524 exact matches (43.74%) and a corpus BLEU-4 score of 77.67, compared with 4.92% and 43.26 for the task-specific ViT baseline. An ablation that adds 2,000 manually written, formula-guided samples decreases exact match to 42.32%, exposing a domain-alignment issue rather than an automatic benefit from more data. The principal contribution is therefore an evidence-backed, resource-efficient formulation of HMER as multimodal structural generation, together with a controlled data-composition ablation and an analysis of the remaining gap between local token overlap and globally correct structure.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Accepted at SIMC 2026 (Springer LNEE)

</details>

### 262. [LiDAR Signals for Robot Locomotion: Spherical Range Images Enable Robust Sim-to-Real Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27223v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27223

**作者：** Nian Liu、Xinyi Tong、Ming Ren、Long Ye、Zhaofeng He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27223v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27223v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向足式机器人运动控制的 LiDAR 球面距离图像感知与 sim-to-real 迁移，不涉及 VLM 或 latent 推理状态；其表示对齐仅为单一几何观测的编码选择，与视觉细节保留、证据筛选或停止决策等问题无具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LiDAR Signals for Robot Locomotion: Spherical Range Images Enable Robust Sim-to-Real Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Existing legged-locomotion systems commonly rely on depth images or online elevation maps. However, depth-based policies suffer from perceptual sim-to-real discrepancies due to imperfect depth sensor modeling, while map-based approaches are vulnerable to drift and misalignment. LiDAR offers direct measurements of the surrounding geometry, but raw LiDAR scans lack a compact, structured representation for high-frequency control. We propose a LiDAR perception pipeline that maintains perceptual consistency across simulation and reality through sensing- and representation-level alignment. At the sensor level, we recover frame-wise angular sampling patterns from the physical LiDAR and replay them in simulation with GPU ray casting. At the representation level, simulated and real returns undergo the same spherical range image encoding, yielding a fixed-size policy observation for end-to-end training and direct deployment without teacher--student distillation, online elevation mapping, or adaptation. Experiments show that our range-image policies achieve stronger sim-to-real transfer than depth- and elevation-map-based baselines, while ablations confirm the importance of the proposed sensing components. More visualizations and real-world videos are available at the \\href{https://lidar-img.github.io/}

</details>

<a id="paper-263"></a>

### 263. [EAST: Event-Anchored Surface Transport for Event-Based Gaussian Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27224v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27224

**作者：** Ke Luo、Wei Zhai、Yuliang Wu、Pengfei Yu、Yang Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27224v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27224v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究事件相机监督的3D高斯表面重建，用事件约束SDF锚定零水平集并校正高斯中心；其核心是几何重建精度与渲染，不涉及VLM推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可核查的LVR方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EAST: Event-Anchored Surface Transport for Event-Based Gaussian Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Event-supervised Gaussian reconstruction combines the high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range of event cameras with efficient explicit rendering. However, recovering a continuous surface remains difficult: changes in rendered appearance can compensate for depth errors while preserving the predicted brightness changes. We introduce Event-Anchored Surface Transport (EAST), which couples an event-constrained signed distance field to Gaussian rendering. Triangulation-supported candidates anchor the zero set, while normal flow measured from event timing constrains ray-surface intersection depths. Gaussian centers receive bounded corrections from the descriptor difference between the live field and a frozen initial reference. The correction is zero at initialization, yet transmits image gradients to the live field. Experiments on PAEv3D and NeRF Synthetic show improved surface accuracy and reference-view rendering over Gaussian baselines, together with efficient mesh extraction from the learned field.

</details>

### 264. [WINSO: A Large-Size Very-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Dataset for Wind Turbine Detection and Solar Panel Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27241v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27241

**作者：** Di Wang、Ke Li、Zhaokun Li、Weiping Ni、Junzheng Wu、Yahu Liu、Quan Wang、Xinbo Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27241v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27241v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 WINSO 大规模超高分辨率遥感基准，用于风机检测与光伏板分割，并评测检测、分割及 VLM 基线。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感设施制图的检测与分割数据集工作，虽涉及大尺寸图像与 VLM 推理策略，但未触及内部视觉表示、latent 推理状态或证据获取与停止决策，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WINSO: A Large-Size Very-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Dataset for Wind Turbine Detection and Solar Panel Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Wind and solar energy are central to the global transition toward net-zero emissions and sustainable development. Accurate spatial inventories of wind and solar facilities are essential for future energy planning. Satellite remote sensing provides a practical and efficient means to acquire such geospatial data at scale. However, reliable facility mapping from large-size satellite images remains constrained by the scarcity of carefully annotated datasets and the lack of systematic evaluation protocols. To address these limitations, we introduce \\textbf{WINSO}, a large-scale wind and solar benchmark based on very-high-resolution (VHR) satellite imagery with ground sampling distances (GSDs) of 0.5--0.7\\,m. WINSO comprises 10,962 large-size images sampled from diverse geographic locations across the Asia-Pacific region, covering at least 71,600\\,$\\text{km}^2$ and totaling 286.36 gigapixels, with individual image dimensions reaching 44,032$\\times$62,208 pixels. Through manual annotation and expert verification, a total of 166,473 instances are labeled using horizontal bounding boxes (HBBs) for wind turbines and polygon masks for solar panels. To build baselines for clean-energy facility mapping, we evaluate state-of-the-art detection and segmentation models, specialized ultra-high-resolution (UHR) architectures, and vision-language models across different inference strategies. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluations on two external public datasets illustrate the strong generalization capability of the WINSO. WINSO establishes a standardized benchmark to advance automated clean-energy mapping, paving the way for wide-area monitoring of the clean-energy transition.

</details>

### 265. [PP-CFG: Polarity-Preserving Guidance for Controllable Few-Step Image and Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27266v1>)

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27266v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27266v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PP-CFG，在少步扩散蒸馏中把正、负文本条件分离并在共享视觉主干内以相反符号融合注意力响应，从而在四步生成下保留可调 CFG 尺度与负面提示能力。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向图像/视频生成质量与可控性，核心是条件引导在蒸馏后的保留，未涉及推理状态、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 推理内容使用，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PP-CFG: Polarity-Preserving Guidance for Controllable Few-Step Image and Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Few-step diffusion distillation reduces sampling cost but often weakens adjustable classifier-free guidance (CFG) and runtime negative prompting. We introduce PP-CFG, an architecture that keeps positive and negative text conditions separate and combines their attention responses with opposite signs within a shared visual backbone. Its Scale-Time Polarity Mixer refines each condition using the guidance scale and denoising timestep, allowing prompt influence to vary throughout generation. The student retains a continuous scale input and selectable negative prompts with one visual backbone pass per step. We evaluate PP-CFG on Wan2.1 for video generation with distribution matching distillation (DMD), and on Qwen-Image with both latent consistency model (LCM) distillation and DMD. PP-CFG achieves 81.46 VBench in four steps and lower video teacher-student distances across guidance scales than step-distilled DMD. On NegElement-1K, our benchmark of 1,000 matched image and video prompt cases, it achieves higher target-removal rates than the corresponding teachers. These experiments support the method's generality across attention architectures, generation modalities, and distillation objectives while retaining competitive generation quality.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://pp-cfg.github.io/

</details>

### 266. [SIR-Agent: Skill-Augmented Image Restoration Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27285v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27285

**作者：** Yijian Wang、Qingsen Yan、Weiyu Zhou、Mingde Zhang、Axi Niu、Haokui Zhang、Jiantao Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27285v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27285v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SIR-Agent 将图像复原知识外置为可扩展技能库，用链反馈驱动的技能修订与递归自我改进让 MLLM 智能体在不微调参数的情况下调用复原工具，并在 14 个基准与 7 个未见任务上评测。

**排除原因：** 论文属于图像复原的 MLLM 智能体应用，技能库与工具链修订聚焦复原算子而非视觉推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选或停止决策，未提供可核查的 LVR 线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SIR-Agent: Skill-Augmented Image Restoration Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Agents for Image Restoration (IR) aim to leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to autonomously perceive image degradation and invoke IR tools. However, existing IR agents typically rely on fine-tuning MLLM parameters to adapt to new IR tasks, leading to catastrophic forgetting of IR knowledge. To address this limitation, we propose a skill-augmented IR agent, dubbed SIR-Agent, that maintains IR knowledge in a scalable skill library and uses Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) to facilitate efficient skill-library evolution. Specifically, we design a Chain Feedback-driven Skill Revision (CFSR) to evolve the skill library. The CFSR analyzes restoration chains to identify and revise weak operations in existing skills. The compatibility of a revised skill with known tasks decides if it replaces the original skill or is appended independently. Furthermore, to advance CFSR's skill-library evolution, we present an RSI-driven skill meta-learning mechanism, which identifies CFSR's inefficient operations and autonomously refines its revision strategy. Extensive experiments across 14 IR benchmarks, covering 6 single-degradation and 8 mixed-degradation scenarios, demonstrate SIR-Agent's superior IR performance. Further evaluation on 7 unseen tasks validates SIR-Agent's generalization to new IR tasks.

</details>

### 267. [$γ$-Scape: An Agent-Centric World for Mobile Embodied Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27306v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27306

**作者：** Xiaotao Zhang、Lin Qian、Hao Zhang、Xuanrui Cui、Yuehao Huang、Yunzi Wu、Yucheng Deng、Jiankun Dong、Hujun Yin、Xinhai Li、Chenjia Bai、Xuelong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27306v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27306v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 γ-Scape：面向移动具身智能体的仿真与基准平台，让智能体组合运动工具与已学技能在持久物理世界中完成导航加操作任务，并提供任务实例、参考程序、示范采集与评测流程。

**排除原因：** 属机器人仿真与基准基础设施，关注可执行程序与物理交互反馈，未涉及 VLM 内部视觉表示、latent 推理状态或证据筛选与停止决策的具体机制，故与本轮 LVR 主线缺少可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $γ$-Scape: An Agent-Centric World for Mobile Embodied Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** Recent embodied agents construct and adapt robot behavior through executable programs, tool use, and interaction feedback. Studying these systems in mobile manipulation calls for physical worlds that connect agent-directed execution with its physical consequences and reusable interaction experience. We introduce $γ$-Scape, an agent-centric simulation and benchmark platform for mobile embodied intelligence. Agents compose motion tools and learned skills to carry out complete tasks that combine navigation and manipulation in a persistent physical world. BenchForge grounds structured objectives in physical task instances with instructions, reference programs, and goal evaluators, defining task goals while preserving agents' choice of valid solutions. An agentic collection harness further enables agents to produce demonstrations by carrying out contact-rich interactions through sustained control and physical feedback. Assisted reference execution and human guidance provide complementary experience. The resulting tool traces and aligned observation-action records support program refinement and policy learning, with common task-goal criteria for subsequent evaluation. We benchmark representative agent systems on complete tasks and demonstrate contact collection and trace-informed program revision. Together, these workflows provide infrastructure for research on embodied recursive self-improvement.

</details>

### 268. [H-MSAE: Hierarchical Sparse Concept Coverage for Efficient and Diverse Instruction Data Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27329

**作者：** Bohan Cui、Jingyi Cui、Hongwei Wen、Feng Wang、Simiu Gu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27329v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 H-MSAE，用 Matryoshka 稀疏自编码器学到的多分辨率概念构建由粗到细的层级，配合质量加权、递减收益的概念覆盖与池内相对长度约束，从大规模指令语料中选出紧凑且多样的 SFT 子集。

**排除原因：** 该工作是纯文本指令微调数据选择，以减少标注与预处理开销、提升子集多样性为目标，未涉及视觉信息保留、推理状态生成或答案对 latent 内容的使用，与 LVR 的 THINK/LOOK/STOP 及表示诊断等问题缺少实质连接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** H-MSAE: Hierarchical Sparse Concept Coverage for Efficient and Diverse Instruction Data Selection

**abstract/A1：** Large instruction-tuning corpora contain substantial semantic redundancy, motivating the selection of compact subsets that preserve useful supervision while reducing training cost. Existing diversity-aware selectors often rely on generated semantic tags and explicit graph structures, introducing considerable annotation and preprocessing overhead. We introduce H-MSAE, a hierarchical sparse instruction selection framework that organizes multi-resolution concepts learned by a Matryoshka sparse autoencoder into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. Quality-weighted, diminishing-return coverage with ancestor propagation promotes high-quality and non-redundant semantic coverage, while pool-relative length constraints regulate subset composition. We motivate this design through a local quality-diversity analysis of subset SFT utility and examine it using example-quality and representation-space diversity diagnostics. On Tulu 3 data pool, H-MSAE achieves the highest Final Avg among the evaluated 50K selectors across scales and initialization. The selected subset also exhibits the highest mean quality and effective rank among the evaluated methods. By avoiding full-pool generative tagging and supporting efficient sparse selection, H-MSAE reduces measured end-to-end runtime by approximately $5\\times$ relative to MIG.

</details>

### 269. [CoGITAtion: Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-adaptive Representation for Privacy-sensitive Applications](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27340v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27340

**作者：** Mayar Elfares、Yasmine Elfares、Andreas Bulling

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27340v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27340v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoGITAtion 框架：将纵向分布的眼动数据在本地转为可解释的眼动转移图与描述子，并用任务与可用性门控适配异构、缺失特征，减少加密计算。

**排除原因：** 主题为眼动数据的隐私保护与分布式推理部署，其中的「门控」只是静态跳过可用性已知的分支，不涉及 VLM 的 latent 状态生成、证据筛选或推理期动态计算，缺少可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoGITAtion: Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-adaptive Representation for Privacy-sensitive Applications

**abstract/A1：** Gaze-based analysis provides rich behavioural information for privacy-sensitive applications, but deployment is challenged by heterogeneous, scarce, and distributed data. We present CoGITAtion (Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-Adaptive Representation), a framework for privacy-preserving inference over vertically distributed gaze data. CoGITAtion transforms variable-length gaze recordings into compact, interpretable gaze-transition graphs and descriptors computed locally by each data owner. Task-adaptive models accommodate heterogeneous and missing feature blocks through task and availability gating, avoiding unnecessary private computation. Results show that shared components capture patterns that generalise across tasks, while task-specific components provide capacity without increasing model size. Public availability gating further reduces encrypted computation by removing branches determined by publicly known task and owner availability without altering predictions. Overall, CoGITAtion enables gaze model reuse, efficient privacy-preserving deployment, and robust inference under incomplete owner availability while retaining interpretable gaze representations.

</details>

### 270. [EcomWriteBench: A Real-World Benchmark for E-commerce Writing Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27349v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27349

**作者：** Kaicheng Liang、Jingyi Cui、Hongwei Wen、Feng Wang、Simiu Gu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27349v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27349v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EcomWriteBench 电商写作基准，含 3,861 条查询、69 个任务与 37 项能力，并提出将人类成对偏好转化为能力加权评分的评估框架。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦电商写作任务的能力分解与偏好校准评分，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EcomWriteBench: A Real-World Benchmark for E-commerce Writing Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used throughout e-commerce workflows to generate content that informs purchasing decisions and supports customer interactions. Evaluating these systems requires accounting for user needs, product evidence, structural constraints, and business requirements that are not fully captured by general-purpose writing benchmarks. We introduce EcomWriteBench, a real-world benchmark containing 3,861 queries from 69 e-commerce writing tasks across 13 categories and five languages. Through task-level extraction, cross-task consolidation, and refinement guided by human feedback, EcomWriteBench constructs a shared pool of 37 abilities and identifies the subset applicable to each task. Ability-wise scoring enables us to compare state-of-the-art LLMs on EcomWriteBench and characterize their relative strengths and weaknesses across fine-grained e-commerce writing abilities. We further propose a preference-calibrated weight-learning framework that converts human pairwise preferences into interpretable scoring rubrics through global, category-level, and task-level aggregation weights. This framework provides a reusable approach for translating human preferences into automated evaluation by combining ability discovery, ability-wise scoring, and preference-calibrated aggregation, and can potentially be applied to a broad range of benchmarks beyond EcomWriteBench. On held-out queries, our scoring framework achieves 88.96% agreement with human preferences, compared with 78.54% for conventional LLM-as-a-judge scoring.

</details>

### 271. [Borrow the Style, Follow the Prompt: Progressive Reference-Guided Stylization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27360v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27360

**作者：** Yu Lin、Zi-Jun Wang、Zhilin Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27360v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27360v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ProStyle，通过跨内容监督学习抑制内容的风格先验并在辅助层弱注入，再以残差通路修正，用于参考图引导的文本到图像风格化，并构建 ReConStyle 数据集与评测。

**排除原因：** 属图像风格化生成与内容-风格解耦的美学控制，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未给出可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Borrow the Style, Follow the Prompt: Progressive Reference-Guided Stylization

**abstract/A1：** Reference-guided text-to-image stylization transfers visual appearance to content specified by text, but the reference subjects and backgrounds can interfere with what the generated image depicts. Suppressing this unwanted information can also discard transferable texture and material cues, creating a tension between content control and faithful preservation of the reference style. Accordingly, we propose ProStyle, which learns a content-suppressed style prior through cross-content supervision on same-style pairs with different subjects and backgrounds and injects it with weak conditioning at auxiliary layers to strengthen style expression. Building on this prior, a complementary pathway learns residual corrections from full reference patch features with the content-suppressed style pathway frozen, before both pathways undergo joint refinement under the same objective. For cross-content learning and evaluation, we introduce ReConStyle, which provides 134K training pairs, two test settings, and object-level measures of target-content fidelity and reference-object overlap alongside style similarity and text alignment. ProStyle outperforms all compared SD1.5-based baselines and achieves the best average rank of 1.86 across four automatic and three human-evaluation criteria on ReConStyle-Full. In the user study, it substantially outperforms the baselines in text alignment and combined suitability, with selection rates of 84.36% and 50.90%, respectively. On the external Style-Rank benchmark, it also achieves the highest CLIP-Text and ImageReward scores.

</details>

**排除原因：** 属于无人机全景视觉跟踪的基准与方法工作，缺少 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或训练监督方面的具体联系，ERP 畸变只是跟踪检测层面的几何问题，与 LVR 主线没有可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PanoUAV-Track: Full-Spherical UAV Visual Tracking in the Wild

**abstract/A1：** Existing UAV tracking benchmarks predominantly rely on perspective cameras with limited fields of view. Omnidirectional cameras enable full-spherical observation, yet tracking from moving UAV platforms remains largely unexplored. Unlike ground-level panoramic tracking, UAV-mounted omnidirectional cameras observe targets across both azimuth and elevation, introducing geometric challenges under equirectangular projection (ERP). To investigate this setting, we capture approximately \\textbf{40 hours of real-world 8K panoramic UAV video} and construct \\textbf{PanoUAV-Track} through temporal clip extraction, target annotation, and the integration of simulated sequences. The resulting benchmark comprises 1,895 sequences, 507,961 frames, and 2,872 annotated objects, covering real-world daytime and nighttime scenes alongside simulation, and supporting both visual and language-based target specification. Through extensive evaluations, we reveal a clear \\textbf{perspective-to-spherical} generalization gap and identify tracking degradation associated with latitude-dependent distortion and ERP seam discontinuity. To address these challenges, we propose \\textbf{PanoUAV-Tracker (PUTrack)}, a geometry-aware tracking framework combining latitude-adaptive search and seam-aware periodic sampling. PUTrack improves zero-shot and in-domain tracking, particularly for seam-crossing targets. Code and benchmark resources will be available\\footnote{\\textcolor{tabpink}{https://libingyu01.github.io/PanoUAV-Track/}}.

</details>

### 273. [BladeR: Scalable, Shape-Guided 3D Reconstruction of Wind Turbine Blades from UAV Imagery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27407v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27407

**作者：** Jonathan Sterckx、Michiel Vlaminck、Hiep Luong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27407v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27407v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向无人机巡检影像的风机叶片三维重建工程流程，关注分层稀疏重建、分块 Gaussian Splatting 与细节增强，与视觉推理中的内部状态表示、证据获取或停止决策无具体可借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BladeR: Scalable, Shape-Guided 3D Reconstruction of Wind Turbine Blades from UAV Imagery

**abstract/A1：** The structural integrity of wind turbine blades is critical for safe, efficient energy production. Accurate 3D reconstruction allows precise localization and quantification of wear and early damage detection. While drone-based inspections are standard, their high-resolution imagery poses challenges such as weak texture, lighting variability, and high computational cost. We present BladeR, a scalable pipeline for high-quality 3D reconstruction from inspection imagery. A hierarchical sparse reconstruction strategy leverages GPS/IMU data and blade shape priors to guide advanced keypoint matchers on full-resolution images. For dense reconstruction, a tiled Gaussian Splatting framework processes 45 MP images at native resolution, isolates the blade from background, and adaptively enhances detail recovery along the leading edge. Validation against commercial and open-source baselines shows significant improvements in model completeness and detail.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 13 figures. Published at VISAPP 2026

</details>

**排除原因：** 属于事件相机—投影仪几何与时钟标定的硬件系统工作，虽涉及「时间码」与自标定，但未触及 VLM 内部状态、视觉证据使用、推理停止或训练监督，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Projector Time Map Calibration for Event-based Structured Light

**abstract/A1：** Existing event-based structured light obtains a dense projector-camera correspondence from the timestamp of an event. It assumes that the projector scans a parallel raster and that the sensor timestamps each pixel independently, and we show that neither assumption holds. We obtain the correspondence from a temporal code that identifies the projector pixel directly and therefore needs no prior calibration, we calibrate the projector time map from the same recording, and we recover the geometry of the system and the offsets between its clocks by a spatio-temporal self-calibration in a preamble of a few seconds that needs no reference object and no second camera.

**comment/C1：** accepted to IEICE Transactions on Information and Systems

</details>

### 275. [Pose-based Co-speech Gesture Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27415v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27415

**作者：** Frances Yung、Ninlawat Phuangschoke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27415v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27415v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在统一交叉验证设置下重新基准测试语音伴随手势检测（基于姿态的时序片段检测），并提出 CTR-GCN + BiLSTM 模型。

**排除原因：** 研究目标为人类交际行为中的手势时段标注与基准比较，不涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态或证据使用决策，也未见可供核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pose-based Co-speech Gesture Detection

**abstract/A1：** Co-speech gesture detection identifies the spans in which a speaker gestures (e.g., beat or semantic gestures) in video. As a first step in gesture annotation, it is crucial for studying human communicative behaviour. Despite the availability of automatic tools and models, co-speech gesture detection remains underexplored, and its current state of the art is unclear: existing methods have been evaluated using different datasets, experimental setups, and metrics, making their performance difficult to compare. To address this gap, we benchmark existing detectors using the same cross-validation folds across three datasets (MULTISIMO, GESRes, and CABBS), with evaluation at the frame, gesture-span, and window levels. Our results show that previously reported performance does not consistently transfer to this common evaluation setting. Performance varies substantially across datasets, particularly with gesture frequency, indicating that the task remains far from solved. We further propose a CTR-GCN + BiLSTM model that outperforms the previous best method, MPSL, on CABBS. Our results provide a more consistent basis for comparing existing approaches and highlight key challenges for future research on co-speech gesture detection.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 GenAI4SimPy 多智能体框架，用开源权重模型从制造事件日志自动生成可执行的 SimPy 离散事件仿真模型，并报告参数精度与系统级行为保真度不一致的现象。

**排除原因：** 研究面向工业事件日志到仿真代码的生成，不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选与推理停止决策；「参数准确不等于行为保真」虽可与局部正确性混淆类比，但缺少可核查的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Manufacturing Event Logs to Simulation: Enabling SMEs with Agentic AI for SimPy Simulation Generation

**abstract/A1：** High expertise and cost barriers exclude small and medium-sized enterprises from discrete-event simulation (DES). We introduce GenAI4SimPy, a multi-agent framework powered by Qwen 3.6 (27B), an open-weight large language model, that automates end-to-end DES generation from manufacturing event logs to executable SimPy models through tool-augmented reasoning and human-in-the-loop validation. Evaluated on three execution blocks from a public smart factory dataset, the framework achieved a mean absolute percentage error of 7.9\\% and coverage of failure rate for all three blocks and of throughput for two of three. A fidelity paradox was observed: accurate process-level parameters did not ensure system-level behavioral fidelity, with flow-time and throughput errors driven by misinterpreted observation-window truncation and absent system-level calibration. These results demonstrate that local open-weight models can perform DES generation at accuracy comparable to cloud-based alternatives, while identifying parameter extraction as necessary but insufficient for behavioral validity. Source code and results are available at https://github.com/RaikoPipe/genai4simpy

**comment/C1：** 12 Pages, 2 figures, Winter Simulation Conference 2026 Glasgow, Scotland

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.IR

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 306 座外观高度相似的铜矮人雕像检索基准 Doppeldwarf-306（1,691 张照片），用冻结的 DINOv2 等十三种骨干做最近邻检索，并分析候选集规模、位置先验与局部特征匹配对精度的影响。

**排除原因：** 属细粒度实例检索基准，核心是识别准确率与候选集规模效应，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可借用的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Which Dwarf Is This? Doppeldwarf-306 and Near-Identical Instance Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Most established instance-retrieval benchmarks emphasise landmarks; small sculptures from visually similar series offer a complementary challenge. We introduce Doppeldwarf-306: 1,691 openly licensed photographs of 306 bronze dwarf statues in Wrocław, Poland, many made in themed series that share one style, with the photographer of each image recorded. With no training, nearest-neighbour search over frozen DINOv2 features names the right statue first for 93.1% of leave-one-out queries, and the best of thirteen backbones from seven pretraining paradigms reaches 94.7%. Accuracy falls slowly as candidates are added: for DINOv2, by about 0.8 points each time their number doubles. A natural design for a phone guide compares a photograph only with statues near the user. In simulation this helps, but less than an equal number of randomly chosen statues does, so random subsampling overstates its value; a permutation test ties the penalty to the real layout (p = 0.005). Used as a weight instead of a filter, location raises DINOv2 from 93.4% to 98.7% for a simulated user 25 m from the statue. With the query's photographer withheld, learned local-feature matching raises DINOv2 accuracy from 81.8% to 85.5% and also improves DINOv3. Comparing the two best candidates cuts DINOv2's false acceptance of unknown statues from 38.3% to 19.6% at 90% known acceptance.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 6 figures in the main text; includes appendix. Project page: https://turhancan97.github.io/doppeldwarf-306/

</details>

### 278. [PGS-PCQA: Perceptually Gated Synthetic Supervision for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27512

**作者：** Qinyuan Fan、Meng Zhang、Clemens Gühmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 PGS-PCQA，利用合成失真库的严重度排序作为训练监督，并用渲染视图的感知门控软化排序目标，以提升无参考点云质量评估的跨数据集泛化。

**排除原因：** 研究属于点云质量评估与跨域泛化，感知门控仅用于质量排序标签的修正，不涉及视觉推理的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，缺少可供迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PGS-PCQA: Perceptually Gated Synthetic Supervision for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment

**abstract/A1：** Existing no-reference point cloud quality assessment (NR-PCQA) methods perform well within individual datasets but generalize poorly across datasets. We identify insufficient distortion coverage in training data as a key cause: on unseen distortion types, models rank severity levels near chance or even in reverse. We propose perceptually gated synthetic supervision for no-reference point cloud quality assessment (PGS-PCQA), an NR-PCQA model trained with synthetic distortion ranking. It builds a synthetic distortion bank from source reference point clouds and uses within-type severity ranking as supervision in addition to the source mean opinion scores (MOS), requiring no subjective scores for the synthetic samples. Since higher severity does not always mean lower perceived quality, a perceptual gate softens the ranking targets of pairs with small perceptual differences in rendered views. The synthetic supervision is used only during training and needs no target-domain data. In a single training run on SJTU-PCQA, PGS-PCQA achieves Spearman rank correlation coefficients (SRCCs) of 0.709 on WPC and 0.751 on BASICS. The corresponding published DSP-PCQA scores are 0.595 and 0.723. Under reference-disjoint cross-validation, PGS-PCQA achieves SRCCs of 0.955 on SJTU-PCQA, 0.934 on WPC, 0.906 on BASICS, and 0.791 on LS-PCQA Part I. In the exclusion experiment, the reported scores for the reduced synthetic bank represent 58-65% of the full-bank SRCC gain relative to training without the bank. Code is available at https://github.com/Fan-QY/PGS-PCQA.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages (including supplementary material), 4 figures, 16 tables. Code: https://github.com/Fan-QY/PGS-PCQA

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦多语言覆盖与任务基准评测，未涉及视觉证据筛选、latent 状态生成与使用、动态计算或停止决策等具体机制，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PaMuk-Bench: A Parallel Multilingual Multitask Benchmark for Vision-Language Tasks

**abstract/A1：** While vision-language models (VLMs) currently obtain notable results for highly-studied languages, e.g. English, they exhibit significant performance drops in certain low-resource languages, especially due to the lack of annotated datasets. To this end, we propose PaMuk-Bench, a novel parallel multilingual multitask benchmark for vision-language tasks. Our benchmark includes a set of over 10K curated English prompts that are translated into six languages: Arabic, Danish, French, Hindi, Italian, Romanian. Parallel translations, which are obtained via a semi-automatic process, along with accompanying generated and retrieved images enable the comparative development and evaluation of VLMs across a wide range of tasks: text-to-image generation, image captioning, content-based image retrieval, image generation difficulty estimation, image retrieval difficulty estimation. We empirically show that zero-shot English and multilingual pre-trained VLMs compare consistently worse than their fine-tuned counterparts, confirming that PaMuk-Bench is a valuable resource that can drastically reduce the language gap across a variety of VLM architectures and tasks. PaMuk-Bench is available at https://github.com/ghesil/pamuk\_bench.

</details>

### 280. [Points, Not Pixels: Keypoints, Masks and Oblique Geometry for Floating-Roof Tank Fill Estimation from Overhead Imagery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27624

**作者：** Mohd Shahid Kaleem、Mohd Hozaifa Khan、Maaz Khan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27624v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从俯视影像估计浮顶油罐的充装深度：将罐缘与阴影边界回归为亚像素关键点，并结合太阳与相机几何的闭式斜视估计器反演深度，同时发布 SynTank-25k 合成数据集。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感几何测量应用，单步关键点回归加闭式反演，不涉及多步推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 表示，也未提供可供 LVR 核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Points, Not Pixels: Keypoints, Masks and Oblique Geometry for Floating-Roof Tank Fill Estimation from Overhead Imagery

**abstract/A1：** The floating roof of an oil storage tank rests on the stored liquid, so its depth below the rim measures how full the tank is. In optical overhead imagery, this depth appears only as the crescent shadow the rim casts on the roof, often a few pixels wide. Thin shadows are difficult to measure at overhead-image resolution, and nadir-based height conversions introduce errors in oblique views. Under a local affine camera, roof depth determines the rim-to-shadow displacement through the sun and camera geometry. We regress the boundaries as sub-pixel keypoints and invert this relation with a closed-form estimator that models oblique views. Available public tank datasets do not label roof position, so we introduce SynTank-25k, 24,987 physically based renders with exact roof, shadow and camera geometry. Using the oblique estimator, the keypoint pipeline reduces fill error by $22\\%$ relative to the segmentation baseline on SynTank-25k. On shadows narrower than $6\\text{ px}$, its error is also lower than that obtained by converting true binary masks. Our oblique estimator reduces the error of the nadir formula elevenfold at the steepest views. On 6,558 real NAIP tank crops defined by bounding boxes, about half of the fill estimates pass internal consistency checks; NAIP has no fill labels. Data and code will be released.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Workshop on Computer Vision for Earth Observation Applications at WACV 2027

</details>

### 281. [Segment Everything, Materialize Nothing: Exact Streaming Inference for Many-Class 3D Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27630v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27630

**作者：** Fabian Isensee、Jakob Wasserthal、Lalith Kumar Shiyam Sundar、Maximilian Rokuss、Moritz Langenberg、Constantin Ulrich Harsy、Stefan Michael Happold、Klaus H. Maier-Hein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27630v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27630v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对多类别 3D 分割推理的内存瓶颈，指出瓶颈来自推理过程物化的 logits，并利用分类头与重采样可交换的性质，用单层 patch 的环形缓冲流式完成推理，从而在精确结果下大幅降低内存并加速。

**排除原因：** 该工作是医学 3D 分割的推理内存与工程效率优化，虽然涉及「流式状态」与「物化内容」，但不涉及推理期 latent 视觉状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线无具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Segment Everything, Materialize Nothing: Exact Streaming Inference for Many-Class 3D Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Whole-body segmentation tools such as TotalSegmentator and MOOSE split more than 100 classes across five or six networks. The reason is memory. Standard 3D inference, as implemented in nnU-Net and MONAI, holds a logit for every class and voxel, on the working grid and again on the scanner's grid. For a median whole-body CT at 125 classes, that is 76 GB. We show that this memory wall lies in what inference materializes, not in the task, and remove it without approximation. Every step after the network is local along one axis, so its outputs can be streamed through a ring one patch thick. The classification head commutes with blending and resampling, so the ring carries the network's features instead of the class logits. Memory thus no longer grows with classes times volume, and the label map on the scanner's grid stays exact. This holds for any model with a pointwise affine head, without retraining. Many-class models, previously impractical, become deployable. One model trained on all classes matches or slightly exceeds the toolkits' five or six specialists in segmentation quality and runs 5.8-15x faster end to end, with 3.2-7.6x less RAM and under 8 GB of VRAM. We provide the method as a drop-in for nnU-Net, TotalSegmentator and MOOSE. The code is part of nnU-Net (https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/nnUNet), and unified models for both toolkits will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-282"></a>

### 282. [SMSD: A Semantic Misinformation Spreader Detection Approach](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27661v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27661

**作者：** Zeeshan Bin Siddique、Michael Bewong、Md Geaur Rahman、Sajal Halder、Selasi Kwashie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27661v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27661v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究社交媒体虚假信息传播者的时序检测与立场感知标注，属于用户行为建模与社会网络应用，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，也无具体可迁移的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SMSD: A Semantic Misinformation Spreader Detection Approach

**abstract/A1：** Misinformation spreaders on social media significantly amplify false narratives, making early detection critical to mitigate their spread. Existing approaches often overlook evolving user behavior, introduce semantic noise by ignoring user stance, and fail to handle newly emerging users effectively. To address these limitations, we propose a \\underline{S}emantic \\underline{M}isinformation \\underline{S}preader \\underline{D}etection approach (SMSD) that models misinformation propagation as a temporal, user-centric, and behavior-driven learning problem. SMSD adopts a multimodal architecture that incorporates three categories of features: user-level, network-level, and conversation propagation. A stance-aware labeling strategy is also introduced to differentiate supportive, opposing, questioning, and neutral users towards misinformation claims. Additionally, a monthly quantile analysis is introduced to simulate realistic, evolving user participation. Initial experimental results on the FibVid COVID-19 benchmark dataset demonstrate that SMSD improves recall by 45.0\\%, F1 by 49.5\\%, and MCC by 74.6\\% under stance-aware labeling and by 17.7\\%, 7.4\\%, and 11.4\\%, respectively, under threshold-based labeling. It consistently outperforms all baselines in both settings.

</details>
