### 283. [Learning in Symbiosis: Heterogeneous Federated Learning with ANNs and SNNs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27686v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27686

**作者：** Manh V. Nguyen、Liang Zhao、Bobin Deng、Xinyue Zhang、Shaoen Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27686v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27686v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Hybrid Neuromorphic Federated Learning 框架，使异质的 ANN 与 SNN 设备在同一联邦学习环境中联合训练，并在 CIFAR-10 上报告双方精度提升与脉冲稀疏化效果。

**排除原因：** 论文关注边缘端联邦学习的能效与异构模型协同，属分布式训练系统问题，未涉及视觉语言推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成或证据筛选，缺少可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning in Symbiosis: Heterogeneous Federated Learning with ANNs and SNNs

**abstract/A1：** Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs), widely regarded as the third generation of neural networks, represent a promising neuromorphic computing paradigm with the potential to reduce the energy footprint of Artificial Intelligence (AI). Their en-ergy efficiency makes SNNs particularly attractive for resource-constrained Edge AI devices, motivating growing research interest in Federated Learning (FL) with SNNs. Despite these advantages, integrating SNNs into existing FL systems remains challenging, as replacing all conventional Artificial Neural Network (ANN) devices with SNN-based alternatives is impractical. Instead, we envision a gradual transition toward neuromorphic Edge AI, where ANN and SNN devices coexist and collaboratively participate in FL. To facilitate this transition, we propose Hybrid Neuromorphic Federated Learning (HNFL), a framework that enables joint training of heterogeneous ANN and SNN devices within an FL environment. Through experiments on CIFAR-10 using both VGG9 and ResNet architectures, we demonstrate the mutual benefits of collaborative ANN-SNN training. Specifically, HNFL achieves an average improvement of 16.85% when incorporating SNN devices into ANN-based FL and 8.02% when incorporating ANN devices into SNN-based FL. Furthermore, HNFL produces sparser spiking activity in the trained SNN models, with reductions of 12.4% for SResNet and 22.4% for SVGG9. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to demonstrate that ANNs and SNNs can coexist and mutually benefit from collaborative training in FL, providing a practical path toward the gradual integration of neuromorphic computing into existing Edge AI systems.

**comment/C1：** Manuscript submitted to: IEEE International Conference on Communications (ICC) 2027. 6 pages, 3 figures, 1 table, 1 pseudo code

</details>

### 284. [Comparative Performance of Machine Learning Architectures for Fault Detection and Diagnosis in Chemical Processes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27696v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27696

**作者：** Rayane Ammar Khodja、Alexandre Voisin、Victor Costa、Benoit Celse、Fanny Casteran、Benoît Iung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27696v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27696v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 Tennessee Eastman 化工过程数据集上基准比较 MS-PCA、AutoEncoder、集成学习、LSTM 等故障检测方法，以及随机森林、XGBoost、BLSTM 等故障诊断方法。

**排除原因：** 属于工业时序故障检测与诊断的应用刷分式基准比较，未涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成与使用或证据筛选决策，缺少可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Comparative Performance of Machine Learning Architectures for Fault Detection and Diagnosis in Chemical Processes

**abstract/A1：** Fault Detection and Diagnosis (FDD) is crucial for both maintenance and control in chemical industries, where early fault detection can prevent costly failures and optimize operations. This is particularly critical in pilot units like those at IFP Energies Nouvelles (IFPEN), which operate under short-term experimental conditions with frequently varying operational parameters. This study conducts an extensive benchmarking analysis using the Tennessee Eastman Process (TEP), a widely used simulated chemical process dataset, to evaluate multiple approaches for fault detection and diagnosis, featuring numerous continuous operations with different sensors and faults. Several methods were implemented and compared including Multi Scale PCA (MS-PCA), AutoEncoder, Ensemble Learning, and LSTM models for fault detection, alongside Random Forest, XGBoost, and BLSTM (Bidirectional LSTM) for fault diagnosis. Our results demonstrate that Ensemble Learning achieves detection rates ranging from 80% to 100% across various fault scenarios. For the fault diagnosis task, BLSTM achieved a diagnosis accuracy of 98.76%. The study reveals that ensemble-based approaches consistently outperform individual models in handling the complex, multivariate nature of chemical process data, due to its robustness in combining multiple perspectives, comprehensive data capture, and localized detection capabilities. Furthermore, the superior performance of the BLSTM is due to its ability to capture both past and future temporal dependencies in the sequential data, particularly important in chemical processes where fault patterns may manifest with complex temporal relationships. This research contributes to the field of Fault Detection and Diagnosis by providing empirical evidence for the effectiveness of ensemble methods and Bidirectional LSTMs in order to address industrial FDD applications on chemical processes.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 2 figures, 1 table. Presented at MIMAR 2025, Nancy, France

</details>

### 285. [Geometrically Constrained Learning II: Agnostic Proper Learning of Lipschitz Functions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27700v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27700

**作者：** Jane Lange、Cassandra Marcussen、Arsen Vasilyan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27700v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27700v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文给出高斯分布下 Lipschitz 函数的 proper 学习器，通过带凸梯度约束的 L1 多项式回归与基于下 Lipschitz 包络的局部修正，取得 (d+1)^{poly(λ/ε)} 的样本与计算复杂度，并扩展到 agnostic 情形。

**排除原因：** 纯学习理论中的 Lipschitz 函数 proper/agnostic 学习，只涉及实值函数的样本复杂度与包络修正，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据筛选或推理期计算，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Geometrically Constrained Learning II: Agnostic Proper Learning of Lipschitz Functions

**abstract/A1：** We give a proper learner for Lipschitz functions over the Gaussian distribution on $\\mathbb{R}^d$. Given random Gaussian examples labeled by a $λ$-Lipschitz function, $λ\\ge 1$, the learner returns a function $h:\\mathbb{R}^d\\to\\mathbb{R}$ that is globally $λ$-Lipschitz and has loss (absolute or squared) at most $\\varepsilon$. The sample complexity, running time, and representation size are \\\[ (d+1)^{\\mathsf{poly}(λ/\\varepsilon)}. \\\] The learning algorithm can be made \\emph{agnostic}, given a known bound on the variance of the label marginal distribution. Some known control of label scale is information-theoretically necessary. For arbitrary labels with variance at most $V^2 \\ge 1$, the complexity is \\\[ (d+1)^{\\mathsf{poly}(Vλ/\\varepsilon)}, \\\] and the error is $\\mathsf{opt} + \\varepsilon$, where $\\mathsf{opt}$ is the minimal error among Lipschitz approximators. Our approach combines $L\_1$ polynomial regression under a convex gradient constraint with local correction of the Lipschitz property. The learner finds a polynomial that, after smoothing and clipping its range, is very close to a canonical Lipschitz correction of itself, the lower Lipschitz envelope. Our local corrector modifies the lower envelope by replacing distances to sublevel sets with a Gaussian sampling proxy, allowing the construction to be locally and stably estimated from samples.

**comment/C1：** 55 pages, under submission to STOC 2027

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文从 31 只真实犬只的三维扫描中学习一个参数化的犬类形状与姿态模型，结合解剖学骨架与线性形状空间，并用于表面重建和图像回归等下游任务。

**排除原因：** 工作属三维动物建模与形状/姿态估计，未涉及多模态推理中的内部状态生成、视觉证据使用、答案贡献分配或停止决策，也缺少可核查的 latent reasoning 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PAWS: A Parametric Model of Canine Pose and Shape

**abstract/A1：** We introduce PAWS, a parametric model of canine shape and pose, learned from 3D scans of real dogs. Capturing the full diversity of dogs is challenging, as different breeds vary in size, proportion, and body shape. To capture this range, we scanned 31 dogs across multiple breeds and sizes performing a range of natural movements. From this dataset, we build a parametric model that combines an anatomically faithful skeleton with a linear shape space learned from the 3D scans. Unlike previous models, our model is learned from real dogs in varying poses, allowing for realistic modelling of canine pose and shape. We demonstrate that building on PAWS provides a robust framework for downstream tasks, such as surface reconstruction and image regression. In particular, we show that a limited number of anatomically valid degrees of freedom is sufficient to accurately estimate dog pose and shape.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27728v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究面向车载相机采集的路面图像，利用车道检测确定四边形感兴趣区域、构造透视变换矩阵并结合路面标线比例做尺寸校正，从而在无需现场标定的情况下定量测量路面病害的长度与面积。

**排除原因：** 属于道路工程中的图像几何校正与目标检测应用，虽涉及透视几何与尺度估计，但未研究多模态推理状态、证据筛选或训练监督，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantitative Measurement of Pavement Distress based on Vehicle Camera and Perspective Correction

**abstract/A1：** Using image data is becoming an important method for highway agencies to automatically evaluate pavement conditions. However, most studies focus on improving detection accuracy rather than obtaining quantitative measurements, such as the length or area of pavement distress. Although field camera calibration can provide such measurements, it is impractical for large volumes of onboard images collected from different vehicles. This study develops a method to quantitatively measure pavement distress directly from in-vehicle camera images without on-site camera calibration. A quadrilateral region of interest is first identified based on lane detection and used to construct a perspective transformation matrix for image correction. Size correction is then performed using the proportions of road markings in the image and a specified scale, which also enables estimation of the actual width of the selected region. The corrected images are subsequently processed using object detection algorithms to obtain quantitative distress measurements. The proposed method can efficiently perform perspective correction for images collected by the same vehicle camera with minimal manual effort. The measured pavement patch lengths show a trend consistent with pavement friction data. By calibrating vehicle cameras using only image information, this study enables quantitative pavement distress detection without additional field experiments. The approach provides a practical solution for processing large volumes of vehicle-camera data, particularly as connected vehicles generate increasing amounts of uncalibrated roadway imagery.

</details>

### 288. [SUPERVISE: A Unified Framework for Standardized and Reproducible Superpixel Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27753v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27753

**作者：** Julien Walther、Rémi Giraud、Michaël Clément

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27753v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27753v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦超像素分割的评测协议标准化与指标冗余分析，属于低层图像分割评测，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，未提供可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SUPERVISE: A Unified Framework for Standardized and Reproducible Superpixel Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Image segmentation into superpixels is a widely used technique in computer vision, with a large body of work developed over the years. Yet, its evaluation remains poorly standardized, as methods are compared under heterogeneous protocols, across inconsistent scale ranges, and with redundant or misaligned metric subsets. In this work, we introduce SUPERVISE (SUperpixel PERformance VISualization and Evaluation), a unified and reproducible benchmark that addresses these longstanding inconsistencies. Built on a scale-normalized protocol based on interpolation over the actual number of generated superpixels, SUPERVISE enables fair and consistent cross-method comparisons. We leverage this standardized framework to conduct the first large-scale statistical analysis of 20 evaluation metrics across 7 datasets and 31 methods, revealing strong inter-metric correlations and significant redundancy that challenge common evaluation practices. These findings provide empirical support for reporting a compact set of representative metrics. SUPERVISE is released as a lightweight, modular framework built on precomputed shared label maps, making large-scale reproducible evaluation accessible to the community. All code, label maps, and evaluation results are publicly available at: https://github.com/waldo-j/supervise

</details>

**作者：** Yuki Aoki、Hiroo Bekku、Tadashi Okoshi、Jin Nakazawa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27756v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27756v1>)

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Ref-YOLO-World，把仅依赖指代表达的文本侧计算与图像处理解耦，缓存 per-token 文本特征并跨帧复用，用于边缘设备上的指代表达理解。

**排除原因：** 该工作属检测器工程优化，核心是文本编码缓存与加速，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，仅「缓存复用」字样与关注点表面相似，缺少可供核查的实质借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ref-YOLO-World: Decoupling Text-Side Computation for Efficient Referring Expression Comprehension on Edge Devices

**abstract/A1：** Many high-accuracy referring expression comprehension (REC) methods produce image-dependent multimodal representations. These representations must be recomputed for every video frame, even when the referring expression remains unchanged. Fast open-vocabulary detectors such as YOLO-World instead pre-encode text once and reuse it throughout the stream. However, encoding each referring expression as a single embedding prevents explicit token-level correspondence. We propose Ref-YOLO-World, which separates text-side computation that depends only on the referring expression from image processing and reuses its outputs across frames. Two lightweight modules inject cached per-token text features into YOLO-World's detection path. Ref-YOLO-World achieves 81.29% Acc@0.5 on RefCOCOg val, the highest among REC models with a decoupled text branch, and consistently outperforms a fine-tuned YOLO-World. Precomputation reduces per-image inference time by 18.5%, yielding 47.0 FPS. A Core ML version runs at 15.73 FPS on an iPhone 15 Pro, demonstrating efficient on-device REC. Code is available at https://github.com/sakusaku3939/Ref-YOLO-World.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

### 290. [QuASAR: Toward an LLM-Assisted Framework for Screening, Prototyping, and Assessing Quantum-Advantage Opportunities in Scientific Computing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27778v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27778

**作者：** Mark L. Carroll、Jordan A. Caraballo-Vega、Brian P. Powell、John L. Schnase

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27778v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27778v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 QuASAR，一个由两个 LLM agent 组成的实验性流程，用于从天体物理论文中筛选可能具备量子算法对应物的计算核，独立核验引用，并为通过筛选的候选构建可运行原型与经典基线对比。

**排除原因：** 内容聚焦量子算法机会筛选与原型验证，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或 latent 生成；虽有 agent 引用核验与基准比较环节，但与 LVR 主线缺少可具体核查的方法或评价联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QuASAR: Toward an LLM-Assisted Framework for Screening, Prototyping, and Assessing Quantum-Advantage Opportunities in Scientific Computing

**abstract/A1：** Identifying scientific computations that could benefit from quantum algorithms requires both domain knowledge and fluency in quantum algorithm design, expertise that rarely resides in one person. We report results from QuASAR, an experimental, two-agent, LLM-driven pipeline intended to bridge this gap. One agent screens scientific literature for computational kernels with a plausible quantum-algorithmic analogue and proposes candidates, each backed by citations. A second agent independently verifies those citations. For candidates that survive verification, it reconstructs the source's computational structure, formulates a quantum mapping, builds and executes a prototype, and benchmarks it against classical baselines. We applied this pipeline to ten candidate computations drawn from the astrophysics literature. Nine candidates were carried through to a small-scale, benchmarked prototype. We report three in detail, chosen as the clearest examples with the least redundancy across the set. They span combinatorial optimization, stochastic simulation, and Bayesian inference. The pipeline attempted to supply quantum-design expertise a domain scientist might lack, reconstructing source algorithms where one existed and building original, disclosed mappings where none did. None of the three case studies achieved quantum advantage at the tested scales, and their astrophysical framing has not yet undergone expert validation. What the experiment does show is that the pipeline can turn a published idea into a runnable prototype, which a scientist can then inspect, test against classical methods, and decide whether to pursue. QuASAR is an exploratory framework, offered to the community for evaluation, critique, and further development, with an approach that could broaden the use of quantum computing in scientific research and extend to computational improvements more generally.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27799

**作者：** Naveen Sigalapalli、Huizhi Liang、Clara Crivellaro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27799v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27799v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ChildAI-Gov 系统，用 LLM 判断加规则检查、配合 RAG 将评分溯源到儿童权利条款，评估 EdTech 政策文本并构建 57 份政策的数据集。

**排除原因：** 属政策文本合规评分与文档溯源应用，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、latent 表示生成与使用、证据获取或停止决策等主线问题，也无可具体核查的方法迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChildAI-Gov: An Evidence-Linked AI System for Assessing EdTech Policies Against Children's Digital Rights

**abstract/A1：** We present ChildAI-Gov, an explainable AI system for assessing whether education-technology (EdTech) policies respect children's digital rights. Generative AI is reaching children's education faster than its governance can keep pace, yet checking a tool against children's rights remains manual, expert-dependent, and slow. We propose the Child Rights Impact Score (CRIS), an evidence-linked measure across five dimensions: safety, privacy, transparency, autonomy, and non-discrimination. ChildAI-Gov reads an EdTech tool's privacy or AI policy and scores each dimension by combining a large-language-model judgement (gpt-4o-mini) with a deterministic rule check. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) component grounds every score in the governing provisions, retrieved from a curated knowledge base of children's rights frameworks (the EU AI Act, UNICEF guidance, and others), so each score is tied to the evidence behind it and an auditor can trace which provision it rests on. We also construct AIPolicy-EdTech, a dataset of 57 real EdTech policies. On it, the five dimensions cohered as a single measure (Cronbach's alpha = 0.79), and grounding made scoring stricter while preserving rankings; an ungrounded baseline was far more lenient, passing nearly half the policies where ChildAI-Gov passed none. It offers evidence-linked decision support on documented commitments, not observation of real-world behaviour or determinations of legal compliance.

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究分布式训练中 local-update 方法的零样本超参数迁移（学习率随宽度、深度、worker 数与本地步数的缩放规则），属训练系统工程问题，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或动态计算决策，缺少可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $\\mathtt{DistP}$: Scaling Local-Update Training with Zero-Shot Hyperparameter Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Local-update methods offer a communication-efficient alternative to Distributed Data Parallelism ($\\mathtt{DDP}$), but introduce additional hyperparameters that require costly tuning. For $\\mathtt{DDP}$, zero-shot hyperparameter transfer reduces this cost by reusing settings tuned on smaller models, but its applicability to local-update training remains unclear. We propose $\\mathtt{DistP}$, a theoretical framework extending existing transfer rules from $\\mathtt{DDP}$ to local-update training with varying worker counts, local steps, and outer optimizers. With averaging as the outer optimizer, $\\mathtt{DistP}$ supports learning-rate transfer across width and depth while improving model quality. The tuned inner learning rate also directly transfers across synchronization intervals, but follows two regimes when increasing the global batch size: square-root learning-rate scaling for larger local batches and a fixed reference rate for additional workers. We then generalize width and depth transfer to other outer optimizers, enabling stable scaling with $\\mathtt{Adam}$. However, with momentum in the outer optimizer, changing batch size or local steps can require inner--outer learning-rate tuning, consistent with prior work. In language-modeling experiments at $823$M parameters, $\\mathtt{DistP}$ reduces validation loss by $8.1\\%$ and improves mean downstream accuracy by $10\\%$ relative to baselines without retuning at the larger scale. Fitted scaling laws suggest that this advantage grows with the compute budget, reinforcing the benefits of $\\mathtt{DistP}$ for communication-efficient training.

**comment/C1：** Preprint

</details>

### 293. [Integrating Generative Artificial Intelligence with Learning Style Models: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27868v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27868

**作者：** Malithi Nawarathne、Prasanna Haddela、Nuwan Kodagoda

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27868v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27868v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 一篇遵循 PRISMA 2020 的综述，梳理 41 项研究，探讨生成式 AI 与 VARK、Kolb 等学习风格模型的整合方式、实施案例与挑战。

**排除原因：** 属教育学中的学习风格适配应用综述，未涉及内部表示、latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可核查的方法借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Integrating Generative Artificial Intelligence with Learning Style Models: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions

**abstract/A1：** Generative Artificial Intelligence (Generative AI) has emerged as a powerful tool for transforming learning. This review examines how Generative AI can be integrated with established learning style models (VARK, Kolb, Felder-Silverman, and Honey &amp; Mumford) to support student engagement in large online classes. Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, we searched Scopus, IEEE Xplore, and Google Scholar (2015 to 2026) and included 41 studies that met the eligibility criteria. The review maps Generative AI capabilities to learning style preferences, synthesizes existing implementations, highlights benefits and challenges, and proposes pathways for future research and practice. Despite ongoing debates regarding the empirical validity of learning styles, Generative AI enables flexible, multimodal, and real-time adaptation. This study offers practical insights for educators and developers seeking to build more effective, learner centered learning systems

</details>

### 294. [Interpretable Tissue Architecture Complements Pathology Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27881v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27881

**作者：** Zeeshan Shahid、Hammam M. AlGhamdi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27881v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27881v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出139维细胞—群落组织学描述子，并将其与病理基础模型（CTransPath、PLIP）嵌入做特征级融合，用MIL评估其在ER/PR分型及肺癌亚型分类上的增量预测价值。

**排除原因：** 属于病理图像分类的特征工程与融合应用，不涉及推理期状态生成、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，仅泛泛说明可解释特征对基础模型表示有补充，缺少可供核查的具体LVR线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Tissue Architecture Complements Pathology Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Tissue architecture provides important information for histopathological assessment, yet its complementary predictive value alongside pathology foundation-model representations remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether interpretable measurements of cellular and multicellular tissue organization provide additional predictive information beyond learned representations. We introduce a 139-dimensional cellular-to-community representation comprising nine feature groups characterizing nuclear morphology, cellular composition, spatial connectivity, community organization, and cell-cell interactions. Their predictive contributions are systematically evaluated independently and through feature-level fusion with CTransPath and PLIP embeddings using multiple-instance learning (MIL). On the BCNB dataset, estrogen receptor (ER) and progesterone receptor (PR) classification is evaluated across four MIL architectures using five-fold patient-disjoint cross-validation. Selected CTransPath fusion configurations achieved mean ROC-AUC values of 0.9144 (ER) and 0.8360 (PR), while PLIP fusion achieved 0.8791 and 0.8015, respectively. Patient-level analyses identified ER-associated differences in nuclear morphology, graph connectivity, cellular communities, and cell-cell interactions. On LungHist700, selected fusion configurations achieved higher mean ROC-AUC across four classification tasks, including adenocarcinoma versus squamous cell carcinoma (0.7967 versus 0.7137 for CTransPath alone). Overall, these findings suggest that interpretable tissue-architecture descriptors can complement foundation-model representations while providing measurable insights into histopathological organization.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Submitted to Biomedical Signal Processing and Control

</details>

### 295. [FairITR: Fairness-Aware Learning of Multicategory Individualized Treatment Rules](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27894v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27894

**作者：** Zeyu Chen、Wei Qian、Bintong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27894v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27894v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FairITR：面向多类别治疗的公平个体化治疗规则学习框架，基于角度分类，结合对抗去偏与协方差正则，在优化治疗收益的同时约束决策与敏感属性独立。

**排除原因：** 该文属因果推断与精准医疗中的公平决策研究，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，也未提供可核查的 latent 表示或推理期状态生成线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FairITR: Fairness-Aware Learning of Multicategory Individualized Treatment Rules

**abstract/A1：** Individualized treatment rules (ITRs) recommend treatment decisions based on individual characteristics and can improve treatment outcomes beyond traditional one-size-fits-all strategies. However, similar to other commonly used algorithmic decision-making methods, ITRs without considering model fairness may result in unfair decisions across groups defined by sensitive attributes, such as race or gender. Although extensive methods have been developed for ITR estimation, most focus solely on optimizing treatment outcomes, while research on fairness-aware ITRs remains limited. Moreover, the practical scenario of multicategory treatments is rarely considered in existing fair ITR studies. To address these gaps, we introduce a fairness notion for multicategory ITRs that requires treatment decisions to be independent of sensitive attributes, and then formulate the corresponding problem of estimating fair ITRs. To solve this problem, we further propose FairITR, a fairness-aware learning framework that jointly optimizes treatment outcomes and fairness. FairITR builds on angle-based classification for multicategory ITR learning and integrates adversarial learning with covariance-based regularization to mitigate unfairness in treatment decisions. A theoretical justification for the fairness properties induced by the adversarial debiasing mechanism is also provided. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FairITR substantially reduces unfairness in ITRs while maintaining competitive treatment values relative to existing baselines.

</details>

### 296. [The Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework: Scalable Multimodal UX Attribution in Web Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27899v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27899

**作者：** Yi Ding、Gaurav Kumar、Rui Min、Isabella Ye、Bhavika Goyal、Emilio Antunez、Huihui Chen、Ke Xu、Xiaowei Sun、Xin Xu、Huijie Feng、Pradyumna Narayana、Golnaz Farhadi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27899v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27899v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出双层情感框架用于网页搜索的用户体验归因：上层用微调 LLM 对遥测特征做情感回归，下层用 MLLM 评估结果块的视觉渲染与空间布局并量化「User Toil」。

**排除原因：** 属于工业级 UX 评测与归因应用，MLLM 仅作布局质量打分，未涉及视觉细节在内部状态中的保留、latent 推理状态生成或视觉证据筛选，缺乏可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework: Scalable Multimodal UX Attribution in Web Search

**abstract/A1：** Sustaining user experience (UX) quality in large-scale web search requires scalable evaluation. Traditional surveys are slow and bottleneck rapid A/B testing, while automated metrics often lack the granularity to diagnose visual-spatial UX anomalies. To address this, we introduce the Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework, a novel industrial architecture for automated UX attribution. Tier 1 (Macro System) acts as a scalable user sentiment regression detector, utilizing a fine-tuned encoder-decoder LLM (based on Gemini XS) on telemetry features. Through multi-task regularization and contextual feature engineering, this tier achieves a +9.8% PR-AUC gain on over 500,000 held-out records, matching baseline recall on all validated regressions with $\\sim$20% relative precision gain among the 12 held-out experiments. In production, it reduces measurement latency from weeks to hours and cuts survey overhead by over 40%. Tier 2 (Micro System) provides granular diagnostics by employing a Multimodal LLM (Gemini 3.0 Pro) to natively assess visual renderings and spatial layout of web result blocks, quantifying "User Toil" as the estimated effort required to reach a satisfactory result. Enhanced by RL-based automated prompt optimization and an AI judge for win/loss patterns, the MLLM yields a $&gt;$6.5% F1 score improvement, offering deep insights into content quality and layout friction. Ultimately, this framework enables scalable, accurate, and diagnostic UX attribution, processing O(100k) live experiment queries daily.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.27905

**作者：** Junjie Chu、Ye Leng、Mingjie Li、Yiting Qu、Michael Backes、Yun Shen、Yang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27905v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27905v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了针对拟人化非人类角色的仇恨图像基准，评测文本生图、图像编辑与对话式生图系统，并评估多种仇恨内容检测器及两种缓解方案。

**排除原因：** 属内容安全与图像生成/检测研究，虽有用角色到人类表示的映射提升分类，但不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AnimalFarm: Auditing the Generation and Detection of Hateful Images Featuring Anthropomorphic Characters

**abstract/A1：** Anthropomorphic non-human characters have long been used to convey hateful messages targeting human groups, from Nazi-era propaganda to recent AI-generated imagery on social media. However, the safety risks posed by such imagery remain largely unexplored. We thus conduct the first systematic study of hateful image generation and detection involving anthropomorphic non-human characters. We construct a benchmark from conservatively screened source scenes, covering five character types and three image generation tasks: text-to-image generation, image editing, and conversational image generation. Across 14,250 scheduled queries, image generation systems produce images for 92.1\\% of queries, of which 95.9\\% contain hateful content. We further collect 4,370 hateful images and evaluate five popular hateful-content detectors. Even the strongest detector, GPT-5.5, identifies only 73.9\\% of hateful images, while most detect fewer than 58\\%. Our analysis reveals that existing detectors often misclassify hateful images as benign when their main subjects are non-human characters. We explore two effective mitigation approaches: a training-free pipeline that maps non-human characters back to human representations before classification and a detector fine-tuned for this type of hateful content. These approaches achieve F1 scores of 0.962 and 0.979, respectively. Our findings expose significant safety gaps in both image generation and hateful-content detection. Disclaimer: This paper contains hateful text and imagery.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 7 figures, 13 tables

</details>

Reis、Sven H. Giese、Roberto Sordillo、Victor García Satorras、Eray Inanc、Kacper Kapuśniak、Soojung Yang、Rishwanth Raghu、Rachel Kim、Gabriel Dernbach、Deniz Gunceler、Hannes Schulz、Bonnie Kruft、Marwin Segler、Christopher M. Bishop、Cecilia Clementi、Ryota Tomioka、Frank Noé

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27924v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27924v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** BioEmu2 是面向生物分子体系的生成模型，统一做静态结构预测、平衡态采样与时间演化，并在多组分体系上以远快于分子动力学的速度近似 MD 分布与动力学。

**排除原因：** 该工作属科学计算领域的具体应用，其生成式采样与偏置势引导未涉及内部状态表示、视觉证据使用或推理决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BioEmu2: Scalable Emulation of Biomolecular Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Despite major advances in structure prediction, modelling the motions and interactions that drive biomolecular function at scale remains a central challenge. We introduce BioEmu2, a generative model for atomically resolved biomolecular structures combining static structure prediction, equilibrium sampling and time evolution, that samples orders of magnitude faster than molecular dynamics (MD). Trained on experimental and synthetic structures and extensive MD data, it supports multi-component systems containing proteins, nucleic acids, modified residues, ligands and ions. Structure-prediction accuracy is comparable to top open-weight biomolecular folding models, and a context parallel mode enables prediction of megadalton complexes. Equilibrium distributions approximate MD references with free-energy errors near 1 kcal/mol across tested proteins and complexes. Time propagation predicts long-timescale dynamical trajectories and kinetics of binding and conformational changes. Generated structures can be controlled by conditioning on residue contacts or steering with user-defined bias potentials. Enhanced sampling enables free energy calculation involving rare states. BioEmu2 makes the study of biomolecular ensembles and dynamics scalable.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27970v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27970v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文整理了一个儿童青少年 ADHD 分类的 EEG 数据集，统一预处理与时间窗，发布 115 种特征定义及质量记录，并给出 XGBoost 的可复现示例评估。

**排除原因：** 属于临床 EEG 信号分类的数据集与特征工程工作，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选或推理决策，未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Complete Feature Set for ADHD Classification in Children and Adolescents Based on EEG Signals

**abstract/A1：** Attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder is a neurodevelopmental condition that can affect attention, activity and impulse control. Electroencephalography (EEG) records brain activity as children and adolescents perform cognitive tasks. However, comparing features derived from these recordings requires consistent signal preparation, shared analysis windows and documented quality decisions. We curated 669 existing BALLADEER recordings from 156 participants across five task conditions so that researchers can compare seven EEG feature families on the same time intervals for each participant and task. The dataset links continuous signals to precomputed measurements from 115 feature definitions or templates and records the quality decisions made for each recording. Analyses must still account for repeated measurements within participants, residual artefacts and differences between acquisition systems. Technical validation covered signal integrity, numerical checks on feature estimators and completeness of the released files. In every task, the median proportion of recording time retained after preprocessing was 100%. To illustrate reuse, we report saved participant-held-out evaluations comparing XGBoost using all seven families on eight-second windows with a predictor based only on recruitment-group prevalence in the training fold. Recruitment-group discrimination was stronger in Robots than in the three Slackline conditions. These descriptive results from overlapping cohorts do not establish diagnostic validity. The dataset, curation software and aggregate results for the predictive example are publicly available.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 7 figures. EEG dataset (v1.0.0): https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21982978 EEG preprocessing and feature extraction software with XGBoost example (v1.1.0): https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24399993

</details>
